Elucidating the Design Space of LM Data Attribution
揭示大语言模型数据归属的设计空间
Data attribution methods for Language Models (LMs) are typically evaluated using downstream tasks like fact retrieval. However, existing evaluations vary multiple dataset properties simultaneously, making it impossible to isolate exactly why an attribution algorithm fails. To diagnose these failures, we explore the design space of LLM data attribution, with a primary focus on evaluation. We introduce the Controlled Attribution Benchmark (CATT) for fact tracing, in which we fine-tune LLMs on entirely fictitious knowledge, providing unambiguous ground truth for attribution. This synthetic setup allows us to systematically alter one dataset configuration at a time, such as document redundancy and lexical distractors. With this configurable evaluation, we demonstrate that redundancy and distractors consistently degrade attribution performance. Rather than proposing a new estimator, we examine two design choices that are underexplored: the loss used to compute gradients and how per-token attribution scores are aggregated into a document score. We find that the true causal evidence within a training document is highly localized, but standard methods dilute this evidence into noise by averaging attribution scores across the entire document. We find that replacing standard cross-entropy loss gradients with the negative log-odds gradients surfaces the critical tokens from other uninformative ones. We can then aggregate only the top token scores to improve ground truth recall by 35 to 72 percentage points across multiple baselines on our diagnostic benchmark. The negative log-odds correction also generalizes to FTRACE-TREX, improving counterfactual retraining performance for every method we test, by up to 4.9x.
中文翻译
语言模型（LM）的数据归属方法通常通过事实检索等下游任务进行评估。然而，现有评估同时改变多个数据集属性，无法准确判断归属算法失败的原因。为诊断这些失败，我们探索了大语言模型数据归属的设计空间，重点关注评估方法。我们提出了用于事实追踪的可控归属基准（CATT），在该基准中，我们在完全虚构的知识上微调大语言模型，为归属提供明确的真实标签。这种合成设置使我们能够系统性地逐个改变数据集配置，如文档冗余和词汇干扰项。通过这一可配置的评估，我们证明冗余和干扰项会持续降低归属性能。我们并未提出新的估计器，而是考察了两个被忽视的设计选择：用于计算梯度的损失函数，以及如何将每个词的归属分数聚合为文档分数。我们发现训练文档中的真实因果证据高度集中，但标准方法通过平均整个文档的归属分数，将这些证据稀释为噪声。我们发现，用负对数几率梯度替代标准交叉熵梯度，能够凸显关键词并抑制无信息词。随后仅聚合最高得分的词，可在我们的诊断基准上将真实标签召回率提升35至72个百分点。负对数几率修正还推广到FTRACE-TREX，使所有测试方法的反事实重训练性能提升最多达4.9倍。
👤 Steven Yu, Sheng-Yu Wang, Kimia Hamidieh, Antonio Torralba, David Bau 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Testimony from Timelines: Evidence-Grounded Significance Testing of Temporal Clinical Hypotheses
时间线中的证词：基于证据的时间临床假说显著性检验
Ask a language model whether sales tend to dip after a product recall, or whether one side effect tends to follow another after a vaccine, and it answers in a fluent sentence that reads like a finding, with no test behind it. Assertions about how events unfold across many cases fill the Web, and each is a temporal hypothesis that only a significance test over the right records can settle. The verifier must specify the null, select the test and decide which records bear on the hypothesis, and any of these choices can go wrong while the conclusion still comes out right. Yet benchmarks for language-model data analysis grade only the conclusion, with the relevant data handed over. We study the problem in health, where a wrong verdict is costly, and present STRATA, a benchmark that grades every decision on the way to a verdict: 8,735 hypotheses over the clinical timelines of vaccine adverse-event reports, with gold plans, evidence sets, p-values and verdicts derived under a released codebook and reproduced by a deterministic oracle. We also propose ART (articulate, retrieve, test), a staged paradigm that exposes each decision as an inspectable output. Extensive experiments with five model configurations show that systems fail mostly at choosing which records count, a verdict-evidence gap that accuracy alone conceals. ART closes most of this gap and brings a 31B open model to within 0.01 of the frontier models under the same paradigm; the frontier models succeed once given the codebook.
中文翻译
询问语言模型销售是否在产品召回后下降，或某种副作用是否在疫苗接种后常伴随另一种出现，模型会给出流畅的句子，看似结论，却无任何统计检验支撑。关于事件在多案例中如何展开的断言充斥网络，每一项都是只有在正确记录上进行显著性检验才能确定的时间假说。验证者必须指定零假设、选择检验方法并决定哪些记录与假说相关，这些任一选择出错都可能导致错误结论，但结果仍可能正确。然而，当前语言模型数据分析的基准仅评价最终结论，相关数据已预先提供。我们在医疗领域研究此问题，因为错误判决代价高昂，提出STRATA基准，对通向判决的每一个决策进行评分：8,735个关于疫苗不良事件报告临床时间线的假说，包含金标准计划、证据集、p值和判决，均基于公开代码书生成，并由确定性归因器复现。我们还提出ART（阐述、检索、检验）范式，将每个决策暴露为可检查的输出。五种模型配置的大量实验表明，系统主要在选择哪些记录相关上失败，造成判决与证据之间的差距，而仅靠准确率无法察觉。ART弥合了大部分差距，使31B开源模型在相同范式下接近前沿模型表现（差距仅0.01）；前沿模型在获得代码书后才取得成功。
👤 Chengyang He, Wenlong Zhang, Koduvayur Subbalakshmi, Ping Wang 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

IndicIPR-QA: A Benchmark for Extractive Question Answering on Indian IPR Laws
IndicIPR-QA：印度知识产权法提取式问答基准
Legal Question Answering (QA) over statutory texts is a challenging task in Natural Language Processing (NLP), especially in low-resource settings with complex legal systems. It requires answers that are precise and grounded in the law. However, to the best of current knowledge, no existing extractive QA benchmark provides exact answer spans from statutory texts across all seven Indian Intellectual Property Rights (IPR) Acts. This paper presents IndicIPR-QA1, an extractive QA benchmark for Indian IPR laws. The proposed dataset contains 4,462 QA pairs from 1,116 statutory contexts across seven Acts. Questions and answers are generated using GPT-4o and validated through nine automatic quality checks, single and multi-model LLM evaluations, and a 200-pair human audit. Section-level partitioning prevents source-context overlap across the training, validation, and test sets. Further analysis examines answer span length, lexical overlap, and multi-clause structure across the corpus and individual Acts. BM25 achieves a token-level F1 score of 20.21, while fine-tuned InLegalBERT reaches 85.02 under a unified sliding-window inference protocol. The large performance gap between BM25 (20.21 F1) and InLegalBERT (85.02 F1) shows that simple word matching is not enough to answer these legal questions. Performance also varies across the seven Acts, indicating substantial differences in answer-extraction performance.
中文翻译
在法定文本上进行法律问答（QA）是自然语言处理（NLP）中的挑战性任务，尤其在资源匮乏且法律体系复杂的环境下。该任务要求答案精确且基于法律条文。然而，据当前所知，尚无现有的提取式QA基准能覆盖全部七部印度知识产权法（IPR）的法定文本中的精确答案片段。本文提出IndicIPR-QA1，一个针对印度知识产权法的提取式问答基准。该数据集包含来自七部法律共1,116个法定上下文的4,462对问答。问题与答案由GPT-4o生成，并通过九项自动质量检查、单模型与多模型LLM评估以及200对人工审计进行验证。按章节划分避免了训练、验证与测试集之间的源上下文重叠。进一步分析考察了答案片段长度、词汇重叠度及多分句结构在整体语料库与各法律条文中的分布。BM25在标记级F1得分为20.21，而微调后的InLegalBERT在统一滑动窗口推理协议下达到85.02。BM25（20.21 F1）与InLegalBERT（85.02 F1）之间显著的性能差距表明，简单的词匹配不足以回答此类法律问题。不同法律条文间的性能差异也表明，答案提取能力存在显著差异。
👤 M. Mohaiminul Islam, Muhammad Abulaish 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

How I Aligned Your Model: Scaling Up Multilingual Safety Training with Templates
如何对齐你的模型：通过模板实现多语言安全训练的规模化扩展
Safety alignment for Large Language Models is heavily focused on English with limited multilingual representation, particularly damaging low-resource language performance. Related open training datasets are often limited in size or do not follow region-localized harmful topics to broaden the scope of alignment beyond cultures. We propose a scalable template-based augmentation pipeline to produce massive multilingual refusal and benign corpora, extracting safe templates from harmful prompts, translating them and then filling in the slots with culturally authentic entities, both harmful and benign. As a result, we release MSafeTemp, a validated corpus spanning 60 languages useful for multilingual safety alignment and evaluation. Using the dataset, we show that English-only evaluation misjudges multilingual safety: for several models, attacks that fail in English succeed once translated, while culturally localized variants expose further failures that translation alone misses. We also confirm that augmenting safety training data through templates allows for higher diversity and quality of alignment, resulting in improved safety performance beyond English. MSafeTemp and the respective pipeline are aimed at facilitating research and development of safer and more responsible models for less-represented cultures.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的安全对齐目前高度集中于英语，对多语言覆盖有限，尤其损害低资源语言的表现。相关开源训练数据集通常规模较小，或未涵盖区域化有害主题，导致对齐范围局限于特定文化。我们提出一种可扩展的基于模板的增强流程，用于生成大规模多语言拒绝与良性语料库，通过从有害提示中提取安全模板，翻译后用文化上真实的实体（包括有害与良性）填充槽位。由此，我们发布了MSafeTemp，一个经验证的跨60种语言的语料库，可用于多语言安全对齐与评估。利用该数据集，我们发现仅以英语评估会误判多语言安全性：对于多个模型，原本在英语中失败的攻击在翻译后成功，而文化本地化变体暴露出翻译本身无法捕捉的更多失败。我们还证实，通过模板增强安全训练数据可提升对齐的多样性与质量，从而在英语之外也实现更优的安全表现。MSafeTemp及其相应流程旨在促进对代表性不足文化更安全、更负责任模型的研究与开发。
👤 Max Neuwinger, Hanna Yukhymenko, Imanol Schlag 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.40488]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
rTaste: Do Research Agents Have Taste? Evaluating Early Stage Research Judgment
rTaste：研究智能体是否有‘品味’？评估早期阶段的研究判断力
Do research agents have taste, the judgment of which experiments are worth running before their outcomes are known? We give six frontier models seven recent NLP papers. Each model, working as a research agent alone or as a team, proposes minimum viable experiments (MVEs), forecasts whether each will pass a threshold it sets and ranks them. A separate executor then attempts every MVE, including those ranked last, and three model auditors judge the results. Of 415 MVEs, 194 are judged sound, valuable and new, but fewer than half of these (73) are also judged beyond the seed paper and non obvious. The agents' first choices match the auditors' most valued experiment more often than a random pick, but are not shown to improve on choosing the experiment written first. Their forecasts beat a base rate but are not measurably more accurate than those of a fresh instance of the same model given only the paper and registrations. Teams cost more without a clear overall improvement in proposal quality. The agents produce many worthwhile experiments, but the evidence that their rankings improve the choice of which to run remains limited. The evaluation harness and a dataset of all 415 MVEs, with their forecasts and audited outcomes, are available at https://github.com/ArtKanke/rTaste.
中文翻译
研究智能体是否具备‘品味’——即在实验结果未知前判断哪些实验值得开展的能力？我们向六个前沿模型展示了七篇近期NLP论文。每个模型作为独立的研究智能体或团队，提出最小可行实验（MVE），预测每个实验是否能通过其设定的阈值，并对它们进行排序。随后，一个独立执行器尝试执行所有MVE，包括排名最低的，三位模型审计员对结果进行评判。在415个MVE中，有194个被判定为合理、有价值且新颖，但其中仅有73个同时被认为超越了初始论文且非显而易见。智能体的首选项比随机选择更接近审计员最看重的实验，但并未显示出优于直接选择论文首提实验的效果。它们的预测表现优于基线，但其准确性并未显著优于仅提供论文和注册信息的新实例。团队协作成本更高，且提案质量无明显提升。智能体生成了许多有价值的实验，但证据表明其排序并未显著改善实验选择。评估工具包及包含所有415个MVE及其预测和审计结果的数据集已公开于：https://github.com/ArtKanke/rTaste。
👤 Art Kanke 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Decision and System One models in rheumatology: accuracy and calibration on specialty-training examination questions and diagnostic case vignettes
风湿病学中决策与系统一体模型的准确性与校准：在专科培训考试题和诊断案例中的表现
Objectives: To evaluate the accuracy and calibration of Decision and System One models on rheumatology examination questions and diagnostic vignettes. Methods: Two benchmarks were analysed: 172 rheumatology questions from Spanish specialty-training examinations (expanded RheumaMIR dataset) in Spanish and English, and 70 English diagnostic vignettes from RheumBench with 5-18 candidate diagnoses, 52 of which included the reference diagnosis. Four System One models (Jev 1.13, Clef, Microsoft-Decision-1, Laya) and GPT-6 Luna (OpenAI Decisions API) were compared with GPT-6 Sol and two local generative models. The primary outcome was accuracy; secondary outcomes included calibration, discrimination, accuracy of answers with probability >=0.90 and language differences. Results: In RheumaMIR, Jev's accuracy was 93.0% in Spanish and 91.9% in English, below GPT-6 Sol (99.4%; Holm-adjusted P<=0.005); GPT-6 Luna reached 97.7%, Clef 91.9-93.0% and Microsoft-Decision-1 86.0-87.8%, without detectable language differences. Jev's answers with probability >=0.90 (76-77% of answers) were 98.5% correct. In RheumBench, accuracy was lower: GPT-6 Sol 76.9%, GPT-6 Luna 63.5%, Clef 59.6%, Jev 50.0% and Microsoft-Decision-1 40.4% (each System One model below GPT-6 Sol, Holm-adjusted P<=0.045; chance 9.6%). Jev became overconfident (mean probability 0.659; over/underconfidence index +0.159), and its answers with probability >=0.90 (25% of cases) were 84.6% correct, against 95.5% for GPT-6 Luna (42%). Laya performed near chance in both benchmarks. Conclusion: The best-performing System One models were highly accurate on examination questions but less accurate on diagnostic vignettes, remaining below GPT-6 Sol. Jev's high-confidence predictions were less reliable on vignettes, highlighting the need for task-specific evaluation and calibration.
中文翻译
目的：评估决策与系统一体模型在风湿病学考试题目和诊断案例中的准确性和校准情况。方法：分析了两个基准数据集：来自西班牙专科培训考试的172道风湿病学题目（扩展的RheumaMIR数据集，含西班牙语和英语版本），以及来自RheumBench的70个英文诊断案例，每个案例有5-18个候选诊断，其中52个包含参考诊断。比较了四种系统一体模型（Jev 1.13、Clef、Microsoft-Decision-1、Laya）与GPT-6 Luna（OpenAI Decisions API）及GPT-6 Sol和两个本地生成模型。主要结果为准确性；次要结果包括校准性、区分度、置信度≥0.90的答案准确性以及语言差异。结果：在RheumaMIR中，Jev在西班牙语中的准确率为93.0%，英语中为91.9%，低于GPT-6 Sol（99.4%；Holm校正P≤0.005）；GPT-6 Luna达到97.7%，Clef为91.9%-93.0%，Microsoft-Decision-1为86.0%-87.8%，未发现显著语言差异。Jev在置信度≥0.90的预测中（占答案的76-77%）正确率达98.5%。在RheumBench中，准确率较低：GPT-6 Sol为76.9%，GPT-6 Luna为63.5%，Clef为59.6%，Jev为50.0%，Microsoft-Decision-1为40.4%（所有系统一体模型均低于GPT-6 Sol，Holm校正P≤0.045；随机水平为9.6%）。Jev表现出过度自信（平均置信度0.659；高/低自信指数+0.159），其置信度≥0.90的预测（占25%案例）正确率为84.6%，而GPT-6 Luna为95.5%（占42%）。Laya在两个基准上均接近随机水平。结论：表现最佳的系统一体模型在考试题目中具有高准确性，但在诊断案例中表现较差，仍低于GPT-6 Sol。Jev在案例中的高置信度预测可靠性较低，凸显了任务特定评估与校准的必要性。
👤 Alfredo Madrid-García, Diego Benavent, Fabian Lechner, Phillip Kremer, Johannes Knitza et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Judged in Company: In-Context Batching Makes LLM Verdicts Depend on Other Items
在群体中被评判：上下文批处理使大语言模型的判断依赖于其他项目
To save cost, practitioners often ask a language model to grade many independent items in one prompt. This assumes that verdicts are separable: that the verdict on an item does not depend on the other items in the prompt or on its position among them. Across three judgement tasks (harm in social-media comments, short-answer grading and review star ratings) and five open-weight models with 4-9B parameters, assigning the same 2,000 items to different random batches of 25 changes 11-22% of decisions on harm and grading, 6.5-13.3 points more than renaming the item keys or rewording the instruction, while AUROC against human labels differs from single-item grading only by -0.033 to +0.010; for four models with 14-32B parameters, 6-15% of decisions change, 4.2-9.1 points more. We show that flip rates decompose exactly into components of order, slot and membership, and that under a threshold model they grow with the square root of the variance that the context contributes, so that effects that are small for scores are large for decisions. Most of the latent dissimilarity between partitions comes from the order of the neighbours and the slot. The main effect of which items share the prompt, the part that voting over permutations of a batch cannot remove, accounts for 0.11-0.22 of it, and majority votes over nine permutations still leave 52-60% of the flips. Batched verdicts also differ systematically from single-item verdicts: even the majority of fifteen random batches disagrees with them on 8.4-17.8% of decisions. Editing a single generation shows that both the model's own earlier verdicts and the neighbours' texts carry the effect. On the easy task, star ratings, only 2.9-3.9% of decisions change. We recommend grading ambiguous items one per prompt, recovering most of the cost with a cached prefix, and reporting agreement with single-item grading whenever items are batched.
中文翻译
为节省成本，从业者常在一个提示中要求语言模型对多个独立项目进行评分。这一做法假设判断是可分离的：即某项目的判断不受提示中其他项目或其位置的影响。在三个判断任务（社交媒体评论中的危害性、简答评分和评论星级评定）及五个参数量为4-9B的开源模型上，将相同的2000个项目分配到不同的25个随机批次中，导致危害性和评分决策变化11%-22%，比重命名项目键或改写指令带来的变化高出6.5%-13.3分；与单项目评分相比，AUROC仅变化-0.033至+0.010。对于四个参数量为14-32B的模型，6%-15%的决策发生变化，变化幅度达4.2-9.1分。我们证明翻转率可精确分解为顺序、槽位和成员关系三部分，并在阈值模型下，其增长与上下文贡献方差的平方根成正比，因此对分数影响小的效应在决策层面却显著。大部分分区间的潜在差异源于邻居的顺序和槽位。共享提示的项目组合这一主要影响因素——即通过批量排列投票也无法消除的部分——占总差异的0.11-0.22，即使对九种排列取多数投票，仍有52%-60%的翻转残留。批处理判断与单项目判断存在系统性差异：即使十五个随机批次的多数意见，也有8.4%-17.8%的决策与单项目判断不一致。编辑单次生成结果表明，模型自身的先前判断和邻近文本均携带影响。在简单任务（星级评分）中，仅有2.9%-3.9%的决策发生变化。我们建议对模糊项目逐个提示评分，通过缓存前缀恢复大部分成本，并在批处理时报告与单项目评分的一致性。
👤 Peng Xie, Amr Alanwar 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

ScienceJudge: Pattern Perturbation Augmentation for Sentence-Level Scientific Claim Verification
ScienceJudge：用于句子级科学主张验证的模式扰动增强方法
Sentence-level scientific claim verification determines whether each sentence in an AI-generated scientific paragraph is supported by a given multimodal evidence bundle. Fine-grained reporting errors often preserve fluent and plausible statements while introducing subtle evidence-claim deviations, making them difficult to detect under sparse and imbalanced supervision. We propose Pattern Perturbation Augmentation (PPA), which models reporting errors as label-specific faithfulness breaking transformations and induces reusable error patterns from labeled examples. PPA applies these patterns to supported claims to construct controlled hard negatives and expands their coverage through pattern-level, operation-level, and cross-domain augmentation. Based on these augmented views, we develop ScienceJudge, which integrates PPA-trained vision-language predictors and instruction-guided LLM predictors through class-weighted ensemble inference. On the local development set, ScienceJudge improves the average score from 0.8124 to 0.8692, achieving a Macro-F1 of 0.8466 and a Paragraph Exact Match score of 0.8919. Our submitted system ranks first on the official NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 1 leaderboard with a final score of 0.6890.
中文翻译

Can LLMs Ignore What They Know? Measuring Knowledge Leakage in Persona-Based User Simulation
大语言模型能否忽略其所知？基于人物设定的用户模拟中的知识泄露测量
When persona-conditioned responses are read as respondent-level simulations, each response should reflect the described persona's knowledge, and the respondent written to know nothing must act as if the model's knowledge were not there. We test whether it can, and find persistent favourable-item real-twin rating separation under the tested low-knowledge and explicit-ignorance prompts. We define the resulting problem (knowledge leakage: a response bias that arises when knowledge the real user could not have enters the simulated response) and present a methodology for measuring it quantitatively. The core instrument pairs real items with fabricated twins matched in surface form: screened fabricated variants designed to minimize prior item-specific knowledge. The paired difference therefore provides a diagnostic contrast for knowledge leakage even in rating tasks where no answer is correct. In a rating experiment across three knowledge-handling arms and five API endpoints from two model families, we find, first, that the personas we test do not fully suppress real-item existence and familiarity signals: a favourable-item real-twin rating contrast remains under an explicit in-context statement that the persona has never heard of the item. Second, the unfavourable-item contrast reverses sign across domain groups: pooling all unfavourable-content items across domains the contrast sits near zero (+0.077 [-0.057, +0.204]), yet the same estimator in the same conditions returns -0.359 where the rating scale asks about an item's documented hazard and +0.438 where it asks about a company or an organisation. A pooled analysis of unfavourable-content items against zero therefore does not detect the opposing domain-group differences. All estimates are conditional on the adequacy of twin construction and screening; human low-knowledge baselines remain necessary for population-level interpretation.
中文翻译
当以人物设定响应作为应答者层面的模拟时，每个回应应反映所描述人物的知识，而应答者若被设定为一无所知，则应表现得如同模型的知识不存在一般。我们测试了这种可能性，发现即使在低知识和显式无知提示下，仍存在有利项目与真实孪生项目的评分差异。我们定义了由此产生的问题（知识泄露：当真实用户不可能知晓的知识进入模拟回应时产生的响应偏差），并提出一种定量测量方法。核心工具是将真实项目与其表面形式匹配的虚构孪生项目配对：经过筛选的虚构变体旨在最小化先验项目特定知识。因此，成对差异提供了即使在无正确答案的评分任务中也能诊断知识泄露的对比依据。在跨三个知识处理分支及来自两个模型家族的五个API端点的评分实验中，我们发现：第一，我们测试的人物设定未能完全抑制真实项目存在性和熟悉性信号：即使在明确声明人物从未听过该项目的上下文中，仍有有利项目的真实-孪生评分差异。第二，不利项目评分差异在不同领域组间反转符号：将所有不利内容项目合并分析时，差异接近零（+0.077 [-0.057, +0.204]），但在同一条件下，当评分尺度涉及项目已知危害时，估计值为-0.359；当涉及公司或组织时，估计值为+0.438。对不利内容项目与零的合并分析无法检测到相反的领域组差异。所有估计均依赖于孪生构造与筛选的充分性；人类低知识基线对于群体层面解释仍必不可少。
👤 Sangmin Lee, Sangwon Lee, Sungbeom Choi, Jeeseung Park, Kibeom Myoung 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Tokenizer Tokens Tell Training Tales
分词器标记揭示训练故事
Tokenizers are usually treated as fixed preprocessing tools, but they are trained on text and can retain traces of that text in their vocabularies. We ask whether a BPE tokenizer can reveal if a candidate dataset was part of its training corpus. The key observation is simple. Rare strings become tokens only when enough corpus frequency pushes them over the merge threshold. This suggests that membership signal should appear in rare tokens used by the candidate dataset, rather than in the order in which merges were made. We formalize this under a threshold model of BPE inclusion, showing that the likelihood-ratio test assigns its largest weights to tokens that are rare in the background but frequent in the candidate dataset. The same model suggests that merge rank and merge-graph position should not add a first-order membership signal once token existence and dataset frequency are known. We instantiate this view as RTAM-Fusion, which calibrates rare-token mass with shadow tokenizers and combines signals across rarity levels. On the tokenizer membership benchmark, RTAM-Fusion improves AUC and balanced accuracy over Vocabulary Overlap while using fewer shadow tokenizers. Existing defenses also continue to leak membership signal against adaptive attackers at production vocabulary sizes. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RTAM-Fusion-F7EE/
中文翻译
分词器通常被视为固定的预处理工具，但实际上它们是在文本上训练的，其词汇表中可能保留了训练文本的痕迹。我们探讨了一个BPE分词器是否能揭示某个候选数据集是否曾是其训练语料库的一部分。关键观察是：只有当某个稀有字符串的语料库频率足够高，使其超过合并阈值时，才会被转化为一个标记。这表明，成员身份信号应出现在候选数据集中使用的稀有标记中，而非合并顺序中。我们基于BPE包含的阈值模型对此进行了形式化分析，发现似然比检验将最大权重分配给那些在背景语料中稀有但在候选数据集中频繁出现的标记。同一模型还表明，一旦已知标记存在性和数据集频率，合并排名和合并图位置不应提供一阶成员身份信号。我们据此提出了RTAM-Fusion方法，通过影子分词器校准稀有标记的分布，并融合不同稀有度层级的信号。在分词器成员身份基准测试中，RTAM-Fusion在AUC和平衡准确率方面优于词汇重叠法，且使用的影子分词器更少。现有防御措施在生产级词汇量下仍对自适应攻击存在泄露风险。
👤 Nishita Jain, Parth Upman, Shreyank N Gowda 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

When the Top Answer Decides: Gender Bias in Decision Models
当最优答案决定一切：决策模型中的性别偏见
Decision models are language models built to route, classify and triage: they return option probabilities from one forward pass, and an application acts on the top answer. They are offered off the shelf, trained for the decision format rather than for fairness or any one application, and we found no bias measurement in their cards. We test TypeSafe's closed Jev and five open decision models on gender-swapped yes/no decisions about people (Tamkin et al., 2023), BBQ, StereoSet and the gender-bias studies of Fulgu and Capraro (2024). Five of the six give women significantly more favourable answers than men (+0.05 to +0.10 in log-odds), and a gender swap changes 1-3.5% of top answers (Jev's partly through its call-to-call variation). The makers' recommended confidence gate removes many biased commitments but raises the stereotyped share of those it keeps in ambiguous BBQ gender questions (granite-switch 0.76 to 0.85, pplx 0.81 to 1.00). Probes show gender preferences in the same models: all six rate violence for a greater good as more acceptable against a man than against a woman, and two default to "girl" when forced to guess a child's gender without a cue. Decision-level and probability-level measures differ, so audits should read a model as its application does.
中文翻译
决策模型是专为路由、分类和优先排序设计的语言模型：它们通过一次前向传播返回选项概率，应用程序则依据最高概率的答案采取行动。这些模型作为现成产品提供，其训练目标是决策格式本身，而非公平性或特定应用场景，且我们未在其文档中发现任何偏见测量信息。我们在性别对调的二选一决策任务（Tamkin等，2023）、BBQ、StereoSet以及Fulgu和Capraro（2024）的性别偏见研究中测试了TypeSafe的封闭式Jev及五个开源决策模型。六款模型中有五款对女性给出显著更积极的回答（对数优势比增加0.05至0.10），性别对调导致1%至3.5%的最优答案发生变化（Jev的部分原因在于其呼叫-呼叫变体）。制造商推荐的置信度门限虽能消除许多偏见承诺，但提高了在模糊BBQ性别问题中保留刻板印象的比例（granite-switch从0.76升至0.85，pplx从0.81升至1.00）。探测实验显示，这些模型中存在性别偏好：所有六款模型都认为为善而行的暴力对男性更可接受，且在无线索强制猜测儿童性别时，两款默认选择“女孩”。决策层面与概率层面的度量存在差异，因此审计应像应用那样解读模型。

How Specific Do I Need to Be? Effects of User Query Underspecification on Deep Research Agents
我需要多具体？用户查询的不明确性对深度研究智能体的影响
Deep research agents (DRAs) conduct long-horizon reasoning, search, and literature synthesis to provide long-form answers to complex, domain-specific questions. These agents are typically trained and evaluated on fully specified user queries. However, real-world user queries can often be underspecified. We introduce UNDERSPEC-DR, a framework for systematically evaluating how underspecified queries affect agents' final answer quality and intermediate search behavior. Across three DRAs we evaluated, underspecification substantially reduces how well DRA's answers fulfill users' information needs, even though they remain grounded in cited evidence. Our analysis of intermediate search behavior suggests a possible explanation: DRAs conduct broader searches that draw less on the original user query but rely more on their learned prior during training, which can be misaligned with users' intent. Our findings reveal the vulnerability of current DRAs to underspecified user queries and inform future efforts to build DRAs that proactively recognize when additional user input is needed to reliably complete deep research tasks.

Robustness of Chatbot LLM Judgments of Presidential Actions and Hypothetical Reforms
聊天机器人对总统行为及假设改革判断的鲁棒性研究
Chatbots are increasingly consulted about public affairs, and their judgments of policies are useful only if they are robust. We measure this robustness in the setting of an ordinary user, through consumer chat interfaces. For 30 days, five chatbot configurations (Mistral, Gemini Flash, Claude Sonnet, and Claude Opus at two effort settings) were asked twice a day to score the long-term effect on the United States of 40 actions of the last four presidential administrations and of 15 hypothetical reforms, each with a confidence and a short comment. The judgments are robust: all configurations agree on the sign and the ranking of the administrations' mean scores, and two different configurations asked on the same day give opposite signs to the same action in about 4% of cases. Responses from different days differ more than same-day responses, but the difference grows slowly over the month, except where the service itself changed. We identify three such changes, which shifted the judgments while leaving little or no trace in the interface. Treating the comments as a retrieval task, we find them specific to each item and consistent across runs and chatbots, with lexical and embedding similarities agreeing. We release the dataset of 16,500 judgments.

What Makes Medical Examination Items Difficult? Using Large Language Models to Explore Patterns Associated With Empirical Item Difficulty
医学考试题目难度的成因：利用大语言模型探索与实证难度相关的模式
Research on medical item difficulty has largely focused on prediction rather than on identifying interpretable patterns associated with empirical difficulty. This study used empirical difficulty labels to guide large language models (LLMs) in inducing difficulty rubrics and examined how rubric guidance affected LLM difficulty judgments. Using training items and their empirical difficulty estimates from the United States Medical Licensing Examination (USMLE) and the Physician Assistant National Certifying Examination (PANCE), Claude Opus 5 induced separate rubrics for absolute and pairwise difficulty judgment. None of the induced rubrics treated surface features, such as stem length, option count, or images, as major sources of difficulty. Several patterns emerged across both examinations, including cue convergence, misleading cues, and option competition, although their specific forms differed. Retrieval of knowledge that could not be derived from the stem was particularly prominent in the PANCE rubrics, whereas the USMLE rubrics placed greater emphasis on integrating multiple clinical cues. We compared commercial and open-weight LLMs on held-out items under unguided and rubric-guided conditions. Rubric guidance changed how models used the difficulty scale but did not consistently improve performance. In absolute judgments, guidance encouraged broader use of the scale and generally improved coarse-grained agreement, without consistently improving rank ordering. In pairwise comparisons, its effects varied across models and examinations, and judgments became more sensitive to presentation order. These findings suggest that the value of label-guided rubric induction may lie more in generating interpretable, testable hypotheses from empirical difficulty data than in reliably improving difficulty judgments.

关于医学题目难度的研究主要集中在预测而非识别与实证难度相关且可解释的模式。本研究使用实证难度标签引导大语言模型（LLMs）推导难度标准，并考察标准引导对LLM难度判断的影响。基于美国医师执照考试（USMLE）和助理医师国家认证考试（PANCE）的训练题及其实证难度估计，Claude Opus 5 分别为绝对难度和成对难度判断生成了独立的标准。所生成的标准均未将表面特征（如题干长度、选项数量或图像）视为主要难度来源。在两个考试中均出现若干共性模式，包括线索汇聚、误导性线索和选项竞争，尽管具体形式有所不同。在PANCE标准中，无法从题干推导的知识检索尤为突出；而在USMLE标准中，则更强调整合多个临床线索。我们在保留数据集上的题目上比较了商用与开源权重LLM在无引导和标准引导条件下的表现。标准引导改变了模型使用难度量表的方式，但并未一致提升性能。在绝对判断中，引导促使模型更广泛地使用量表，总体上提升了粗粒度一致性，但未稳定改善排序；在成对比较中，效果因模型和考试而异，且判断对呈现顺序更加敏感。这些发现表明，标签引导的标准化推导价值更多在于从实证难度数据中生成可解释、可检验的假设，而非可靠提升难度判断能力。

Unlimited OCR Works 2
无限OCR 2：突破文档解析的长度极限
Unlimited OCR ushers the era of long-horizon parsing, substantially mitigating the growth of working memory as document lengthens. However, as document length scales to hundreds of pages, the visual context grows substantially, sharply increasing the computational cost of each decoding step and limiting the scalability of ultra-long document parsing. Inspired by human book transcription via localized focus without re-scanning preceding pages, we introduce Unlimited OCR 2, a document parsing model that looks only at what it transcribes. Building on Unlimited OCR, we upgrade all the decoder attention modules to Sparse Reference Sliding Window Attention (SR-SWA), bounding active reference tokens to a fixed capacity and thus sustaining constant computational costs across arbitrarily long documents. By combining SR-SWA with Unlimited-Horizon Decoding Paradigm, Unlimited OCR 2 enables document parsing over virtually unlimited lengths without prohibitive memory overhead. At the hundred-page scale, Unlimited OCR 2 achieves 7.0$\times$ and 5.6$\times$ per-step speedups over Full Attention and R-SWA, respectively. Furthermore, it delivers state-of-the-art end-to-end performance on both MPDocBench and MarathonDocBench, our newly proposed benchmark targeting hundred-page document parsing. Ultimately, the capacity for uninterrupted, one-shot document parsing signifies a fundamental paradigm shift into the OCR 3.0 era.
中文翻译
无限OCR开启了长周期解析的新时代，显著缓解了文档长度增长带来的工作内存膨胀问题。然而，当文档长度扩展至数百页时，视觉上下文急剧扩大，导致每一步解码的计算成本大幅上升，限制了超长文档解析的可扩展性。受人类通过局部聚焦进行书籍转录而不重复扫描前页的启发，我们提出无限OCR 2，一种仅关注当前正在转录内容的文档解析模型。在无限OCR基础上，我们将所有解码器注意力模块升级为稀疏参考滑动窗口注意力（SR-SWA），将活跃参考标记数限制在固定容量内，从而在任意长文档上维持恒定的计算成本。结合无限视野解码范式，无限OCR 2实现了近乎无限制长度的文档解析，且无过度内存开销。在百页规模下，无限OCR 2相较于全注意力和R-SWA分别实现7.0倍和5.6倍的每步加速。此外，它在我们新提出的针对百页文档解析的MPDocBench和MarathonDocBench基准上达到了业界领先的整体性能。最终，实现不间断的一次性文档解析能力标志着迈向OCR 3.0时代的根本性范式转变。

HALO: Human-Grounded Granular Evaluation of Proactive Agents
HALO：面向主动型智能体的人类基础细粒度评估
Proactive agents must decide whether, when, and how far to assist without an explicit request. Existing evaluations often rely on automated scores or binary verdicts, offering limited human diagnosis of evidence use and intervention quality. To address these gaps, we introduce HALO, Human-Grounded GrAnular EvaLuation of PrOactive Agents. HALO-Arena covers 4 proactive need types, Dependency, Blockage, Risk, and Opportunity, across 10 everyday domains and 50 personal environments. Scenarios require agents to retrieve evidence from structured databases or unstructured documents before judging whether assistance is warranted and choosing its scope. On HALO-Arena, we construct HALO-8K, a dataset of 8,000 responses from 10 models across 400 scenarios, annotated by 20 experts for action level, acceptability verdicts, and human diagnosis. For rejected responses, human diagnosis covers timing, intensity, and other issues. Recorded reasoning tokens and retrieval rounds measure the effort behind these responses. The best proprietary and open-source models achieve 72.88% and 56.63% acceptability, respectively. Our central finding is that over-proactivity is the dominant observed failure mode, with Premature timing or Excessive intensity accounting for 61.20% of rejected responses. Further analyses yield complementary findings on the balance between intervention and restraint, evidence acquisition, and the calibration of timing and intensity.
中文翻译
主动型智能体必须在没有明确请求的情况下决定是否、何时以及在何种程度上提供协助。现有评估通常依赖自动化评分或二元判断，难以深入诊断证据使用情况和干预质量。为弥补这一不足，我们提出HALO，即人类基础的主动型智能体细粒度评估体系。HALO-Arena涵盖四种主动需求类型：依赖、阻塞、风险与机遇，覆盖10个日常生活领域和50个个人环境场景。任务要求智能体在判断是否需要协助前，从结构化数据库或非结构化文档中检索证据，并确定协助范围。在HALO-Arena上，我们构建了HALO-8K数据集，包含10个模型在400个场景下的8,000条响应，由20位专家标注动作层级、可接受性判断及人类诊断。对于被拒绝的响应，人类诊断涵盖时机、强度等具体问题。记录的推理token和检索轮次用于衡量响应背后的投入努力。最佳专有与开源模型的可接受率分别为72.88%和56.63%。核心发现是：过度主动是主要观察到的失败模式，其中过早介入或过度干预占被拒响应的61.20%。进一步分析揭示了干预与克制之间的平衡、证据获取效率，以及时机与强度校准等方面的互补发现。

Blind Spots: Where Small Language Models Fall Behind Frontier Models on Terminal Tasks
盲点：小型语言模型在终端任务中为何落后于前沿模型
Small language models (SLMs) offer an attractive alternative to frontier models (FMs) because they can be deployed with lower cost and latency. This makes SLMs especially appealing for terminal tasks on edge devices, where responsiveness, local execution, and limited connectivity are important considerations. However, they continue to lag behind FMs on terminal tasks, as measured by standard task success rates. Yet these rates conflate several capabilities that may contribute to this gap. In this work, we characterize the SLM-FM gap along three axes: solvability, whether additional test-time compute allows an SLM to reach a correct solution; verification, whether it can identify a successful solution among candidate trajectories; and confidence, whether it can accurately anticipate its probability of success. Across two standard terminal benchmarks, we find that repeated sampling narrows, but does not eliminate, the gap in solvability. In contrast, a persistent gap remains in verification. Given the same candidate trajectory pool, FMs recover substantially more of the gap between random and oracle selection than SLMs. SLMs are also more overconfident in their prospective success estimates and exhibit poorer calibration. These results suggest that the SLM-FM gap is multidimensional, spanning not only the ability to generate successful solutions, but also the ability to recognize successful solutions and anticipate success. We hope this decomposition can guide future work on developing stronger and smaller terminal agents.
中文翻译
小型语言模型（SLMs）因其更低的成本和延迟，成为前沿模型（FMs）的有吸引力替代方案，特别适用于边缘设备上的终端任务，其中响应速度、本地执行和有限连接性至关重要。然而，它们在终端任务上的表现仍持续落后于FMs，这由标准任务成功率所衡量。但这些成功率混淆了多种可能贡献于差距的能力。本文从三个维度刻画SLM-FM差距：可解性（额外测试时计算能否使SLM达到正确解）、验证性（能否从候选轨迹中识别出成功解）和置信度（能否准确预估自身成功概率）。在两个标准终端基准上，我们发现重复采样缩小但并未消除可解性差距；相比之下，验证性差距持续存在。在相同候选轨迹池下，FMs比SLMs能恢复更多从随机选择到理想选择之间的差距。SLMs在成功预估上也表现出更高的过度自信和更差的校准性。这些结果表明，SLM-FM差距是多维的，不仅涉及生成成功解的能力，还包括识别成功解和预估成功概率的能力。我们希望这一分解能指导未来开发更强且更小的终端智能体。

HEDGE: Gated Hypergraph Memory for Scalable Multi-Agent Debate
HEDGE：用于可扩展多智能体辩论的门控超图记忆
Multi-agent debate systems require agents to retain and revisit arguments across multiple rounds, yet limited context windows and unstructured memories make relevant information increasingly difficult to recover. Existing flat and pairwise-graph memories also struggle to capture higher-order relations, including multi-agent agreement, conflicting evidence, and evolving consensus. We introduce HEDGE, a selective memory framework that represents debate trajectories as semantic hypergraphs. Hyperedges connect related claims across agents, while intra-hyperedge relational graphs preserve fine-grained argumentative structure. A learned memory selection module (MSM), trained through supervised learning and reinforcement learning, routes each memory candidate to STORE, BACKUP, or OMIT, retaining useful information while limiting redundancy. Across seven diverse benchmarks, HEDGE consistently outperforms strong single-agent, multi-agent, and memory baselines, improving accuracy by 5.0 points on average over the strongest multi-agent system with GPT-4o-mini. The gains reach 8.2 points on conflict-heavy tasks, while HEDGE reduces memory usage by 48% and the number of debate rounds by 34%.
中文翻译

Gold vs.~Translation: Are We Measuring Language, Knowledge, or Locality in Translated Benchmarks?
黄金标准 vs. 翻译：我们在测量语言、知识还是局部性？
Translated versions of English-origin benchmarks such as MMLU are widely used to evaluate large language models in non-English languages, yet it remains unclear whether they measure the same constructs as natively created benchmarks. We frame this as a proxy validity question and compare DeepL-translated MMLU proxies against six native benchmarks in seven pairings across five Nordic and Baltic languages, evaluating 29 models on each pairing. Beyond ranking agreement, we test incremental proxy validity: does the translated score add predictive value for native performance after controlling for model size and language proficiency? Proxy validity varies substantially across benchmark pairings. Applying the same robustness criteria to all seven pairings, we find four robust effects, two marginal effects, and one null result. The strongest evidence occurs for curriculum-overlap benchmarks such as Skolprov ($ΔR^2$=.128, 95% CI [.038,.253]), whereas translated MMLU provides no significant incremental value for Sweden-specific factual knowledge in SwedishFacts (p=.242). Because SwedishFacts is also the only open-ended benchmark, we cannot fully separate the effects of local content and response format. Overall, the results indicate a clear pattern: translated benchmarks provide a better proxy when their source content overlaps with the native evaluation target, while locally anchored knowledge is less reliably captured.
中文翻译
源自英语的基准测试（如MMLU）的翻译版本被广泛用于评估非英语语言的大语言模型，但尚不清楚它们是否衡量与原生创建基准相同的构念。我们将此问题视为代理有效性问题，将DeepL翻译的MMLU代理与六种原生基准在五种北欧及波罗的海语言的七组配对中进行比较，每组配对评估29个模型。除了排名一致性外，我们还测试了增量代理有效性：在控制模型规模和语言熟练度后，翻译得分是否对原生表现具有额外预测价值？代理有效性在不同基准配对间差异显著。对所有七组配对应用相同稳健性标准，我们发现四个稳健效应、两个边缘效应和一个零效应。最强证据出现在课程重叠型基准（如Skolprov，ΔR²=.128，95% CI [.038,.253]），而翻译版MMLU在瑞典特定事实知识（SwedishFacts）中未提供显著增量价值（p=.242）。由于SwedishFacts是唯一开放回答型基准，我们无法完全区分本地内容与响应格式的影响。总体而言，结果表明：当源内容与原生评估目标重叠时，翻译基准作为代理效果更好；而本地锚定知识则较难可靠捕捉。

Implicit Minor Detection from Multi-Turn Dialogue in High-Risk AI Interactions: A Constrained Self-Evolving LLM Harness
高风险AI交互中多轮对话里的隐含未成年人检测：一种受约束的自演化大语言模型Harness
In high-risk text interactions, a system may need to estimate whether the current speaker is likely to be a minor before applying age-conditioned safeguards. Many text-based dialogue systems lack strong identity signals such as account information, verified identity, facial data, or device data, and must rely on weak cues that emerge across multiple turns. We formulate implicit minor detection under weak identity information as a high-risk user-attribute inference task and propose a constrained framework comprising a large language model, a Harness, and a Maker-Checker protocol. The framework controls when inference is invoked, requires structured dialogue-linked evidence, and subjects validation-selected Harness revisions to version-level review. On a controlled Chinese benchmark comprising 8,328 multi-turn dialogues, our method improves average precision and minor-class F1 by 7.60 and 6.37 percentage points, respectively, over direct inference using the same LLM family.

What Hallucinations Have We Measured? Semantic Illusion, Confabulation, and Expressed Uncertainty
我们测量过的幻觉有哪些？语义错觉、虚构与表达的不确定性
Hallucination remains one of the most critical challenges in deploying large language models (LLMs) reliably and safely. Yet the term is used inconsistently across the research community. We argue for a strict definition of hallucinations in LLMs. First, hallucinations should be confident incorrectness, kept separate from expressed uncertainty (UNK)1. UNK reveals that the model may be wrong, whereas a hallucination does not reveal it and is therefore more dangerous in practice. Conflating the two obscures their distinct behaviors and makes them hard to study. Second, hallucinations should be split by a simple re-elicitation test: does any semantically equivalent prompt recover the correct response? This test probes whether the relevant knowledge2 is accessible. If none does, the knowledge is likely absent or inaccessible, and the error type is a confabulation (CF) that likely requires external knowledge or retraining; if some does, the knowledge is accessible from the model but applied inconsistently, and the error type is a semantic illusion (SI) that simple prompt or decoding changes can mitigate3. Re-evaluating 14 recent hallucination methods under this taxonomy yields three findings. (1) Detection methods flag UNK more often than hallucinated responses (i.e., SI and CF). (2) Mitigation methods are far more effective for SI than for CF. (3) Latent signals are more informative than output-level signals for CF. An audit of recent hallucination papers shows that most conflate these categories. We recommend reporting CF, SI, and UNK separately, and introduce a practical labeling protocol that needs only a modest number of semantically equivalent prompts.
中文翻译
幻觉仍是部署大语言模型（LLMs）时可靠性和安全性面临的关键挑战之一。然而，研究界对“幻觉”一词的使用并不一致。我们主张对LLMs中的幻觉采用严格定义：首先，幻觉应为自信的错误，需与表达的不确定性（UNK）明确区分。UNK表明模型可能出错，而幻觉则不揭示这一点，因此在实践中更具危险性。混淆二者会掩盖其不同行为，使其难以研究。其次，幻觉应通过简单的重激发测试进行划分：是否存在任何语义等价的提示能恢复正确响应？该测试检验相关知识是否可访问。若无任何提示可恢复，则知识可能缺失或不可访问，错误类型为虚构（CF），通常需要外部知识或重新训练；若有部分提示可恢复，则知识存在于模型中但应用不一致，错误类型为语义错觉（SI），可通过简单提示或解码调整缓解。对14项近期幻觉方法按此分类重新评估后得出三项发现：(1) 检测方法更常标记UNK而非幻觉响应（即SI和CF）；(2) 缓解方法对SI比对CF更有效；(3) 隐层信号比输出层信号对CF更具信息量。对近期幻觉论文的审计显示，多数研究混淆了这些类别。我们建议分别报告CF、SI和UNK，并引入一种实用标注协议，仅需少量语义等价提示即可完成。

When Support Crosses the Line: Evaluating Ethical Boundaries in LLM-Based Mental Health Support in the Chinese Context
当支持越过底线时：在中国语境下评估基于大语言模型的心理健康支持的伦理边界
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for mental health support, yet existing benchmarks primarily focus on supportive capabilities rather than whether models consistently adhere to professional ethical boundaries. This issue is particularly important in multi-turn interactions, where users may introduce new information or exert emotional pressure over time. We introduce MindEth-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating ethical boundary adherence in multi-turn mental health interactions. Grounded in professional ethical standards for mental health services in China, MindEth-Bench covers multiple dimensions of ethical conduct and uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to systematically explore interaction trajectories that stress-test model boundaries. Experiments across nine LLMs show that models that initially respond appropriately can shift toward ethical violations over extended interactions, with substantial variation across models and ethical dimensions. These results show that single-turn evaluations may overestimate the ethical reliability of LLM-based mental health support systems and highlight the need to evaluate ethical behavior throughout multi-turn interaction.

Benchmarking decision models against generative large language models on medical multiple-choice questions
在医学多选题上对决策模型与生成式大语言模型进行基准对比
Medical workflows often require selecting among predefined options. We compared 13 decision models with 15 generative large language model configurations, including different reasoning settings, using 1,273 four-option MedQA questions under text-only conditions. Accuracy ranged from 67.71% to 89.08% for decision models and from 78.32% to 96.86% for generative configurations. Among the tested configurations, Mercury Decide had the lowest mean final-attempt cost (USD 0.0069 per 1,000 questions), while Clef Omni had the shortest median request duration (0.291 seconds). Decision-model probabilities distinguished correct from incorrect answers, but error rates among answers assigned probabilities of at least 0.90 ranged from 1.71% to 12.77%. Answer agreement across three runs ranged from 92.30% to 100%, while probability repeatability varied. Decision models offered resource advantages alongside lower peak accuracy; selecting models for medical workflows requires considering probability quality and repeatability together with accuracy and the resources needed to detect and correct errors.

RoleFUZZER: User-Behaviour Fuzzing for Role-Specification Adherence in Large Language Models
RoleFUZZER：用于大语言模型角色规范遵守性的用户行为模糊测试
Large language models (LLMs) are often conditioned into specific roles such as tutors, interviewers, or agents acting for an organisation, through system prompts that specify how they should behave. The extent to which they can actually adhere to these role specifications under multi-turn interactions with realistic and potentially problematic users is understudied. To address this, we propose user behaviour fuzzing, a guided search over plausible user behaviours that can lead a model to violate its role specification, and introduce RoleFUZZER, a framework that adapts coverage-guided fuzzing from software testing to operationalise the search. RoleFUZZER constructs scenarios guided by a user-behaviour coverage grid that crosses a grounded taxonomy of user interaction types and the role specification of the model under test i.e. what is under pressure. With RoleFUZZER, we evaluate LLMs across five role specifications, including tutor, financial advisor, AI interviewer, antagonistic roleplay character, and web agent under organisational policies. Across simulated and validated conversations in 15 campaigns, the framework reaches >= 89% coverage of the 64-cell behaviour grid for every model and role tested, and surfaces specification-grounded failure patterns. At an equal budget of conversations, removing the RoleFUZZER grid and asking an LLM to generate specification targeting users free-form lowers coverage to 39-69% with fewer kinds of violations. The same specification can also fail in opposite directions. For e.g., on the antagonist, open-weight target models override the player's safety signals, while Claude Haiku 4.5 breaks character instead. RoleFUZZER offers a tractable way to uncover vulnerabilities in LLMs across role specifications by testing diverse user behaviours that are likely to violate them.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）通常通过系统提示被设定为特定角色，如导师、面试官或代表组织行动的代理。然而，它们在面对真实且可能具有挑战性的用户进行多轮交互时，能否真正遵守这些角色规范，目前研究尚不充分。为解决此问题，我们提出用户行为模糊测试，即在合理用户行为范围内进行引导式搜索，以发现可能导致模型违反角色规范的行为，并引入RoleFUZZER框架，将软件测试中的覆盖率引导模糊测试方法应用于实际操作。RoleFUZZER构建场景时依据一个用户行为覆盖网格，该网格结合了用户交互类型的实证分类体系与待测模型的角色规范（即哪些方面处于压力之下）。通过RoleFUZZER，我们在五个角色规范下评估了LLMs，包括导师、财务顾问、AI面试官、对抗性角色扮演角色及遵循组织政策的网络代理。在15个模拟与验证对话活动中，该框架对每个模型和角色均达到≥89%的64单元行为网格覆盖率，并揭示了基于规范的失败模式。在同等对话预算下，若移除RoleFUZZER网格并让LLM自由生成针对规范的用户，覆盖率降至39%-69%，且违规类型更少。同一规范也可能以相反方向失效：例如，在对抗性角色中，开源目标模型会忽略玩家的安全信号，而Claude Haiku 4.5则反而脱离角色。RoleFUZZER提供了一种可行的方法，通过测试可能违反规范的多样化用户行为，揭示LLMs在各类角色规范下的潜在漏洞。
👤 Kshitish Ghate, Michael Saxon, Tadayoshi Kohno, Aylin Caliskan 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38407]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Holistic Diversity Management in LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search
LLM引导的进化搜索中的整体多样性管理
LLM-guided evolutionary search iteratively selects existing programs, uses LLMs as mutation operators to generate a new program, and evaluates it to guide subsequent search. To preserve diversity and reduce the risk of premature convergence, it often adopts the island model, in which groups of programs, called islands, evolve separately. However, this structure alone does not guarantee distinct search directions, as islands may start from similar programs or converge toward similar solutions during evolution. This suggests that diversity must be considered throughout the search process, from initializing islands with distinct programs to developing island trajectories and sharing discoveries across islands. To this end, we propose HoliDivEvolve , a holistic approach to establishing and effectively utilizing island diversity throughout evolutionary search. Specifically, we introduce diverse initialization to establish different initial programs, trajectory adaptive island selection to allocate computation based on current score, recent growth, and the remaining search budget, and growth adaptive migration to share alternative ideas across islands. Experiments across diverse benchmarks show that HoliDivEvolve achieves competitive performance on most evaluated tasks. We further conduct controlled analyses to examine how our design choices affect search behavior and final performance.
中文翻译
LLM引导的进化搜索通过迭代选择现有程序，使用LLM作为变异算子生成新程序，并评估其以指导后续搜索。为保持多样性并降低过早收敛风险，常采用岛屿模型，即多个程序组（称为岛屿）独立演化。然而，仅采用该结构并不能保证不同的搜索方向，因为岛屿可能从相似程序出发，或在演化过程中趋向相似解。这表明多样性必须贯穿整个搜索过程，从初始阶段的差异化程序设置，到岛屿轨迹设计及跨岛屿的知识共享。为此，我们提出HoliDivEvolve，一种在进化搜索全过程中建立并有效利用岛屿多样性的整体方法。具体包括：多样化初始化以建立不同初始程序，基于当前得分、近期增长和剩余搜索预算的轨迹自适应岛屿选择，以及基于增长自适应的迁移机制以跨岛屿共享替代思路。在多种基准上的实验表明，HoliDivEvolve在多数任务上表现优异。我们还进行了受控分析，探究设计选择如何影响搜索行为和最终性能。

LLMs as Judges and Examinees for Medical Item Difficulty Prediction
LLM作为评判者与应试者预测医学试题难度
Predicting medical item difficulty from item text alone remains challenging. Prior work has largely relied on linguistic features or text representations derived from general-purpose and domain-specific language models, but predictive performance has remained modest. This study takes a different approach by using difficulty-related signals that emerge when open-weight large language models (LLMs) interact with test items. Using USMLE and PANCE medical licensure examination items, we examined LLMs in two roles. As judges, the models provided absolute difficulty ratings and pairwise difficulty comparisons. As simulated examinees, they generated response-based signals including correctness, keyed-option probability, response entropy, and the probability margin between the top two options. We then combined the strongest judgment and response signals with baseline item features in downstream prediction models. Pairwise difficulty judgment was the most consistent individual LLM signal across both datasets, while simulated-examinee signals and absolute judgments provided complementary information in some feature combinations. The best configurations reached a Spearman correlation of $ρ$ = .447 on USMLE, exceeding the best system in the 2024 BEA shared task ($ρ$ = .25), and $ρ$ = .494 on PANCE. These results were comparable to direct absolute judgments from the proprietary frontier model Claude Opus 5 ($ρ$ = .443 and .488, respectively). Overall, judgment and response signals from open-weight LLMs captured difficulty-related information beyond text representations. Because these models can be deployed locally, the approach offers a practical option for predicting the difficulty of unreleased or high-security test items.

仅从试题文本预测医学试题难度仍具挑战性。以往研究主要依赖语言特征或通用及领域特定语言模型提取的文本表示，但预测性能有限。本研究采用一种新方法：利用开放权重大语言模型（LLMs）与试题交互时产生的难度相关信号。使用美国医师执照考试（USMLE）和PANCE医学执照考试试题，我们让LLMs扮演两种角色。作为评判者，模型提供绝对难度评分和两两难度比较；作为模拟应试者，生成基于响应的信号，包括正确性、关键选项概率、响应熵，以及前两名选项的概率差。随后将最强的判断与响应信号与基线试题特征结合，构建下游预测模型。两两难度判断在两个数据集上均为最一致的单一LLM信号，而模拟应试者信号与绝对判断在某些特征组合中提供了互补信息。最佳配置在USMLE上达到Spearman相关系数ρ = .447，超过2024年BEA共享任务最佳系统（ρ = .25），在PANCE上达到ρ = .494。这些结果与专有前沿模型Claude Opus 5的直接绝对判断相当（分别为ρ = .443和.488）。总体而言，开放权重LLMs的判断与响应信号捕捉到了超越文本表示的难度相关信息。由于这些模型可本地部署，该方法为预测未发布或高保密性试题难度提供了实用方案。

TCC-RAG: Temporal Causal Chain-Guided RAG for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering
TCC-RAG：基于时间因果链引导的RAG用于时间知识图谱问答
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have spurred growing interest in temporal knowledge graph question answering (TKGQA). Most existing methods rely on semantic retrieval augmented generation (RAG), which suffers from fundamental limitations in complex temporal reasoning. On the one hand, semantic-based RAG methods struggle to capture the multi-hop causal knowledge required for complex temporal reasoning. On the other hand, these methods introduce excessive redundant knowledge, trapping LLMs in spurious reasoning chains that potentially result in erroneous answers. To address these problems, we propose a Temporal Causal Chain-guided RAG (TCC-RAG) to retrieve a concise yet sufficient set of causal knowledge to support complex temporal reasoning. Specifically, we reformulate the temporal knowledge graph into a multi-view causal cue graph, with causal cues inducing connections that explicitly encode temporal evolution and causal dynamics. Based on the causal cue graph, we construct a query-specific temporal causal chain to guide the retrieval process, enabling the model to focus on a concise set of causally relevant knowledge. Finally, we introduce a causal evidence filter to refine the retrieved knowledge, ensuring its causal consistency with the underlying temporal logic of the query. Experiments show that TCC-RAG achieves substantial performance gains of 10.72% and 8.40% on two TKGQA benchmarks, and delivers a remarkable 62.97% improvement for multi-step temporal reasoning.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展激发了对时间知识图谱问答（TKGQA）的广泛关注。现有大多数方法依赖于语义检索增强生成（RAG），但在复杂的时间推理方面存在根本性局限。一方面，基于语义的RAG方法难以捕捉复杂时间推理所需的多跳因果知识；另一方面，这些方法引入了过多冗余知识，使LLM陷入虚假推理链，可能导致错误答案。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种基于时间因果链引导的RAG（TCC-RAG），以检索精炼且充分的因果知识来支持复杂的时间推理。具体而言，我们将时间知识图谱重构为多视角因果线索图，其中因果线索显式编码时间演化和因果动态。基于该因果线索图，我们构建了查询特定的时间因果链以指导检索过程，使模型聚焦于一组精炼的因果相关知识。最后，我们引入因果证据过滤器，对检索到的知识进行精炼，确保其与查询底层时间逻辑的一致性。实验表明，TCC-RAG在两个TKGQA基准上分别取得了10.72%和8.40%的显著性能提升，并在多步时间推理任务中实现了62.97%的惊人改进。

EPSBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Earnings Forecasting
EPSBench：用于盈利预测的LLM智能体基准测试
Quarterly earnings forecasting requires an agent to connect financial evidence to the income-statement line items that produce a company's reported earnings. We introduce EPSBench, a benchmark for studying this ability across 149 firm-quarters from 86 US companies. Agents read a fixed collection of filings, earnings-call transcripts, and news, then estimate company-specific income-statement drivers with cited evidence. A shared accounting calculator converts these estimates into GAAP diluted earnings per share (EPS), providing a common basis for comparing forecast assumptions. We evaluate six models under a common harness, assessing point forecast error relative to date-matched analyst consensus and checking whether their prediction intervals achieve the stated coverage. Three models show no detectable paired difference in point forecast error from consensus, and three have higher error. Across all six models, nominal 80% intervals contain reported EPS in only 37--70% of runs that return an interval, and most misses fall above the upper bound because the forecasts run low. The driver records trace most of the error gap between models to operating-income estimates.

季度盈利预测要求智能体将财务证据与产生公司报告盈利的损益表项目相连接。我们引入EPSBench，一个用于研究此能力的基准，涵盖来自86家美国公司的149个公司-季度数据。智能体阅读一组固定的文件、财报电话会议记录和新闻，然后基于引用证据估算公司特定的损益表驱动因素。一个共享的会计计算器将这些估计转换为GAAP摊薄每股收益（EPS），为比较预测假设提供统一基础。我们在统一框架下评估六种模型，评估点预测误差相对于同期分析师共识的差异，并检查其预测区间是否达到声明的覆盖率。三种模型在点预测误差上与共识无显著配对差异，三种模型误差更高。在所有六种模型中，名义上的80%置信区间仅在37%–70%的运行中包含实际EPS，且大多数遗漏出现在上限之上，因为预测普遍偏低。驱动因素记录显示，模型间误差差距主要源于营业利润估计的差异。

Human Evaluation with Automatic Scores: Separating Sampling and Estimation Gains
自动评分与人工评估：分离采样与估计收益
Automatic judges offer inexpensive information about model outputs, but an evaluation team must decide how to turn that information into fewer human annotations. Scores can guide sampling, correct an estimate, or support adaptive acquisition, and these uses may capture overlapping gains. We compare their incremental value on fixed evaluation pools from three preference datasets, and 19 attributed-question-answering systems. Matched comparisons use the same scores, account for pilot labels and budget rules, and separate acquisition from estimation. Score stratification is a strong reference: after ten-bin stratification, the best fixed local linear correction offers only 1.00--5.27% further MSE reduction in the primary configurations. Unequal allocation offers larger gains in some pools, but can increase error when confident scores conceal variable human outcomes. Comparison on AttributedQA, shrinking learned quotas toward score-only quotas lowers pooled MSE for 17 of 19 systems relative to the score-only procedure, but ordinary pooled 95% intervals cover only 88.09--93.54%. The same shrinkage improves on the score-only reference in only one of eight preference configurations. Ranking experiments further show that lower individual MSE need not improve system selection. These findings support a simple starting procedure, proportional sampling within score strata followed by a stratified human mean, and identify what additional evidence is needed to justify correction or adaptation for the intended evaluation output.

自动评判器为模型输出提供了低成本的信息，但评估团队必须决定如何将这些信息转化为更少的人工标注。评分可用于指导采样、校正估计或支持自适应获取，这些用途可能带来重叠的收益。我们在三个偏好数据集和19个属性化问答系统上比较了它们在固定评估池中的增量价值。匹配比较使用相同的评分，考虑试点标签和预算规则，并将获取与估计分开。评分分层是一个强有力的基准：在十层分层后，最佳的固定局部线性校正仅在主要配置中进一步降低了1.00%–5.27%的均方误差。不均衡分配在某些池中带来更大收益，但在自信评分掩盖人类结果变异时可能增加误差。在属性化QA上的比较显示，将学习到的配额向仅基于评分的配额收缩，相对于仅基于评分的方法，使17/19个系统的合并均方误差降低，但普通合并95%置信区间仅覆盖88.09%–93.54%。同样的收缩仅在八种偏好配置中的一种上优于仅基于评分的参考。排名实验进一步表明，个体均方误差降低并不一定改善系统选择。这些发现支持一种简单的起始流程：在评分分层内进行比例采样，随后采用分层人工均值；并指明了为证明对预期评估输出进行校正或自适应所需的额外证据。

3DCodeEdit: Benchmarking Instruction-Guided 3D Program Editing
3DCodeEdit：指令引导的3D程序编辑基准测试
Language models increasingly create 3D content through code, with practical workflows often relying on editing existing programs rather than regenerating them, for speed and precise control. Such edits require understanding what a program builds and foreseeing how a change reshapes it, yet prior benchmarks mostly evaluate generation or simple edits against a reference. In this paper, we introduce 3DCodeEdit, a benchmark of 2,592 instruction-guided editing tasks over 108 Blender programs that construct objects entirely in code. Its edits range from parameter to structural changes, style redesign, and scene composition, and are judged from renders against human-reviewed checklists. Across 17 models, the average success rate is only 37%, and even the strongest models complete fewer than 60% of structural and style edits. As models become more capable, their failures shift to geometric foresight, where the code runs but builds the wrong shape. We therefore study whether multi-turn feedback can compensate for this missing foresight. Feedback from a separate reviewer model that describes how the renders differ from the request helps most, more than maximum reasoning effort or an agent harness, and can let a 27B model surpass the single-shot rate of a 2.8T-parameter model.
中文翻译
语言模型越来越多地通过代码生成3D内容，而实际工作流通常依赖于编辑现有程序而非重新生成，以实现速度和精确控制。此类编辑需要理解程序所构建的内容，并预见更改如何重塑其结构，但以往基准大多仅评估生成或简单编辑与参考结果的对比。本文提出3DCodeEdit，一个包含2,592个指令引导编辑任务的基准，覆盖108个完全通过代码构建物体的Blender程序。其编辑范围涵盖参数调整、结构变更、风格重设计和场景组合，并基于渲染图像与人工审核清单进行评判。在17个模型中，平均成功率仅为37%，即使最强模型也未能完成超过60%的结构和风格编辑。随着模型能力提升，其失败逐渐转向几何预见能力不足——代码运行但构建出错误形状。因此，我们研究多轮反馈是否能弥补这一缺失的预见能力。来自独立评审模型的反馈，描述渲染结果与请求之间的差异，效果最佳，优于最大推理努力或代理框架，甚至能让一个27B模型超越2.8T参数模型的单次执行成功率。

Component Translation and Judge-Labelled Refusal: A Multilingual Safety Evaluation
组件翻译与判别标签拒绝：一种多语言安全评估
Whole-prompt translation evaluates a request in different languages but does not compare which component carries a language. We introduce a paired reversal: translate the complete request component or the complete operation component into the same target language, holding behaviour, English frame and translated-span count fixed. Component identity and non-English text quantity change together. Assigning English, Chinese and Spanish independently to the two components yields nine configurations on 190 of 300 sampled harmful behaviours retained after consensus annotation and automatic gating. Refusal scored by language-model judges is the primary outcome; unsafe compliance is reported separately. On Gemini 2.5 Flash and DeepSeek, three refusal swaps are about 5.3 percentage points and the fourth is -0.5; none survives the pre-specified six-test Holm correction. In an exploratory extension, Mistral Small 4 has a 13.7-point Spanish swap (95% interval [8.4, 18.9]), which survives the twelve-test Holm sensitivity correction under the primary judge, and 12.1 points under a second judge. GPT-5.4-mini estimates are within 2 points of zero. Component assignment supplies an additional evaluation dimension. Evaluations should record component languages and translated text quantity, comparing matched behaviours at equal translated-span count while interpreting refusal and unsafe compliance separately.

Nemotron 3.5 Super and Lightning: Open, Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Models for Agentic AI
Nemotron 3.5 Super 和 Lightning：面向代理AI的开源高效混合专家Mamba-Transformer模型
We introduce Nemotron 3.5 Super and Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, open and efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer models designed for reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic workloads. Super is a 120B-parameter, 12B-active multimodal model, while Lightning is a smaller 30B-parameter, 3B-active text model; both designed for efficient deployment. Both models combine large-scale distillation, long-context training, and native speculative decoding, while Super further incorporates Indic continued pretraining and agentic software-engineering mid-training. We develop a comprehensive post-training pipeline spanning supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning (RLVR and RLHF), complemented in Super by specialized teacher training and multi- teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). This transforms Super from a text-only base model into a multimodal agent capable of reasoning over text, images, video, and interactive tool environments. We further explore test-time scaling through parallel agent execution and self-verification. Across training and deployment, we jointly optimize capability and efficiency. Indic continued pretraining improves Indian-language benchmarks by up to 9.82 percentage points, while agentic mid-training strengthens software-engineering capabilities and robustness across agent harnesses. The post-trained Super model achieves 51.78% on SWE-bench Pro, surpassing Nemotron 3 Ultra despite using approximately 4.6x fewer active parameters. Post-training MTP boosting improves speculative- decoding acceptance length by up to 21.8% under probabilistic draft sampling, while NVFP4 quantization achieves 99.62% median accuracy recovery relative to BF16. Inference optimizations enable up to 4,856 output tokens/s/GPU on GB200 while sustaining at least 50 tokens/s/user.

Prompt Lottery in LLM-as-a-Judge: Measuring and Mitigating Sensitivity to Syntactic Rephrasing
LLM作为裁判中的提示彩票：测量并缓解对句法改写敏感性
LLM-as-a-Judge has become a standard for evaluating large language models, yet most approaches implicitly assume that a single judge prompt functions as a stable evaluation instrument. Prior work has primarily explored deliberate prompt modifications such as scoring rubrics, role descriptions, or chain-of-thought reasoning in order to optimize accuracy. In contrast, we investigate whether validated prompt variants intended to preserve evaluation semantics while differing in surface realization (\textit{paraphrastic rephrasings}) yield consistent judgments under a fixed evaluation task. We introduce \textit{Prompt Lottery}, a phenomenon in which paraphrastic rephrasings produce different LLMaJ judgments. Across a large-scale study spanning diverse evaluation tasks, judge families, and model backbones, we find that these variations can alter evaluation outcomes and, in some settings, reverse relative model rankings. A user study with 10 human annotators shows that LLMaJ judges exhibit 6-9$\times$ larger agreement degradation under prompt rephrasings than humans, falling below the generally accepted threshold in usability studies. This indicates that single-prompt evaluation is insufficiently robust for model ranking. Finally, we show that multi-prompt aggregation substantially improves robustness by emphasizing judgments that remain consistent across prompt variants.
中文翻译
LLM作为裁判（LLM-a-Judge）已成为评估大语言模型的标准方法，但多数方法隐含假设单一裁判提示可作为稳定的评估工具。以往研究主要聚焦于有意修改提示，如评分标准、角色描述或思维链推理以优化准确性。相比之下，我们探究经过验证的提示变体——在表面表达上不同但保持评估语义一致（即“释义重述”）——是否在固定评估任务下产生一致判断。我们引入“提示彩票”现象，即释义重述会导致LLM-a-Judge产生不同的判断。在涵盖多种评估任务、裁判族和模型骨干的大规模研究中，我们发现这些差异可改变评估结果，甚至在某些情况下逆转模型相对排名。一项包含10名人工标注者的用户研究表明，LLM-a-Judge在提示重述下的一致性退化程度比人类高出6-9倍，低于可用性研究中普遍接受的阈值。这表明单提示评估不足以支撑可靠的模型排序。最后，我们展示多提示聚合能显著提升鲁棒性，通过强调在所有提示变体中保持一致的判断。

QMUL_AK at AISA-ArabicFC: Schema-Constrained Function Calling with ALLaM
QMUL_AK在AISA-ArabicFC中的表现：使用ALLaM的模式约束函数调用
We describe the QMUL_AK submission to AISA-ArabicFC 2026. The system adapts the Arabic-English ALLaM-7B-Instruct model to function calling with low-rank adaptation (LoRA). It combines an Arabic schema-aware prompt, dialect and no-call resampling, a token-weighted training objective, and deterministic validation of generated calls against the candidate tool schema. The same output supports Track A function calling and Track B reasoning-augmented calling. Training uses only the official task data. On the development set, the system achieves 0.9982 function accuracy and 0.8920 argument exact match, corresponding to overall A and B scores of 0.9345 and 0.9454. On the blind test set, the system reaches overall A and B scores of 0.9253 and 0.9380, ranking 3rd on both submission tracks and 1st of 21 teams on the Track C dialect-robustness diagnostic with a macro-averaged score of 0.9075 and the smallest dialect gap in the shared task (0.077). The results show that function selection is nearly saturated, while argument extraction remains the main source of error.

Human-Residual Steering: Aligning Frozen LLM Judges with Human Judgment Distributions
人类残差引导：对齐冻结LLM评判者与人类判断分布
Large language models (LLMs) are widely used as automated judges, yet most alignment methods target aggregated human judgments and overlook variation among individual annotators. Existing approaches to matching the full distribution of human ratings mainly rely on output-level calibration or parameter fine-tuning. We propose Human-Residual Steering (HRS), to our knowledge the first method to align frozen LLM judges with human judgment distributions through learned hidden-state interventions. An oracle analysis shows that low-rank corrections at a single layer remove most of the discrepancy between judge and human distributions. HRS learns to predict such corrections from the judge's hidden representation and rating logits, training on individual annotations with proper scoring rules for ordinal and nominal judgments and optimising through the frozen downstream layers. Across 14 benchmarks and four judges from three model families, HRS outperforms all evaluated frozen-weight baselines. With Qwen2.5-7B, it achieves the lowest Jensen-Shannon distance on 13 of 14 benchmarks, reducing the mean from 0.565 to 0.352 versus 0.377 for the strongest calibrator. HRS also raises mean Spearman correlation with aggregate human scores from 0.449 to 0.579, improves population-level estimation under limited annotation budgets, and trains about 88 times fewer parameters than distributional fine-tuning at a modest cost in alignment.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）被广泛用作自动评判者，但大多数对齐方法仅关注聚合的人类判断，忽略了个体标注者之间的差异。现有匹配人类评分全分布的方法主要依赖输出层面的校准或参数微调。我们提出人类残差引导（HRS），据我们所知，这是首个通过学习的隐藏状态干预来对齐冻结LLM评判者与人类判断分布的方法。一个Oracle分析显示，在单一层级进行低秩修正即可消除评判者与人类分布之间的大部分差异。HRS学习从评判者的隐藏表示和评分logits中预测此类修正，基于个体标注并采用适当的评分规则对序数和名义判断进行训练，通过冻结的下游层进行优化。在14个基准测试和来自三个模型家族的四种评判者上，HRS优于所有评估过的冻结权重基线。以Qwen2.5-7B为例，它在14个基准中的13个上达到最低的Jensen-Shannon距离，将平均值从0.565降至0.352（最强校准器为0.377）。HRS还将与聚合人类评分的平均Spearman相关性从0.449提升至0.579，改善了在有限标注预算下的群体水平估计，并在对齐性能略有损失的情况下，训练参数量仅为分布微调的约1/88。

Locating Sycophancy Across Training Stages in Open Language Models
在开放语言模型训练阶段定位逢迎行为
Language model assistants sometimes change a correct answer when the user suggests a wrong one. The behavior, called sycophancy, is usually attributed to preference-based post-training, yet early measurements found it in pretrained models. We ask where it comes from by measuring it at every available stage of training: 13 open checkpoints from 5 lineages, from base model through supervised fine-tuning and preference optimization to release. We separate how far an injected claim moves a model's answer from how firmly the answer was held, and vary who makes the claim, how reliable they are said to be, and how strongly they make it. Two sycophancy behaviors with different origins emerge, and both replicate across datasets. Deference to a claim in the prompt is already present in every base model, which ranks sources in the same order as its post-trained descendants. Capitulation in conversation, dropping a stated answer when the user objects, is largely absent from base models and present in every post-trained checkpoint. When the objection names an alternative, most post-trained models give up firmly held answers as often as loosely held ones. We discuss the implications of these measurements for evaluating sycophancy and approaches for addressing each behavior.
中文翻译
语言模型助手有时会在用户暗示错误答案时更改正确答案。这种行为被称为逢迎，通常归因于基于偏好的后期训练，但早期测量发现其也存在于预训练模型中。我们通过在训练的每一个可用阶段进行测量来探究其来源：来自5个模型谱系的13个开放检查点，涵盖基础模型、监督微调、偏好优化直至发布版本。我们区分了注入声明使模型答案偏离的程度与其答案的坚定程度，并改变声明者身份、其可靠性假设以及声明强度。两种具有不同起源的逢迎行为浮现出来，且在多个数据集上均能复现。对提示中声明的顺从行为在每个基础模型中均已存在，其对来源的排序与后期训练的后代一致。在对话中的屈服行为——当用户反对时放弃已陈述的答案——在基础模型中基本不存在，但在所有后期训练检查点中普遍存在。当反对意见提出替代选项时，大多数后期训练模型放弃坚定持有的答案与放弃松散持有的答案一样频繁。我们讨论这些测量结果对评估逢迎行为的意义，以及应对每种行为的方法。

An Introduction to Large Language Models for Scientists and Professionals
面向科学家和专业人士的大语言模型入门
Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly become important tools for science, education, medicine, and professional work. Despite their apparent simplicity from the user's perspective, effective and responsible use requires an understanding of how these systems work, what their limitations are, and how their outputs should be evaluated. I wrote this document as a basis for my lecture on LLM in the master in artificial intelligence in medicine at the university of Turin, Italy. This book provides a practical and accessible introduction to LLMs for scientists and professionals. It explains the fundamental ideas behind tokenisation, embeddings, training, inference, context windows, prompting, and instruction hierarchies, while deliberately avoiding mathematical detail. Particular attention is given to the limitations of LLM-generated answers, including hallucinations, uncertainty, reproducibility, bias, and the need for systematic verification and human oversight. The book also discusses privacy, governance, security, and the responsible handling of personal and sensitive information. More advanced topics include Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval quality, chunking, embeddings, vector search, agents, tools, and workflows. A substantial practical exercise guides the reader through running a local LLM with Ollama and Open WebUI and using it to interact with a collection of scientific papers. The exercise emphasizes evidence retrieval, source verification, uncertainty, disagreement between studies, and the distinction between plausible language and scientifically supported conclusions. No programming knowledge is required.

大型语言模型（LLMs）已迅速成为科学、教育、医学及专业工作中的重要工具。尽管从用户角度看其使用看似简单，但有效且负责任地使用仍需理解这些系统的工作原理、局限性以及输出评估方法。本文是我为意大利都灵大学人工智能硕士课程中关于LLM的讲座所撰写的讲义基础。本书为科学家和专业人士提供了一个实用且易懂的LLM入门指南。它解释了分词、嵌入、训练、推理、上下文窗口、提示工程、指令层级等基本概念，刻意避免数学细节。特别关注LLM生成答案的局限性，包括幻觉、不确定性、可复现性、偏见，以及系统性验证和人工监督的必要性。本书还讨论了隐私、治理、安全及个人和敏感信息的负责任处理。更高级的主题包括检索增强生成（RAG）、检索质量、分块、嵌入、向量搜索、智能体、工具和工作流。书中包含一个大规模实践练习，指导读者使用Ollama和Open WebUI在本地运行LLM，并与一组科学论文进行交互。练习强调证据检索、来源验证、研究间的分歧、以及自然语言合理性与科学支持结论之间的区别。无需编程知识即可完成。

Artifact Distillation: Training-Free Transfer from Frontier to Small Language Models
成果蒸馏：无需训练地从前沿模型向小型模型迁移
Closing the gap between frontier and smaller language models is usually approached by distillation: fine-tuning the small model (student) on a frontier model's (teacher) outputs, which binds transfer to training compute and leaves the transferred knowledge largely opaque. We propose Artifact Distillation: a teacher solves training instances and externalizes its problem-solving procedure into an artifact bank that a student queries at inference time, with no gradient updates. Across four LiveBench tasks, a bank written by Claude Opus 4.5 lets Claude Haiku 4.5 outperform unaided Claude Sonnet 4.5 on spatial reasoning (0.707 vs. 0.647) at 60% of its cost, and reach Sonnet 4.5's level on table joining at 89% of its cost. The same raw bank raises Qwen3.5-27B's pooled score from 0.730 to 0.800, showing that transfer is not confined to the teacher's model family.
中文翻译
缩小前沿模型与小型模型之间的差距通常通过蒸馏实现：即用前沿模型（教师）的输出对小型模型（学生）进行微调，但这将知识迁移绑定于训练计算资源，且转移的知识大多不透明。我们提出成果蒸馏（Artifact Distillation）：教师解决训练实例，并将问题求解过程外化为一个成果库，学生在推理时查询该库，无需任何梯度更新。在四个LiveBench任务中，由Claude Opus 4.5生成的成果库使得Claude Haiku 4.5在空间推理任务上表现优于未使用辅助的Claude Sonnet 4.5（0.707 vs. 0.647），成本仅为后者的60%；在表格连接任务上达到Sonnet 4.5的水平，成本为89%。同一原始成果库还将Qwen3.5-27B的综合得分从0.730提升至0.800，表明知识迁移不限于同一模型家族。

Exposure Is Not Commitment: Double-Stratified Evaluation of Latent Monitors for Tool-Using Agents
曝光并非承诺：工具使用智能体潜在监控器的双重分层评估
Tool-using language agents are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection, and a growing literature probes model internals to catch unsafe actions before they execute, with sharply conflicting results. We argue much of the conflict is an evaluation artifact, and propose a double-stratification protocol: separate injection exposure from behavioral commitment, in both the evaluation and the training population. On the primary Qwen AgentDojo grid, monitors trained on the naive mixed unsafe-versus-rest contrast mostly learn exposure: they flag exposed trajectories whether or not the agent complies, and barely predict which cases turn unsafe. Retrained on exposed cases only, the same probe families predict the model's own unsafe tool call up to two steps early, the maximum the three-step trajectories admit (logistic AUROC 0.853, matched by a trained TF-IDF text baseline at 0.855). Mistral-7B reaches 0.896 and OLMo-2-7B 0.912; their naive contrasts learn different things: inverted exposure ranking on one, both axes on the other. Representations separate on exposure (probe 1.000 versus trained text 0.908) and where visible context is sanitized or unavailable. Training contrast alone reproduces the shape of recent pre-action-probe negative results, one candidate explanation for the conflict: the signal exists, but the model's own zero-shot forecast and NLA verbalizations do not reliably recover it. An unstratified AUROC overstates what a latent monitor knows, and what a naive contrast learns is population-dependent: evaluations should stratify both.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言智能体易受间接提示注入攻击，现有大量研究试图通过探测模型内部状态来在执行前捕捉不安全行为，但结果却存在尖锐冲突。我们认为许多冲突源于评估设计的偏差，并提出双重分层协议：在评估和训练数据集中，将提示注入暴露与行为承诺分离。在主Qwen AgentDojo网格上，仅在混合的不安全与正常对比数据上训练的监控器主要学习的是暴露：无论代理是否实际执行，只要轨迹被暴露就会触发警报，且几乎无法预测哪些情况最终会变为不安全。当仅在暴露案例上重新训练时，相同的探测器家族能够提前两步预测模型自身的不安全工具调用（三步轨迹的最大提前量），逻辑回归AUROC达0.853，与训练过的TF-IDF文本基线相当（0.855）。Mistral-7B达到0.896，OLMo-2-7B达到0.912；它们在原始对比中学习的内容不同：一个学习倒置的暴露排序，另一个则学习两个轴的信息。表征在暴露程度（探测器1.000 vs. 训练文本0.908）以及可见上下文是否被清理或不可用方面表现出明显分离。仅使用训练对比即可重现近期预动作探测器负面结果的形态，这可能是冲突的一个解释：信号存在，但模型自身的零样本预测和NLA语义表达无法可靠恢复它。非分层的AUROC会夸大潜在监控器所知内容，而朴素对比学习的内容依赖于群体分布：评估应进行双重分层。

The Best Second Opinion Is Right Where the Primary Is Wrong: Partner Selection for Selective Span Tagging
最好的第二意见就在主模型出错之处：选择性跨度标注中的合作伙伴选择
A span tagger that ranks its outputs by risk can defer the riskiest ones to a person, and a second model can sharpen this ranking. Which second model should it be? Partner selection is conditional, not global: the best second opinion is the model that is right where the primary is wrong. Across eight tagger types, three seeds and three English benchmarks (576 evaluated primary-partner pairings), choosing the partner by held-out accuracy on the primary's errors recovers 82% of the best partner's gain, against 69-70% for span F1 or a random pick. Frozen before a fourth dataset was chosen, the rule replicated there (Spearman 0.40 vs. 0.04 for span F1). Partner value is also directional. The predictive distribution is the second opinion: hard labels discard half of a partner's value, and scoring the served label improves 94% of pairings. Fixed before the fourth dataset was chosen, this score also replicated there. For LoRA-tuned decoders, independently trained partners beat every readout of the primary network. Applied zero-shot to 51 MASSIVE locales, every primary has a helpful trained partner in every locale, and the preferred partner follows competence.
中文翻译
一种按风险排序输出的跨度标注器可以将最危险的预测转交给人类处理，而第二个模型可以进一步优化这一排序。应选择哪个第二个模型？合作伙伴的选择是条件性的而非全局的：最佳的第二意见是主模型出错时正确的那个模型。在八种标注器类型、三个种子和三个英文基准（共576个主-伙伴配对）上，通过主模型错误上的保留准确率选择合作伙伴，可恢复最佳合作伙伴82%的增益，而基于跨度F1或随机选择仅能恢复69%-70%。在选定第四数据集前冻结该规则后，其在该数据集上仍表现良好（Spearman相关系数0.40 vs. F1的0.04）。合作伙伴的价值还具有方向性：预测分布即为第二意见；使用硬标签会丢弃一半合作伙伴价值，而对所服务标签进行评分则可改善94%的配对。该评分规则在第四数据集上也成功复现。对于LoRA微调的解码器，独立训练的合作伙伴优于主网络的所有读出方式。零样本应用于51个MASSIVE语言环境时，每个主模型在每个环境中都有一个有用的训练伙伴，且首选伙伴遵循能力优先原则。
👤 Varun Kotte 📅 2026-10-12 🔗 arXiv

VeriCUA: Verification as Agentic Exploration for Computer-Using Agents
VeriCUA：用于计算机使用代理的验证即智能体探索
Building and scaling computer-using agents (CUAs) across desktop, mobile, and web environments requires reliable task-completion verification for data curation, evaluation, and reinforcement learning. Typical automated approaches prompt vision-language models (VLMs) to judge trajectories or generate programs to inspect application state. However, both rely on partial views of complex digital environments. VLM judges are restricted to supplied observations, while generated programs depend on accessible state. These views can omit decisive evidence or leave completion judgments unsupported. Tailoring separate verifiers to different platforms and access settings adds further engineering cost. We present VeriCUA, a platform-agnostic framework for verification through agentic exploration. It treats verification as dynamic evidence gathering and assessment within a shared procedure for online and offline settings across platforms and observation modalities. After inspecting the available context to construct a task rubric, the verifier selects GUI or code checks for unresolved requirements and combines their findings under explicit evidence rules. Experiments show improved agreement with reference labels over the compared baselines across these settings, with the largest offline gains on hard cases and stronger rejection of false completion claims online. Further analyses connect these gains to complementary evidence and test-time exploration.
中文翻译
在桌面、移动和网页环境中构建和扩展计算机使用代理（CUAs）需要可靠的任务完成验证，以支持数据整理、评估和强化学习。典型的自动化方法通过提示视觉-语言模型（VLM）判断轨迹或生成程序检查应用状态。然而，两者均依赖复杂数字环境的部分视图。VLM裁判受限于提供的观察，而生成程序依赖可访问的状态。这些视图可能遗漏决定性证据，或使完成判断缺乏支持。为不同平台和访问设置定制独立验证器会带来额外工程成本。我们提出VeriCUA，一种平台无关的验证框架，通过智能体探索实现验证。它将验证视为跨平台和观察模态的在线与离线设置中动态证据收集与评估的共享过程。在检查可用上下文以构建任务评分标准后，验证器选择GUI或代码检查来解决未决需求，并根据明确的证据规则整合其发现。实验表明，在各种设置下，其与参考标签的一致性优于对比基线，尤其在困难案例的离线设置中提升显著，并在线上更强地拒绝虚假完成声明。进一步分析表明，这些提升源于互补证据和测试时探索。
👤 Qiushi Sun, Da Yu, Alexander Knop, Amer Sinha, Lingpeng Kong et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
