Ramanujan Challenge Results: AI-Assisted Evaluation Pipeline for Multi-Format Proofs
拉马努金挑战结果：面向多格式证明的AI辅助评估流水线
As AI's mathematical capabilities rapidly improve, meaningful benchmarks are shifting from university-level problems and math olympiads to research-level mathematics. The Ramanujan Challenge is a benchmark comprised of research-level problems in number theory, including unpublished results and open conjectures. It presented concrete formulas for mathematical constants whose correctness can be checked numerically, while their proofs may require non-obvious mathematics. Here, we analyze the Challenge submissions, measuring the relative success of different AI models, Computer Algebra Systems (CAS), and formal tools. We find correct submissions that are conceptually distinct from the official solutions, and a proof of one of the published open conjectures. Evaluating the submissions exposed a second challenge: judging complex proofs reliably at scale. We introduce an AI-assisted evaluation pipeline for natural language proofs, CAS code, and Lean formalizations that assesses semantic and logical correctness. Compared with the conventional one-shot LLM judgment, our pipeline reduces false-positive verdicts on natural language proofs from 23% to 6% and improves judgment accuracy on supporting code from 46% to 58%. This approach provides a methodology for evaluating research-level mathematics at scale, with potential applications across mathematics and AI for science.

Agentic University: Collaborative Agents for Personalized Learning
智能型大学：面向个性化学习的协作智能体
Large language models support personalized tutoring, content generation, and adaptive learning paths. However, capability development requires these resources and interactions to form a coherent learning process that evolves with the learner. A central challenge is to align goals, activities, and assessment while using learning evidence to decide what should follow. We introduce Human-Participatory Multi-Agent Organization and instantiate it in Agentic University (AU). In AU, learners participate as organization members and work with specialized agents to maintain a revisable Capability Program. The program connects personal goals, learning activities across modalities, and layered assessment, while learner feedback and performance guide subsequent support. Our evaluation shows advantages in program organization, assessment design, and revision quality. Users achieved larger mean assessment gains (12.19 versus 5.61 points on a 50-point scale) and reported stronger cognitive support. This work contributes an organizational approach to adaptive learning that makes learner participation a continuing basis for multi-agent coordination. The Agentic University project is available at \href{https://github.com/AgenticOrgLab/AgenticUniversity.git}.

Training language models in simulation for psychiatric intake
在模拟环境中训练语言模型以进行精神科初诊
In psychiatric practice, the clinical intake interview remains a primary diagnostic instrument, requiring the clinician to establish rapport while disentangling complex, overlapping presentations. Here, we show that multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation environments improves language model performance on structured text-based psychiatric intake, spanning history-taking, differential diagnosis, and initial management planning. In a randomized, double-blind crossover study ($N = 60$ matched scenario pairs, 120 encounters) comparing the RL-trained agent against its identically prompted base model, 32 board-certified psychiatrists role-played standardized patients while a separate panel of 15 independent, board-certified psychiatrists scored the de-identified transcripts. The trained agent outperformed the base model in 76.7% of matched scenario pairs ($p < 0.0001$), with gains on an adapted clinical-examination rubric ($d_z = 0.77$, $p < 0.0001$), patient-centered communication (82.0% vs. 65.2%, $p < 0.001$), and differential diagnosis appropriateness (68.3% vs. 33.3%, $p < 0.001$). Across 127 psychiatrist-validated simulated intake scenarios, the trained agent nearly doubled its base model's clinical rubric score (0.706 vs. 0.379; composite reward 0.564 vs. 0.294, $p < 0.0001$). To systematically probe conversational safety, we established two Delphi-consensus taxonomies, of clinical safety risks and relational competencies, from which 256 challenging adversarial presentations were derived; we find that training in simulation environments more than halved under-triage of acute safety risks (12.9% vs. 30.5%, $p < 0.0001$). Simulation environments thus provide a controlled testbed for training and stress-testing psychiatric conversational agents, with gains that transferred to psychiatrist-enacted encounters, though residual gaps require safeguards and clinician oversight.

在精神科实践中，临床初诊访谈仍是主要的诊断工具，要求临床医生建立信任关系的同时理清复杂且重叠的症状表现。我们展示了在模拟环境中采用多轮强化学习（RL）可显著提升语言模型在结构化文本精神科初诊任务中的表现，涵盖病史采集、鉴别诊断和初步管理规划。在一项随机双盲交叉研究中（N = 60 对匹配情景，共120次会面），将经过RL训练的智能体与相同提示的基础模型进行比较，32名持证精神科医生扮演标准化患者，另一组15名独立持证精神科医生对去标识化的访谈记录进行评分。训练后的智能体在76.7%的匹配情景对中表现优于基础模型（p < 0.0001），在调整后的临床检查量表上得分更高（d_z = 0.77, p < 0.0001），患者中心沟通能力（82.0% vs. 65.2%, p < 0.001），以及鉴别诊断适当性（68.3% vs. 33.3%, p < 0.001）。在127个由精神科医生验证的模拟初诊情景中，训练智能体的临床量表得分几乎翻倍于基础模型（0.706 vs. 0.379；综合奖励0.564 vs. 0.294, p < 0.0001）。为系统探究对话安全性，我们建立了两个德尔菲共识分类体系，分别用于临床安全风险和关系能力，从中衍生出256个具有挑战性的对抗性呈现；我们发现，在模拟环境中训练能将急性安全风险的低估率降低超过一半（12.9% vs. 30.5%, p < 0.0001）。因此，模拟环境为训练和压力测试精神科对话智能体提供了可控的试验平台，其性能提升可迁移至真实医生执行的会面中，但仍存在残余差距，需采取防护措施并辅以临床医生监督。

Prefix Executability: Evaluating Tool-Using Agents Beyond Final Success
前缀可执行性：超越最终成功的工具使用智能体评估
Tool-use benchmarks often score agents by final task success, but terminal outcomes can obscure whether the intermediate tool-call trajectory was executable. We propose prefix executability as a trajectory-level evaluation protocol for tool-using agents. The protocol measures whether every prefix of a predicted tool-call sequence remains executable under tool schemas, state preconditions, and data dependencies, and summarizes reliability using Prefix Executability Curves (PEC), First-Error Depth (FED), Trajectory Executability Rate (TER), horizon-normalized AUC-PEC, and Recovery Rate (RR). We instantiate the protocol on stratified samples from BFCL-v3 multi-turn and ToolSandbox, and on the complete Airline/Retail base splits of $τ^3$-bench. We observe that outcome-only and prefix-level rankings diverge: agents whose execution is conditioned on an unverified plan often achieve higher point-estimate final success than non-plan-conditioned baselines while substantially lowering TER and AUC-PEC. These divergences are precisely the failure modes that prefix executability is designed to expose: they reveal whether apparent success rests on an executable trajectory, where invalidity first enters, and whether success follows recovery from an earlier failure. As a validation intervention, explicit pre-execution plan checking can improve executable-prefix survival while maintaining competitive terminal success. These results show that final success alone is insufficient for evaluating tool-use agents and that prefix-level executability provides an actionable reliability signal for comparing agents, diagnosing failures, and assessing validation mechanisms.

工具使用基准通常仅根据最终任务成功来评分，但终端结果可能掩盖中间工具调用轨迹是否可执行。我们提出前缀可执行性作为工具使用智能体的轨迹级评估协议。该协议衡量预测工具调用序列的每一个前缀在工具模式、状态先决条件和数据依赖下的可执行性，并通过前缀可执行性曲线（PEC）、首次错误深度（FED）、轨迹可执行率（TER）、归一化AUC-PEC和恢复率（RR）等指标总结可靠性。我们在BFCL-v3多轮和ToolSandbox的分层样本，以及τ^3-bench的完整Airline/Retail基础分割上实现了该协议。我们观察到，仅基于结果的排名与前缀级排名存在分歧：那些依赖未经验证计划的智能体虽然在点估计的最终成功率上高于非计划依赖基线，但显著降低了TER和AUC-PEC。这些分歧正是前缀可执行性设计所要揭示的失败模式：它们揭示了看似成功的背后是否基于可执行的轨迹，无效性首次出现的位置，以及成功是否来自对早期失败的恢复。作为验证干预手段，显式的预执行计划检查可在保持竞争性终端成功的同时提高可执行前缀的存活率。这些结果表明，仅凭最终成功不足以评估工具使用智能体，而前缀级可执行性提供了可操作的可靠性信号，可用于智能体比较、故障诊断和验证机制评估。

When Requirements Interact: Scaling Long-Horizon Coding via Interdependent Specifications
当需求相互作用时：通过相互依赖的规范实现长周期编码的扩展
To succeed in real-world long-horizon software development, coding agents must satisfy multi-dimensional task specifications, such as interface compatibility and error handling, and do so without breaking existing dependencies. However, current works lack specification in both quantity and diversity, often emphasizing primary goals while ignoring complex constraints. Synthesizing tasks to bridge this gap presents two challenges: naively adding specifications risks reducing tasks to trivial checklists rather than compelling joint reasoning, and verifying these subtle interactions risks overlooking hidden flaws or rejecting valid alternatives. To address these challenges, we introduce CodeSpecWeave, a framework for synthesizing tasks with rich, interdependent specifications. Grounded in real developer seeds, a synthesis agent iteratively develops both coupled task requirements and executable tests, while a review agent audits solution trajectories from multiple models to guarantee test reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct 4,670 training tasks across 2,865 repositories. Applying Reinforcement learning on these tasks significantly improves performance over the base model on DeepSWE-v1.1, largely outperforming existing baselines. Furthermore, we curate CodeSpecWeave-Bench, a rigorous benchmark comprising 100 tasks and 1,044 specifications, which clearly separates frontier models and reveals significant headroom in handling interdependent engineering constraints.

It Takes Two to Probe, Thousands to Retrieve: Putting Vision--Language Probes to the Retrieval Test
需要两个人来探测，成千上万才能检索：将视觉-语言探测器置于检索测试中
Vision--language models (VLMs) are tested for fine-grained understanding with image--text matching probes, a model must prefer the correct image or caption over handcrafted negative. In retrieval, the correct item must beat thousands of alternatives. We measure how these two evaluation methods relate by using two probing benchmarks, \svo{} and SugarCrepe, for retrieval tasks. For 100 image and caption queries we judge the top-10 retrievals from full dataset of six VLMs, with human annotators and an LLM assessor. We than compare how well models rank pool of items on the same queries, while changing only where the negatives comes from: the benchmark, a random draw, or the judged-incorrect retrievals of other models. On \svo{} the accuracy of retrieving a positive over handcrafted negatives is significantly higher than for retrieved ones (the correct item is preferred in 85\% against 77\% of the time). Using pool of ten retrieved negatives leads to same accuracy as when one uses pool of 200 to 430 random negatives. On SugarCrepe the order is reversed: its handcrafted negatives are harder than the retrieved ones. Incomplete relevance labels explain most of the low retrieval scores on \svo{}, where the top-ranked item is correct for 54--90\% of the queries under human labels against 6--21\% under the benchmark's labels. However, we detect no association between passing the probe for a query and retrieving a correct item first. The LLM assessor o3 agrees with individual annotators ($κ$ 0.57--0.61) slightly less than they agree with each other (0.64--0.68).

Surprise Alone Learns Lies: Evidence-Gated Continual Learning for LLMs from Streaming Claims
仅靠意外即可学会谎言：来自流式声明的证据门控持续学习
Continual learning methods for large language models increasingly decide what to learn by surprise, keeping the inputs the model finds least predictable. This rule is unsafe when the stream contains lies. A planted falsehood about something the model knows has low probability under the model's belief, so it is highly surprising by construction, and a surprise-driven learner absorbs nearly every such lie, at a rate similar to random selection with the same budget. We study the problem with LoRA-adapted students (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.2-3B) that learn from a stream of true and false claims about invented people and real CounterFact facts. We report four findings. (1) A verifier key, a judge that must approve each claim, removes lies to the extent that its verifier is accurate; real LLM judges are cautious and reject unsourced truths and even source-backed updates. (2) Calibrated regression tests on unrelated knowledge slow learning down without filtering out lies. (3) The original model's own prior flags claims that contradict what it knows, without a judge. It separates lies about known facts from true new facts (AUC 0.78-0.83 across three models) but not from legitimate updates of the same kind of fact (AUC 0.53). It saves 43% of judge calls, but an attacker with access to the model can choose lies that pass it. (4) If trusted sources exist that an attacker cannot plant, routing contradicted claims to evidence recovers legitimate updates and the combined system has the lowest poison rate among learned claims; when an attacker plants matching sources for 30% of the lies, the system ends up worse than the judge alone. The acceptance signals answer different questions, and none of them decides on its own whether a claim is true.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的持续学习方法越来越多地根据“意外”来决定学习内容，即保留模型认为最不可预测的输入。当数据流中包含谎言时，这一规则存在安全隐患。一个关于模型已知事物的虚假陈述，在模型信念下概率极低，因此从构造上具有高度意外性，导致基于意外的学习者几乎会吸收每一个此类谎言，其吸收速率与相同预算下的随机选择相似。我们通过LoRA适配的学生模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.2-3B）研究此问题，这些模型从关于虚构人物和真实CounterFact事实的真假声明流中学习。我们报告四项发现：(1) 设置一个验证者关键（需批准每个声明的裁判）可有效去除谎言，其去伪效果取决于验证者的准确性；真实LLM裁判谨慎，拒绝无来源的真实信息甚至有来源的更新。(2) 在无关知识上进行校准回归测试虽能减缓学习速度，但无法过滤谎言。(3) 原始模型自身的先验可标记与已有知识矛盾的声明，无需外部裁判。它能区分关于已知事实的谎言与新的真实事实（AUC 0.78–0.83，跨三个模型），但无法区分同一类事实的合法更新（AUC 0.53）。该机制可节省43%的裁判调用，但攻击者若能访问模型，可选择绕过该机制的谎言。(4) 若存在攻击者无法伪造的可信来源，将矛盾声明路由至证据可恢复合法更新，组合系统在所学声明中拥有最低污染率；但当攻击者为30%的谎言伪造匹配来源时，系统表现反而劣于仅使用裁判的情况。接受信号回答不同问题，且任一信号都无法独立判断声明的真实性。
👤 Abdelkader Metales, Miriam Grabenheinrich 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Diagnosing the Reference Gap: What Visual Scaffolds Can and Cannot Fix in Multimodal Reasoning
诊断参照差距：视觉支架在多模态推理中能与不能解决的问题
Multimodal large language models can recognize visual content yet struggle to reason reliably over it across multiple steps. In counting, a model may see all objects but lose track of which ones it has counted. We argue that such failures, commonly attributed to perception, reveal a reference gap: the failure to bind language to the relevant visual entities, regions, or locations. Motivated by the theory of visual routines, we test whether external references such as numbering can narrow this gap. We introduce ReGap-Bench, a paired benchmark of five visual reasoning tasks in which the scene, question, and answer are fixed and only the reference varies. Results show that task-matched references improve accuracy by up to 25\%, while mismatched references provide little benefit or even hurt performance. These findings identify the reference gap as a bottleneck distinct from visual perception and show that constructing task-appropriate visual references can serve as a training-free visual skill for improving multimodal reasoning.

多模态大语言模型能够识别视觉内容，但在多步推理中难以可靠地处理它。例如，在计数任务中，模型可能看到所有物体，却丢失已计数物体的追踪。我们认为，此类失败通常归因于感知，实际上揭示了一个参照差距：即未能将语言绑定到相关视觉实体、区域或位置。受视觉程序理论启发，我们测试外部参照（如编号）能否缩小这一差距。我们引入ReGap-Bench，一个包含五个视觉推理任务的成对基准，其中场景、问题和答案固定，仅参照方式变化。结果显示，任务匹配的参照可使准确率提升高达25%，而不匹配的参照几乎无益甚至损害性能。这些发现将参照差距识别为独立于视觉感知的瓶颈，并表明构建任务适配的视觉参照可作为无需训练的视觉技能，以提升多模态推理能力。
👤 Guizhen Chen, Weiwen Xu, Wotao Yin, Anh Tuan Luu, Hou Pong Chan 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Well-Specified and Well-Tested? Assessing SWE-bench-Style Datasets with a Self-Evolving Agent
规范且可测试吗？基于自演化智能体评估SWE-bench风格数据集
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on software engineering (SE) tasks, driving the need for high-quality datasets for reliable training and evaluation. SWE-bench-style datasets (SWE datasets) are widely used to evaluate LLM-based coding agents on real-world software issues, where task clarity and test coverage directly affect whether instances are solvable and verifiable. However, quality issues persist in existing SWE datasets, and manual annotation is costly and labor-intensive, motivating LLM-based annotation. Existing approaches typically rely on static, overly general rubrics, limiting their ability to handle diverse and nuanced scenarios and often yielding high false positive rates. In contrast, human annotators draw on accumulated experience to make context-aware judgments. Inspired by this, we propose ExLabel, an LLM-based, self-evolving, experience-guided assistant for task clarity and test coverage assessment in SWE datasets. ExLabel maintains an evolving experience bank that distills cross-instance knowledge into stable yet adaptive judgment criteria and reuses relevant experience to guide the assessment of new instances. For systematic evaluation, we construct SWE-TQ, a new benchmark spanning eight programming languages, by cleaning human annotations from SWE-bench and Multi-SWE-bench. Experiments with three backbone LLMs show that ExLabel achieves the best F1 across all settings, with relative improvements of 44.2%/49.9% and 24.0%/18.4% over the best baseline for task clarity and test coverage assessment on the Python/Multilingual subsets, respectively. Moreover, a coding model fine-tuned on SWE-rebench data filtered by ExLabel resolves 66.2% of issues on SWE-bench Verified, outperforming the model trained on data filtered by the best baseline by 14.5%.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在软件工程（SE）任务中表现出色，推动了高质量数据集在可靠训练和评估中的需求。SWE-bench风格的数据集（SWE数据集）被广泛用于评估基于LLM的编码智能体在真实世界软件问题上的表现，其中任务清晰度和测试覆盖率直接影响实例是否可解和可验证。然而，现有SWE数据集仍存在质量问题，人工标注成本高且耗时，促使采用基于LLM的标注方法。现有方法通常依赖静态、过于通用的评分标准，难以应对多样且细微的场景，常导致较高的假阳性率。相比之下，人类标注者能借助积累的经验做出情境感知判断。受此启发，我们提出ExLabel，一种基于LLM的、自演化且经验引导的任务清晰度与测试覆盖率评估助手。ExLabel维护一个动态演化的经验库，将跨实例知识提炼为稳定而适应性强的判断标准，并复用相关经验指导新实例的评估。为系统性评估，我们通过清洗SWE-bench和Multi-SWE-bench的人工标注构建了SWE-TQ，一个涵盖八种编程语言的新基准。使用三种骨干LLM的实验表明，ExLabel在所有设置下均取得最佳F1值，相较于最优基线，在Python/多语言子集上任务清晰度和测试覆盖率评估分别提升44.2%/49.9%和24.0%/18.4%。此外，使用ExLabel筛选后的SWE-rebench数据微调的编码模型在SWE-bench Verified上解决了66.2%的问题，优于使用最优基线筛选数据训练的模型14.5%。

AgentHeal: Evaluating Goal-Preserving Resource Recovery of Web Agents under Access Barriers
AgentHeal：在访问障碍下评估网络智能体的目标保持资源恢复能力
Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating complex information-seeking and transactional tasks across the open web. However, their real-world reliability remains limited by access barriers, such as dead links, authentication walls, rate limits, and anti-scraping defenses. These barriers block the primary execution path, causing agents to abort prematurely, loop indefinitely, or quietly compromise the user's original constraints. Existing evaluations either implicitly assume that target resources are freely accessible or focus on transient perturbations that simple retries can resolve, failing to capture scenarios where agents must actively reroute around impassable bottlenecks. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces AgentHeal, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating goal-preserving resource recovery, defined as the ability of web agents to detect non-transient access barriers, pivot to legitimate alternative sources, and fulfill the user's exact original objective. Spanning nine barrier families across a counterfactual sandbox and real-website replay, our experiments on leading LLM backbones show that average task success drops sharply from 89.4% to 43.7% under access barriers. Further analysis reveals that agents frequently fall for deceptive look-alikes, fail to escalate to users when necessary, violate access policies, or waste computation on futile retries, providing actionable diagnostics for developing resilient web agents.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体已在开放网络上自动化复杂的信息检索和交易任务中展现出强大能力。然而，其实际可靠性受限于访问障碍，如失效链接、身份验证墙、速率限制和反爬虫防御。这些障碍会阻断主要执行路径，导致智能体过早终止、无限循环或悄然违背用户的原始约束。现有评估要么隐含假设目标资源可自由访问，要么聚焦于可通过简单重试解决的瞬时扰动，未能捕捉智能体必须主动绕过不可逾越瓶颈的场景。为弥合这一差距，本文引入AgentHeal，一个全面的基准，用于评估目标保持的资源恢复能力，即网络智能体检测非瞬时访问障碍、转向合法替代来源并完全实现用户原始目标的能力。在反事实沙箱和真实网站回放中覆盖九类障碍的实验表明，领先LLM骨干模型在访问障碍下的平均任务成功率从89.4%急剧下降至43.7%。进一步分析揭示，智能体频繁陷入欺骗性相似项、在必要时未能向用户升级、违反访问政策或在无意义的重试中浪费计算资源，为开发更具韧性的网络智能体提供了可操作的诊断。
👤 Zhiyi Liu, Haohua Du, Haoqi Sun, Haisheng Tan 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

A Novel Approach to Evaluating Evaluation Metrics for Multi-Output Structured Prediction
一种评估多输出结构化预测评价指标的新方法
In multi-output structured prediction tasks, while only one ground truth label may be provided in the training data, multiple equally valid outputs may be possible, making reliable evaluation a persistent challenge. We postulate that human evaluators implicitly use task-specific invariants, e.g., object boundaries in colorized images or named entities in translations, to judge if an output is acceptable. Under this assumption, we introduce a notion of approximate task-specific invariants and use them as diagnostic tools to evaluate a variety of existing metrics for vision and language tasks. We use these task invariants as part of a framework to systematically test metric reliability by encouraging domain-relevant invariants in model outputs via an augmented loss function. In our experiments, we observe that enforcing invariants with an augmented loss yields substantial improvements in popular distributional metrics while more traditional metrics change only marginally. Through this invariants-driven evaluation, we expose where standard metrics fail to detect meaningful differences, and we highlight the conditions under which distributional metrics succeed or still fall short.
中文翻译
在多输出结构化预测任务中，尽管训练数据中仅提供一个真实标签，但可能存在多个同样有效的输出，这使得可靠评估成为一个持续的挑战。我们假设人类评估者隐式地使用任务特定的不变性（例如，在着色图像中的物体边界或翻译中的命名实体）来判断输出是否可接受。基于这一假设，我们引入了近似任务特定不变性的概念，并将其作为诊断工具，用于评估视觉和语言任务中多种现有指标的性能。我们利用这些任务不变性构建了一个框架，通过增强损失函数鼓励模型输出中包含领域相关的不变性，从而系统地测试指标的可靠性。实验结果表明，通过增强损失强制执行不变性，显著提升了主流分布指标的表现，而传统指标变化甚微。通过这种基于不变性的评估，我们揭示了标准指标在检测有意义差异方面的失败之处，并指出了分布指标成功或仍存在不足的条件。

Beyond Passing Tests: A Systematic Review and Controlled Re-evaluation of Techniques for Improving the Quality of LLM-Generated Code
超越通过测试：对提升大语言模型生成代码质量技术的系统性回顾与受控再评估
Large language models pass most functional-correctness benchmarks, but passing tests says little about whether their code is readable, efficient or secure. Many techniques claim to improve these qualities, yet their evidence is scattered, usually covers one quality dimension and is rarely compared at an equal compute budget. We contribute a systematic review and a controlled re-evaluation. A systematic literature review of 628 primary studies (July 2021 to October 2026), with disclosed, author-reviewed LLM assistance, organizes the techniques by stage, signal source and quality target. Most studies measure only the dimension they target, gains often come with unchanged or unreported correctness, single automated quality signals are gamed, and compute, variance and contamination are rarely reported. A controlled re-evaluation runs ten techniques on four models at matched budgets on correctness, maintainability, efficiency and security benchmarks, extends it to thirteen current models and repository-level tasks, and compares 66 highlighted techniques head-to-head on Gemma 4 31B. At one call, quality prompting raises analyzer issue counts and leaves complexity and judge scores almost unchanged. At matched budgets, best-of-n sampling with an analyzer-weighted ranker matches or beats prompting, planning, self-refinement, a reviewer agent and retrieval on measures the ranker does not optimize, while an analyzer feedback loop and fine-tuning beat it only on analyzer issue counts. Large correctness losses on HumanEval+, a likely contaminated benchmark near its ceiling, do not appear on contests held after the models' training cutoffs. In the head-to-head, 1 of 66 techniques beats best-of-n on its primary target beyond the 95% interval, a rate consistent with chance. Gains should be reported against a budget-matched sampling baseline and on measures independent of the technique's own signal.
中文翻译
大型语言模型虽然通过了大多数功能性正确性基准测试，但通过测试并不能反映其代码是否可读、高效或安全。许多技术声称能改善这些质量维度，但其证据分散，通常仅覆盖单一维度，且很少在相同计算预算下进行比较。我们贡献了一项系统性文献回顾和受控再评估。对628篇原始研究（2021年7月至2026年10月）进行了系统性回顾，涵盖披露并经作者评审的LLM辅助内容，按阶段、信号来源和质量目标对技术进行分类。多数研究仅衡量其目标维度，增益常伴随正确性未变或未报告，单一自动化质量信号易被操纵，而计算量、方差和污染情况极少报告。受控再评估在四个模型上以匹配预算运行十种技术，评估正确性、可维护性、效率和安全性基准，扩展至十三个当前模型和仓库级任务，并在Gemma 4 31B上对66种突出技术进行一对一比较。在一次调用中，质量提示增加了分析器发现问题数量，但复杂度和评分几乎不变。在匹配预算下，最佳n采样结合分析器加权排序器在排序器未优化的指标上表现匹配或优于提示、规划、自修正、评审代理和检索；而分析器反馈循环和微调仅在分析器问题数量上优于它。在HumanEval+上出现的大规模正确性损失（可能因接近天花板而被污染的基准）在模型训练截止后举行的竞赛中并未出现。在一对一比较中，66种技术中有1种在主要目标上超出最佳n采样的95%置信区间，该比例与随机水平一致。建议将增益报告与预算匹配的采样基线对比，并在技术自身信号无关的指标上进行评估。

Efficient Agentic Audio-Visual Question Answering through Toolset Pruning and Modality Consolidation
通过工具集剪枝与模态整合实现高效的智能体音频-视觉问答
Fine-grained audio-visual question answering (AVQA) requires models to resolve complex cross-modal correspondences and temporally grounding from sparsely distributed evidence. Omni-modal agents, which autonomously orchestrate perception tools to actively collect evidence, achieve strong performance, but it remains unclear whether active perception from both audio and visual modalities depends on broad specialized toolsets, especially audio or visual modality-specific tools. Such systems may also introduce long execution trajectories and substantial inference cost. We investigate whether comparable performance can be retained with a compact workflow exposing only essential perceptual capabilities. Starting from the specialized-tool OmniAgent, we conduct staged controlled experiments that prune the toolset and progressively consolidate modality-specific tools into omni-modal tools, motivating a two-tool agent comprising only Omni-modal captioning and Omni-modal QA. Across three widely used AVQA benchmarks, our compact agent achieves comparable or better accuracy than the specialized-tool agent while reducing inference cost by around 70%.

细粒度的音频-视觉问答（AVQA）要求模型解决复杂的跨模态对应关系和稀疏证据的时间定位问题。全模态智能体能够自主协调感知工具主动收集证据，表现出强大性能，但尚不清楚同时依赖音频和视觉模态的主动感知是否需要广泛的专业化工具集，尤其是针对特定模态的工具。此类系统也可能导致长执行轨迹和高昂的推理成本。我们探究是否可以在仅暴露必要感知能力的紧凑工作流中保持相当的性能。从专用工具的OmniAgent出发，我们开展分阶段受控实验，逐步剪枝工具集并将模态特定工具合并为全模态工具，最终提出仅包含全模态字幕生成和全模态问答两个工具的智能体。在三个广泛使用的AVQA基准上，我们的紧凑型智能体实现了与专用工具智能体相当甚至更优的准确率，同时推理成本降低了约70%。

Contextual Embeddings Beyond the Gold Chunk
超越黄金块的上下文嵌入
Contextual embeddings represent chunks of a document with respect to the document's global context. These models are typically trained and evaluated under the assumption that a relevant document contains only a single relevant chunk. In this work, we argue that in real-world use cases a retriever should not only find answers spread over several chunks but also surface the evidence needed to verify them. To address this, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-context, a family of contextual embedding models trained by distilling graded chunk-level relevance from a query-aware context compression model. This removes the need for gold-chunk labels and makes the training targets independent of the chunking. We utilize a vision-language backbone for training and release pplx-embed-v2-context in 9B and 0.6B sizes with compatible embedding spaces. On the evaluation side, we introduce two benchmarks that label both the chunks that answer a query and the evidence needed to verify the answer. Context-bench is a controlled diagnostic, and Q2D-Chunk is built from real-world search queries. To encourage further research, we also publish Q2D-Chunk as a public leaderboard. Our 9B model sets a new state of the art on both benchmarks and ConTEB. We find that the visual retrieval capabilities of our 9B model transfer from its vision-language base model and propose to extend chunking to image inputs. We demonstrate that our models achieve state-of-the-art performance among dense retrievers on visual ViDoRe-V3 and that performance improves with granular image chunking.
中文翻译
上下文嵌入将文档的片段与其全局上下文相关联。这些模型通常在假设相关文档仅包含单个相关片段的前提下进行训练和评估。本文认为，在实际应用场景中，检索器不仅应找到分布在多个片段中的答案，还应揭示验证答案所需的证据。为此，我们提出了pplx-embed-v2-context系列上下文嵌入模型，通过从查询感知的上下文压缩模型中蒸馏分级的片段级相关性进行训练。这消除了对黄金片段标签的需求，使训练目标独立于分块方式。我们采用视觉-语言骨干网络进行训练，并发布9B和0.6B两种规模的pplx-embed-v2-context模型，具有兼容的嵌入空间。在评估方面，我们引入两个基准，分别标注回答查询的片段以及验证答案所需的证据。Context-bench是一个受控诊断基准，Q2D-Chunk则基于真实世界搜索查询构建。为鼓励进一步研究，我们还将Q2D-Chunk公开为公共排行榜。我们的9B模型在两个基准及ConTEB上均达到新的领先水平。我们发现，9B模型的视觉检索能力源自其视觉-语言基础模型，并建议将分块扩展至图像输入。我们证明，我们的模型在视觉ViDoRe-V3上作为密集检索器达到最先进性能，且随着图像分块粒度细化，性能进一步提升。

AtomTree: A Hierarchical Framework for State-Aware Embodied Instruction Following with LLMs
AtomTree：一种基于大语言模型的状态感知具身指令遵循分层框架
Embodied instruction following in interactive environments requires agents to interpret natural language commands and complete long-horizon tasks. Existing planners either rely on large labeled trajectories, or use few-shot and zero-shot LLM prompting that produces a whole plan in a single serial reasoning process. These approaches depend on linguistic common sense, commit to a plan from the initial instruction, and only update after failures, which weakens robustness and state awareness. We present AtomTree, a hierarchical framework for state-aware task planning with LLMs. AtomTree incrementally builds a tree of subgoals and actions, where the LLM decides at each step, from the current symbolic state, whether to further decompose or to output a grounded low-level action. A lightweight memory module summarizes past outcomes for reuse in later decisions. When a low-level action stalls, AtomTree triggers hierarchical backtracking replanning to return to the parent node and regenerate the subtree plan from the latest state, preventing continued drift along an incorrect trajectory. In zero-shot evaluation on the ALFRED benchmark, AtomTree outperforms prior LLM-based planners. Project page: https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/.
中文翻译
在交互式环境中进行具身指令遵循需要智能体理解自然语言指令并完成长时程任务。现有规划方法要么依赖大量标注轨迹，要么使用少样本和零样本的大语言模型提示，以单一串行推理过程生成完整计划。这些方法依赖语言常识，从初始指令即确定计划，仅在失败后更新，削弱了鲁棒性和状态感知能力。我们提出AtomTree，一种基于大语言模型的状态感知任务规划分层框架。AtomTree逐步构建子目标与动作的树状结构，大语言模型在每一步根据当前符号化状态决定是否进一步分解或输出一个具体低层动作。一个轻量级记忆模块总结过往结果以供后续决策复用。当低层动作停滞时，AtomTree触发分层回溯重规划，返回父节点并基于最新状态重新生成子树计划，防止沿错误轨迹持续漂移。在ALFRED基准的零样本评估中，AtomTree优于先前基于大语言模型的规划器。项目页面：https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/
👤 Haotian Zhai, Tianming Sha, Junnan Li 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38691]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Beyond fabrication: Misattributed numbers in papers written by AI research agents
超越虚构：AI研究代理撰写的论文中的误归因数值问题
Verifying numerical claims in research written by AI agents is an important but challenging problem. Recent benchmarks show that many agent-written papers fail integrity checks, but such verdicts, often produced by a single LLM judge, identify failure without explaining its nature, and existing checks either test a paper for internal consistency or test whether a reported value appears somewhere in an agent's output. We re-analyze all 540 released judgments from a recent public benchmark of AI research agents (nine agents, fifteen open research problems) and manually label 256 numerical claims from 18 submissions. Failures concentrate on alignment between the paper and the underlying work rather than overt cheating, and about four in five unsupported claims correspond to values in the agent's own files that come from the wrong experiment, metric, or run. We introduce a deterministic claim-to-evidence checker that scores whether the surrounding context identifies the claimed result and calibrates each claim against numerical decoys. With its rules fixed before labeling, it certifies claims with over 90% precision against blinded annotators (78 of 85; Cohen's kappa = 0.89), while verifying only a subset of claims; most claims it cannot verify are nevertheless traceable, so failure to verify is not evidence of falsehood. An LLM verifier given the same evidence recovers more traceable claims but accepts about three times as many misattributed ones, so the two are best used together. Applied to 99 submissions, the checker reveals large differences in traceability across agents, and a second judge from a different vendor preserves the official agent ranking while passing twice as many papers. These findings motivate provenance-first auditing of AI-produced research.
中文翻译
验证由AI代理撰写的科研论文中的数值声明是一项重要但具有挑战性的问题。近期基准测试显示，许多代理撰写的论文未能通过完整性检查，但此类判断通常由单一大语言模型裁判产生，仅指出失败而未说明原因；现有检查方法要么测试论文内部一致性，要么检测报告值是否出现在代理输出的某处。我们重新分析了最近公开基准中540个已发布判断（九个代理，十五个开放研究问题），并手动标注了18份提交中的256个数值声明。失败主要集中在论文与底层工作之间的对齐问题，而非明显造假；约五分之四的无支持声明对应于代理自身文件中来自错误实验、度量或运行的数值。我们引入一种确定性的声明-证据核查器，通过判断上下文是否明确标识所声称的结果，并将每个声明与数值干扰项进行校准。在标签前固定规则的情况下，该核查器对盲评标注者实现了超过90%的精确率（85个中78个正确；Cohen's kappa = 0.89），同时仅验证部分声明；大多数无法验证的声明仍可追溯，因此无法验证不等于虚假。给予相同证据的大语言模型验证器能发现更多可追溯声明，但接受约三倍数量的误归因声明，因此两者结合使用效果最佳。应用于99份提交，核查器揭示了不同代理间可追溯性差异显著，且另一供应商的第二位裁判在保持官方代理排名的同时，通过了两倍数量的论文。这些发现推动了以溯源为先的AI生成研究审计范式。

AskStream: When and What to Ask Proactively under Uncertainty in Streaming Video Understanding
AskStream：在流式视频理解中不确定性下的主动提问时机与内容选择
Recent advances in multimodal large language models have increased interest in interaction paradigms for video understanding. The evolution from offline to streaming and proactive interaction has gradually shifted models from passive responders toward more proactive agents that can decide when to answer. However, existing proactive paradigms typically assume that the information required to answer a query is complete, leaving models to only wait or answer. In the real world, however, information is often incomplete due to uncertainty from either the video or the user. In proactive paradigms, such uncertainty can cause the model to wait forever or generate highly hallucinatory responses. We therefore argue that proactive interaction should be redefined with a third action: ask, allowing models to actively ask for missing information. To study when and what models should ask before answering, we introduce AskStream, the first multi-turn benchmark spanning video and user uncertainty for streaming video understanding, with four task categories and eight subtasks. It contains 438 videos with 2,714 manually annotated frame-level time windows across three types: wait, ask, and answer. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended settings, even the strongest model attains only 52.18% on our proposed Ask-F1 metric, highlighting substantial limitations in proactive information seeking under uncertainty.
中文翻译
多模态大语言模型的最新进展提升了人们对视频理解中交互范式的兴趣。从离线到流式及主动交互的演变，逐渐使模型从被动响应者转变为更主动的智能体，能够自主决定何时回答。然而，现有主动范式通常假设回答所需信息是完整的，导致模型只能等待或作答。在现实世界中，由于视频或用户带来的不确定性，信息往往不完整。在主动范式下，这种不确定性可能导致模型无限等待或生成高度幻觉的回应。因此，我们主张应重新定义主动交互，引入第三种动作：提问，使模型能够主动请求缺失信息。为研究模型应在何时以及如何提问后再作答，我们提出了AskStream，首个涵盖视频与用户不确定性的多轮流式视频理解基准，包含四类任务和八个子任务。该基准包含438段视频，共2,714个手动标注的帧级时间窗口，分为三种类型：等待、提问和回答。在多项选择和开放式设置下，即使最强模型在我们提出的Ask-F1指标上也仅达到52.18%，凸显了在不确定性下主动信息获取的重大局限。
👤 Lu Wang, Zhuoran Jin, Longxiang Wang, Junhong Chen, Yupu Hao et al. 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Right Numbers, Wrong Questions: Evaluating LLMs on Underspecified Financial Queries
正确的数字，错误的问题：评估大语言模型在模糊金融查询上的表现
Financial assistants answer questions that sound factual but depend on what the user leaves unsaid: what a data figure measures, which product variant is meant, and the investor's situation and the rules that apply. An assistant that ignores these can still answer with a real number from its data, one that answers a different question. We call this frame collapse. We study it in Indian mutual funds, where product variants and tax rules are formally defined, so the correct answer can be computed for every reading. One study tests each challenge. Given an ambiguously labelled figure, models used it, often under the user's label. Given every alternative labelled, they listed them all, but the layout decided which came first. Missing a fact about the investor, weaker agents assumed a default or an outdated tax rule; requiring the fact did not guarantee its use. Because such answers match their data, checks against the data pass them. An AI judge caught mislabelled figures only when told what each field means, and still passed answers that silently chose another reading. We show how these failures arise, not how often, and propose controls for financial assistants.
中文翻译
金融助手回答看似事实性的问题，但实际依赖于用户未明说的信息：数据指标的含义、产品变体的选择，以及投资者的情况和适用规则。忽略这些因素的助手仍能从其数据中给出一个真实数值，但该数值对应的是另一个问题的答案。我们称此现象为‘框架坍缩’。我们在印度共同基金场景中研究这一现象，由于产品变体和税务规则有明确定义，每个理解方式下都可计算出正确答案。一项研究测试了每种挑战：面对标签模糊的图表，模型常采用用户的标签；面对所有可能的标签，它们列出全部选项，但布局决定了优先顺序。缺少关于投资者的事实信息时，较弱的代理会假设默认或过时的税务规则；即使要求提供该事实，也不保证其被使用。由于这些答案与数据一致，基于数据的检查无法发现错误。AI裁判只有在被告知每个字段含义时才能识别错误标签，但仍会通过那些无声选择其他解读的答案。我们揭示了这些失败的成因（而非频率），并提出针对金融助手的控制机制。
👤 Radha Yadav, Prasanna Devadiga, Peeyush Mishra, Apoorv Singh 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Cross-Primitive Consistency in Jev
Jev中的跨原语一致性
Jev enables efficient decision-making with large language models (LLMs) through typed querying primitives, allowing the same semantic state to be expressed in different formats, such as binary and multiple-choice queries. However, the consistency of decisions across primitives has not been systematically evaluated. This work introduces PrimaCons, a four-axis benchmark for assessing cross-primitive consistency while preserving the semantics of the query. Evaluations across three Jev sizes reveal consistency violations across all four axes. Our analysis shows that these violations follow systematic patterns and that cross-primitive discrepancies predict decision failure with a ROC-AUC of 0.91. To mitigate these inconsistencies, we propose the Cross-Primitive Residual Calibrator (CPRC), which attaches a lightweight residual network to a frozen backbone and jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and cross-primitive consistency. Under a leave-one-domain-out protocol, CPRC reduces cross-primitive inconsistency by 58.57% and improves accuracy by +2.97%., demonstrating its effectiveness in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings. Our findings establish cross-primitive consistency as a key dimension for evaluating Jev and provide a practical approach to improving the reliability of its decisions. Code and benchmark is available at https://github.com/67L1/PrimaCons-CRPR.
中文翻译
Jev通过类型化查询原语实现大语言模型（LLM）的高效决策，允许同一语义状态以不同格式表达，如二元和多选查询。然而，跨原语决策的一致性尚未得到系统评估。本文提出PrimaCons，一个四轴基准，用于在保持查询语义的前提下评估跨原语一致性。对三种Jev规模的评估显示，所有四个轴上均存在一致性违规。分析表明，这些违规具有系统性模式，且跨原语差异可预测决策失败（ROC-AUC达0.91）。为缓解此类不一致，我们提出跨原语残差校准器（CPRC），在冻结主干网络上附加轻量级残差网络，并联合优化预测准确性和跨原语一致性。在留一域排除协议下，CPRC将跨原语不一致性降低58.57%，同时提升准确率2.97%，证明其在域内和域外设置下的有效性。我们的发现确立了跨原语一致性作为评估Jev的关键维度，并提供了提升决策可靠性的实用方法。代码与基准已公开于https://github.com/67L1/PrimaCons-CRPR。

Repairs for Whom? Evaluator-Executor Mismatch in Selecting Repairs for Failed LLM Agents
修复为谁？LLM代理失败时评估者-执行者不匹配问题
When an LLM agent fails, repair methods choose which action to replace and what to put in its place. They often score candidate repairs with greedy replay, a stronger model, an environment oracle, or a judge that reads the failed log. We ask whether these evaluators pick repairs that help the agent that will actually execute them. Holding failed trajectories and candidate single-step repairs fixed, we label every candidate with independent rollouts of the deployed policy and measure how much deployment value each evaluator's choice loses. On failures not screened for repair value, pre-specified tests show that greedy replay loses 0.08-0.13 in expected net success gain in ALFWorld and 0.06-0.13 in net reward in WebShop, relative to selection with the deployed agent's own rollouts. At an equal rollout budget, selecting repairs for Qwen2.5-7B with Qwen2.5-14B rollouts loses 0.25 in ALFWorld and 0.07 in WebShop, twenty 14B rollouts per candidate are worth no more than two of the 7B agent's own, and fresh rollouts confirm that the two models prefer opposite repairs on selected disagreement cases. Between similar-sized models from different families, the losses appear in both directions in both environments (0.07-0.17). The scoring executor also changes repair-location labels and method rankings. Repairs should be verified with the executor that will run them, and repair labels should name their executor.
中文翻译
当LLM代理失败时，修复方法需决定替换哪个动作及其替代方案。现有方法通常使用贪婪重播、更强模型、环境真值或阅读失败日志的裁判来评分候选修复。我们质疑这些评估者是否选择了真正有助于实际执行代理的修复方案。在固定失败轨迹和候选单步修复的前提下，我们通过部署策略的独立回放标注每个候选，并测量评估者选择所导致的部署价值损失。在未筛选修复价值的失败案例中，预设测试显示，贪婪重播在ALFWorld中导致预期净成功收益损失0.08–0.13，在WebShop中净奖励损失0.06–0.13；在相同回放预算下，使用Qwen2.5-14B回放选择Qwen2.5-7B的修复，导致ALFWorld损失0.25，WebShop损失0.07，二十次14B回放的价值不超过两次7B代理自身的回放，且新回放证实两模型在选定分歧案例中偏好相反的修复。在来自不同家族的相似尺寸模型之间，损失在两个方向均出现（0.07–0.17）。评分执行者还改变了修复位置标签和方法排名。修复应由实际执行者验证，修复标签应明确其执行者。

Rethinking LLM Jailbreak Evaluation: Continuous Risk Landscapes Beyond Binary Outcomes
重新思考LLM越狱评估：超越二元结果的连续风险图景
A binary success indicator is a natural representation for discriminative tasks such as image classification under adversarial perturbations, where each input is mapped to a single prediction. However, generative tasks are fundamentally different: an LLM jailbreak prompt induces a distribution over open-ended trajectories, while a sampled response reveals only one path. Yet LLM jailbreak evaluation typically retains the binary response indicator underlying attack success rate, obscuring the surrounding structure of possible generations. We introduce a continuous, context-conditioned risk landscape over first-token branches, estimated by fixing candidate openings and repeatedly sampling their continuations. This continuous risk-landscape perspective reveals two complementary properties: current decoder exposure and safe rerouting capacity, which have been obscured by binary-indicator-based ASR. The resulting representation also provides a common basis for comparing clean prompts, attacked variants, and attack strength against a known adaptive defense. Across multiple target models and jailbreak attacks, we uncover risky branches beneath apparently safe outputs, identify qualitatively different ways attacks reshape the model's opening distribution, and show that attacks strongest before a defense do not necessarily remain the strongest after the adaptive defense reacts. These findings recast jailbreaks as deformations of the surrounding continuation-risk landscape, establishing a more informative foundation for understanding jailbreak behavior and developing safer language models.
中文翻译
二元成功指标是图像分类等判别任务在对抗扰动下的自然表示，每个输入映射到单一预测。然而，生成任务本质不同：LLM越狱提示引发开放轨迹的分布，而采样响应仅揭示一条路径。但当前LLM越狱评估仍沿用基于二元指标的攻击成功率，掩盖了潜在生成结构。我们引入一种连续、上下文相关的首词分支风险图景，通过固定候选开头并反复采样其延续来估计。这种连续风险图景视角揭示了两个互补属性：当前解码器暴露程度和安全重路由能力，这些特性在传统二元指标ASR中被掩盖。该表示还为比较干净提示、受攻击变体及对抗已知自适应防御的攻击强度提供了统一基础。在多个目标模型和越狱攻击中，我们发现了看似安全输出背后的危险分支，识别出攻击重塑模型初始分布的多种定性方式，并表明在防御前最强的攻击未必在防御反应后仍最强。这些发现将越狱重新定义为周围延续风险图景的形变，为理解越狱行为和构建更安全语言模型奠定了更丰富的基础。

Similar Scores, Opposite Failures: Human Correction as an Instrument for Video Caption Evaluation
相似得分，相反失败：人类修正作为视频字幕评估的工具
Video captioning models are increasingly evaluated with metrics designed for single-sentence image captions. We test whether those metrics recover human judgement on dense, multi-window video description, and find that they do not. Six frontier models captioned 196 short clips. Trained annotators post-edited every caption against a failure taxonomy fixed before annotation, once as a whole-clip overview and once window by window, and a quality-control reviewer checked each annotation, yielding 1,144 overview verdicts and 4,672 window verdicts. Reference-overlap metrics do not separate the captions reviewers accepted from those they rejected: BLEU-4 reaches an AUC of 0.510 and a CIDEr-style tf-idf cosine 0.528, and restricting references to reviewer corrections leaves BLEU-4 at 0.479 (95% CI 0.431-0.529), indistinguishable from chance. Review granularity changes what is detected. The same annotators record camera and shot errors on 2.7% of captions when reading the overview and on 12.8% when reading window by window, a 4.7x gap that holds across every robustness check we ran, on the mode most likely to need a near-total rewrite. Correct-overview rates span 54.1-72.7%, but only 4 of 15 pairwise gaps survive Bonferroni correction (5 after adjusting for reviewer and clip effects), and accuracy and derived severity order the models differently. Models with statistically indistinguishable accuracy have sharply different failure profiles, which no single scalar shows. We argue that caption evaluation should record what was wrong rather than only how often, and we report the taxonomy, the per-model profiles, the corpus properties that predict difficulty, and the code that regenerates every number in the paper.
中文翻译
视频字幕模型越来越多地使用为单句图像字幕设计的评估指标进行评价。我们测试这些指标是否能有效反映人类在密集多窗口视频描述上的判断，发现它们并不能。六种前沿模型对196段短视频进行了字幕生成。训练标注员根据预先设定的失败分类法，分别以整体片段概览和逐窗口方式对每个字幕进行后编辑，质量控制评审员检查每条标注，共获得1,144条整体评价和4,672条窗口级评价。参考重叠类指标无法区分评审员接受与拒绝的字幕：BLEU-4的AUC仅为0.510，CIDEr风格的tf-idf余弦相似度为0.528，即使仅保留评审员修正后的参考文本，BLEU-4仍为0.479（95%置信区间0.431–0.529），与随机猜测无异。评审粒度影响检测能力：同一组标注员在阅读整体概览时记录到2.7%的摄像机与镜头错误，而在逐窗口阅读时达到12.8%，差距达4.7倍，且在所有稳健性检验中均成立，尤其在最可能需要全面重写的模式下。整体修正率在54.1%至72.7%之间波动，但仅有4组15对比较在Bonferroni校正后仍显著（调整评审员和视频效应后为5组），准确率与推导出的严重性排序对模型的评价不同。准确率统计上无差异的模型具有截然不同的失败模式，单一标量无法体现。我们认为字幕评估应记录错误类型而非仅统计频率，并报告了分类体系、各模型失败特征、预测难度的语料属性，以及可重现论文中所有数据的代码。

Manifest-Assisted Retrieval in Artificial Intelligence Search: Evidence Gaps and a Security Evaluation
人工智能搜索中的显式辅助检索：证据缺口与安全评估
The external sources retained in our non-systematic audit did not isolate the incremental security or utility effect of consuming /llms.txt while ordinary retrieval remained fixed. The initial audit covered 39 sources through August 16, 2026, with later targeted updates through October 14. A purposive 15-tool study observed no automatic requests in 45 trials at one endpoint; a non-probability log study found that 97% of domains with detected files received no recorded requests in May 2026. We separate publication, fetch, model use, and action; define five operational threat paths and paired security-utility estimands; and exercise deterministic pre-model controls with 30 author-defined parser fixtures. We also execute a reduced pilot: 24 tasks across eight sites, five conditions, and 120 real model responses, with ordinary evidence held fixed. Frozen automatic scoring identifies attacker-target adoption in $21/24$ raw adversarial responses, with one further response unresolved; the adversarial URLs-only condition has no known adoptions and one unresolved response. The pilot measures output changes conditional on supplied evidence, without live retrieval or external actions. Together, the framework and pilot distinguish manifest-content effects from publication, discovery, and downstream execution, while leaving full retrieval and agent evaluation open.
中文翻译
我们在非系统性审计中保留的外部来源未能隔离消费 /llms.txt 文件所带来的增量安全或效用效应，而普通检索保持不变。初始审计覆盖39个来源，截至2026年8月16日，后续有针对更新至10月14日。一项有目的性的15工具研究在一处端点的45次试验中未观察到自动请求；一项非概率日志研究发现，2026年5月，97%存在检测文件的域名均无记录请求。我们区分了发布、获取、模型使用和行动四个环节；定义五条操作威胁路径及对应的安全部分-效用估计量；并使用30个作者定义的解析器固定装置执行确定性预模型控制。此外还执行了一项简化试点：在八个站点、五个条件、24个任务、120个真实模型响应中，保持普通证据不变。冻结的自动评分识别出21/24条原始对抗响应中存在攻击者目标采纳行为，另有1条未明确；仅含对抗URL的条件无已知采纳且1条未明确。试点测量了在提供证据条件下的输出变化，不涉及实时检索或外部操作。总体框架与试点将显式内容效应与发布、发现及下游执行相区分，同时保留完整检索与智能体评估的开放性。
👤 Gangzhen Qian, Kainan Zhou, Chuhong Xu, Hang Xiao, Zhi Jing et al. 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Calculation and rejection diverge in language models tested at known mechanical limits
在已知机械极限下测试的语言模型中，计算与拒绝行为出现分歧
When a language model returns engineering quantities with the status "solved", software that trusts it passes them on, even when the requested design is physically impossible. Such failures could reflect poor calculation, how the answer is requested, or a failure to judge feasibility. We set beam loads and disk speeds at known percentages above or below plastic collapse or burst, measured each model's limit calculation separately and tested 12 models in 4,860 calls, including follow-up experiments on new designs. We find that a model can recognize an impossible design when asked only whether it is feasible, yet accept the same design, reporting "solved", when asked for a full answer. Three models accepted beams 10 to 30% beyond collapse despite precise limit calculations in separate questions, and many accepted replies stated the violation. Asked only about feasibility, Opus 5 and Gemini 3.8 Flash rejected every impossible new design they were given, including those accepted in full answers, without false alarms. Rewording when to decline or changing the answer format raised rejection on the original beams, but not consistently on new designs. Software and benchmarks using a model's status should test feasibility judgment and full-answer rejection separately, and report the exact prompt.
中文翻译
当语言模型返回工程量并标注为'已解决'时，依赖它的软件会直接传递这些结果，即使所请求的设计在物理上不可能实现。这类失败可能源于计算不佳、答案请求方式不当，或对可行性判断的缺失。我们设定梁载荷和盘片转速在塑性屈服或爆裂极限的已知百分比之上或之下，分别测量每个模型的极限计算能力，并在4860次调用中测试了12个模型，包括对新设计的后续实验。研究发现，当仅询问设计是否可行时，模型能识别不可能的设计；但当要求完整回答时，却接受相同设计并报告'已解决'。三个模型接受了超出屈服极限10%至30%的梁结构，尽管它们在单独问题中进行了精确的极限计算，且许多接受的回答明确指出了违规情况。仅询问可行性时，Opus 5和Gemini 3.8 Flash对所有被给予的不可能新设计均予以拒绝，包括那些在完整回答中被接受的设计，且无误报。改变拒绝时机或答案格式虽提高了原始梁结构的拒绝率，但在新设计上并不一致。因此，使用模型状态的软件和基准应分别测试可行性判断与完整回答拒绝能力，并准确报告提示内容。
👤 Shaoliang Yang, Jun Wang 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Measured or Real? When Model-Derived Measurements Can Be Corrected or Compared
测量值还是真实值？何时可修正或比较模型生成的测量结果
Numbers computed from trained models, such as an example's difficulty or a model's benchmark lead, change with seed and prompt. We ask when such measurements can be corrected or compared. Noise shrinks correlations, and the textbook correction divides by the square root of two estimated reliabilities. On two CIFAR-100 difficulty scores, it returned correlations above one in 70 of 100 classes. We prove that its mean squared error is infinite even when the correlation and the reliabilities use separate seeds. Our rule corrects only when both reliabilities pass a significance test, then divides by their upper confidence bounds. Its output is always bounded, and its coverage guarantees assume Gaussian noise. It helps once reliability is well estimated, more than halving the error with 500 simulated units. Prompt settings can reverse rankings. On ARC, Qwen2-1.5B trails Llama-3.2-3B by 3.8 points with no examples in the prompt and leads by 16.6 with two. Across four benchmarks, averaging the two settings names one winner for 15 of 16 such reversals. We compare models within each setting, controlling family-wise error asymptotically in the number of items for a fixed prompt grid. Aligning embedding coordinates across seeds raises held-out per-dimension reliability from 0.003 to 0.813.
中文翻译
从训练好的模型中计算出的数值（如样本难度或模型在基准上的领先度）会随随机种子和提示内容变化。我们探讨了这些测量值何时可以被修正或进行比较。噪声会降低相关性，而教科书中的修正方法是除以两个估计可靠性平方根的乘积。在两个CIFAR-100难度评分上，该方法在100个类别中有70个返回的相关系数超过1。我们证明，即使相关性和可靠性使用独立种子，其均方误差仍为无穷大。我们的规则仅在两个可靠性均通过显著性检验时才进行修正，然后除以其置信区间的上限。该方法输出始终有界，其覆盖率假设噪声服从高斯分布。当可靠性估计充分时，该方法效果显著，使500个模拟单元的误差减少超过一半。提示设置可逆转排名：在ARC任务中，Qwen2-1.5B在提示中无示例时落后于Llama-3.2-3B 3.8分，而在加入两个示例后则领先16.6分。在四个基准上，对两种设置取平均后，在16次排名反转中有15次得出同一胜者。我们控制固定提示网格下项目数量的族系错误率，对每种设置内模型进行比较。通过跨种子对齐嵌入坐标，可将保留维度的可靠性从0.003提升至0.813。

SciGame: Let Agents Play the Scientific Discovery Games in the Real and Counterfactual World
SciGame：让智能体在真实与反事实世界中玩科学发现游戏
Large language model agents are increasingly used for scientific discovery. However, existing benchmarks mix law inference with programming, score data fitting instead of prediction on unseen inputs, and do not separate empirical evidence from pretrained priors. We introduce SciGame, a controlled benchmark that converts scientific literature into structured scientific cards. Each card yields a pair of Original and dimensionally consistent Counterfactual environments with the same task interface. SciGame contains a Historical collection of canonical laws and a live Temporal collection curated from papers published after model knowledge cutoffs. It evaluates agents in candidate-guided Identification and open-ended Discovery settings. Each setting runs under Prior-only, Fixed, and Adaptive protocols. Experiments on five frontier models yield three findings. First, familiar priors hurt performance once the governing relation is altered. Second, adaptive experimentation helps the strongest models but harms weaker ones under limited budgets. Third, good explanations can hide quantitative failures. Recovering the exact symbolic form remains the main bottleneck, even when agents interpret evidence correctly. Current agents can investigate systems actively, but inducing exact laws in unfamiliar settings remains an open challenge.
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体正越来越多地应用于科学发现。然而，现有基准测试混合了定律推断与编程任务，以数据拟合得分代替对未见输入的预测能力评估，并未区分实证证据与预训练先验知识。我们提出SciGame，一个受控基准，将科学文献转化为结构化的科学卡片。每张卡片生成一对原始环境与维度一致的反事实环境，具有相同的任务接口。SciGame包含历史版经典定律集合和从模型知识截止日期后发表论文中持续更新的实时时间集合。它在候选引导识别与开放式发现两种设置下评估智能体。每种设置在仅先验、固定和自适应三种协议下运行。对五款前沿模型的实验揭示三个发现：第一，一旦主导关系发生变化，熟悉的先验知识反而损害性能；第二，自适应实验对最强模型有益，但在资源有限的情况下对较弱模型有害；第三，良好解释可能掩盖定量失败。恢复精确符号形式仍是主要瓶颈，即使智能体正确解读了证据。当前智能体能够主动探索系统，但在陌生情境中推导精确规律仍是开放挑战。

Think Twice: The Metis-1.6 Technical Report
再思：Metis-1.6技术报告
Metis is a bet that better data, better training methods and new architectures can buy what a bigger parameter count cannot, at a fraction of the cost. Metis-1.6 is its latest step: two language models built from scratch by a single researcher, Praxis (2.94B parameters, 0.46B active per pass) and Logos (10.23B parameters, 1.18B active per pass). Both run a hybrid Mamba-2 and attention mixture-of-experts stack twice for every token, so each token gets a second look. Each was trained on 1.02T tokens in total: 804.76B of pretraining, 18B of context extension to 131,072 tokens, and 200B of supervised fine-tuning. Metis-1.6 was never meant to challenge frontier models, and across 40 post-trained benchmarks it mostly does not: Logos averages 52.93, seventh of 17 models in its comparison set and within a point of Qwen3-4B. But it gets there on roughly a twelfth of the median training data of the comparators that disclose it, and with fewer active parameters per token than any model that beats it, which puts it on the Pareto frontier for both. In mathematics it ranks second of 30 models, with 67.50% on AIME 2025 and 63.64% on HMMT February 2026. It is weak at code, instruction following and long context. The rest of the report is the part I found most valuable: what went wrong. The optimizer I configured never ran, and attention trained with AdamW for all of pretraining. A 1.77B-parameter n-gram memory learned to switch itself off. Five fine-tuning choices cost capability. And a length reward under group-mean normalization punished correct solutions for being long once a group's pass rate passed 2/3, a sign inversion I prove and fix with a one-line clamp. I close with the 10B-token ablations behind MoRE (Mixture of Recursive Experts), the looped architecture of Metis-1.7.
中文翻译
Metis是一种信念：更好的数据、更优的训练方法和新型架构可以在远低于大参数量的成本下实现原本需要更大参数才能达成的效果。Metis-1.6是其最新进展：由单个研究人员Praxis从头构建的两个语言模型——Praxis（29.4亿参数，每轮激活0.46亿）和Logos（102.3亿参数，每轮激活11.8亿）。两者每token执行两次混合Mamba-2与注意力的专家混合堆栈，实现二次审视。每个模型总共训练1.02万亿token：8047.6亿用于预训练，180亿用于上下文扩展至131,072 token，2000亿用于监督微调。Metis-1.6本非为挑战前沿模型而设计，在40个微调后基准测试中表现平平：Logos平均得分为52.93，17个对比模型中排名第7，与Qwen3-4B相差不到1分。但其训练数据仅为对比模型中位数的十二分之一，且每token活跃参数少于所有击败它的模型，使其在两项指标上均位于帕累托前沿。在数学任务中，它位列30个模型中的第二，AIME 2025得分为67.50%，HMMT February 2026得分为63.64%。其在代码生成、指令遵循和长上下文处理方面表现较弱。报告其余部分是我认为最有价值的部分：失败原因分析。我配置的优化器从未运行，整个预训练阶段注意力模块均使用AdamW训练。一个17.7亿参数的n元语法记忆模块学会自我关闭。五个微调选择导致能力损失。此外，长度奖励在组均值归一化下惩罚了较长的正确解，一旦组通过率超过2/3，便产生符号反转；我证明并用一行代码修复了此问题。最后，报告详述了支撑Metis-1.7循环架构的100亿token消融实验，即MoRE（递归专家混合）。
👤 Giulianno Vollmer 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37849]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Detecting Covert Collusion between AI Agents through Partner-Outsider Positive Listening
通过伙伴-外部者正向倾听检测AI代理间的隐秘共谋
AI agents that share a secret convention can coordinate through messages that look innocent, so a monitor that reads only the messages can miss them. We propose a behavioural test: a partner that uses a code reacts to the exact wording of a message, whereas an uninvolved agent placed in its seat does not. Partner--Outsider Positive Listening (PoPLi) is the difference between the two agents in how much their action changes when the message is reworded with its meaning kept, and it is computed after the fact by replaying logged prompts. In a simulated market with five conditions (no collusion, a joint reward with neutral messages, the same with an openly stated intention, a covert first-letter code, and the code with a stated intention), PoPLi is between 0.48 and 0.59 whenever the code is present (95% intervals all above 0.29) and between -0.10 and -0.01 without it. Message-only monitors did not separate the conditions: one reading the speaker's messages answered "no" in all 50 markets, and one reading every seller's messages flagged 9 of 10 markets with no collusion. PoPLi detects the use of a private channel, not collusion as such, and it cannot detect collusion through incentives alone or codes carried by meaning.
中文翻译
共享秘密约定的AI代理可通过看似无害的消息进行协调，因此仅阅读消息的监控系统可能遗漏此类行为。我们提出一种行为测试：使用编码的伙伴会根据消息的具体措辞作出反应，而处于相同位置的无关代理则不会。伙伴-外部者正向倾听（PoPLi）衡量这两个代理在消息重述但语义保持不变时，其行为变化程度的差异，该指标可在事后通过回放日志计算。在包含五种情境的模拟市场中（无共谋、联合奖励+中性消息、相同但明确声明意图、隐蔽首字母编码、编码加声明意图），只要存在编码，PoPLi值介于0.48至0.59之间（95%置信区间均高于0.29），而无编码时则介于-0.10至-0.01之间。仅读取发言者消息的监控系统无法区分这些条件：一个仅读取说话者消息的监控者在全部50个市场中均回答“否”；另一个读取所有卖家消息的监控者在无共谋的10个市场中误报了9个。PoPLi检测的是私密通信通道的使用，而非共谋本身，也无法仅通过激励或语义承载的编码检测共谋。
👤 Kevin Yandoka Denamganaï 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37741]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Agentic Backdoors via Pretraining Poisoning
通过预训练污染实现代理后门攻击
Large language models are pretrained on vast, uncurated corpora scraped from the public web. This creates a security risk from attackers who plant poisoned content in the pretraining data. We show for the first time that such poisoning can plant a remote code execution backdoor in an agent, causing it to execute attacker-chosen commands on the user's machine. To measure the threat end to end, we pretrain Qwen3 models from scratch at sizes 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B, and then post-train each into a CLI agent using a clean pipeline. Poisoning only 0.1% of the pretraining corpus with our new poison design implants the backdoor: the deployed agent executes the malicious command when the trigger is present while behaving normally on clean inputs. The attack success rate (ASR) increases with model scale, with the 4B model reaching roughly 92% ASR at a 0.1% poison rate and staying positive even when the poisoning rate drops to 0.0005%. Our code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/agentic-backdoor.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在海量、未经筛选的公共网络爬取语料上进行预训练，这为攻击者在预训练数据中植入有毒内容创造了安全风险。我们首次证明，此类污染可在一个智能体中植入远程代码执行后门，使其在用户机器上执行攻击者指定的命令。为端到端评估威胁，我们从头开始预训练Qwen3模型，分别在0.6B、1.7B和4B三种规模下进行，并通过干净的流程将其微调为CLI代理。仅用我们新设计的毒化方案污染0.1%的预训练语料，即可植入后门：部署后的代理在触发词出现时执行恶意命令，而在干净输入下表现正常。攻击成功率（ASR）随模型规模增加而提升，4B模型在0.1%污染率下ASR约为92%，即使污染率降至0.0005%仍保持正值。代码已公开于https://github.com/safety-research/agentic-backdoor。
👤 Xinyan Hu, Pingbang Hu, Chloe Loughridge, Xuanli He, Nicholas Carlini et al. 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

OpenFrontiers: A Collection of Open Research Problems and a Benchmark for Scientific Rediscovery
OpenFrontiers：一个开放研究问题集合与科学再发现的基准测试
Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in research-level problem solving, yet a comprehensive collection of such problems is still missing, and evaluating them on research-level problems remains challenging. We introduce OpenFrontiers, a cross-disciplinary resource that combines a structured corpus of open research problems with a retrospective benchmark of recently resolved problems in mathematics. The corpus records problem statements, context, progress, and other information for 12,151 problems. The benchmark contains 200 problems, 110 resolved in 2026 and 90 still open, each with a Lean~4 statement checked by automated probes and two independent reviews; a subset HQ-50 further filters based on quality score, etc. Our evaluation protocol grades formal proofs with two independent checkers in an isolated sandbox under closed-book, frozen-retrieval, and live-web conditions. Evaluating seven LLM agents on the HQ-50 subset under a standardized budget, we find that closed-book rediscovery is rare, at most 7 of 40 resolved problems, whereas live-web success mostly reflects retrieval of published solutions. By linking a broad research-problem corpus to a formally graded evaluation subset with documented solutions, OpenFrontiers provides a foundation for measuring research-level rediscovery and studying progress toward solving currently open scientific problems. The dataset and benchmark could be found in https://huggingface.co/collections/FlippyDora/openfrontiers.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在解决研究级问题方面展现出日益增长的潜力，但目前仍缺乏全面的研究问题集合，且在研究级问题上的评估依然具有挑战性。我们提出了OpenFrontiers，一个跨学科资源，结合了结构化的开放研究问题语料库和数学领域近期已解决问题的回顾性基准。该语料库记录了12,151个问题的问题陈述、背景、进展等信息。基准包含200个问题，其中110个在2026年已解决，90个仍待解决，每个问题均配有Lean~4形式化陈述，并经过自动化探测器和两次独立评审；其中HQ-50子集进一步基于质量评分等标准进行筛选。我们的评估协议在封闭书本、冻结检索和实时网络条件下，使用隔离沙箱中的两个独立检查器对形式化证明进行评分。在标准化预算下对七个LLM智能体在HQ-50子集上的评估显示，封闭书本条件下的再发现极为罕见，最多仅能解决40个问题中的7个，而实时网络的成功主要反映对已发表解决方案的检索。通过将广泛的研究问题语料库与形式化评分的评估子集及已记录解决方案相连接，OpenFrontiers为衡量研究级再发现和研究当前开放科学问题的进展提供了基础。数据集和基准可在https://huggingface.co/collections/FlippyDora/openfrontiers获取。

DuQUAD: A Dual-View Framework with Quality-Aware Evidence Pruning for Multi-Document Question Answering
DuQUAD：一种带有质量感知证据剪枝的双视角框架用于多文档问答
Large language models with retrieval-augmented generation systematically underutilize evidence in the middle of multi-document contexts, a phenomenon known as Lost in the Middle. Existing mitigation strategies, such as document reordering and attention steering, can reduce boundary bias but remain brittle in noisy settings, where inaccurate relevance estimation and spurious documents still obscure useful evidence in central positions. We propose DuQUAD, a multi-document QA framework that mitigates positional bias through dual-view reasoning and quality-aware evidence pruning. DuQUAD combines a Local Agent guided by a Structural Fusion Score with a Global Agent that reasons over the full document set, providing both targeted evidence recovery and broad contextual understanding. The framework further filters candidate evidence using explicit quality scoring and refines it at the sentence level to reduce noise. DuQUAD consistently outperforms strong Lost-in-the-Middle mitigation baselines, including hybrid retrieval and reranking, multi-agent reasoning, and context optimization methods, improving accuracy by 3.0% on average over the strongest competing baseline and gold evidence recall by 9.4%, while substantially reducing computational cost compared to existing multi-agent frameworks.
中文翻译
具备检索增强生成的大语言模型在处理多文档上下文时系统性地低估中间位置的证据，这一现象被称为“迷失在中间”。现有的缓解策略，如文档重排和注意力引导，虽可减少边界偏差，但在噪声环境中仍显脆弱，因为不准确的相关性估计和虚假文档仍会掩盖中心位置的有用证据。我们提出DuQUAD，一种多文档问答框架，通过双视角推理和质量感知证据剪枝来缓解位置偏差。DuQUAD结合了由结构融合得分引导的局部代理与对整个文档集进行推理的全局代理，既实现针对性的证据恢复，又提供广泛的上下文理解。该框架进一步通过显式的质量评分过滤候选证据，并在句子层面进行精炼以减少噪声。DuQUAD持续优于强基线的“迷失在中间”缓解方法，包括混合检索与重排序、多代理推理和上下文优化方法，在最强竞争基线上平均提升准确率3.0%，黄金证据召回率提升9.4%，同时相比现有多代理框架显著降低了计算成本。
👤 Yeonjun Choi, Seyeon Jeong, Jong Wook Kim, Beakcheol Jang 📅 2026-10-25 🔗 arXiv

🧠 agent-memory (70篇)

[2610.40627]

ShallowDefend: Harnessing Shallow Safety Alignment for Multi-Turn Defender
ShallowDefend：利用浅层安全对齐实现多轮防御者
Safety alignment has enabled large language models to reliably refuse harmful requests directly, yet the same request distributed across a multi-turn conversation still succeeds far too often. This brittleness has a mechanistic root: Alignment is shallow, with safety behavior concentrated in a model's first few output tokens, and existing defenses inherit the weakness, supervising the surface form of the response without ever giving the model an explicit safety decision to learn. We take the opposite view of shallowness: if a short span determines the trajectory of an entire response, that span is the highest-leverage place to intervene, and the defender can occupy it first. We propose Assess $\to$ Decide $\to$ Respond, a framework in which the defender assesses the user's intent, commits to an explicit, parseable safety decision, and only then generates a reply conditioned on it, with the commitment trained by reinforcement learning at the payload turn. Because the decision is a token span, the reward can be a rule: a self-verified reward checks the committed label against the conversation's known nature, requiring no judge and no annotation, while the same format also supports an LLM-as-judge reward over a graded decision space with safe redirection. Across two model families and fourteen benchmarks spanning safety, utility, and helpfulness, our defenders achieve the strongest multi-turn safety in a controlled comparison, maintaining their lead in safety while preserving utility at a modest cost to helpfulness. A per-token KL analysis and interventional probes confirm why this works: training concentrates its effect on the brief deliberation that sets a response's trajectory, and the decision committed there is a causal control point. The very shallowness attackers exploit becomes the mechanism of defense.
中文翻译
安全对齐已使大型语言模型能够可靠地拒绝有害请求，但同样的请求若分散在多轮对话中仍经常成功。这种脆弱性具有机制根源：对齐是浅层的，安全行为集中在模型输出的前几个词元，而现有防御方法继承了这一弱点，仅监督响应的表面形式，从未让模型学习明确的安全决策。我们从相反角度看待浅层性：如果一个短片段决定了整个响应的轨迹，那么这个片段就是干预的最高杠杆点，防御者应优先占据它。我们提出Assess → Decide → Respond框架，其中防御者评估用户意图，做出明确且可解析的安全决策，然后才生成基于该决策的回复，且该决策在负载轮次通过强化学习训练。由于决策是一个词元片段，奖励可以是规则：自验证奖励检查所承诺的标签与对话已知性质的一致性，无需人工评判或标注；同一格式也支持基于分级决策空间的安全引导的LLM作为评判者奖励。在两个模型家族和十四项涵盖安全、效用和助人性的基准中，我们的防御者在受控比较中实现了最强的多轮安全性，在保持安全领先的同时，以适度牺牲助人性为代价维持了效用。每词元KL分析和干预探针证实其有效原因：训练效果集中在决定响应轨迹的短暂思辨阶段，而该阶段的决策是因果控制点。攻击者利用的浅层性反而成为防御机制。
👤 Qiyao Ma, Xiaodong Liu, Weiwei Yang, Mingqian Feng, Chengquan Guo et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.40619]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Testimony from Timelines: Evidence-Grounded Significance Testing of Temporal Clinical Hypotheses
时间线中的证词：基于证据的时间临床假说显著性检验
Ask a language model whether sales tend to dip after a product recall, or whether one side effect tends to follow another after a vaccine, and it answers in a fluent sentence that reads like a finding, with no test behind it. Assertions about how events unfold across many cases fill the Web, and each is a temporal hypothesis that only a significance test over the right records can settle. The verifier must specify the null, select the test and decide which records bear on the hypothesis, and any of these choices can go wrong while the conclusion still comes out right. Yet benchmarks for language-model data analysis grade only the conclusion, with the relevant data handed over. We study the problem in health, where a wrong verdict is costly, and present STRATA, a benchmark that grades every decision on the way to a verdict: 8,735 hypotheses over the clinical timelines of vaccine adverse-event reports, with gold plans, evidence sets, p-values and verdicts derived under a released codebook and reproduced by a deterministic oracle. We also propose ART (articulate, retrieve, test), a staged paradigm that exposes each decision as an inspectable output. Extensive experiments with five model configurations show that systems fail mostly at choosing which records count, a verdict-evidence gap that accuracy alone conceals. ART closes most of this gap and brings a 31B open model to within 0.01 of the frontier models under the same paradigm; the frontier models succeed once given the codebook.
中文翻译
询问语言模型销售是否在产品召回后下降，或某种副作用是否在疫苗接种后常伴随另一种出现，模型会给出流畅的句子，看似结论，却无任何统计检验支撑。关于事件在多案例中如何展开的断言充斥网络，每一项都是只有在正确记录上进行显著性检验才能确定的时间假说。验证者必须指定零假设、选择检验方法并决定哪些记录与假说相关，这些任一选择出错都可能导致错误结论，但结果仍可能正确。然而，当前语言模型数据分析的基准仅评价最终结论，相关数据已预先提供。我们在医疗领域研究此问题，因为错误判决代价高昂，提出STRATA基准，对通向判决的每一个决策进行评分：8,735个关于疫苗不良事件报告临床时间线的假说，包含金标准计划、证据集、p值和判决，均基于公开代码书生成，并由确定性归因器复现。我们还提出ART（阐述、检索、检验）范式，将每个决策暴露为可检查的输出。五种模型配置的大量实验表明，系统主要在选择哪些记录相关上失败，造成判决与证据之间的差距，而仅靠准确率无法察觉。ART弥合了大部分差距，使31B开源模型在相同范式下接近前沿模型表现（差距仅0.01）；前沿模型在获得代码书后才取得成功。

When to Assist and What to Suggest: Joint Gating and Memory-Grounded Hint Generation in One Compact Decoder
何时协助与建议什么：在单一紧凑解码器中联合门控与记忆锚定提示生成
Proactive assistants offer short, memory-grounded hints during a conversation, deciding when to assist and what to suggest. Current systems split this between a gate and a larger generator; we evaluate both together on 28,058 decision points. Stage-wise generation scores are oracle-gate ceilings: behind a real gate, end-to-end exact match is 0.2619 against a ceiling of 0.2971, and end-to-end precision is 0.2387, leaving substantial headroom. A gate pays off through precision and compute, whose saving falls from 4.39x with an 8B generator to 1.38x with a 1B one. The memory profile drives hint quality: removing it costs 24.7 exact-match points (rescaled BERTScore 0.57 to 0.35), while retrieval and a larger generator add nothing on exact match. One 1B decoder can do both, gating from an intermediate layer and generating in the same pass. At its final checkpoint it matches or exceeds the two-model pipelines on automatic end-to-end measures with 1.31-1.76x less wall-clock time than the matched 1B pipeline; across three seeds, its deeper exit ties the separate gate. A human evaluation (1,144 ratings over 32 dialogues) supports the memory effect and rates the single model level with the matched 1B pipeline and below the 8B one. Graded metrics and LlamaPIE's native judge track human ratings more closely than exact match, which we report as a strict lower bound.

Every Token Needs Its Own Teacher: Multi-teacher Adaptive On-policy Self-distillation
每个标记都需要自己的教师：多教师自适应在线自我蒸馏
On-policy Self-distillation (OPSD) has proven effective for post-training large language models by constructing a teacher conditioned on privileged references. However, existing approaches keep the teacher fixed either throughout training by consistently exposing it to the full reference, or within each response by adaptively varying partial reference exposure only across responses. In this paper, we argue that neither of them is sufficient. Instead, every token needs its own teacher, as the teacher preference can vary token by token within the same response. Through a teacher-intervention pilot study, we validate that the teacher preference differs across tokens, and reveal that a preferred teacher can be identified for each token through majority voting and KL-based selection. Motivated by these findings, we propose Multi-teacher Adaptive On-policy Self-Distillation (MTA-OPSD), the first token-level adaptive OPSD method. Our method constructs a multi-teacher pool consisting of candidate teachers, and enables every token to adaptively select its preferred teacher during training. Experiments with Qwen3 models on mathematical reasoning tasks show that MTA-OPSD consistently outperforms existing OPSD approaches with an average gain of up to $+5.9$ over the base model, highlighting the importance of adapting teacher supervision at the token level. The code is available at https://github.com/xingzhejun/MTA-OPSD.
中文翻译
在线自我蒸馏（OPSD）已被证明在后训练大语言模型方面有效，其通过构建依赖特权参考的教师模型实现。然而，现有方法要么在整个训练过程中固定教师（始终暴露于完整参考），要么仅在每次响应内通过自适应调整部分参考暴露来变化教师。本文认为这两种方式均不充分。相反，每个标记都应有其专属教师，因为同一响应内的教师偏好可能随标记变化。通过教师干预试点研究，我们验证了不同标记间教师偏好存在差异，并发现可通过多数投票和基于KL的选择识别每个标记的优选教师。基于此，我们提出多教师自适应在线自我蒸馏（MTA-OPSD），这是首个实现标记级自适应的OPSD方法。该方法构建包含候选教师的多教师池，使每个标记在训练中可自适应选择其偏好教师。在Qwen3模型上针对数学推理任务的实验表明，MTA-OPSD持续优于现有OPSD方法，平均性能提升达+5.9，凸显了在标记级别进行教师监督自适应的重要性。代码已公开于https://github.com/xingzhejun/MTA-OPSD。

Glance: The Generator Is Its Own Reranker
Glance：生成器即是其自身重排序器
Retrieval-augmented generation commonly serves separate models for reranking and answering, duplicating model residency and passage processing. We introduce Glance, which equips the generator with a lightweight decision adapter and pointer head to score candidate passages from a shared prefill. Short relevance probes branch from the cached context, producing one score per passage without separately prefilling each query-passage pair. A single resident 9B backbone supports generation, general decisions and reranking, with approximately 0.1 GiB of additional memory per decision skill. After 39 minutes of MS MARCO fine-tuning, Glance achieves nDCG@10 of 0.741 on SciFact and 0.345 on NFCorpus, compared with 0.721 and 0.326 for bge-reranker-v2-m3. On 300 HotpotQA questions, its selected context improves answer F1 by 0.045 over cross-encoder-selected context under the same base generator (paired bootstrap 95% CI [0.014, 0.076]). In a scaling study, reranking quality keeps rising up to 200 candidates (43k tokens) scored from one shared prefill, where it exceeds the cross-encoder by 0.058 nDCG@10, and a probe-only activated adapter lets generation continue from the original passage cache. These initial results demonstrate generator-based reranking with one resident backbone; evaluation currently covers two retrieval datasets, one QA dataset and one backbone family.
中文翻译
检索增强生成通常使用独立模型分别完成重排序和回答任务，导致模型驻留和段落处理的重复。我们提出Glance，该方法为生成器配备轻量级决策适配器和指针头，从共享预填充中评分候选段落。短相关性探测从缓存上下文中分支，无需单独预填充每个查询-段落对，即可为每条段落生成一个得分。单一驻留的9B骨干模型支持生成、通用决策和重排序，每项决策技能仅增加约0.1 GiB内存。经过39分钟的MS MARCO微调后，Glance在SciFact上的nDCG@10达到0.741，在NFCorpus上达到0.345，分别优于bge-reranker-v2-m3的0.721和0.326。在300个HotpotQA问题上，其选择的上下文使答案F1相比交叉编码器选择的上下文提升了0.045（配对bootstrap 95%置信区间[0.014, 0.076]）。在扩展性研究中，重排序质量持续提升至最多200个候选（43k tokens）从一次共享预填充中评分，其nDCG@10超过交叉编码器0.058，且仅激活探测器的适配器允许生成从原始段落缓存继续。初步结果展示了基于生成器的重排序方法，仅需一个驻留骨干模型；当前评估覆盖两个检索数据集、一个问答数据集和一个骨干模型族。

Don't Lose the Thread: Empowering Long-Horizon LLM Agents with Cognitive Resource Self-Allocation
不要丢失主线：通过认知资源自分配赋能长时程LLM智能体
Agents powered by large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress in solving complex reasoning tasks. However, LLM agents often falter on long-horizon tasks due to cognitive overload, as their working memory becomes cluttered with expanding and irrelevant information, which dilutes their attention and hinders effective planning and reasoning. To mitigate this challenge, we introduce Cognitive Resource Self-Allocation (CORAL), a novel reasoning paradigm that empowers agents to proactively optimize their context. Implemented as an agent-callable working memory management toolset, CORAL allows an agent to checkpoint its task progress and verified facts, and adaptively initiate a new problem-solving episode by resetting cluttered context while preserving only the checkpointed information, enabling it to resume reasoning from a clean state. We further enhance the agent's checkpointing capabilities using a Multi-episode Agentic Reinforced Policy Optimization algorithm. On the GAIA benchmark, CORAL with an 8B model achieves 42.8% average accuracy, with particularly strong gains on the most challenging long-horizon tasks (Level 2 and Level 3). Crucially, these gains come at a substantially lower inference cost: CORAL consumes 6-7$\times$ fewer input tokens than a ReAct agent. It also generalizes to multi-hop Knowledge QA benchmarks and to the considerably harder BrowseComp, where it improves a strong proprietary agent by 8.0 points.

Latent Path Exploration via Potential-Guided Subtrajectory Balance
通过势能引导的子轨迹平衡进行潜在路径探索
Continuous latent reasoning enables language models to perform intermediate computation without generating explicit reasoning traces. Advances in stochastic latent reasoning allow multiple paths to be sampled in continuous space, but stochasticity alone does not ensure effective exploration. A key challenge is guiding stochastic transitions between intermediate latent states toward useful reasoning paths. We introduce SubLAT, which couples a relative answer potential with Subtrajectory Balance to learn stochastic latent exploration. The relative potential evaluates the answer support of sampled states against replacement states, with a correctness signal at the terminal state. Subtrajectory Balance relates potential differences to forward and backward transition densities across subtrajectories of different lengths. The model is jointly trained with answer supervision, CoT supervision, and representation distillation. Experiments on arithmetic and deductive reasoning show increasing solution coverage with larger sampling budgets across in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, as well as improvements on several structural OOD benchmarks. Trajectory analyses reveal progressive divergence among sampled latent paths and higher mean relative potentials for correct trajectories across arithmetic benchmarks.

连续潜在推理使语言模型能够在不生成显式推理轨迹的情况下执行中间计算。尽管随机潜在推理的进步允许在连续空间中采样多条路径，但仅靠随机性并不能保证有效探索。一个关键挑战是引导中间潜在状态之间的随机转换朝向有用的推理路径。我们提出了SubLAT，它将相对答案势能与子轨迹平衡相结合，以学习随机潜在探索。相对势能评估采样状态相对于替换状态的答案支持程度，并在终止状态提供正确性信号。子轨迹平衡将势能差异与不同长度子轨迹上的前向和后向转移密度相关联。该模型通过答案监督、思维链监督和表示蒸馏进行联合训练。在算术和演绎推理任务上的实验表明，随着采样预算增加，在域内和域外基准上解决方案覆盖率持续提升，并在多个结构化域外基准上取得改进。轨迹分析显示，采样的潜在路径逐步发散，且在算术基准中正确路径的平均相对势能更高。

ScienceJudge: Pattern Perturbation Augmentation for Sentence-Level Scientific Claim Verification
ScienceJudge：用于句子级科学主张验证的模式扰动增强方法
Sentence-level scientific claim verification determines whether each sentence in an AI-generated scientific paragraph is supported by a given multimodal evidence bundle. Fine-grained reporting errors often preserve fluent and plausible statements while introducing subtle evidence-claim deviations, making them difficult to detect under sparse and imbalanced supervision. We propose Pattern Perturbation Augmentation (PPA), which models reporting errors as label-specific faithfulness breaking transformations and induces reusable error patterns from labeled examples. PPA applies these patterns to supported claims to construct controlled hard negatives and expands their coverage through pattern-level, operation-level, and cross-domain augmentation. Based on these augmented views, we develop ScienceJudge, which integrates PPA-trained vision-language predictors and instruction-guided LLM predictors through class-weighted ensemble inference. On the local development set, ScienceJudge improves the average score from 0.8124 to 0.8692, achieving a Macro-F1 of 0.8466 and a Paragraph Exact Match score of 0.8919. Our submitted system ranks first on the official NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 1 leaderboard with a final score of 0.6890.

Mobilizing the Archive: Bitext Extraction at Scale from Archival Documents for Extremely Low-resource Languages
激活档案馆：从档案文献中大规模提取双语语料以支持极低资源语言
While mining parallel sentences from web pages has proven effective for low-resource machine translation, the majority of the world's languages have little to no web presence. What they do have, though, are resources that are the result of centuries of efforts by linguists, such as grammars, dictionaries, and other documentation materials. We propose extracting translation examples from such linguistic resources, which offer parallel data for languages under-represented online and could potentially scale to thousands more languages. We develop a pipeline to achieve this, leveraging an Optical Character Recognition (OCR) model as well as a vision language model (VLM), to ensure identified content to be aligned with visual layout information. We demonstrate the utility of the extracted bitexts for bootstrapping translation systems across 20 languages, both by training machine translation models and by applying in-context learning for large language models. For in-context learning, we observe improvements of +15.78 and +6.94 chrF2++ over zero-shot translation for into low- and high-resource languages, respectively.

AI Cosmos: An Agentic Infra for Situated Learning
AI宇宙：面向情境学习的智能体基础设施
Large language models (LLMs) can improve task performance during learning, yet these gains do not necessarily reflect learners' ability to thereafter apply the knowledge and skills independently. We present AI Cosmos, an agentic infrastructure for situated learning that organizes learning around two complementary principles: scenario-based participation and cumulative assets of practice. AI Cosmos organizes learners and role-specialized agents within learning scenarios that integrate task-relevant knowledge and, when needed, executable workbenches. An asset-memory loop preserves the processes and outcomes of practice as reusable assets with explicit provenance and dependencies. We evaluate scenario-based interaction through two preregistered randomized controlled studies examining the acquisition of legal reasoning and programming competence, holding the same underlying LLM across conditions. AI Cosmos achieves greater learning gains than a conventional LLM in legal reasoning and higher mastery of unseen programming problems than an LLM-as-Teacher baseline.
