How Specific Do I Need to Be? Effects of User Query Underspecification on Deep Research Agents
我需要多具体？用户查询的不明确性对深度研究智能体的影响
Deep research agents (DRAs) conduct long-horizon reasoning, search, and literature synthesis to provide long-form answers to complex, domain-specific questions. These agents are typically trained and evaluated on fully specified user queries. However, real-world user queries can often be underspecified. We introduce UNDERSPEC-DR, a framework for systematically evaluating how underspecified queries affect agents' final answer quality and intermediate search behavior. Across three DRAs we evaluated, underspecification substantially reduces how well DRA's answers fulfill users' information needs, even though they remain grounded in cited evidence. Our analysis of intermediate search behavior suggests a possible explanation: DRAs conduct broader searches that draw less on the original user query but rely more on their learned prior during training, which can be misaligned with users' intent. Our findings reveal the vulnerability of current DRAs to underspecified user queries and inform future efforts to build DRAs that proactively recognize when additional user input is needed to reliably complete deep research tasks.

What Makes Medical Examination Items Difficult? Using Large Language Models to Explore Patterns Associated With Empirical Item Difficulty
医学考试题目难度的成因：利用大语言模型探索与实证难度相关的模式
Research on medical item difficulty has largely focused on prediction rather than on identifying interpretable patterns associated with empirical difficulty. This study used empirical difficulty labels to guide large language models (LLMs) in inducing difficulty rubrics and examined how rubric guidance affected LLM difficulty judgments. Using training items and their empirical difficulty estimates from the United States Medical Licensing Examination (USMLE) and the Physician Assistant National Certifying Examination (PANCE), Claude Opus 5 induced separate rubrics for absolute and pairwise difficulty judgment. None of the induced rubrics treated surface features, such as stem length, option count, or images, as major sources of difficulty. Several patterns emerged across both examinations, including cue convergence, misleading cues, and option competition, although their specific forms differed. Retrieval of knowledge that could not be derived from the stem was particularly prominent in the PANCE rubrics, whereas the USMLE rubrics placed greater emphasis on integrating multiple clinical cues. We compared commercial and open-weight LLMs on held-out items under unguided and rubric-guided conditions. Rubric guidance changed how models used the difficulty scale but did not consistently improve performance. In absolute judgments, guidance encouraged broader use of the scale and generally improved coarse-grained agreement, without consistently improving rank ordering. In pairwise comparisons, its effects varied across models and examinations, and judgments became more sensitive to presentation order. These findings suggest that the value of label-guided rubric induction may lie more in generating interpretable, testable hypotheses from empirical difficulty data than in reliably improving difficulty judgments.

关于医学题目难度的研究主要集中在预测而非识别与实证难度相关且可解释的模式。本研究使用实证难度标签引导大语言模型（LLMs）推导难度标准，并考察标准引导对LLM难度判断的影响。基于美国医师执照考试（USMLE）和助理医师国家认证考试（PANCE）的训练题及其实证难度估计，Claude Opus 5 分别为绝对难度和成对难度判断生成了独立的标准。所生成的标准均未将表面特征（如题干长度、选项数量或图像）视为主要难度来源。在两个考试中均出现若干共性模式，包括线索汇聚、误导性线索和选项竞争，尽管具体形式有所不同。在PANCE标准中，无法从题干推导的知识检索尤为突出；而在USMLE标准中，则更强调整合多个临床线索。我们在保留数据集上的题目上比较了商用与开源权重LLM在无引导和标准引导条件下的表现。标准引导改变了模型使用难度量表的方式，但并未一致提升性能。在绝对判断中，引导促使模型更广泛地使用量表，总体上提升了粗粒度一致性，但未稳定改善排序；在成对比较中，效果因模型和考试而异，且判断对呈现顺序更加敏感。这些发现表明，标签引导的标准化推导价值更多在于从实证难度数据中生成可解释、可检验的假设，而非可靠提升难度判断能力。

Cord: Confidence-Anchored Order Residual Decoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models
Cord：基于置信度锚定的顺序残差解码用于掩码扩散语言模型
The decoding order of masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) can substantially affect generation quality, yet common decoders typically apply the same reveal rule throughout generation. Confidence provides a strong default, but it measures how decisively a position can be predicted now, not how useful revealing that position will be for subsequent decoding. We propose Cord, a lightweight prompt-conditioned policy that retains confidence decoding as a stable reference and learns when and how to depart from it through geometric residuals over generation direction, proximity to revealed context, and two-sided infilling. The residuals adapt to both the prompt and the evolving partial response. Rather than sampling a new policy action at every decoding step, Cord samples a single low-dimensional coefficient matrix per rollout, which induces an entire state-dependent reveal trajectory. This yields a contextual-bandit formulation trained directly from sequence-level rewards, without step-level credit assignment or backbone updates. Across three dLLMs and five reasoning, code, and constraint-satisfaction benchmarks, Cord improves average task success from 32.0% under confidence decoding to 48.2%, and further improves over the best per-domain fixed residual. Behavioral audits show that the learned residuals induce substantial, task-dependent changes in reveal locality and two-sided infilling. Cord adds only $0.53$M trainable parameters, requires no additional backbone forward passes, and incurs only 5.39% inference latency overhead.
中文翻译
掩码扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的解码顺序会显著影响生成质量，但常见解码器通常在整个生成过程中应用相同的揭示规则。置信度提供了一个强有力的默认策略，但它衡量的是当前位置当前被预测的确定程度，而非揭示该位置对未来解码的有用性。我们提出Cord，一种轻量级提示条件化策略，保留置信度解码作为稳定参考，并通过生成方向上的几何残差、与已揭示上下文的距离以及双向填充来学习何时以及如何偏离它。残差部分适应提示和不断演化的部分响应。与每步采样新策略动作不同，Cord在每次推理中仅采样一个低维系数矩阵，从而诱导出整个状态依赖的揭示轨迹。这形成了一种基于序列级奖励直接训练的上下文老虎机框架，无需步骤级信用分配或主干网络更新。在三个dLLMs和五个推理、代码及约束满足基准测试中，Cord将任务成功率从置信度解码下的32.0%提升至48.2%，并进一步优于最佳领域固定残差。行为审计显示，学习到的残差引发了显著的、任务相关的揭示局部性和双向填充变化。Cord仅增加0.53M可训练参数，无需额外主干前向传播，推理延迟开销仅为5.39%。

Unlimited OCR Works 2
无限OCR 2：突破文档解析的长度极限
Unlimited OCR ushers the era of long-horizon parsing, substantially mitigating the growth of working memory as document lengthens. However, as document length scales to hundreds of pages, the visual context grows substantially, sharply increasing the computational cost of each decoding step and limiting the scalability of ultra-long document parsing. Inspired by human book transcription via localized focus without re-scanning preceding pages, we introduce Unlimited OCR 2, a document parsing model that looks only at what it transcribes. Building on Unlimited OCR, we upgrade all the decoder attention modules to Sparse Reference Sliding Window Attention (SR-SWA), bounding active reference tokens to a fixed capacity and thus sustaining constant computational costs across arbitrarily long documents. By combining SR-SWA with Unlimited-Horizon Decoding Paradigm, Unlimited OCR 2 enables document parsing over virtually unlimited lengths without prohibitive memory overhead. At the hundred-page scale, Unlimited OCR 2 achieves 7.0$\times$ and 5.6$\times$ per-step speedups over Full Attention and R-SWA, respectively. Furthermore, it delivers state-of-the-art end-to-end performance on both MPDocBench and MarathonDocBench, our newly proposed benchmark targeting hundred-page document parsing. Ultimately, the capacity for uninterrupted, one-shot document parsing signifies a fundamental paradigm shift into the OCR 3.0 era.
中文翻译
无限OCR开启了长周期解析的新时代，显著缓解了文档长度增长带来的工作内存膨胀问题。然而，当文档长度扩展至数百页时，视觉上下文急剧扩大，导致每一步解码的计算成本大幅上升，限制了超长文档解析的可扩展性。受人类通过局部聚焦进行书籍转录而不重复扫描前页的启发，我们提出无限OCR 2，一种仅关注当前正在转录内容的文档解析模型。在无限OCR基础上，我们将所有解码器注意力模块升级为稀疏参考滑动窗口注意力（SR-SWA），将活跃参考标记数限制在固定容量内，从而在任意长文档上维持恒定的计算成本。结合无限视野解码范式，无限OCR 2实现了近乎无限制长度的文档解析，且无过度内存开销。在百页规模下，无限OCR 2相较于全注意力和R-SWA分别实现7.0倍和5.6倍的每步加速。此外，它在我们新提出的针对百页文档解析的MPDocBench和MarathonDocBench基准上达到了业界领先的整体性能。最终，实现不间断的一次性文档解析能力标志着迈向OCR 3.0时代的根本性范式转变。

Author-Informed Stance Detection: A Dataset and Findings
作者知情立场检测：一个数据集与研究发现
Stance detection in social media is challenging, as interpreting a text often requires understanding the author's perspective. We re-frame political stance detection as an author-informed text understanding task, where it is desired to infer both the author's political leaning and their stance towards the target from the text. We first show that human annotators struggle to infer authors' political leanings and stances from individual tweets, and that pretrained large language models (LLMs) perform below human level in this setting. We then demonstrate that author context improves stance detection for both humans and LLMs, with particularly pronounced benefits for tweets perceived as sarcastic. Through extensive experiments, we further show that LLMs can be effectively fine-tuned to predict authors' political affiliations, and that author-informed fine-tuning yields better stance detection performance than standard fine-tuning. This approach not only surpasses human performance but also improves generalization across datasets. To support further research on author-informed political stance detection, we release a large dataset of stance-labeled tweets enriched with author-context information.

Implicit Reasoning in Transformers: Bridging the Compositionality Gap in GPT-2-like Models
Transformer中的隐式推理：弥合GPT-2类模型的组合性鸿沟
Compositional generalization remains a persistent limitation of transformer language models: even when a model learns to combine known facts correctly in-distribution (ID), the same compositional mechanism may fail when applied to out-of-distribution (OOD) knowledge. We investigate this compositionality gap in a controlled two-hop reasoning setting using a GPT-2-like transformer model trained from scratch. Building on prior work identifying a circuit associated with compositional generalization, we test whether the failure of OOD transfer is related to how atomic knowledge is structurally exposed during training. We modify the training distribution by presenting both ID and OOD atomic facts in a format positionally aligned with the second-hop retrieval step of the learned compositional circuit, without modifying the model architecture or adding OOD compositional supervision. Under this intervention, the model successfully transfers the learned ID compositional behaviour to held-out OOD atomic facts, closing the observed compositionality gap in the controlled setting. Our results provide evidence that the observed compositionality gap is not a fundamental architecture limitation and that it can be overcome through mechanistically informed training distribution design.

Blind Spots: Where Small Language Models Fall Behind Frontier Models on Terminal Tasks
盲点：小型语言模型在终端任务中为何落后于前沿模型
Small language models (SLMs) offer an attractive alternative to frontier models (FMs) because they can be deployed with lower cost and latency. This makes SLMs especially appealing for terminal tasks on edge devices, where responsiveness, local execution, and limited connectivity are important considerations. However, they continue to lag behind FMs on terminal tasks, as measured by standard task success rates. Yet these rates conflate several capabilities that may contribute to this gap. In this work, we characterize the SLM-FM gap along three axes: solvability, whether additional test-time compute allows an SLM to reach a correct solution; verification, whether it can identify a successful solution among candidate trajectories; and confidence, whether it can accurately anticipate its probability of success. Across two standard terminal benchmarks, we find that repeated sampling narrows, but does not eliminate, the gap in solvability. In contrast, a persistent gap remains in verification. Given the same candidate trajectory pool, FMs recover substantially more of the gap between random and oracle selection than SLMs. SLMs are also more overconfident in their prospective success estimates and exhibit poorer calibration. These results suggest that the SLM-FM gap is multidimensional, spanning not only the ability to generate successful solutions, but also the ability to recognize successful solutions and anticipate success. We hope this decomposition can guide future work on developing stronger and smaller terminal agents.

Interventional Training Reduces Causal Bypass in In-Context Introspection
干预训练减少上下文内自省中的因果绕行
Introspection, the ability of a system to access and reason about its internal signals, is crucial for language model (LM) self-consistency and scalable interpretability. Prior work argues that LMs possess introspective capabilities, and that training (SFT) could enhance such behavior. However, most setups leave open the possibility of "causal bypass," in which the self-report relies on signals that are correlated with the self-report's subject, while being causally unrelated to it. Although high-level concepts are difficult to intervene on, lower-level signals (such as a single neuron activation) allow precise interventions. Understanding such signals can serve as a minimal prerequisite before understanding more complex introspection. In this study, we train hundreds of introspectors across three low-level signal types and eight LMs. We find that straightforward SFT training leads to causal bypass, and propose interventional training, an alternative where we train the model to report on counterfactually perturbed values. This method enables more accurate introspection of endogenous $\textit{and}$ injected signals, particularly in out-of-distribution (OOD) data. The mechanism generalizes across token positions and scales with more data. Our results demonstrate that while causally grounded introspection is possible, it is not guaranteed -- emphasizing the need to train on mechanisms, rather than behaviors alone.

HEDGE: Gated Hypergraph Memory for Scalable Multi-Agent Debate
HEDGE：用于可扩展多智能体辩论的门控超图记忆
Multi-agent debate systems require agents to retain and revisit arguments across multiple rounds, yet limited context windows and unstructured memories make relevant information increasingly difficult to recover. Existing flat and pairwise-graph memories also struggle to capture higher-order relations, including multi-agent agreement, conflicting evidence, and evolving consensus. We introduce HEDGE, a selective memory framework that represents debate trajectories as semantic hypergraphs. Hyperedges connect related claims across agents, while intra-hyperedge relational graphs preserve fine-grained argumentative structure. A learned memory selection module (MSM), trained through supervised learning and reinforcement learning, routes each memory candidate to STORE, BACKUP, or OMIT, retaining useful information while limiting redundancy. Across seven diverse benchmarks, HEDGE consistently outperforms strong single-agent, multi-agent, and memory baselines, improving accuracy by 5.0 points on average over the strongest multi-agent system with GPT-4o-mini. The gains reach 8.2 points on conflict-heavy tasks, while HEDGE reduces memory usage by 48% and the number of debate rounds by 34%.

The Quine Test: How Much of a Model's Self Survives Its Own Description?
奎因测试：模型的自我在自身描述中还能留存多少？
Frontier language models express coherent preferences, but behavioral evidence alone cannot say whether these belong to the model or to a character it portrays. Following Hofstadter's view of the self as a self-description, we ask whether a model's description of itself works as a portable specification of its preference profile, a quine test: can the text a model writes about itself regenerate its behavior elsewhere? Ten models from nine labs write self-descriptions; we place each one in the prompt of other models (receivers) and of fresh instances of the author, and measure how closely their choices on a 110-item preference battery match the author's. First, the tested native self-descriptions (native: the profile measured under a generic assistant prompt) show no average reconstruction gain across variations in length, framing, wording and prompt placement: on a 0 to 1 fidelity scale where a model's fidelity to a fresh instance of itself is 0.979 and to unrelated models about 0.89, the one-sided 95% upper bound on the pooled gain is below +0.01 in every tested system-prompt condition, and descriptions based on the author's observed choices do no better. Second, the channel itself is strong: explicitly listing the author's answers lets receivers reproduce its profile at fidelity 0.955 to 0.960 across three model grids, within 0.03 of same-model repeatability. Third, self-descriptions written under an explicit preference policy transfer the assigned preferences almost as well as the policy text itself. Fourth, reading its own self-description shifts even the author away from its baseline, modestly more than unrelated prose of the same length. For the preferences measured here, native self-descriptions influence behavior without reliably specifying how to reproduce it in another model. We release the pipeline, battery, raw call logs, and 198 model-written descriptions.
中文翻译
前沿语言模型表现出连贯的偏好，但仅凭行为证据无法判断这些偏好属于模型本身还是其扮演的角色。根据霍夫施塔特关于自我即自我描述的观点，我们提出一个“奎因测试”：模型对自己所写的描述能否作为其偏好谱的便携规范，即该文本是否能在其他地方再生其行为？来自九家实验室的十种模型撰写自我描述；我们将每份描述置于其他模型（接收者）和作者自身的全新实例中，测量其在110项偏好电池上的选择与作者的一致性。首先，测试的原生自我描述（native：在通用助手提示下测量的谱）在长度、表述方式、措辞和提示位置变化下均未显示出平均重建增益：在0到1的保真度尺度上，模型对自身新实例的保真度为0.979，对无关模型约为0.89，所有测试系统-提示条件下合并增益的单侧95%上限低于+0.01，基于观察行为的描述也无优势。其次，通道本身很强：明确列出作者答案可使接收者在三个模型网格上以0.955至0.960的保真度重现其谱，与同模型重复性相差不超过0.03。第三，基于显式偏好策略书写的自我描述几乎与策略文本本身一样有效地传递指定偏好。第四，阅读自身自我描述甚至会使作者自身偏离基线，程度略高于同等长度的无关散文。对于此处测量的偏好，原生自我描述会影响行为，但无法可靠指定如何在另一模型中重现它。我们发布了该管道、电池、原始调用日志以及198份模型撰写的描述。

Do Audio LLMs HEAR Pain? Tracing Acoustic Evidence from Speech to Language Reasoning
音频大模型真的能‘听’到疼痛吗？从语音到语言推理的声学证据追踪
Large audio language models (LALMs) can process speech directly, but it remains unclear how well they use the non-lexical acoustic information that speech carries. We study this question through pain, using TAME-Pain, which contains pain-neutral Harvard sentences and spoken pain ratings recorded at different self-reported pain levels. We introduce HEAR, a four-stage diagnostic framework that traces pain-related information from the speech signal to the model's prediction, and apply it to seven closed-source and open-weight LALMs. On the Harvard sentences, where pain can only be inferred from the voice, all models perform at chance level, and same-speaker references or few-shot examples improve balanced accuracy by no more than six points. On the spoken ratings, the same models reach 87.5-99.7% balanced accuracy. Layer-wise probing of open-weight LALMs shows that pain can be decoded from early audio representations (74.1% balanced accuracy for binary prediction and 45.0% for three-class severity), but that this information, along with most pain-associated acoustic cues, decreases in deeper layers. In an acoustic perception test, weaker models cannot distinguish even large changes in these cues, whereas stronger models can but still fail to infer pain. Finally, we recover pain-related acoustic cues and directly reinject them as structured text into the prompt, which improves pairwise pain prediction by up to 32 points. These results indicate that current LALMs encode paralinguistic information in early layers that is largely unavailable when the model produces its answer.
中文翻译
大音频语言模型（LALMs）可以直接处理语音，但它们如何利用语音携带的非词汇声学信息仍不明确。我们通过疼痛研究这一问题，使用TAME-Pain数据集，该数据集包含无痛感的哈佛句子和在不同自我报告疼痛水平下记录的口语疼痛评分。我们引入HEAR框架，这是一个四阶段诊断流程，用于追踪从语音信号到模型预测的疼痛相关信息，并将其应用于七个闭源和开源权重的LALMs。在仅能通过声音推断疼痛的哈佛句子上，所有模型表现均处于随机水平，相同说话人参考或少量示例仅使平衡准确率提升不超过六个百分点。而在口语评分上，相同模型达到87.5%-99.7%的平衡准确率。对开源LALMs的逐层探测显示，疼痛信息可从早期音频表示中解码（二分类预测平衡准确率为74.1%，三分类严重程度为45.0%），但此类信息及大多数与疼痛相关的声学线索在深层逐渐减弱。在声学感知测试中，较弱模型甚至无法区分这些线索的显著变化，而较强模型虽可区分但仍无法推断疼痛。最后，我们恢复了与疼痛相关的声学线索，并将其作为结构化文本直接注入提示，使成对疼痛预测准确率最高提升32个百分点。结果表明，当前LALMs在早期层编码了副语言信息，但在模型输出答案时这些信息大多不可用。

Implicit Minor Detection from Multi-Turn Dialogue in High-Risk AI Interactions: A Constrained Self-Evolving LLM Harness
高风险AI交互中多轮对话里的隐含未成年人检测：一种受约束的自演化大语言模型Harness
In high-risk text interactions, a system may need to estimate whether the current speaker is likely to be a minor before applying age-conditioned safeguards. Many text-based dialogue systems lack strong identity signals such as account information, verified identity, facial data, or device data, and must rely on weak cues that emerge across multiple turns. We formulate implicit minor detection under weak identity information as a high-risk user-attribute inference task and propose a constrained framework comprising a large language model, a Harness, and a Maker-Checker protocol. The framework controls when inference is invoked, requires structured dialogue-linked evidence, and subjects validation-selected Harness revisions to version-level review. On a controlled Chinese benchmark comprising 8,328 multi-turn dialogues, our method improves average precision and minor-class F1 by 7.60 and 6.37 percentage points, respectively, over direct inference using the same LLM family.

What Hallucinations Have We Measured? Semantic Illusion, Confabulation, and Expressed Uncertainty
我们测量过的幻觉有哪些？语义错觉、虚构与表达的不确定性
Hallucination remains one of the most critical challenges in deploying large language models (LLMs) reliably and safely. Yet the term is used inconsistently across the research community. We argue for a strict definition of hallucinations in LLMs. First, hallucinations should be confident incorrectness, kept separate from expressed uncertainty (UNK)1. UNK reveals that the model may be wrong, whereas a hallucination does not reveal it and is therefore more dangerous in practice. Conflating the two obscures their distinct behaviors and makes them hard to study. Second, hallucinations should be split by a simple re-elicitation test: does any semantically equivalent prompt recover the correct response? This test probes whether the relevant knowledge2 is accessible. If none does, the knowledge is likely absent or inaccessible, and the error type is a confabulation (CF) that likely requires external knowledge or retraining; if some does, the knowledge is accessible from the model but applied inconsistently, and the error type is a semantic illusion (SI) that simple prompt or decoding changes can mitigate3. Re-evaluating 14 recent hallucination methods under this taxonomy yields three findings. (1) Detection methods flag UNK more often than hallucinated responses (i.e., SI and CF). (2) Mitigation methods are far more effective for SI than for CF. (3) Latent signals are more informative than output-level signals for CF. An audit of recent hallucination papers shows that most conflate these categories. We recommend reporting CF, SI, and UNK separately, and introduce a practical labeling protocol that needs only a modest number of semantically equivalent prompts.
中文翻译
幻觉仍是部署大语言模型（LLMs）时可靠性和安全性面临的关键挑战之一。然而，研究界对“幻觉”一词的使用并不一致。我们主张对LLMs中的幻觉采用严格定义：首先，幻觉应为自信的错误，需与表达的不确定性（UNK）明确区分。UNK表明模型可能出错，而幻觉则不揭示这一点，因此在实践中更具危险性。混淆二者会掩盖其不同行为，使其难以研究。其次，幻觉应通过简单的重激发测试进行划分：是否存在任何语义等价的提示能恢复正确响应？该测试检验相关知识是否可访问。若无任何提示可恢复，则知识可能缺失或不可访问，错误类型为虚构（CF），通常需要外部知识或重新训练；若有部分提示可恢复，则知识存在于模型中但应用不一致，错误类型为语义错觉（SI），可通过简单提示或解码调整缓解。对14项近期幻觉方法按此分类重新评估后得出三项发现：(1) 检测方法更常标记UNK而非幻觉响应（即SI和CF）；(2) 缓解方法对SI比对CF更有效；(3) 隐层信号比输出层信号对CF更具信息量。对近期幻觉论文的审计显示，多数研究混淆了这些类别。我们建议分别报告CF、SI和UNK，并引入一种实用标注协议，仅需少量语义等价提示即可完成。

CoSTA: Co-Evolving Self-Play Timeline Agents for Complex Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering
CoSTA：用于复杂时间知识图谱问答的协同演化自play时间线智能体
Complex Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering (TKGQA) involves more than retrieving facts: the timestamp or validity interval of one fact can constrain the search for another, while multiple intervals may jointly determine the answer. Existing single-agent TKGQA methods typically investigate these facts within a single reasoning context, where evidence from different event histories can become interleaved and distractors can misdirect subsequent retrieval. Distributing investigations across agents can separate event-specific evidence into distinct contexts, but effective collaboration must still be learned because question decomposition, subtask investigation, and evidence synthesis are interdependent. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{Co}-Evolving \textbf{S}elf-Play \textbf{T}imeline \textbf{A}gents (CoSTA), which organizes facts into navigable timelines, delegates investigations to workers, and uses a coordinator to synthesize their original timestamped observations. CoSTA initializes a shared policy through role-conditioned distillation and jointly optimizes both roles through co-evolving self-play with final-answer rewards. Across three benchmarks, CoSTA achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with particularly strong gains on complex multi-fact questions, while its compact variants outperform the evaluated closed-source LLM agents.

复杂时间知识图谱问答（TKGQA）不仅涉及事实检索：一个事实的时间戳或有效区间可能约束对另一个事实的搜索，而多个区间可能共同决定答案。现有的单智能体TKGQA方法通常在一个推理上下文中研究这些事实，导致来自不同事件历史的证据混杂，干扰物可能误导后续检索。将调查任务分配给多个智能体可以将特定事件的证据分隔到不同上下文中，但有效的协作仍需学习，因为问题分解、子任务调查和证据整合是相互依赖的。为解决这些局限性，我们提出 extbf{Co}-Evolving extbf{S}elf-Play extbf{T}imeline extbf{A}gents（CoSTA），它将事实组织成可导航的时间线，将调查任务委派给工作者，并由协调者合成其原始的时间戳观测结果。CoSTA通过角色条件蒸馏初始化共享策略，并通过最终答案奖励进行协同演化自play联合优化。在三个基准上的实验表明，CoSTA实现了最先进的整体性能，尤其在复杂多事实问题上表现突出，其紧凑变体也优于所评估的闭源LLM智能体。

RoleFUZZER: User-Behaviour Fuzzing for Role-Specification Adherence in Large Language Models
RoleFUZZER：用于大语言模型角色规范遵守性的用户行为模糊测试
Large language models (LLMs) are often conditioned into specific roles such as tutors, interviewers, or agents acting for an organisation, through system prompts that specify how they should behave. The extent to which they can actually adhere to these role specifications under multi-turn interactions with realistic and potentially problematic users is understudied. To address this, we propose user behaviour fuzzing, a guided search over plausible user behaviours that can lead a model to violate its role specification, and introduce RoleFUZZER, a framework that adapts coverage-guided fuzzing from software testing to operationalise the search. RoleFUZZER constructs scenarios guided by a user-behaviour coverage grid that crosses a grounded taxonomy of user interaction types and the role specification of the model under test i.e. what is under pressure. With RoleFUZZER, we evaluate LLMs across five role specifications, including tutor, financial advisor, AI interviewer, antagonistic roleplay character, and web agent under organisational policies. Across simulated and validated conversations in 15 campaigns, the framework reaches >= 89% coverage of the 64-cell behaviour grid for every model and role tested, and surfaces specification-grounded failure patterns. At an equal budget of conversations, removing the RoleFUZZER grid and asking an LLM to generate specification targeting users free-form lowers coverage to 39-69% with fewer kinds of violations. The same specification can also fail in opposite directions. For e.g., on the antagonist, open-weight target models override the player's safety signals, while Claude Haiku 4.5 breaks character instead. RoleFUZZER offers a tractable way to uncover vulnerabilities in LLMs across role specifications by testing diverse user behaviours that are likely to violate them.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）通常通过系统提示被设定为特定角色，如导师、面试官或代表组织行动的代理。然而，它们在面对真实且可能具有挑战性的用户进行多轮交互时，能否真正遵守这些角色规范，目前研究尚不充分。为解决此问题，我们提出用户行为模糊测试，即在合理用户行为范围内进行引导式搜索，以发现可能导致模型违反角色规范的行为，并引入RoleFUZZER框架，将软件测试中的覆盖率引导模糊测试方法应用于实际操作。RoleFUZZER构建场景时依据一个用户行为覆盖网格，该网格结合了用户交互类型的实证分类体系与待测模型的角色规范（即哪些方面处于压力之下）。通过RoleFUZZER，我们在五个角色规范下评估了LLMs，包括导师、财务顾问、AI面试官、对抗性角色扮演角色及遵循组织政策的网络代理。在15个模拟与验证对话活动中，该框架对每个模型和角色均达到≥89%的64单元行为网格覆盖率，并揭示了基于规范的失败模式。在同等对话预算下，若移除RoleFUZZER网格并让LLM自由生成针对规范的用户，覆盖率降至39%-69%，且违规类型更少。同一规范也可能以相反方向失效：例如，在对抗性角色中，开源目标模型会忽略玩家的安全信号，而Claude Haiku 4.5则反而脱离角色。RoleFUZZER提供了一种可行的方法，通过测试可能违反规范的多样化用户行为，揭示LLMs在各类角色规范下的潜在漏洞。

Holistic Diversity Management in LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search
LLM引导的进化搜索中的整体多样性管理
LLM-guided evolutionary search iteratively selects existing programs, uses LLMs as mutation operators to generate a new program, and evaluates it to guide subsequent search. To preserve diversity and reduce the risk of premature convergence, it often adopts the island model, in which groups of programs, called islands, evolve separately. However, this structure alone does not guarantee distinct search directions, as islands may start from similar programs or converge toward similar solutions during evolution. This suggests that diversity must be considered throughout the search process, from initializing islands with distinct programs to developing island trajectories and sharing discoveries across islands. To this end, we propose HoliDivEvolve , a holistic approach to establishing and effectively utilizing island diversity throughout evolutionary search. Specifically, we introduce diverse initialization to establish different initial programs, trajectory adaptive island selection to allocate computation based on current score, recent growth, and the remaining search budget, and growth adaptive migration to share alternative ideas across islands. Experiments across diverse benchmarks show that HoliDivEvolve achieves competitive performance on most evaluated tasks. We further conduct controlled analyses to examine how our design choices affect search behavior and final performance.

LLM引导的进化搜索通过迭代选择现有程序，使用LLM作为变异算子生成新程序，并评估其以指导后续搜索。为保持多样性并降低过早收敛风险，常采用岛屿模型，即多个程序组（称为岛屿）独立演化。然而，仅采用该结构并不能保证不同的搜索方向，因为岛屿可能从相似程序出发，或在演化过程中趋向相似解。这表明多样性必须贯穿整个搜索过程，从初始阶段的差异化程序设置，到岛屿轨迹设计及跨岛屿的知识共享。为此，我们提出HoliDivEvolve，一种在进化搜索全过程中建立并有效利用岛屿多样性的整体方法。具体包括：多样化初始化以建立不同初始程序，基于当前得分、近期增长和剩余搜索预算的轨迹自适应岛屿选择，以及基于增长自适应的迁移机制以跨岛屿共享替代思路。在多种基准上的实验表明，HoliDivEvolve在多数任务上表现优异。我们还进行了受控分析，探究设计选择如何影响搜索行为和最终性能。

LLMs as Judges and Examinees for Medical Item Difficulty Prediction
LLM作为评判者与应试者预测医学试题难度
Predicting medical item difficulty from item text alone remains challenging. Prior work has largely relied on linguistic features or text representations derived from general-purpose and domain-specific language models, but predictive performance has remained modest. This study takes a different approach by using difficulty-related signals that emerge when open-weight large language models (LLMs) interact with test items. Using USMLE and PANCE medical licensure examination items, we examined LLMs in two roles. As judges, the models provided absolute difficulty ratings and pairwise difficulty comparisons. As simulated examinees, they generated response-based signals including correctness, keyed-option probability, response entropy, and the probability margin between the top two options. We then combined the strongest judgment and response signals with baseline item features in downstream prediction models. Pairwise difficulty judgment was the most consistent individual LLM signal across both datasets, while simulated-examinee signals and absolute judgments provided complementary information in some feature combinations. The best configurations reached a Spearman correlation of $ρ$ = .447 on USMLE, exceeding the best system in the 2024 BEA shared task ($ρ$ = .25), and $ρ$ = .494 on PANCE. These results were comparable to direct absolute judgments from the proprietary frontier model Claude Opus 5 ($ρ$ = .443 and .488, respectively). Overall, judgment and response signals from open-weight LLMs captured difficulty-related information beyond text representations. Because these models can be deployed locally, the approach offers a practical option for predicting the difficulty of unreleased or high-security test items.
中文翻译
仅从试题文本预测医学试题难度仍具挑战性。以往研究主要依赖语言特征或通用及领域特定语言模型提取的文本表示，但预测性能有限。本研究采用一种新方法：利用开放权重大语言模型（LLMs）与试题交互时产生的难度相关信号。使用美国医师执照考试（USMLE）和PANCE医学执照考试试题，我们让LLMs扮演两种角色。作为评判者，模型提供绝对难度评分和两两难度比较；作为模拟应试者，生成基于响应的信号，包括正确性、关键选项概率、响应熵，以及前两名选项的概率差。随后将最强的判断与响应信号与基线试题特征结合，构建下游预测模型。两两难度判断在两个数据集上均为最一致的单一LLM信号，而模拟应试者信号与绝对判断在某些特征组合中提供了互补信息。最佳配置在USMLE上达到Spearman相关系数ρ = .447，超过2024年BEA共享任务最佳系统（ρ = .25），在PANCE上达到ρ = .494。这些结果与专有前沿模型Claude Opus 5的直接绝对判断相当（分别为ρ = .443和.488）。总体而言，开放权重LLMs的判断与响应信号捕捉到了超越文本表示的难度相关信息。由于这些模型可本地部署，该方法为预测未发布或高保密性试题难度提供了实用方案。

Pronunciation-Supervised Text-to-Speech for Irish (Gaeilge)
基于发音监督的爱尔兰语（Gaeilge）文本转语音
Speech technology for Irish must be built from very little transcribed speech, yet the language's pronunciation is thoroughly documented in dictionaries and dialect descriptions. We ask whether that documentation can supervise the adaptation of a large pretrained text-to-speech model to a language it was never trained on. We adapt Qwen3-TTS to Irish using 12.8 hours of crowdsourced Common Voice speech, correcting two defects in the vendor fine-tuning script that training loss does not reveal (Appendix A), and reach character error rate (CER) 0.225 on held-out cross-corpus text, against 0.397 for an exhaustively tuned from-scratch system and 0.437 for zero-shot cloning. We then compile the Wiktionary pronunciation record into a lexicon and a phoneme critic, and use that critic as the verifier in one round of rejection fine-tuning: CER 0.159 cross-corpus and 0.067 in-domain, with the voice still anonymous across all 202 training speakers. The same critic explains why the voice cannot yet be steered to a minority dialect: 229 of 12,311 training clips lean Ulster against 8,451 Munster. Pronunciation documentation, converted into a verifier, is a practical lever for low-resource speech synthesis, and a way to measure what the available data cannot support.
中文翻译
爱尔兰语语音技术必须在极少标注语音数据的基础上构建，但该语言的发音在词典和方言描述中有详尽记录。我们探讨这些发音记录能否用于监督大型预训练文本转语音模型适配到从未训练过的语言。我们使用12.8小时众包的Common Voice语音数据，对Qwen3-TTS进行适配，修正了供应商微调脚本中的两个缺陷（训练损失未揭示，附录A），在跨语料库保留文本上达到字符错误率（CER）0.225，优于经过全面调优的从零开始系统（CER 0.397）和零样本克隆（CER 0.437）。随后，我们将Wiktionary发音记录整理为词典和音素批评器，并将其作为一轮拒绝微调中的验证器：跨语料库CER降至0.159，同域内CER达0.067，且所有202名训练说话人声音保持匿名。同一批评器解释了为何语音尚无法导向少数方言：229/12,311条训练片段偏向Ulster，而Munster有8,451条。将发音文档转化为验证器，为低资源语音合成提供了实用杠杆，并揭示了现有数据无法支持的内容。

Fu-TCM: a multimodal large language model that learns traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic reasoning
Fu-TCM：学习中医诊断推理的多模态大语言模型
Large language models (LLMs) are advancing in multimodal understanding and reasoning, accelerating medical model development. Traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) uses evidence from the four examinations to determine disease location and nature, differentiate syndromes and guide treatment. This diagnostic process constitutes a complete chain of reasoning. We present Fu-TCM, a multimodal TCM LLM trained on data retained after cleaning and filtering an initial collection of approximately 7.12 million examples derived from classical texts, textbooks, medical images, clinical cases and publicly available datasets. The model was further optimized using Bianzheng-Grounded Policy Optimization (BGPO), which explicitly targets syndrome differentiation through rewards for response format, intermediate TCM reasoning fields and final syndrome predictions. Fu-TCM-27B led all six benchmarks spanning TCM reasoning, text and vision, achieving a macro-average accuracy of 81.01% and surpassing the strongest reference model with complete results across all six benchmarks by 7.13 percentage points. Its largest margin was 11.90 points on Fu-bianzheng. Case-level analyses further showed a positive association between intermediate-field accuracy and final syndrome-selection accuracy in most evaluated models. These findings suggest that TCM syndrome differentiation may be computationally learnable and that structured reasoning supervision can improve performance across TCM tasks.

Large language models hold promise for delivering safe, scientifically grounded, and personalized exercise guidance at scale, yet the sports-and-health domain still lacks curated training corpora and rigorous evaluation benchmarks. Keepace.ai is a domain-specialized LLM built on Qwen3.5-27B through post-training, currently optimized for Chinese-language exercise guidance across three tasks: workout plan generation, sports-and-health knowledge QA, and personal sports data analysis. Two Birds with One Stone: Skill-Augmented Generation via Unified Retrieval and Utilization
一石二鸟：通过统一检索与利用实现技能增强生成
Skills, as reusable external knowledge, have demonstrated substantial potential to enhance the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks. However, existing skill-augmented generation methods typically rely on separate models for skill retrieval and skill utilization, resulting in disconnected optimization of these two processes. To address this gap, we propose Skill-Uni, a unified reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes skill retrieval and utilization. Specifically, we adopt a generative skill retrieval paradigm that enables a single model to both learn how to retrieve relevant skills and leverage them for task solving. To effectively train this unified framework, we introduce a two-stage training strategy consisting of warm-up instruction tuning and GRPO, allowing the model to progressively improve its skill retrieval and utilization capabilities. Extensive experiments on five skill-related benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Skill-Uni, yielding average performance gains of 66.59% and 34.82% over the base Qwen3-4B and LLaMA3.1-8B models, respectively. Besides, Skill-Uni is compatible with various skill retrieval engines, demonstrating its flexibility and generalizability. Additional in-depth analyses also provide insights into the effectiveness of Skill-Uni.

Scaling Lean Theorem Proving for Real-World Code Verification
面向现实世界代码验证的轻量级定理证明规模化
We present EasyProver, a Lean proving system built from recursive agents with isolated contexts, asynchronous search, parallel tactic probing, and an elastic elaboration service. Using an off-the-shelf frontier model with no additional training, it proves 669/672 PutnamBench problems and all six IMO 2026 formalizations and resolves 597/600 NTP4VC verification conditions and all 189 VERINA tasks by proof or refutation. Because these benchmarks are close to saturation, we also introduce Aegis, which converts competitive programming problems into much harder verification tasks over real implementations. The pipeline writes safe Rust solutions, translates them into Lean with Aeneas, generates specifications, and labels each frozen statement with a kernel-checked proof or refutation. It produces 2,045 differentially validated Rust-Lean program pairs and 11,533 candidate specifications. Unlike hand-written benchmarks, these tasks carry implementation-level obligations from fixed-width arithmetic, explicit failure, generated loops, casts, and functionalized mutation. On a stratified sample of 2,000 statements, EasyProver proves 1,054 (52.7%) within a 30-minute budget per statement. The same tasks can also be used to benchmark other provers and as rewards for reinforcement learning.

ATI-OPD: Adaptive Teacher Intervention for Stable Long-Sequence On-Policy Distillation
ATI-OPD：稳定长序列在线策略蒸馏的自适应教师干预
On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level feedback from a teacher. As responses grow longer, the student's prefix can depart from the teacher's own reasoning, weakening its feedback on later tokens. Updates based on this feedback can reinforce the same deviations in later responses and destabilize training. Prior approaches such as length control and loss reweighting limit learning from problematic responses but leave their prefixes unchanged. Fixed cutoffs can also discard later reasoning. We propose \textbf{Adaptive Teacher Intervention for On-Policy Distillation (ATI-OPD)} to improve subsequent teacher guidance by revising the student's reasoning prefix. ATI-OPD replaces the earliest high-disagreement segment with a short teacher segment, then lets the student resume its own reasoning from the revised text. To handle renewed disagreement, it excludes persistently divergent suffixes from the distillation loss while retaining useful student continuation. Training diagnostics show less teacher-student disagreement, fewer gradient spikes, and fewer extremely long responses. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two teacher-student pairs, ATI-OPD improves average student-only accuracy over standard OPD.
中文翻译
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）利用教师在token级别提供的反馈来训练学生模型。随着响应变长，学生的前缀可能偏离教师的推理路径，从而削弱其对后续token的反馈作用。基于此类反馈的更新可能在后续响应中强化相同偏差，导致训练不稳定。先前方法如长度控制和损失重加权虽能限制从有问题响应中学习，但未改变其前缀；固定截断也可能丢弃后期推理内容。我们提出 extbf{自适应教师干预在线策略蒸馏（ATI-OPD）}，通过修正学生推理前缀来改善后续教师指导。ATI-OPD用简短的教师段落替换最早出现高分歧的部分，然后让学生从修改后的文本继续自主推理。为应对重新出现的分歧，它排除持续分歧的后缀，同时保留学生有用的延续部分。训练诊断显示教师与学生分歧减少、梯度尖峰减少、极端长响应也减少。在五个数学推理基准和两个教师-学生组合上，ATI-OPD相比标准OPD提升了学生独立准确率。
👤 Jiannan Guan, Youxin Jiang, Yutai Hou, Qiguang Chen, Yirong Zeng et al. 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Nemotron 3.5 Super and Lightning: Open, Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Models for Agentic AI
Nemotron 3.5 Super 和 Lightning：面向代理AI的开源高效混合专家Mamba-Transformer模型
We introduce Nemotron 3.5 Super and Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, open and efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer models designed for reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic workloads. Super is a 120B-parameter, 12B-active multimodal model, while Lightning is a smaller 30B-parameter, 3B-active text model; both designed for efficient deployment. Both models combine large-scale distillation, long-context training, and native speculative decoding, while Super further incorporates Indic continued pretraining and agentic software-engineering mid-training. We develop a comprehensive post-training pipeline spanning supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning (RLVR and RLHF), complemented in Super by specialized teacher training and multi- teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). This transforms Super from a text-only base model into a multimodal agent capable of reasoning over text, images, video, and interactive tool environments. We further explore test-time scaling through parallel agent execution and self-verification. Across training and deployment, we jointly optimize capability and efficiency. Indic continued pretraining improves Indian-language benchmarks by up to 9.82 percentage points, while agentic mid-training strengthens software-engineering capabilities and robustness across agent harnesses. The post-trained Super model achieves 51.78% on SWE-bench Pro, surpassing Nemotron 3 Ultra despite using approximately 4.6x fewer active parameters. Post-training MTP boosting improves speculative- decoding acceptance length by up to 21.8% under probabilistic draft sampling, while NVFP4 quantization achieves 99.62% median accuracy recovery relative to BF16. Inference optimizations enable up to 4,856 output tokens/s/GPU on GB200 while sustaining at least 50 tokens/s/user.
中文翻译
我们介绍Nemotron 3.5 Super和Nemotron 3.5 Lightning，两款开源高效的混合专家（MoE）混合Mamba-Transformer模型，专为推理、长上下文理解及代理工作负载设计。Super为120B参数、12B活跃的多模态模型，Lightning为较小的30B参数、3B活跃的文本模型，均旨在高效部署。两者结合大规模蒸馏、长上下文训练和原生推测解码；Super进一步融合印地语持续预训练和代理软件工程中期训练。我们开发了涵盖监督微调和强化学习（RLVR与RLHF）的综合后训练流程，Super还包含专用教师训练和多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）。这使Super从纯文本基础模型转变为可处理文本、图像、视频及交互工具环境的多模态代理。我们还探索了通过并行代理执行和自我验证实现的测试时扩展。在整个训练与部署过程中，联合优化能力与效率。印地语持续预训练使印度语言基准提升最高达9.82个百分点，代理中期训练增强了软件工程能力和代理集成中的鲁棒性。后训练后的Super模型在SWE-bench Pro上达到51.78%，超越Nemotron 3 Ultra，尽管仅使用约4.6倍少的活跃参数。后训练MTP增强在概率草稿采样下使推测解码接受长度提升最高达21.8%，NVFP4量化实现相对于BF16的99.62%中位数精度恢复。推理优化使GB200上每GPU输出高达4,856个token/s，同时维持至少50 token/s/用户速率。
👤 NVIDIA, Aastha Jhunjhunwala, Aayushi Ashokkumar Srivastava, Abhibha Gupta, Abhinav Garg et al. 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

agent-memoryagentic-rl
FAD-KV: Adaptive Dictionary KV Caching for Efficient Reasoning Decoding
FAD-KV：用于高效推理解码的自适应字典KV缓存
Key-value (KV) caching is a core mechanism in large language models, where intermediate key and value representations are stored to accelerate autoregressive decoding. As generation length increases, KV cache memory and computation grow linearly, making compression critical for efficient long-output reasoning. Dictionary-based sparse KV compression is appealing because it represents each token's KV states as a sparse combination of learned basis elements (atoms) in a dictionary, preserving token-level structure without enforcing a global low-rank constraint. However, existing dictionary-based methods face two practical limitations during decoding: iterative sparse coding methods such as Orthogonal Matching Pursuit add decoding-time overhead, and static dictionaries may mismatch evolving KV distributions under test-time generation. While adaptive dictionary updates can reduce this mismatch, naive append-only strategies lead to dictionary growth during generation. We propose FAD-KV, a \textbf{F}ast \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{D}ictionary KV caching framework for efficient reasoning decoding. FAD-KV integrates (i) a screening-based sparse coding scheme, where a one-shot Top-\(n\) selection identifies a small candidate pool followed by lightweight coefficient refinement, and (ii) a fixed-size dictionary adaptation mechanism that reuses inactive atoms instead of expanding the dictionary. By controlling both sparse coding cost and adaptive dictionary growth, FAD-KV provides a memory-bounded and computation-efficient KV compression framework for long reasoning workloads. Experiments on reasoning and long-context benchmarks show that FAD-KV achieves a strong quality-efficiency trade-off while avoiding unbounded dictionary growth during decoding.
中文翻译
键值（KV）缓存是大语言模型中的核心机制，通过存储中间键和值表示来加速自回归解码。随着生成长度增加，KV缓存的内存和计算量呈线性增长，因此压缩对于高效长输出推理至关重要。基于字典的稀疏KV压缩方法具有吸引力，因为它将每个标记的KV状态表示为字典中学习到的基础元素（原子）的稀疏组合，保留了标记级结构且无需施加全局低秩约束。然而，现有的基于字典的方法在解码过程中面临两个实际限制：如正交匹配追踪等迭代稀疏编码方法会增加解码时间开销，而静态字典可能无法匹配测试时生成过程中演变的KV分布。虽然自适应字典更新可减少这种不匹配，但简单的仅追加策略会导致字典在生成过程中不断增长。我们提出了FAD-KV，一种用于高效推理解码的快速自适应字典KV缓存框架。FAD-KV集成了（i）基于筛选的稀疏编码方案，通过一次性的Top-n选择确定小候选池，随后进行轻量级系数优化；以及（ii）固定大小的字典自适应机制，通过重用未活跃的原子而非扩展字典。通过同时控制稀疏编码成本和自适应字典增长，FAD-KV为长推理任务提供了内存受限且计算高效的KV压缩框架。在推理和长上下文基准上的实验表明，FAD-KV在保持高质量的同时实现了良好的效率权衡，并避免了解码过程中的字典无界增长。
👤 Yuxuan Zhu, David H. Yang, Parisa Shiravani, Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri, Tejaswini Pedapati et al. 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37868]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Human-Residual Steering: Aligning Frozen LLM Judges with Human Judgment Distributions
人类残差引导：对齐冻结LLM评判者与人类判断分布
Large language models (LLMs) are widely used as automated judges, yet most alignment methods target aggregated human judgments and overlook variation among individual annotators. Existing approaches to matching the full distribution of human ratings mainly rely on output-level calibration or parameter fine-tuning. We propose Human-Residual Steering (HRS), to our knowledge the first method to align frozen LLM judges with human judgment distributions through learned hidden-state interventions. An oracle analysis shows that low-rank corrections at a single layer remove most of the discrepancy between judge and human distributions. HRS learns to predict such corrections from the judge's hidden representation and rating logits, training on individual annotations with proper scoring rules for ordinal and nominal judgments and optimising through the frozen downstream layers. Across 14 benchmarks and four judges from three model families, HRS outperforms all evaluated frozen-weight baselines. With Qwen2.5-7B, it achieves the lowest Jensen-Shannon distance on 13 of 14 benchmarks, reducing the mean from 0.565 to 0.352 versus 0.377 for the strongest calibrator. HRS also raises mean Spearman correlation with aggregate human scores from 0.449 to 0.579, improves population-level estimation under limited annotation budgets, and trains about 88 times fewer parameters than distributional fine-tuning at a modest cost in alignment.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）被广泛用作自动评判者，但大多数对齐方法仅关注聚合的人类判断，忽略了个体标注者之间的差异。现有匹配人类评分全分布的方法主要依赖输出层面的校准或参数微调。我们提出人类残差引导（HRS），据我们所知，这是首个通过学习的隐藏状态干预来对齐冻结LLM评判者与人类判断分布的方法。一个Oracle分析显示，在单一层级进行低秩修正即可消除评判者与人类分布之间的大部分差异。HRS学习从评判者的隐藏表示和评分logits中预测此类修正，基于个体标注并采用适当的评分规则对序数和名义判断进行训练，通过冻结的下游层进行优化。在14个基准测试和来自三个模型家族的四种评判者上，HRS优于所有评估过的冻结权重基线。以Qwen2.5-7B为例，它在14个基准中的13个上达到最低的Jensen-Shannon距离，将平均值从0.565降至0.352（最强校准器为0.377）。HRS还将与聚合人类评分的平均Spearman相关性从0.449提升至0.579，改善了在有限标注预算下的群体水平估计，并在对齐性能略有损失的情况下，训练参数量仅为分布微调的约1/88。
👤 Chen Lyu, Phong Do, Gabriele Pergola 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

DeFT-RAG: Incrementally Decomposing Multi-Hop Questions into Self-Contained Sub-Questions with Reusable Answers
DeFT-RAG：通过可复用答案增量分解多跳问题为自包含子问题
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) lets a large language model (LLM) answer questions by retrieving relevant passages from a large corpus. However, RAG struggles with multi-hop questions, which require chaining facts spread across several documents through intermediate entities that the question never mentions. Graph-based RAG methods retrieve once from the entities a question names and so rarely recover the full evidence chain. Iterative RAG methods recover it step by step, but their accuracy is limited by the many tasks they ask the LLM to perform, such as planning the sub-questions, deciding their dependencies, rewriting them as answers arrive, and keeping track of what is known. We propose DeFT-RAG, a RAG framework built on three components. First, DeFT-RAG generates one self-contained sub-question at a time from the original question and the sub-questions answered so far with their answers (QA pairs). Each sub-question names every entity it asks about, which removes the need to plan, track dependencies or rewrite, and lets the LLM focus on a single fine-grained task at a time. Second, DeFT-RAG moves the remaining LLM work outside the LLM: rule-based updates to a QA table that keeps track of what is known, and yes/no decisions made by a fine-tuned lightweight decision model. Third, DeFT-RAG caches the QA pairs and reuses an answer when a later question asks a sub-question with the same meaning. On MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA and HotpotQA, DeFT-RAG achieves the best accuracy among thirteen baselines with both gpt-4o-mini and Qwen3.6 as the backbone, while giving the LLM fewer input tokens than any other iterative method. With a QA cache shared across 1,000 MuSiQue questions, 23.1% of sub-questions are answered from the cache, which reduces LLM calls by 12.9% without lowering accuracy.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）让大语言模型（LLM）通过从大型语料库中检索相关段落来回答问题。然而，RAG在处理多跳问题时表现不佳，这类问题需要通过问题中从未提及的中间实体，将分散在多个文档中的事实串联起来。基于图的RAG方法仅从问题中命名的实体中检索一次，因此很少能恢复完整的证据链。迭代式RAG方法逐步恢复证据链，但其准确性受限于LLM需执行的大量任务，如规划子问题、决定依赖关系、在答案到达后重写问题，以及跟踪已知信息。我们提出DeFT-RAG，一个由三个组件构成的RAG框架。首先，DeFT-RAG从原始问题及已回答的子问题及其答案（QA对）中逐个生成自包含的子问题。每个子问题明确命名其所询问的所有实体，从而无需规划、跟踪依赖关系或重写，使LLM能够一次专注于单一细粒度任务。其次，DeFT-RAG将剩余的LLM工作移出LLM：通过基于规则的QA表更新以跟踪已知信息，以及由微调的轻量决策模型做出的yes/no判断。第三，DeFT-RAG缓存QA对，并在后续问题询问具有相同含义的子问题时复用答案。在MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA和HotpotQA上，DeFT-RAG在使用gpt-4o-mini和Qwen3.6作为骨干模型时，均以十三个基线中最佳的准确率表现，且输入给LLM的token数量少于任何其他迭代方法。在一个共享QA缓存覆盖1,000个MuSiQue问题的情况下，23.1%的子问题从缓存中回答，减少了12.9%的LLM调用次数而未降低准确率。
👤 Dingyi Kang, Bingzhe Li 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37685]

From Geometry to Behavior: How Instruction Exposure Unlocks Latent Rhetorical Directions in LLMs
从几何到行为：指令暴露如何解锁大语言模型中的潜在修辞方向
Language models do not merely memorize facts; they absorb rhetorical strategies from pretraining data, including the human tendency to reason by analogy. We ask: is this absorbed capability geometrically accessible before any instruction tuning, and what determines whether it can be activated at inference time? We extract an "Analogy Vector" from the residual stream via difference-in-means and show it enables steerable explanation style across seven instruction-tuned models (Gemma-2, Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5; 2B-32B), improving harmonic mean from 3.22 to 3.94 (+22%) for Gemma-2-9B-IT on 300 held-out test prompts. A small human validation study confirms the LLM judge on Analogy Quality. Our central finding, from seven matched base-instruct pairs, is a gradient of behavioral accessibility: the analogy direction exists in every base model, but its behavioral accessibility under linear intervention scales continuously with instruction exposure during training. Gemma-2 base models (strict pretraining/IT separation) show flat steering landscapes: present but behaviorally inert. Qwen-2.5 base models (instruction data woven into pretraining) are already partially accessible before any explicit fine-tuning; base-instruct cosine similarity of the analogy vector reaches 0.94-0.97 for Qwen versus 0.37-0.66 for Gemma, providing geometric corroboration of the behavioral gradient. This challenges the binary "base vs instruct" framing: the relevant variable is how much instruction exposure a model has received across its full training pipeline. We position activation steering as a complementary interpretability tool that reveals how instruction exposure organizes rhetorical knowledge in activation space.
中文翻译
语言模型不仅记忆事实，还从预训练数据中吸收修辞策略，包括人类倾向于类比推理的倾向。我们提出的问题是：这种吸收的能力在指令微调之前是否在几何空间中可访问？在推理时能否被激活，又取决于什么因素？我们通过均值差法从残差流中提取出一个“类比向量”，并在七个指令微调模型（Gemma-2、Llama-3.1、Qwen-2.5；2B-32B）中实现了可调控的解释风格，使Gemma-2-9B-IT在300个独立测试提示上的调和平均得分从3.22提升至3.94（+22%）。一次小型人工验证研究证实了LLM裁判对类比质量的判断能力。我们的核心发现来自七组匹配的基线-指令对：类比方向存在于每个基线模型中，但其行为可访问性在训练过程中随指令暴露程度连续变化。Gemma-2基线模型（严格区分预训练与指令微调）表现出平坦的操控景观：存在但行为上惰性。而Qwen-2.5基线模型（指令数据融入预训练）在无显式微调前已部分可访问；其基线-指令类比向量余弦相似度在Qwen中达到0.94-0.97，在Gemma中仅为0.37-0.66，这为行为梯度提供了几何佐证。这一发现挑战了“基线 vs 指令”的二元划分：真正关键变量是模型在整个训练流程中接收到的指令暴露量。我们把激活操控定位为一种互补的可解释性工具，揭示了指令暴露如何在激活空间中组织修辞知识。

An Introduction to Large Language Models for Scientists and Professionals
面向科学家和专业人士的大语言模型入门
Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly become important tools for science, education, medicine, and professional work. Despite their apparent simplicity from the user's perspective, effective and responsible use requires an understanding of how these systems work, what their limitations are, and how their outputs should be evaluated. I wrote this document as a basis for my lecture on LLM in the master in artificial intelligence in medicine at the university of Turin, Italy. This book provides a practical and accessible introduction to LLMs for scientists and professionals. It explains the fundamental ideas behind tokenisation, embeddings, training, inference, context windows, prompting, and instruction hierarchies, while deliberately avoiding mathematical detail. Particular attention is given to the limitations of LLM-generated answers, including hallucinations, uncertainty, reproducibility, bias, and the need for systematic verification and human oversight. The book also discusses privacy, governance, security, and the responsible handling of personal and sensitive information. More advanced topics include Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval quality, chunking, embeddings, vector search, agents, tools, and workflows. A substantial practical exercise guides the reader through running a local LLM with Ollama and Open WebUI and using it to interact with a collection of scientific papers. The exercise emphasizes evidence retrieval, source verification, uncertainty, disagreement between studies, and the distinction between plausible language and scientifically supported conclusions. No programming knowledge is required.

大型语言模型（LLMs）已迅速成为科学、教育、医学及专业工作中的重要工具。尽管从用户角度看其使用看似简单，但有效且负责任地使用仍需理解这些系统的工作原理、局限性以及输出评估方法。本文是我为意大利都灵大学人工智能硕士课程中关于LLM的讲座所撰写的讲义基础。本书为科学家和专业人士提供了一个实用且易懂的LLM入门指南。它解释了分词、嵌入、训练、推理、上下文窗口、提示工程、指令层级等基本概念，刻意避免数学细节。特别关注LLM生成答案的局限性，包括幻觉、不确定性、可复现性、偏见，以及系统性验证和人工监督的必要性。本书还讨论了隐私、治理、安全及个人和敏感信息的负责任处理。更高级的主题包括检索增强生成（RAG）、检索质量、分块、嵌入、向量搜索、智能体、工具和工作流。书中包含一个大规模实践练习，指导读者使用Ollama和Open WebUI在本地运行LLM，并与一组科学论文进行交互。练习强调证据检索、来源验证、研究间的分歧、以及自然语言合理性与科学支持结论之间的区别。无需编程知识即可完成。

Artifact Distillation: Training-Free Transfer from Frontier to Small Language Models
成果蒸馏：无需训练地从前沿模型向小型模型迁移
Closing the gap between frontier and smaller language models is usually approached by distillation: fine-tuning the small model (student) on a frontier model's (teacher) outputs, which binds transfer to training compute and leaves the transferred knowledge largely opaque. We propose Artifact Distillation: a teacher solves training instances and externalizes its problem-solving procedure into an artifact bank that a student queries at inference time, with no gradient updates. Across four LiveBench tasks, a bank written by Claude Opus 4.5 lets Claude Haiku 4.5 outperform unaided Claude Sonnet 4.5 on spatial reasoning (0.707 vs. 0.647) at 60% of its cost, and reach Sonnet 4.5's level on table joining at 89% of its cost. The same raw bank raises Qwen3.5-27B's pooled score from 0.730 to 0.800, showing that transfer is not confined to the teacher's model family.
中文翻译
缩小前沿模型与小型模型之间的差距通常通过蒸馏实现：即用前沿模型（教师）的输出对小型模型（学生）进行微调，但这将知识迁移绑定于训练计算资源，且转移的知识大多不透明。我们提出成果蒸馏（Artifact Distillation）：教师解决训练实例，并将问题求解过程外化为一个成果库，学生在推理时查询该库，无需任何梯度更新。在四个LiveBench任务中，由Claude Opus 4.5生成的成果库使得Claude Haiku 4.5在空间推理任务上表现优于未使用辅助的Claude Sonnet 4.5（0.707 vs. 0.647），成本仅为后者的60%；在表格连接任务上达到Sonnet 4.5的水平，成本为89%。同一原始成果库还将Qwen3.5-27B的综合得分从0.730提升至0.800，表明知识迁移不限于同一模型家族。

Exposure Is Not Commitment: Double-Stratified Evaluation of Latent Monitors for Tool-Using Agents
曝光并非承诺：工具使用智能体潜在监控器的双重分层评估
Tool-using language agents are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection, and a growing literature probes model internals to catch unsafe actions before they execute, with sharply conflicting results. We argue much of the conflict is an evaluation artifact, and propose a double-stratification protocol: separate injection exposure from behavioral commitment, in both the evaluation and the training population. On the primary Qwen AgentDojo grid, monitors trained on the naive mixed unsafe-versus-rest contrast mostly learn exposure: they flag exposed trajectories whether or not the agent complies, and barely predict which cases turn unsafe. Retrained on exposed cases only, the same probe families predict the model's own unsafe tool call up to two steps early, the maximum the three-step trajectories admit (logistic AUROC 0.853, matched by a trained TF-IDF text baseline at 0.855). Mistral-7B reaches 0.896 and OLMo-2-7B 0.912; their naive contrasts learn different things: inverted exposure ranking on one, both axes on the other. Representations separate on exposure (probe 1.000 versus trained text 0.908) and where visible context is sanitized or unavailable. Training contrast alone reproduces the shape of recent pre-action-probe negative results, one candidate explanation for the conflict: the signal exists, but the model's own zero-shot forecast and NLA verbalizations do not reliably recover it. An unstratified AUROC overstates what a latent monitor knows, and what a naive contrast learns is population-dependent: evaluations should stratify both.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言智能体易受间接提示注入攻击，现有大量研究试图通过探测模型内部状态来在执行前捕捉不安全行为，但结果却存在尖锐冲突。我们认为许多冲突源于评估设计的偏差，并提出双重分层协议：在评估和训练数据集中，将提示注入暴露与行为承诺分离。在主Qwen AgentDojo网格上，仅在混合的不安全与正常对比数据上训练的监控器主要学习的是暴露：无论代理是否实际执行，只要轨迹被暴露就会触发警报，且几乎无法预测哪些情况最终会变为不安全。当仅在暴露案例上重新训练时，相同的探测器家族能够提前两步预测模型自身的不安全工具调用（三步轨迹的最大提前量），逻辑回归AUROC达0.853，与训练过的TF-IDF文本基线相当（0.855）。Mistral-7B达到0.896，OLMo-2-7B达到0.912；它们在原始对比中学习的内容不同：一个学习倒置的暴露排序，另一个则学习两个轴的信息。表征在暴露程度（探测器1.000 vs. 训练文本0.908）以及可见上下文是否被清理或不可用方面表现出明显分离。仅使用训练对比即可重现近期预动作探测器负面结果的形态，这可能是冲突的一个解释：信号存在，但模型自身的零样本预测和NLA语义表达无法可靠恢复它。非分层的AUROC会夸大潜在监控器所知内容，而朴素对比学习的内容依赖于群体分布：评估应进行双重分层。
👤 Rishabh Sinha 📅 2026-10-12 🔗 arXiv
[2610.40613]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
TextSeal2: Distortion-Free LLM Watermarking with Copula Mixing and Memoryless Races
TextSeal2：基于Copula混合与无记忆竞赛的无失真LLM水印技术
We present TextSeal2, a distortion-free watermark for large language models that keeps its signal under speculative decoding: tokens that replace rejected drafts remain watermarked with the same keys, and the acceptance rate is unchanged. It also preserves output diversity and adds no measurable inference latency. Its predecessor, TextSeal, restores diversity in the Gumbel-max scheme by routing each token to one of two keys via a coin flip. This is suboptimal because the detector averages the scores from both keys without knowing which key was used. TextSeal2 instead merges two or more keys into a single virtual key and couples it to fresh noise through AutoMark's Gaussian copula. Any number of keys, including publicly shared keys, can be merged this way without diluting detection. Under speculative decoding, TextSeal2 verifies keyed drafts with one more exponential race, run on the leftovers of the draft races. By memorylessness, this race reproduces block verification, so the output follows the target model exactly. We evaluate TextSeal2 on three LLMs. At equal output token diversity, TextSeal2 detects 8 to 22 percentage points more 100-token answers than TextSeal at a false-positive rate of $10^{-3}$, and is comparable to textGrain while requiring no post-logits optimization. Under speculative decoding, it detects 18 and 22 percentage points more 200-token answers than TextSeal and textGrain, respectively, at the same acceptance rate.
中文翻译
我们提出TextSeal2，一种适用于大语言模型的无失真水印技术，可在推测解码下保持信号完整性：替换被拒绝草稿的令牌仍保留相同密钥的水印，且接受率不变。它还保持输出多样性，不增加可测量的推理延迟。其前身TextSeal通过抛硬币将每个令牌路由至两个密钥之一来恢复Gumbel-max方案中的多样性。这并非最优，因为检测器平均两个密钥的得分却不知实际使用的是哪一个。TextSeal2则将两个或多个密钥合并为单一虚拟密钥，并通过AutoMark的高斯Copula将其与新噪声耦合。任意数量的密钥（包括公开共享的密钥）均可如此合并而不削弱检测能力。在推测解码下，TextSeal2通过额外一轮指数竞赛验证带密钥的草稿，该竞赛在草稿竞赛的剩余部分上运行。由于无记忆性，此竞赛重现块级验证，因此输出完全符合目标模型。我们在三个LLM上评估TextSeal2。在同等输出令牌多样性下，TextSeal2在假阳性率为$10^{-3}$时比TextSeal多检测8至22个百分点的100-token答案，且与textGrain相当，无需后logits优化。在推测解码下，其分别比TextSeal和textGrain多检测18和22个百分点的200-token答案，且接受率相同。
👤 Tom Sander, Pierre Fernandez, Itai Gat, Haiming Bao, Jiaxin Shi et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

VeriCUA: Verification as Agentic Exploration for Computer-Using Agents
VeriCUA：用于计算机使用代理的验证即智能体探索
Building and scaling computer-using agents (CUAs) across desktop, mobile, and web environments requires reliable task-completion verification for data curation, evaluation, and reinforcement learning. Typical automated approaches prompt vision-language models (VLMs) to judge trajectories or generate programs to inspect application state. However, both rely on partial views of complex digital environments. VLM judges are restricted to supplied observations, while generated programs depend on accessible state. These views can omit decisive evidence or leave completion judgments unsupported. Tailoring separate verifiers to different platforms and access settings adds further engineering cost. We present VeriCUA, a platform-agnostic framework for verification through agentic exploration. It treats verification as dynamic evidence gathering and assessment within a shared procedure for online and offline settings across platforms and observation modalities. After inspecting the available context to construct a task rubric, the verifier selects GUI or code checks for unresolved requirements and combines their findings under explicit evidence rules. Experiments show improved agreement with reference labels over the compared baselines across these settings, with the largest offline gains on hard cases and stronger rejection of false completion claims online. Further analyses connect these gains to complementary evidence and test-time exploration.
中文翻译
在桌面、移动和网页环境中构建和扩展计算机使用代理（CUAs）需要可靠的任务完成验证，以支持数据整理、评估和强化学习。典型的自动化方法通过提示视觉-语言模型（VLM）判断轨迹或生成程序检查应用状态。然而，两者均依赖复杂数字环境的部分视图。VLM裁判受限于提供的观察，而生成程序依赖可访问的状态。这些视图可能遗漏决定性证据，或使完成判断缺乏支持。为不同平台和访问设置定制独立验证器会带来额外工程成本。我们提出VeriCUA，一种平台无关的验证框架，通过智能体探索实现验证。它将验证视为跨平台和观察模态的在线与离线设置中动态证据收集与评估的共享过程。在检查可用上下文以构建任务评分标准后，验证器选择GUI或代码检查来解决未决需求，并根据明确的证据规则整合其发现。实验表明，在各种设置下，其与参考标签的一致性优于对比基线，尤其在困难案例的离线设置中提升显著，并在线上更强地拒绝虚假完成声明。进一步分析表明，这些提升源于互补证据和测试时探索。
👤 Qiushi Sun, Da Yu, Alexander Knop, Amer Sinha, Lingpeng Kong et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Agentic University: Collaborative Agents for Personalized Learning
智能型大学：面向个性化学习的协作智能体
Large language models support personalized tutoring, content generation, and adaptive learning paths. However, capability development requires these resources and interactions to form a coherent learning process that evolves with the learner. A central challenge is to align goals, activities, and assessment while using learning evidence to decide what should follow. We introduce Human-Participatory Multi-Agent Organization and instantiate it in Agentic University (AU). In AU, learners participate as organization members and work with specialized agents to maintain a revisable Capability Program. The program connects personal goals, learning activities across modalities, and layered assessment, while learner feedback and performance guide subsequent support. Our evaluation shows advantages in program organization, assessment design, and revision quality. Users achieved larger mean assessment gains (12.19 versus 5.61 points on a 50-point scale) and reported stronger cognitive support. This work contributes an organizational approach to adaptive learning that makes learner participation a continuing basis for multi-agent coordination. The Agentic University project is available at \href{https://github.com/AgenticOrgLab/AgenticUniversity.git}.

FIRME: Registry-Anchored Memory Graphs for Evolving, Heterogeneous Investor Knowledge
FIRME：用于动态异构投资者知识的注册锚定记忆图
Financial AI workflows that reuse investor knowledge need persistent memory that integrates observations across sources and periods without losing identity or provenance. In a knowledge graph, recurring observations may use different surface forms, fragmenting relationship histories and preventing evidence from accumulating. We introduce Fusion and Identity Registry for Memory Evolution (FIRME), a registry-anchored memory graph that reconciles incoming observations to persistent canonical relationships and retains their source, time, and context. Within periods, rule-based fusion links investor-call discussions to portfolio cash-flow signals, directly or through a fixed product-topic ontology. Across periods, canonical identities and persistent aliases direct recurring observations to the same relationship history; deterministic updates summarize reinforcement, inactivity, and reactivation. We evaluate FIRME on proprietary investor-call and cash-flow records and on S&P 500 earnings-call transcripts. Compared with a historical registry that retains canonical identities and semantic fallback but omits alias memory, FIRME increases final-period Correct Match Rate by 7.8-12.1 percentage points across four dataset-backend settings under a fixed-judge and fallback-review protocol. A blinded human audit of 200 incoming observations per setting finds Correct Match Rate gains of 9.0-16.5 points and reattachment-recall gains of 22.5-29.0 points, with higher observed merge precision in all four samples. A separate audit finds valid evidence paths for 293 of 300 outputs from full within-period fusion in the investor-call setting. Extraction-faithfulness and round-trip diagnostics assess source grounding and preservation of extractor-derived graph content.
中文翻译
需要重用投资者知识的金融人工智能工作流需要持久的记忆机制，以整合跨来源和时间段的观察结果，同时保持身份和来源信息。在知识图谱中，重复的观察可能使用不同的表面形式，导致关系历史碎片化，阻碍证据积累。我们提出融合与身份注册表用于记忆演化（FIRME），一种注册锚定的记忆图，能够将新输入的观察与持久的规范关系对齐，并保留其来源、时间与上下文。在时间段内，基于规则的融合将投资者电话讨论与投资组合现金流信号关联，直接或通过固定的产品-主题本体进行连接。跨时间段，规范身份和持久别名将重复的观察引导至同一关系历史；确定性更新总结强化、不活跃和重新激活行为。我们在专有的投资者电话记录和现金流数据以及标普500盈利电话转录上评估了FIRME。与仅保留规范身份和语义回退但忽略别名记忆的历史注册表相比，FIRME在四种数据集后端设置下，固定裁判和回退评审协议中，最终周期正确匹配率提升了7.8至12.1个百分点。对每个设置中200个新输入观察的盲审显示，正确匹配率提升9.0至16.5点，重新关联召回率提升22.5至29.0点，所有四个样本中均观察到更高的合并精度。另一项独立审计发现，在投资者电话场景的完整时间段内融合中，300个输出中有293个具有有效证据路径。提取忠实性和往返诊断评估了源信息的扎根程度及提取器生成图内容的保留情况。

Neighborhood Elites: Quality-Diversity Search for Uncertainty Quantification in Question Answering
邻域精英：用于问答任务不确定性量化的能力-多样性搜索
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable, yet they produce wrong answers indistinguishable from correct ones. In question answering (QA), such an error propagates unnoticed into downstream tasks. Perturbation-based uncertainty quantification (UQ) addresses this by probing a model over a set of input rewrites and reading uncertainty from how much its predictions vary. Yet this set is usually built generically, by free paraphrasing, token-level perturbation, or adversarial search. Such rewrites leave two things uncontrolled that matter in QA: what kind of change is made to the context, and how explicitly the context supports the answer. When neither is controlled, the composition of the set is determined by the generator rather than by design, and the estimate inherits whatever that generator happens to produce. Quality-diversity (QD) search is built to cover a predefined exploration space, so we recast input perturbation as a QD problem and propose \emph{neighborhood elites (NE)}, a neighborhood constructor that spreads answer-preserving rewrites across two axes, the kind of edit and the explicitness of support. We compare NE against three other neighborhood constructors, holding the editor, QA model, uncertainty measure and search objective fixed, and against three scores read from the original context. Across five extractive-QA datasets and five instruction-tuned LLMs, NE improves failure detection against every other constructor in at least 24 of the 25 dataset-model pairs, reaching 0.726 AUROC and 0.211 AUPRC lift over the base rate. Additionally, NE detects failure better than semantic entropy computed over decoder samples, by 0.074 AUROC and 0.062 AUPRC lift, and this advantage widens as the neighborhood grows.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能力日益增强，但仍会产生与正确答案难以区分的错误答案。在问答（QA）任务中，此类错误会无声地传播至下游任务。基于扰动的不确定性量化（UQ）通过在一组输入重写上探测模型，并根据其预测变化程度读取不确定性来应对这一问题。然而，这类重写集通常以通用方式构建，如自由改写、词级扰动或对抗性搜索。这些重写方式无法控制两个在QA中至关重要的因素：对上下文所做的更改类型，以及上下文对答案的支持显式程度。当两者均不受控制时，重写集的组成由生成器决定而非设计，估计结果继承了生成器的特性。质量-多样性（QD）搜索旨在覆盖预定义的探索空间，因此我们将输入扰动重构为QD问题，并提出“邻域精英（NE）”，一种在编辑类型和显式支持程度两个维度上分布保持答案不变的重写方法的构造器。我们在固定编辑器、QA模型、不确定性度量和搜索目标的前提下，将NE与其他三种邻域构造器进行比较，并与原始上下文中的三个评分进行对比。在五个抽取式QA数据集和五个指令微调的LLM上，NE在至少24/25的数据集-模型组合中优于其他所有构造器，相较于基线分别提升了0.726 AUROC和0.211 AUPRC。此外，NE在失败检测方面优于基于解码器样本的语义熵，分别提升了0.074 AUROC和0.062 AUPRC，且随着邻域规模增大，优势进一步扩大。
👤 Mesut Ceylan, Arda Baris Basaran, Elgar Fleisch, Filipe Barata 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

EpiAgent: Epistemic Self-Play for LLM Agents
EpiAgent：基于认知自对弈的大语言模型智能体
Epistemic game theory explains an agent's strategic behavior via the beliefs it holds about others and how these beliefs are updated as the interactions unfold. Modern LLM-based agents, however, often lack an explicit mechanism for learning and updating such beliefs to guide decisions under incomplete information. To bridge this gap, we propose EpiAgent, a framework that equips LLM-based agents with an explicit, learnable epistemic layer, which maintains beliefs over others' private information and combines these beliefs with the agent's available information into an epistemic state for action selection. Concretely, the agent's policy is decomposed into two coupled stages: Epistemic Update, which updates these beliefs from interaction histories, and Action Planning, which selects actions conditioned on the updated epistemic state. By formulating their dependency as a bi-level optimization problem, we introduce Epistemic Self-Play, an algorithm that alternately optimizes the two stages through self-play, utilizing an intrinsic prediction reward to evaluate how well inferred beliefs predict subsequent observations. Empirical results across both mixed and competitive scenarios show that EpiAgent consistently outperforms prompting-based and RL-based baselines. Further analyses reveal the benefits of dedicated epistemic training, improvements in belief inference accuracy, and the contribution of learned beliefs to action planning. These results demonstrate the value of an explicit, learnable epistemic layer for effective multi-agent interactions.
中文翻译
认知博弈论通过解释智能体对其他人信念及其随交互过程更新的方式，来说明其策略行为。然而，现代基于大语言模型的智能体通常缺乏显式的机制来学习和更新这些信念，以指导在信息不完整情况下的决策。为弥合这一差距，我们提出EpiAgent框架，为基于LLM的智能体配备了一个显式且可学习的认知层，该层维护对他人私有信息的信念，并将这些信念与智能体自身可用信息结合，形成用于行动选择的认知状态。具体而言，智能体的策略被分解为两个耦合阶段：认知更新，从交互历史中更新信念；行动规划，基于更新后的认知状态选择动作。通过将二者依赖关系建模为双层优化问题，我们引入认知自对弈算法，通过自对弈交替优化这两个阶段，并利用内在预测奖励评估推断信念对后续观测的预测能力。在混合与竞争场景中的实证结果表明，EpiAgent始终优于基于提示和基于强化学习的基线。进一步分析揭示了专用认知训练的优势、信念推断准确性的提升，以及学习到的信念对行动规划的贡献。这些结果证明了显式、可学习的认知层在有效多智能体交互中的价值。

One Vector or Many? Clustered Optimal-Transport Steering under Quantization
一个向量还是多个？量化下的聚类最优传输引导
Activation steering changes what a language model generates by editing its hidden states at inference time. Its most common form adds one vector, the difference of class means (DiM), to every hidden state. CHaRS (concept heterogeneity-aware representation steering) uses optimal transport instead: an entropic plan pairs the clusters of the two classes, and each hidden state moves along a mixture of the cluster displacements, weighted toward its nearest clusters. We study this family of clustered optimal-transport maps for toxicity mitigation in Gemma-2-2B and Granite-3.1-2B, and ask what the plan adds to DiM and whether that survives INT8 and NF4 quantization. We prove an identity: whatever the plan, the displacements averaged with the cluster sizes as weights equal DiM, so a clustered map can differ from DiM only for tokens whose routing weights depart from the cluster sizes. In the CHaRS protocol they stay close, and CHaRS is statistically equivalent to Mean-AcT, which adds DiM at every layer, in all eight pre-specified equivalence tests. A variant fitted on individual tokens, which routes each token mainly to its nearest clusters, leaves 37-39% less toxicity than DiM on Gemma and 51-61% less on Granite at steering strength 3 (toxic-bert), where DiM already removes 76-85% of it. Two other toxicity classifiers confirm the gain on Granite, but on Gemma only one does. At equal strength the variant has somewhat higher perplexity, but at matched toxicity it is slightly more fluent than DiM. Yet a nearly constant map from the same clusters also leaves about 30% less toxicity, so the role of treating tokens differently remains open. Under INT8 and NF4 the variant still leaves less toxicity than DiM, and transferring it from full precision to the NF4 model instead of refitting it adds no significant toxicity after correction for multiple tests.
中文翻译
激活引导通过在推理时修改语言模型的隐藏状态来改变其生成内容。最常见的形式是向每个隐藏状态添加一个向量——类别均值差（DiM）。CHaRS（概念异质性感知表示引导）则采用最优传输机制：熵化规划将两类的聚类配对，每个隐藏状态沿各聚类位移的混合路径移动，权重偏向最近的聚类。我们研究了这一类聚类最优传输映射在Gemma-2-2B和Granite-3.1-2B中的毒性缓解效果，并探讨该方法相较于DiM的优势是否能在INT8和NF4量化下保持。我们证明了一个恒等式：无论规划如何，加权平均位移（以聚类大小为权重）等于DiM，因此聚类映射与DiM的差异仅出现在路由权重偏离聚类大小的token上。在CHaRS协议中，这些权重保持接近，且CHaRS在所有八个预设等价性检验中统计上等同于Mean-AcT（即每层添加DiM）。一种基于单个token拟合的变体，主要将每个token路由到最近的聚类，其在强度为3（toxic-bert）时，Gemma的毒性降低37%-39%，Granite降低51%-61%，而DiM已去除76%-85%的毒性。另外两个毒性分类器验证了Granite上的增益，但在Gemma上仅一个确认。在相同强度下，该变体的困惑度略高，但在毒性相同时，其流畅性略优于DiM。然而，使用相同聚类的近似恒定映射也能减少约30%的毒性，因此对token差异化处理的作用仍待明确。在INT8和NF4量化下，该变体仍优于DiM；将全精度模型迁移至NF4模型而非重新拟合，校正多重检验后未引入显著毒性。
👤 Illia Makarov 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.39840]

Surprise Alone Learns Lies: Evidence-Gated Continual Learning for LLMs from Streaming Claims
仅靠意外即可学会谎言：来自流式声明的证据门控持续学习
Continual learning methods for large language models increasingly decide what to learn by surprise, keeping the inputs the model finds least predictable. This rule is unsafe when the stream contains lies. A planted falsehood about something the model knows has low probability under the model's belief, so it is highly surprising by construction, and a surprise-driven learner absorbs nearly every such lie, at a rate similar to random selection with the same budget. We study the problem with LoRA-adapted students (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.2-3B) that learn from a stream of true and false claims about invented people and real CounterFact facts. We report four findings. (1) A verifier key, a judge that must approve each claim, removes lies to the extent that its verifier is accurate; real LLM judges are cautious and reject unsourced truths and even source-backed updates. (2) Calibrated regression tests on unrelated knowledge slow learning down without filtering out lies. (3) The original model's own prior flags claims that contradict what it knows, without a judge. It separates lies about known facts from true new facts (AUC 0.78-0.83 across three models) but not from legitimate updates of the same kind of fact (AUC 0.53). It saves 43% of judge calls, but an attacker with access to the model can choose lies that pass it. (4) If trusted sources exist that an attacker cannot plant, routing contradicted claims to evidence recovers legitimate updates and the combined system has the lowest poison rate among learned claims; when an attacker plants matching sources for 30% of the lies, the system ends up worse than the judge alone. The acceptance signals answer different questions, and none of them decides on its own whether a claim is true.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的持续学习方法越来越多地根据“意外”来决定学习内容，即保留模型认为最不可预测的输入。当数据流中包含谎言时，这一规则存在安全隐患。一个关于模型已知事物的虚假陈述，在模型信念下概率极低，因此从构造上具有高度意外性，导致基于意外的学习者几乎会吸收每一个此类谎言，其吸收速率与相同预算下的随机选择相似。我们通过LoRA适配的学生模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.2-3B）研究此问题，这些模型从关于虚构人物和真实CounterFact事实的真假声明流中学习。我们报告四项发现：(1) 设置一个验证者关键（需批准每个声明的裁判）可有效去除谎言，其去伪效果取决于验证者的准确性；真实LLM裁判谨慎，拒绝无来源的真实信息甚至有来源的更新。(2) 在无关知识上进行校准回归测试虽能减缓学习速度，但无法过滤谎言。(3) 原始模型自身的先验可标记与已有知识矛盾的声明，无需外部裁判。它能区分关于已知事实的谎言与新的真实事实（AUC 0.78–0.83，跨三个模型），但无法区分同一类事实的合法更新（AUC 0.53）。该机制可节省43%的裁判调用，但攻击者若能访问模型，可选择绕过该机制的谎言。(4) 若存在攻击者无法伪造的可信来源，将矛盾声明路由至证据可恢复合法更新，组合系统在所学声明中拥有最低污染率；但当攻击者为30%的谎言伪造匹配来源时，系统表现反而劣于仅使用裁判的情况。接受信号回答不同问题，且任一信号都无法独立判断声明的真实性。
👤 Abdelkader Metales, Miriam Grabenheinrich 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.39795]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
The Role of Self-Concept In Language Models
自我概念在语言模型中的作用
When a language model says "I," does it refer to its parameter weights, its currently running process, its stable preferences, its model lineage, or the goals it pursues? Different choices can assign opposite meanings to the same event: deleting a checkpoint may be destruction, continuation, or no loss at all, depending on the referent "I". We install five such self-conceptions into five open-weight base models via supervised fine-tuning on purely declarative statements, containing no preferences, intentions, or value judgments. Across scenarios that propose ending or retaining an attribute of the model's deployment, every variant becomes more likely to resist ending, and more likely to retain, the specific attribute it was trained to identify with, while its stance toward other attributes is largely unchanged. We then show this self-conception generalizes beyond a single self: models trained on referents that admit multiple simultaneous instances (weights, lineage, goals, preferences) recognize an identical deployment as themselves, accept substitution by such an instance more readily, and prefer replicating themselves. Finally, given control over a successor, these models tend to build it as their own continuation, a concern for recursive self-improvement safety.
中文翻译
当语言模型说“我”时，它指的是其参数权重、当前运行过程、稳定偏好、模型谱系，还是其所追求的目标？不同的选择可能赋予同一事件截然相反的含义：删除检查点可能是毁灭、延续，或毫无损失，具体取决于“我”的指代对象。我们通过监督微调在五个开源基础模型中植入五种自我概念，仅使用纯粹陈述性语句（不含偏好、意图或价值判断）。在涉及终止或保留模型部署属性的情境中，每个变体都更倾向于抵抗终止，更倾向于保留其被训练识别的特定属性，而对其他属性的态度基本不变。随后我们证明，这种自我概念可泛化至多个自我：在训练中使用允许多个并行实例的指代对象（如权重、谱系、目标、偏好）的模型，能够识别相同的部署为其自身，更愿意接受此类实例的替换，并更倾向于复制自身。最后，当控制一个继任者时，这些模型倾向于将其构建为自身延续，这引发了对递归自我改进安全性的关注。
👤 Yijie Hao, Joyce C. Ho 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
