Evaluating Cultural Knowledge Editing in Vision-Language Models
评估视觉-语言模型中的文化知识编辑
Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently fail to recognize culture-specific concepts, especially from underrepresented regions. Effectively injecting this long-tail knowledge into existing models remains a challenge. In this work, we investigate knowledge editing as a targeted alternative by introducing CulturalBind, a benchmark comprising 3.2K images of 1K concepts from 6 domains, designed to evaluate culture-aware cross-modal knowledge as well as concept-level knowledge editing in VLMs. Pre-edit evaluation reveals that textual and visual knowledge is decoupled: models often demonstrate text-based knowledge of cultural concepts but fail to identify them from images. We then evaluate editing methods in single and sequential scenarios. Although current methods successfully correct individual visual recognition errors, they exhibit low cross-modal portability, failing to integrate edits into the model's broader parametric knowledge. Furthermore, performance degrades rapidly under sequential editing, highlighting the need for comprehensive adaptation approaches that better consolidate multimodal knowledge over time.
中文翻译
当前的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）经常无法识别特定文化概念，尤其是来自代表性不足地区的概念。有效将这类长尾知识注入现有模型仍是挑战。本文通过引入CulturalBind基准，探索知识编辑作为一种针对性替代方案，该基准包含来自6个领域的1000个概念的3200张图像，旨在评估文化感知的跨模态知识以及VLMs中的概念级知识编辑。编辑前评估显示，文本和视觉知识是解耦的：模型常表现出对文化概念的文本知识，却无法从图像中识别它们。随后我们在单次和序列场景下评估编辑方法。尽管现有方法能成功修正个别视觉识别错误，但表现出较低的跨模态可迁移性，未能将编辑整合进模型更广泛的参数知识中。此外，序列编辑下的性能迅速下降，凸显了需要更全面的适应方法，以更好地随时间整合多模态知识。
👤 Malvina Nikandrou, Georgios Pantazopoulos, Ioannis Konstas, Alessandro Suglia 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Diagnosing the Reference Gap: What Visual Scaffolds Can and Cannot Fix in Multimodal Reasoning
诊断参照差距：视觉支架在多模态推理中能与不能解决的问题
Multimodal large language models can recognize visual content yet struggle to reason reliably over it across multiple steps. In counting, a model may see all objects but lose track of which ones it has counted. We argue that such failures, commonly attributed to perception, reveal a reference gap: the failure to bind language to the relevant visual entities, regions, or locations. Motivated by the theory of visual routines, we test whether external references such as numbering can narrow this gap. We introduce ReGap-Bench, a paired benchmark of five visual reasoning tasks in which the scene, question, and answer are fixed and only the reference varies. Results show that task-matched references improve accuracy by up to 25\%, while mismatched references provide little benefit or even hurt performance. These findings identify the reference gap as a bottleneck distinct from visual perception and show that constructing task-appropriate visual references can serve as a training-free visual skill for improving multimodal reasoning.
中文翻译
多模态大语言模型能够识别视觉内容，但在多步推理中难以可靠地处理它。例如，在计数任务中，模型可能看到所有物体，却丢失已计数物体的追踪。我们认为，此类失败通常归因于感知，实际上揭示了一个参照差距：即未能将语言绑定到相关视觉实体、区域或位置。受视觉程序理论启发，我们测试外部参照（如编号）能否缩小这一差距。我们引入ReGap-Bench，一个包含五个视觉推理任务的成对基准，其中场景、问题和答案固定，仅参照方式变化。结果显示，任务匹配的参照可使准确率提升高达25%，而不匹配的参照几乎无益甚至损害性能。这些发现将参照差距识别为独立于视觉感知的瓶颈，并表明构建任务适配的视觉参照可作为无需训练的视觉技能，以提升多模态推理能力。
👤 Guizhen Chen, Weiwen Xu, Wotao Yin, Anh Tuan Luu, Hou Pong Chan 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

AgentHeal: Evaluating Goal-Preserving Resource Recovery of Web Agents under Access Barriers
AgentHeal：在访问障碍下评估网络智能体的目标保持资源恢复能力
Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating complex information-seeking and transactional tasks across the open web. However, their real-world reliability remains limited by access barriers, such as dead links, authentication walls, rate limits, and anti-scraping defenses. These barriers block the primary execution path, causing agents to abort prematurely, loop indefinitely, or quietly compromise the user's original constraints. Existing evaluations either implicitly assume that target resources are freely accessible or focus on transient perturbations that simple retries can resolve, failing to capture scenarios where agents must actively reroute around impassable bottlenecks. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces AgentHeal, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating goal-preserving resource recovery, defined as the ability of web agents to detect non-transient access barriers, pivot to legitimate alternative sources, and fulfill the user's exact original objective. Spanning nine barrier families across a counterfactual sandbox and real-website replay, our experiments on leading LLM backbones show that average task success drops sharply from 89.4% to 43.7% under access barriers. Further analysis reveals that agents frequently fall for deceptive look-alikes, fail to escalate to users when necessary, violate access policies, or waste computation on futile retries, providing actionable diagnostics for developing resilient web agents.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体已在开放网络上自动化复杂的信息检索和交易任务中展现出强大能力。然而，其实际可靠性受限于访问障碍，如失效链接、身份验证墙、速率限制和反爬虫防御。这些障碍会阻断主要执行路径，导致智能体过早终止、无限循环或悄然违背用户的原始约束。现有评估要么隐含假设目标资源可自由访问，要么聚焦于可通过简单重试解决的瞬时扰动，未能捕捉智能体必须主动绕过不可逾越瓶颈的场景。为弥合这一差距，本文引入AgentHeal，一个全面的基准，用于评估目标保持的资源恢复能力，即网络智能体检测非瞬时访问障碍、转向合法替代来源并完全实现用户原始目标的能力。在反事实沙箱和真实网站回放中覆盖九类障碍的实验表明，领先LLM骨干模型在访问障碍下的平均任务成功率从89.4%急剧下降至43.7%。进一步分析揭示，智能体频繁陷入欺骗性相似项、在必要时未能向用户升级、违反访问政策或在无意义的重试中浪费计算资源，为开发更具韧性的网络智能体提供了可操作的诊断。
👤 Zhiyi Liu, Haohua Du, Haoqi Sun, Haisheng Tan 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Reasoning or Pattern Exploitation? Mechanistic Insights from RL-Trained Language Models
推理还是模式利用？来自强化学习训练语言模型的机制洞察
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly described as acquiring "reasoning" skills after reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. However, whether these gains reflect genuine reasoning or more superficial adaptations remains underexplored. In this work, we probe LLMs trained in a finite and exhaustively analyzable logical domain, namely Tic-Tac-Toe, and trace how internal representations evolve across reinforcement learning (RL) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Quantitatively, RL yields high accuracy and robustness across prompt variations, suggesting the emergence of game reasoning. Mechanistic interpretability, however, paints a different picture: features extracted with sparse autoencoders (SAEs) reveal that models primarily adapt to rely more heavily on information already explicit in the prompt, such as whose turn it is, game progression and board occupancy. In comparison, high-level concepts like board symmetries and strategic forks remain weakly represented, raising concerns that reasoning benchmarks overestimate model abilities. This suggests that RL-driven "reasoning" may conflate task-specific adaptations to input information with reasoning ability. We provide, for the first time: (i) a systematic interpretability pipeline tracing representation dynamics across RL training in small-scale LLMs; (ii) an extension of SAE-based feature discovery to hypothesis-driven testing in a finite logical domain; and (iii) an interpretability-based demonstration that RL post-training neither adds nor removes concept information but reorganizes which features dominate the representation, with causal interventions locating the decision downstream of it, in the late-layer state.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在经过带有可验证奖励的强化学习后，越来越多地被描述为获得了“推理”能力。然而，这些提升是否反映真正的推理能力，还是更表面化的适应行为，仍缺乏深入探讨。在本研究中，我们探测在一个有限且可穷尽分析的逻辑领域——井字棋——中训练的LLMs，并追踪其在组相对策略优化（GRPO）强化学习过程中内部表示的演变。定量上，强化学习带来了在提示变化下的高准确性和鲁棒性，暗示了游戏推理的出现。然而，机制可解释性却呈现出不同的图景：通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）提取的特征显示，模型主要适应于更依赖提示中已明确的信息，如轮次归属、游戏进程和棋盘占据情况。相比之下，高层次概念如棋盘对称性和战略分支仍表现薄弱，引发对推理基准高估模型能力的担忧。这表明，强化学习驱动的“推理”可能混淆了对输入信息的任务特定适应与真正的推理能力。我们首次提供：(i) 一套系统性可解释性流程，追踪小规模LLMs在强化学习训练中表示动态的变化；(ii) 将SAE-based特征发现扩展至有限逻辑领域的假设驱动测试；(iii) 基于可解释性的实证证明：强化学习后处理既未添加也未移除概念信息，而是重新组织了主导表示的特征，因果干预定位决策位于下游的晚期层状态。

Beyond Passing Tests: A Systematic Review and Controlled Re-evaluation of Techniques for Improving the Quality of LLM-Generated Code
超越通过测试：对提升大语言模型生成代码质量技术的系统性回顾与受控再评估
Large language models pass most functional-correctness benchmarks, but passing tests says little about whether their code is readable, efficient or secure. Many techniques claim to improve these qualities, yet their evidence is scattered, usually covers one quality dimension and is rarely compared at an equal compute budget. We contribute a systematic review and a controlled re-evaluation. A systematic literature review of 628 primary studies (July 2021 to October 2026), with disclosed, author-reviewed LLM assistance, organizes the techniques by stage, signal source and quality target. Most studies measure only the dimension they target, gains often come with unchanged or unreported correctness, single automated quality signals are gamed, and compute, variance and contamination are rarely reported. A controlled re-evaluation runs ten techniques on four models at matched budgets on correctness, maintainability, efficiency and security benchmarks, extends it to thirteen current models and repository-level tasks, and compares 66 highlighted techniques head-to-head on Gemma 4 31B. At one call, quality prompting raises analyzer issue counts and leaves complexity and judge scores almost unchanged. At matched budgets, best-of-n sampling with an analyzer-weighted ranker matches or beats prompting, planning, self-refinement, a reviewer agent and retrieval on measures the ranker does not optimize, while an analyzer feedback loop and fine-tuning beat it only on analyzer issue counts. Large correctness losses on HumanEval+, a likely contaminated benchmark near its ceiling, do not appear on contests held after the models' training cutoffs. In the head-to-head, 1 of 66 techniques beats best-of-n on its primary target beyond the 95% interval, a rate consistent with chance. Gains should be reported against a budget-matched sampling baseline and on measures independent of the technique's own signal.
中文翻译
大型语言模型虽然通过了大多数功能性正确性基准测试，但通过测试并不能反映其代码是否可读、高效或安全。许多技术声称能改善这些质量维度，但其证据分散，通常仅覆盖单一维度，且很少在相同计算预算下进行比较。我们贡献了一项系统性文献回顾和受控再评估。对628篇原始研究（2021年7月至2026年10月）进行了系统性回顾，涵盖披露并经作者评审的LLM辅助内容，按阶段、信号来源和质量目标对技术进行分类。多数研究仅衡量其目标维度，增益常伴随正确性未变或未报告，单一自动化质量信号易被操纵，而计算量、方差和污染情况极少报告。受控再评估在四个模型上以匹配预算运行十种技术，评估正确性、可维护性、效率和安全性基准，扩展至十三个当前模型和仓库级任务，并在Gemma 4 31B上对66种突出技术进行一对一比较。在一次调用中，质量提示增加了分析器发现问题数量，但复杂度和评分几乎不变。在匹配预算下，最佳n采样结合分析器加权排序器在排序器未优化的指标上表现匹配或优于提示、规划、自修正、评审代理和检索；而分析器反馈循环和微调仅在分析器问题数量上优于它。在HumanEval+上出现的大规模正确性损失（可能因接近天花板而被污染的基准）在模型训练截止后举行的竞赛中并未出现。在一对一比较中，66种技术中有1种在主要目标上超出最佳n采样的95%置信区间，该比例与随机水平一致。建议将增益报告与预算匹配的采样基线对比，并在技术自身信号无关的指标上进行评估。

Contextual Embeddings Beyond the Gold Chunk
超越黄金块的上下文嵌入
Contextual embeddings represent chunks of a document with respect to the document's global context. These models are typically trained and evaluated under the assumption that a relevant document contains only a single relevant chunk. In this work, we argue that in real-world use cases a retriever should not only find answers spread over several chunks but also surface the evidence needed to verify them. To address this, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-context, a family of contextual embedding models trained by distilling graded chunk-level relevance from a query-aware context compression model. This removes the need for gold-chunk labels and makes the training targets independent of the chunking. We utilize a vision-language backbone for training and release pplx-embed-v2-context in 9B and 0.6B sizes with compatible embedding spaces. On the evaluation side, we introduce two benchmarks that label both the chunks that answer a query and the evidence needed to verify the answer. Context-bench is a controlled diagnostic, and Q2D-Chunk is built from real-world search queries. To encourage further research, we also publish Q2D-Chunk as a public leaderboard. Our 9B model sets a new state of the art on both benchmarks and ConTEB. We find that the visual retrieval capabilities of our 9B model transfer from its vision-language base model and propose to extend chunking to image inputs. We demonstrate that our models achieve state-of-the-art performance among dense retrievers on visual ViDoRe-V3 and that performance improves with granular image chunking.
中文翻译
上下文嵌入将文档的片段与其全局上下文相关联。这些模型通常在假设相关文档仅包含单个相关片段的前提下进行训练和评估。本文认为，在实际应用场景中，检索器不仅应找到分布在多个片段中的答案，还应揭示验证答案所需的证据。为此，我们提出了pplx-embed-v2-context系列上下文嵌入模型，通过从查询感知的上下文压缩模型中蒸馏分级的片段级相关性进行训练。这消除了对黄金片段标签的需求，使训练目标独立于分块方式。我们采用视觉-语言骨干网络进行训练，并发布9B和0.6B两种规模的pplx-embed-v2-context模型，具有兼容的嵌入空间。在评估方面，我们引入两个基准，分别标注回答查询的片段以及验证答案所需的证据。Context-bench是一个受控诊断基准，Q2D-Chunk则基于真实世界搜索查询构建。为鼓励进一步研究，我们还将Q2D-Chunk公开为公共排行榜。我们的9B模型在两个基准及ConTEB上均达到新的领先水平。我们发现，9B模型的视觉检索能力源自其视觉-语言基础模型，并建议将分块扩展至图像输入。我们证明，我们的模型在视觉ViDoRe-V3上作为密集检索器达到最先进性能，且随着图像分块粒度细化，性能进一步提升。

AtomTree: A Hierarchical Framework for State-Aware Embodied Instruction Following with LLMs
AtomTree：一种基于大语言模型的状态感知具身指令遵循分层框架
Embodied instruction following in interactive environments requires agents to interpret natural language commands and complete long-horizon tasks. Existing planners either rely on large labeled trajectories, or use few-shot and zero-shot LLM prompting that produces a whole plan in a single serial reasoning process. These approaches depend on linguistic common sense, commit to a plan from the initial instruction, and only update after failures, which weakens robustness and state awareness. We present AtomTree, a hierarchical framework for state-aware task planning with LLMs. AtomTree incrementally builds a tree of subgoals and actions, where the LLM decides at each step, from the current symbolic state, whether to further decompose or to output a grounded low-level action. A lightweight memory module summarizes past outcomes for reuse in later decisions. When a low-level action stalls, AtomTree triggers hierarchical backtracking replanning to return to the parent node and regenerate the subtree plan from the latest state, preventing continued drift along an incorrect trajectory. In zero-shot evaluation on the ALFRED benchmark, AtomTree outperforms prior LLM-based planners. Project page: https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/.
中文翻译
在交互式环境中进行具身指令遵循需要智能体理解自然语言指令并完成长时程任务。现有规划方法要么依赖大量标注轨迹，要么使用少样本和零样本的大语言模型提示，以单一串行推理过程生成完整计划。这些方法依赖语言常识，从初始指令即确定计划，仅在失败后更新，削弱了鲁棒性和状态感知能力。我们提出AtomTree，一种基于大语言模型的状态感知任务规划分层框架。AtomTree逐步构建子目标与动作的树状结构，大语言模型在每一步根据当前符号化状态决定是否进一步分解或输出一个具体低层动作。一个轻量级记忆模块总结过往结果以供后续决策复用。当低层动作停滞时，AtomTree触发分层回溯重规划，返回父节点并基于最新状态重新生成子树计划，防止沿错误轨迹持续漂移。在ALFRED基准的零样本评估中，AtomTree优于先前基于大语言模型的规划器。项目页面：https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/
👤 Haotian Zhai, Tianming Sha, Junnan Li 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38691]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Beyond fabrication: Misattributed numbers in papers written by AI research agents
超越虚构：AI研究代理撰写的论文中的误归因数值问题
Verifying numerical claims in research written by AI agents is an important but challenging problem. Recent benchmarks show that many agent-written papers fail integrity checks, but such verdicts, often produced by a single LLM judge, identify failure without explaining its nature, and existing checks either test a paper for internal consistency or test whether a reported value appears somewhere in an agent's output. We re-analyze all 540 released judgments from a recent public benchmark of AI research agents (nine agents, fifteen open research problems) and manually label 256 numerical claims from 18 submissions. Failures concentrate on alignment between the paper and the underlying work rather than overt cheating, and about four in five unsupported claims correspond to values in the agent's own files that come from the wrong experiment, metric, or run. We introduce a deterministic claim-to-evidence checker that scores whether the surrounding context identifies the claimed result and calibrates each claim against numerical decoys. With its rules fixed before labeling, it certifies claims with over 90% precision against blinded annotators (78 of 85; Cohen's kappa = 0.89), while verifying only a subset of claims; most claims it cannot verify are nevertheless traceable, so failure to verify is not evidence of falsehood. An LLM verifier given the same evidence recovers more traceable claims but accepts about three times as many misattributed ones, so the two are best used together. Applied to 99 submissions, the checker reveals large differences in traceability across agents, and a second judge from a different vendor preserves the official agent ranking while passing twice as many papers. These findings motivate provenance-first auditing of AI-produced research.
中文翻译
验证由AI代理撰写的科研论文中的数值声明是一项重要但具有挑战性的问题。近期基准测试显示，许多代理撰写的论文未能通过完整性检查，但此类判断通常由单一大语言模型裁判产生，仅指出失败而未说明原因；现有检查方法要么测试论文内部一致性，要么检测报告值是否出现在代理输出的某处。我们重新分析了最近公开基准中540个已发布判断（九个代理，十五个开放研究问题），并手动标注了18份提交中的256个数值声明。失败主要集中在论文与底层工作之间的对齐问题，而非明显造假；约五分之四的无支持声明对应于代理自身文件中来自错误实验、度量或运行的数值。我们引入一种确定性的声明-证据核查器，通过判断上下文是否明确标识所声称的结果，并将每个声明与数值干扰项进行校准。在标签前固定规则的情况下，该核查器对盲评标注者实现了超过90%的精确率（85个中78个正确；Cohen's kappa = 0.89），同时仅验证部分声明；大多数无法验证的声明仍可追溯，因此无法验证不等于虚假。给予相同证据的大语言模型验证器能发现更多可追溯声明，但接受约三倍数量的误归因声明，因此两者结合使用效果最佳。应用于99份提交，核查器揭示了不同代理间可追溯性差异显著，且另一供应商的第二位裁判在保持官方代理排名的同时，通过了两倍数量的论文。这些发现推动了以溯源为先的AI生成研究审计范式。

AskStream: When and What to Ask Proactively under Uncertainty in Streaming Video Understanding
AskStream：在流式视频理解中不确定性下的主动提问时机与内容选择
Recent advances in multimodal large language models have increased interest in interaction paradigms for video understanding. The evolution from offline to streaming and proactive interaction has gradually shifted models from passive responders toward more proactive agents that can decide when to answer. However, existing proactive paradigms typically assume that the information required to answer a query is complete, leaving models to only wait or answer. In the real world, however, information is often incomplete due to uncertainty from either the video or the user. In proactive paradigms, such uncertainty can cause the model to wait forever or generate highly hallucinatory responses. We therefore argue that proactive interaction should be redefined with a third action: ask, allowing models to actively ask for missing information. To study when and what models should ask before answering, we introduce AskStream, the first multi-turn benchmark spanning video and user uncertainty for streaming video understanding, with four task categories and eight subtasks. It contains 438 videos with 2,714 manually annotated frame-level time windows across three types: wait, ask, and answer. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended settings, even the strongest model attains only 52.18% on our proposed Ask-F1 metric, highlighting substantial limitations in proactive information seeking under uncertainty.
中文翻译
多模态大语言模型的最新进展提升了人们对视频理解中交互范式的兴趣。从离线到流式及主动交互的演变，逐渐使模型从被动响应者转变为更主动的智能体，能够自主决定何时回答。然而，现有主动范式通常假设回答所需信息是完整的，导致模型只能等待或作答。在现实世界中，由于视频或用户带来的不确定性，信息往往不完整。在主动范式下，这种不确定性可能导致模型无限等待或生成高度幻觉的回应。因此，我们主张应重新定义主动交互，引入第三种动作：提问，使模型能够主动请求缺失信息。为研究模型应在何时以及如何提问后再作答，我们提出了AskStream，首个涵盖视频与用户不确定性的多轮流式视频理解基准，包含四类任务和八个子任务。该基准包含438段视频，共2,714个手动标注的帧级时间窗口，分为三种类型：等待、提问和回答。在多项选择和开放式设置下，即使最强模型在我们提出的Ask-F1指标上也仅达到52.18%，凸显了在不确定性下主动信息获取的重大局限。
👤 Lu Wang, Zhuoran Jin, Longxiang Wang, Junhong Chen, Yupu Hao et al. 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Cross-Primitive Consistency in Jev
Jev中的跨原语一致性
Jev enables efficient decision-making with large language models (LLMs) through typed querying primitives, allowing the same semantic state to be expressed in different formats, such as binary and multiple-choice queries. However, the consistency of decisions across primitives has not been systematically evaluated. This work introduces PrimaCons, a four-axis benchmark for assessing cross-primitive consistency while preserving the semantics of the query. Evaluations across three Jev sizes reveal consistency violations across all four axes. Our analysis shows that these violations follow systematic patterns and that cross-primitive discrepancies predict decision failure with a ROC-AUC of 0.91. To mitigate these inconsistencies, we propose the Cross-Primitive Residual Calibrator (CPRC), which attaches a lightweight residual network to a frozen backbone and jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and cross-primitive consistency. Under a leave-one-domain-out protocol, CPRC reduces cross-primitive inconsistency by 58.57% and improves accuracy by +2.97%., demonstrating its effectiveness in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings. Our findings establish cross-primitive consistency as a key dimension for evaluating Jev and provide a practical approach to improving the reliability of its decisions. Code and benchmark is available at https://github.com/67L1/PrimaCons-CRPR.
中文翻译
Jev通过类型化查询原语实现大语言模型（LLM）的高效决策，允许同一语义状态以不同格式表达，如二元和多选查询。然而，跨原语决策的一致性尚未得到系统评估。本文提出PrimaCons，一个四轴基准，用于在保持查询语义的前提下评估跨原语一致性。对三种Jev规模的评估显示，所有四个轴上均存在一致性违规。分析表明，这些违规具有系统性模式，且跨原语差异可预测决策失败（ROC-AUC达0.91）。为缓解此类不一致，我们提出跨原语残差校准器（CPRC），在冻结主干网络上附加轻量级残差网络，并联合优化预测准确性和跨原语一致性。在留一域排除协议下，CPRC将跨原语不一致性降低58.57%，同时提升准确率2.97%，证明其在域内和域外设置下的有效性。我们的发现确立了跨原语一致性作为评估Jev的关键维度，并提供了提升决策可靠性的实用方法。代码与基准已公开于https://github.com/67L1/PrimaCons-CRPR。

Repairs for Whom? Evaluator-Executor Mismatch in Selecting Repairs for Failed LLM Agents
修复为谁？LLM代理失败时评估者-执行者不匹配问题
When an LLM agent fails, repair methods choose which action to replace and what to put in its place. They often score candidate repairs with greedy replay, a stronger model, an environment oracle, or a judge that reads the failed log. We ask whether these evaluators pick repairs that help the agent that will actually execute them. Holding failed trajectories and candidate single-step repairs fixed, we label every candidate with independent rollouts of the deployed policy and measure how much deployment value each evaluator's choice loses. On failures not screened for repair value, pre-specified tests show that greedy replay loses 0.08-0.13 in expected net success gain in ALFWorld and 0.06-0.13 in net reward in WebShop, relative to selection with the deployed agent's own rollouts. At an equal rollout budget, selecting repairs for Qwen2.5-7B with Qwen2.5-14B rollouts loses 0.25 in ALFWorld and 0.07 in WebShop, twenty 14B rollouts per candidate are worth no more than two of the 7B agent's own, and fresh rollouts confirm that the two models prefer opposite repairs on selected disagreement cases. Between similar-sized models from different families, the losses appear in both directions in both environments (0.07-0.17). The scoring executor also changes repair-location labels and method rankings. Repairs should be verified with the executor that will run them, and repair labels should name their executor.
中文翻译
当LLM代理失败时，修复方法需决定替换哪个动作及其替代方案。现有方法通常使用贪婪重播、更强模型、环境真值或阅读失败日志的裁判来评分候选修复。我们质疑这些评估者是否选择了真正有助于实际执行代理的修复方案。在固定失败轨迹和候选单步修复的前提下，我们通过部署策略的独立回放标注每个候选，并测量评估者选择所导致的部署价值损失。在未筛选修复价值的失败案例中，预设测试显示，贪婪重播在ALFWorld中导致预期净成功收益损失0.08–0.13，在WebShop中净奖励损失0.06–0.13；在相同回放预算下，使用Qwen2.5-14B回放选择Qwen2.5-7B的修复，导致ALFWorld损失0.25，WebShop损失0.07，二十次14B回放的价值不超过两次7B代理自身的回放，且新回放证实两模型在选定分歧案例中偏好相反的修复。在来自不同家族的相似尺寸模型之间，损失在两个方向均出现（0.07–0.17）。评分执行者还改变了修复位置标签和方法排名。修复应由实际执行者验证，修复标签应明确其执行者。

Persona Learning from Feedback for Personalized AI Assistants
基于反馈的人格学习：个性化AI助手的构建
Personalizing AI assistants requires adapting their behavior to users' preferences and task contexts. Existing work elicits assistant personas through persona prompting or finetuning on persona-conditioned generations. However, persona descriptions alone do not identify what to change or preserve in a particular response, or which behaviors suit the current request. We introduce $\textit{Persona Learning from Feedback (PLF)}$, a framework that learns reusable, preference-grounded assistant personas as independent LoRA adapters on a shared frozen backbone. During training, a frozen self-teacher conditions on persona specifications and response-specific feedback to provide token-level supervision on each adapter's own generated prefixes. A feedback gate focuses corrective updates on responses judged to need improvement. At deployment, a supervised router uses the request or dialogue to select a single adapter or a fixed composition, without user-specific finetuning. We evaluate PLF on single-turn personalization with model-generated critiques and multi-turn tutoring with simulated students' reactions. Across both settings, PLF improves model-judged preference fit over fixed-persona policies and strong baselines including persona prompting and persona distillation, demonstrating how feedback-trained behaviors can be reused for adaptive assistant personalization without degrading general capabilities.
中文翻译
个性化AI助手需要根据用户偏好和任务上下文调整行为。现有工作通过人格提示或在人格条件生成上微调来获取助手人格。然而，仅有人格描述无法确定在特定回应中应修改或保留什么，也无法判断何种行为适合当前请求。我们提出《基于反馈的人格学习（PLF）》框架，将可复用、基于偏好的助手人格作为独立LoRA适配器，部署于共享冻结主干之上。训练期间，一个冻结的自教师基于人格规范和响应特定反馈，对每个适配器自身生成的前缀提供逐标记监督。反馈门控聚焦于被判定需要改进的响应进行修正更新。部署时，一个监督路由器根据请求或对话选择单个适配器或固定组合，无需用户特定微调。我们在单轮个性化（使用模型生成批评）和多轮辅导（模拟学生反应）两种场景下评估PLF。在两种设置中，PLF均优于固定人格策略和强基线（包括人格提示和人格蒸馏），展示了反馈训练行为如何被复用于自适应助手个性化，且不损害通用能力。
👤 Yiran Shen, Yu Xia, Liuyi Yao, Prithviraj Ammanabrolu 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Predict the Reward, Not the World: World Reward Models for ML Agents
预测奖励，而非世界：面向机器学习智能体的世界奖励模型
Search-based machine-learning (ML) agents improve a solution by writing candidate programs, executing them, and keeping the best. Execution gives the search reliable feedback, but a single run can take hours, and most runs end in programs the agent discards. Recent agents try to save these runs by letting a large language model act as a world model that predicts how each program would perform. However, a wrong prediction feeds the search false information. We argue that an agent does not need to predict the outcome of execution, only the decision that execution would support. We propose World Reward Models (WRMs), which learn from real execution results to choose directly which candidate will perform better, without predicting any intermediate outcome. We prove that a selector which predicts execution from the same inputs cannot outperform the best direct selector, that predicted outcomes add an error which can make a likelihood-trained world model prefer the worse program on every instance, and that cross-entropy on executed winners drives a WRM toward the optimum. Our WRM, ForeCritic, adapts a 9B typed decision model to 5k executed program pairs. On 12,438 held-out pairs from 26 ML tasks, it picks the better program 79.4% of the time, while general-purpose LLM judges reach 53--62%, and its probabilities are calibrated. Inside AIDE, ML-Master, and ForeAgent, it cuts mean task time by 4.4--7.5 times while keeping their mean scores within 0.01 or raising them.
中文翻译
基于搜索的机器学习（ML）智能体通过编写候选程序、执行并保留最优解来改进解决方案。执行提供可靠的反馈，但一次运行可能耗时数小时，且大多数运行最终被智能体丢弃。近期智能体尝试让大型语言模型充当世界模型，预测每个程序的表现以节省运行时间。然而，错误预测会向搜索过程传递虚假信息。我们主张，智能体无需预测执行结果，只需预测执行将支持的决策即可。为此提出世界奖励模型（WRMs），其从真实执行结果中学习，直接选择哪个候选程序表现更好，而不预测任何中间结果。我们证明：从相同输入预测执行结果的选择器无法优于最佳直接选择器；预测结果会引入误差，可能导致基于似然训练的世界模型在所有实例中偏好较差的程序；而对已执行胜出者的交叉熵损失能引导WRM趋向最优。我们的WRM模型ForeCritic将一个9B参数的类型化决策模型适配至5,000对已执行程序。在来自26个ML任务的12,438个保留样本对上，它以79.4%的准确率选出表现更好的程序，而通用LLM判断者仅达53%-62%，且其概率分布校准良好。在AIDE、ML-Master和ForeAgent中集成后，平均任务时间减少4.4至7.5倍，同时保持或略微提升平均得分。

Measured or Real? When Model-Derived Measurements Can Be Corrected or Compared
测量值还是真实值？何时可修正或比较模型生成的测量结果
Numbers computed from trained models, such as an example's difficulty or a model's benchmark lead, change with seed and prompt. We ask when such measurements can be corrected or compared. Noise shrinks correlations, and the textbook correction divides by the square root of two estimated reliabilities. On two CIFAR-100 difficulty scores, it returned correlations above one in 70 of 100 classes. We prove that its mean squared error is infinite even when the correlation and the reliabilities use separate seeds. Our rule corrects only when both reliabilities pass a significance test, then divides by their upper confidence bounds. Its output is always bounded, and its coverage guarantees assume Gaussian noise. It helps once reliability is well estimated, more than halving the error with 500 simulated units. Prompt settings can reverse rankings. On ARC, Qwen2-1.5B trails Llama-3.2-3B by 3.8 points with no examples in the prompt and leads by 16.6 with two. Across four benchmarks, averaging the two settings names one winner for 15 of 16 such reversals. We compare models within each setting, controlling family-wise error asymptotically in the number of items for a fixed prompt grid. Aligning embedding coordinates across seeds raises held-out per-dimension reliability from 0.003 to 0.813.
中文翻译
从训练好的模型中计算出的数值（如样本难度或模型在基准上的领先度）会随随机种子和提示内容变化。我们探讨了这些测量值何时可以被修正或进行比较。噪声会降低相关性，而教科书中的修正方法是除以两个估计可靠性平方根的乘积。在两个CIFAR-100难度评分上，该方法在100个类别中有70个返回的相关系数超过1。我们证明，即使相关性和可靠性使用独立种子，其均方误差仍为无穷大。我们的规则仅在两个可靠性均通过显著性检验时才进行修正，然后除以其置信区间的上限。该方法输出始终有界，其覆盖率假设噪声服从高斯分布。当可靠性估计充分时，该方法效果显著，使500个模拟单元的误差减少超过一半。提示设置可逆转排名：在ARC任务中，Qwen2-1.5B在提示中无示例时落后于Llama-3.2-3B 3.8分，而在加入两个示例后则领先16.6分。在四个基准上，对两种设置取平均后，在16次排名反转中有15次得出同一胜者。我们控制固定提示网格下项目数量的族系错误率，对每种设置内模型进行比较。通过跨种子对齐嵌入坐标，可将保留维度的可靠性从0.003提升至0.813。
👤 Yifeng Li, Ziang Ni 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

SkillMem: Compact External Skill Memory for Latent Multi-Agent Reasoning
SkillMem：用于潜在多智能体推理的紧凑外部技能记忆
Latent multi-agent systems reduce inference cost by keeping intermediate reasoning and inter-agent communication in continuous representations.\ Reusable skills, however, are commonly supplied as text or stored as layer-wise Key-Value states or skill-specific model parameters, introducing repeated processing or substantial per-skill storage.\ We introduce \textbf{SkillMem}, which represents each reusable skill as a small external latent memory and makes it directly available during latent multi-agent reasoning.\ A role-conditioned cross-attention reader allows the same memory to guide different agents according to their roles and current hidden states.\ We train the skill memories and shared reader through behavioral distillation, matching the change in token predictions induced by a textual skill while keeping the backbone frozen.\ For our experiments, we obtain textual skills from paired successful and failed multi-agent trajectories, although existing skills can also be compiled directly.\ Across six benchmarks and Qwen3 models ranging from 4B to 14B parameters, SkillMem provides strong gains for smaller backbones while preserving efficient latent multi-agent inference across model scales.\ We use the same $8\times256$ memory configuration across all evaluated model scales, requiring 9.6\,KB of skill-specific storage per skill, including its retrieval embedding.\ For a library of 100 skills, SkillMem reduces total storage by more than $38\times$ compared with alternative skill representations and achieves up to a $4.23\times$ end-to-end inference speedup.\ These results show that compact procedural memories can be reused without sacrificing the efficiency of latent multi-agent reasoning.
中文翻译
潜在多智能体系统通过将中间推理和智能体间通信保留在连续表示中来降低推理成本。然而，可复用的技能通常以文本形式提供，或作为逐层键值状态、或特定技能的模型参数存储，导致重复处理或大量每技能存储开销。我们提出 extbf{SkillMem}，将每个可复用技能表示为小型外部潜在记忆，并在潜在多智能体推理过程中直接可用。一种角色条件交叉注意力读取器允许同一记忆根据智能体的角色和当前隐藏状态引导不同智能体。我们通过行为蒸馏训练技能记忆和共享读取器，匹配文本技能引入的标记预测变化，同时保持主干网络冻结。实验中，我们从成对的成功与失败多智能体轨迹中获取文本技能，也可直接编译现有技能。在六个基准测试和参数量从4B到14B的Qwen3模型上，SkillMem在较小主干模型上表现优异，同时在不同模型规模下保持高效的潜在多智能体推理。我们在所有评估模型规模上使用相同的$8 imes256$内存配置，每项技能仅需9.6KB的专用存储（含检索嵌入）。对于包含100项技能的库，SkillMem相比其他技能表示方式将总存储减少超过38倍，并实现最高达4.23倍的端到端推理加速。结果表明，紧凑的过程记忆可在不牺牲潜在多智能体推理效率的前提下被有效复用。
👤 Yuxuan Zhu, Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri, Pin-Yu Chen, Keerthiram Murugesan 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37903]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Think Twice: The Metis-1.6 Technical Report
再思：Metis-1.6技术报告
Metis is a bet that better data, better training methods and new architectures can buy what a bigger parameter count cannot, at a fraction of the cost. Metis-1.6 is its latest step: two language models built from scratch by a single researcher, Praxis (2.94B parameters, 0.46B active per pass) and Logos (10.23B parameters, 1.18B active per pass). Both run a hybrid Mamba-2 and attention mixture-of-experts stack twice for every token, so each token gets a second look. Each was trained on 1.02T tokens in total: 804.76B of pretraining, 18B of context extension to 131,072 tokens, and 200B of supervised fine-tuning. Metis-1.6 was never meant to challenge frontier models, and across 40 post-trained benchmarks it mostly does not: Logos averages 52.93, seventh of 17 models in its comparison set and within a point of Qwen3-4B. But it gets there on roughly a twelfth of the median training data of the comparators that disclose it, and with fewer active parameters per token than any model that beats it, which puts it on the Pareto frontier for both. In mathematics it ranks second of 30 models, with 67.50% on AIME 2025 and 63.64% on HMMT February 2026. It is weak at code, instruction following and long context. The rest of the report is the part I found most valuable: what went wrong. The optimizer I configured never ran, and attention trained with AdamW for all of pretraining. A 1.77B-parameter n-gram memory learned to switch itself off. Five fine-tuning choices cost capability. And a length reward under group-mean normalization punished correct solutions for being long once a group's pass rate passed 2/3, a sign inversion I prove and fix with a one-line clamp. I close with the 10B-token ablations behind MoRE (Mixture of Recursive Experts), the looped architecture of Metis-1.7.
中文翻译
Metis是一种信念：更好的数据、更优的训练方法和新型架构可以在远低于大参数量的成本下实现原本需要更大参数才能达成的效果。Metis-1.6是其最新进展：由单个研究人员Praxis从头构建的两个语言模型——Praxis（29.4亿参数，每轮激活0.46亿）和Logos（102.3亿参数，每轮激活11.8亿）。两者每token执行两次混合Mamba-2与注意力的专家混合堆栈，实现二次审视。每个模型总共训练1.02万亿token：8047.6亿用于预训练，180亿用于上下文扩展至131,072 token，2000亿用于监督微调。Metis-1.6本非为挑战前沿模型而设计，在40个微调后基准测试中表现平平：Logos平均得分为52.93，17个对比模型中排名第7，与Qwen3-4B相差不到1分。但其训练数据仅为对比模型中位数的十二分之一，且每token活跃参数少于所有击败它的模型，使其在两项指标上均位于帕累托前沿。在数学任务中，它位列30个模型中的第二，AIME 2025得分为67.50%，HMMT February 2026得分为63.64%。其在代码生成、指令遵循和长上下文处理方面表现较弱。报告其余部分是我认为最有价值的部分：失败原因分析。我配置的优化器从未运行，整个预训练阶段注意力模块均使用AdamW训练。一个17.7亿参数的n元语法记忆模块学会自我关闭。五个微调选择导致能力损失。此外，长度奖励在组均值归一化下惩罚了较长的正确解，一旦组通过率超过2/3，便产生符号反转；我证明并用一行代码修复了此问题。最后，报告详述了支撑Metis-1.7循环架构的100亿token消融实验，即MoRE（递归专家混合）。

ThinkJev: One Thought, Every Budget for Calibrated Typed Decisions
ThinkJev：一次思考，适用于所有预算的校准型类型决策
Many uses of language models end in a typed decision: a choice among a closed set of options, whose probabilities code can act on. Reading those probabilities from one forward pass is fast but often wrong on decisions that take several steps, such as arithmetic, counting, and date comparison. Letting the model think first fixes most of these errors, but costs seconds per decision instead of milliseconds. To pay for thinking only where it helps, ThinkJev builds a fast and a slow system from one open hybrid-thinking model that can answer directly or after a thought. System 1 reads the model's probabilities for the option letters when it answers directly; System 2 reads the same letters after the model has thought on the same cached prompt, so escalating repeats no work and both systems give probabilities over the same options. Because the letters can be read after any number of thought tokens, one sampled thought also gives the answer under every thinking budget. Without training, ThinkJev lets System 1 answer alone only where a statistical test on labeled dev data guarantees that its accepted answers stay under a target error rate. At a 5% target, Qwen3.5-27B answers 24.5% of 8359 test decisions this way with a realized error of 3.9% (Qwen3.5-4B and 9B: 6.5% and 0.0%). For the rest, stopping each thought as soon as its answer is confident reaches 81.6% accuracy, against 82.7% for always thinking, with 72% of the thought tokens. Choosing when to think from System 1's confidence alone saves less, because the decisions System 1 is least sure of are often not the ones thinking fixes. On the public items of JevBench, an independent benchmark of typed-decision services, Qwen3.5-27B thinks on 29.4% and is not distinguishable in accuracy from the two thinking APIs listed (exploratory). Code and data generators are available at https://github.com/ding-xiaolu/ThinkJev.
中文翻译
许多语言模型的应用最终都以类型化决策收尾：从一组封闭选项中做出选择，其概率可被直接行动所利用。仅通过一次前向传播读取这些概率虽然快速，但在涉及多步推理的任务（如算术、计数和日期比较）中往往出错。让模型先进行思考可以修复大多数此类错误，但代价是每次决策需耗时数秒而非毫秒。为了仅在真正有益时才付出思考成本，ThinkJev构建了一个由单一开放混合思考模型组成的快速系统和慢速系统，该模型可直接回答或在思考后回答。系统1在直接回答时读取模型对选项字母的概率；系统2则在模型对同一缓存提示进行思考后读取相同的字母，因此升级过程不重复工作，两个系统对相同选项给出概率。由于字母可在任意数量的思考标记后读取，一次采样的思考即可覆盖所有思考预算下的答案。无需训练，ThinkJev仅允许系统1单独回答那些在标注开发数据上通过统计检验保证其接受答案误差率低于目标值的情况。在5%的目标误差率下，Qwen3.5-27B以这种方式回答了8359个测试决策中的24.5%，实际误差率为3.9%（Qwen3.5-4B和9B分别为6.5%和0.0%）。对于其余部分，只要在每次思考中一旦答案足够自信就停止，即可达到81.6%的准确率，相比始终思考的82.7%准确率，仅消耗72%的思考标记。仅根据系统1的置信度决定何时思考，效果较差，因为系统1最不确定的决策往往并非思考能修复的。在JevBench公开项目（一个类型化决策服务的独立基准）上，Qwen3.5-27B在29.4%的情况下选择思考，其准确率与列出的两个思考API无显著差异（探索性结果）。代码和数据生成器可在https://github.com/ding-xiaolu/ThinkJev获取。
👤 Xiaolu Ding, Kai Yang, Dongdong Liu 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

Relevance-Guided Residual Calibration for Spectral Compression of Dense Retrieval Embeddings
相关性引导的残差校准用于密集检索嵌入的谱压缩
Spectral compression reduces the storage and scoring costs of dense retrieval by projecting embeddings onto a smaller principal subspace. However, its corpus-derived weighting rules tie all retained directions to a shared spectral trend, without directly accounting for their individual contributions to relevance. To address this, we propose Relevance-Guided Residual Calibration (RRC), a closed-form method for refining spectral weights using relevance judgments. RRC estimates margin-based weights from the means and variances of positive--negative score contributions along each retained direction. It retains directions with positive estimated weights, decomposes their log weights into a fitted power-law trend and a residual, and adds the residual to the compressor's log weights on this support. This allows relevance judgments to adjust individual directions while preserving the compressor's overall spectral trend. RRC applies to PCA, Whitening, and SpecTemp, and incorporates the corrections into the existing transformation shared by documents and queries, without iterative model training or an additional inference stage. Experiments with three encoders on nine datasets covering conventional and reasoning-intensive retrieval show that SpecTemp + RRC achieves the highest mean effectiveness among tested compressors on the BEIR group and BRIGHT at every target dimension. On the five evaluated BRIGHT datasets, it improves over SpecTemp by an average of 2.34 nDCG@10 points at 768 dimensions. The gains also persist under scalar and product quantization, improving the trade-off between per-document storage and retrieval effectiveness. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rrc-spectral-compression-B5CE/
中文翻译
谱压缩通过将嵌入投影到更小的主子空间来降低密集检索的存储和评分成本。然而，其基于语料库的加权规则将所有保留方向绑定到共享的谱趋势上，未直接考虑各方向对相关性的个体贡献。为此，我们提出相关性引导的残差校准（RRC），一种基于相关性判断的闭式方法，用于优化谱权重。RRC从每个保留方向上正负样本得分贡献的均值和方差中估计基于边距的权重。它保留估计权重为正的方向，将其对数权重分解为拟合的幂律趋势和残差，并将残差添加到压缩器在该支持集上的对数权重中。这使得相关性判断能够调整各个方向，同时保持压缩器的整体谱趋势。RRC适用于PCA、去相关化和SpecTemp，并将修正整合到文档和查询共用的现有变换中，无需迭代模型训练或额外推理阶段。在三个编码器和九个涵盖常规与推理密集型检索的数据集上的实验表明，SpecTemp + RRC在BEIR组和BRIGHT的所有目标维度上均取得最高的平均有效性。在五个评估的BRIGHT数据集上，其在768维下平均优于SpecTemp 2.34 nDCG@10分。该增益在标量和乘积量化下依然存在，改善了单文档存储与检索有效性之间的权衡。代码可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rrc-spectral-compression-B5CE/获取。
👤 Yongkang Li, Panagiotis Eustratiadis, Evangelos Kanoulas 📅 2026-10-26 🔗 arXiv

DuQUAD: A Dual-View Framework with Quality-Aware Evidence Pruning for Multi-Document Question Answering
DuQUAD：一种带有质量感知证据剪枝的双视角框架用于多文档问答
Large language models with retrieval-augmented generation systematically underutilize evidence in the middle of multi-document contexts, a phenomenon known as Lost in the Middle. Existing mitigation strategies, such as document reordering and attention steering, can reduce boundary bias but remain brittle in noisy settings, where inaccurate relevance estimation and spurious documents still obscure useful evidence in central positions. We propose DuQUAD, a multi-document QA framework that mitigates positional bias through dual-view reasoning and quality-aware evidence pruning. DuQUAD combines a Local Agent guided by a Structural Fusion Score with a Global Agent that reasons over the full document set, providing both targeted evidence recovery and broad contextual understanding. The framework further filters candidate evidence using explicit quality scoring and refines it at the sentence level to reduce noise. DuQUAD consistently outperforms strong Lost-in-the-Middle mitigation baselines, including hybrid retrieval and reranking, multi-agent reasoning, and context optimization methods, improving accuracy by 3.0% on average over the strongest competing baseline and gold evidence recall by 9.4%, while substantially reducing computational cost compared to existing multi-agent frameworks.
中文翻译
具备检索增强生成的大语言模型在处理多文档上下文时系统性地低估中间位置的证据，这一现象被称为“迷失在中间”。现有的缓解策略，如文档重排和注意力引导，虽可减少边界偏差，但在噪声环境中仍显脆弱，因为不准确的相关性估计和虚假文档仍会掩盖中心位置的有用证据。我们提出DuQUAD，一种多文档问答框架，通过双视角推理和质量感知证据剪枝来缓解位置偏差。DuQUAD结合了由结构融合得分引导的局部代理与对整个文档集进行推理的全局代理，既实现针对性的证据恢复，又提供广泛的上下文理解。该框架进一步通过显式的质量评分过滤候选证据，并在句子层面进行精炼以减少噪声。DuQUAD持续优于强基线的“迷失在中间”缓解方法，包括混合检索与重排序、多代理推理和上下文优化方法，在最强竞争基线上平均提升准确率3.0%，黄金证据召回率提升9.4%，同时相比现有多代理框架显著降低了计算成本。
👤 Yeonjun Choi, Seyeon Jeong, Jong Wook Kim, Beakcheol Jang 📅 2026-10-25 🔗 arXiv

Elucidating the Design Space of LM Data Attribution
揭示大语言模型数据归属的设计空间
Data attribution methods for Language Models (LMs) are typically evaluated using downstream tasks like fact retrieval. However, existing evaluations vary multiple dataset properties simultaneously, making it impossible to isolate exactly why an attribution algorithm fails. To diagnose these failures, we explore the design space of LLM data attribution, with a primary focus on evaluation. We introduce the Controlled Attribution Benchmark (CATT) for fact tracing, in which we fine-tune LLMs on entirely fictitious knowledge, providing unambiguous ground truth for attribution. This synthetic setup allows us to systematically alter one dataset configuration at a time, such as document redundancy and lexical distractors. With this configurable evaluation, we demonstrate that redundancy and distractors consistently degrade attribution performance. Rather than proposing a new estimator, we examine two design choices that are underexplored: the loss used to compute gradients and how per-token attribution scores are aggregated into a document score. We find that the true causal evidence within a training document is highly localized, but standard methods dilute this evidence into noise by averaging attribution scores across the entire document. We find that replacing standard cross-entropy loss gradients with the negative log-odds gradients surfaces the critical tokens from other uninformative ones. We can then aggregate only the top token scores to improve ground truth recall by 35 to 72 percentage points across multiple baselines on our diagnostic benchmark. The negative log-odds correction also generalizes to FTRACE-TREX, improving counterfactual retraining performance for every method we test, by up to 4.9x.
中文翻译
语言模型（LM）的数据归属方法通常通过事实检索等下游任务进行评估。然而，现有评估同时改变多个数据集属性，无法准确判断归属算法失败的原因。为诊断这些失败，我们探索了大语言模型数据归属的设计空间，重点关注评估方法。我们提出了用于事实追踪的可控归属基准（CATT），在该基准中，我们在完全虚构的知识上微调大语言模型，为归属提供明确的真实标签。这种合成设置使我们能够系统性地逐个改变数据集配置，如文档冗余和词汇干扰项。通过这一可配置的评估，我们证明冗余和干扰项会持续降低归属性能。我们并未提出新的估计器，而是考察了两个被忽视的设计选择：用于计算梯度的损失函数，以及如何将每个词的归属分数聚合为文档分数。我们发现训练文档中的真实因果证据高度集中，但标准方法通过平均整个文档的归属分数，将这些证据稀释为噪声。我们发现，用负对数几率梯度替代标准交叉熵梯度，能够凸显关键词并抑制无信息词。随后仅聚合最高得分的词，可在我们的诊断基准上将真实标签召回率提升35至72个百分点。负对数几率修正还推广到FTRACE-TREX，使所有测试方法的反事实重训练性能提升最多达4.9倍。

ShallowDefend: Harnessing Shallow Safety Alignment for Multi-Turn Defender
ShallowDefend：利用浅层安全对齐实现多轮防御者
Safety alignment has enabled large language models to reliably refuse harmful requests directly, yet the same request distributed across a multi-turn conversation still succeeds far too often. This brittleness has a mechanistic root: Alignment is shallow, with safety behavior concentrated in a model's first few output tokens, and existing defenses inherit the weakness, supervising the surface form of the response without ever giving the model an explicit safety decision to learn. We take the opposite view of shallowness: if a short span determines the trajectory of an entire response, that span is the highest-leverage place to intervene, and the defender can occupy it first. We propose Assess $\to$ Decide $\to$ Respond, a framework in which the defender assesses the user's intent, commits to an explicit, parseable safety decision, and only then generates a reply conditioned on it, with the commitment trained by reinforcement learning at the payload turn. Because the decision is a token span, the reward can be a rule: a self-verified reward checks the committed label against the conversation's known nature, requiring no judge and no annotation, while the same format also supports an LLM-as-judge reward over a graded decision space with safe redirection. Across two model families and fourteen benchmarks spanning safety, utility, and helpfulness, our defenders achieve the strongest multi-turn safety in a controlled comparison, maintaining their lead in safety while preserving utility at a modest cost to helpfulness. A per-token KL analysis and interventional probes confirm why this works: training concentrates its effect on the brief deliberation that sets a response's trajectory, and the decision committed there is a causal control point. The very shallowness attackers exploit becomes the mechanism of defense.
中文翻译
安全对齐已使大型语言模型能够可靠地拒绝有害请求，但同样的请求若分散在多轮对话中仍经常成功。这种脆弱性具有机制根源：对齐是浅层的，安全行为集中在模型输出的前几个词元，而现有防御方法继承了这一弱点，仅监督响应的表面形式，从未让模型学习明确的安全决策。我们从相反角度看待浅层性：如果一个短片段决定了整个响应的轨迹，那么这个片段就是干预的最高杠杆点，防御者应优先占据它。我们提出Assess → Decide → Respond框架，其中防御者评估用户意图，做出明确且可解析的安全决策，然后才生成基于该决策的回复，且该决策在负载轮次通过强化学习训练。由于决策是一个词元片段，奖励可以是规则：自验证奖励检查所承诺的标签与对话已知性质的一致性，无需人工评判或标注；同一格式也支持基于分级决策空间的安全引导的LLM作为评判者奖励。在两个模型家族和十四项涵盖安全、效用和助人性的基准中，我们的防御者在受控比较中实现了最强的多轮安全性，在保持安全领先的同时，以适度牺牲助人性为代价维持了效用。每词元KL分析和干预探针证实其有效原因：训练效果集中在决定响应轨迹的短暂思辨阶段，而该阶段的决策是因果控制点。攻击者利用的浅层性反而成为防御机制。
👤 Qiyao Ma, Xiaodong Liu, Weiwei Yang, Mingqian Feng, Chengquan Guo et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

When to Assist and What to Suggest: Joint Gating and Memory-Grounded Hint Generation in One Compact Decoder
何时协助与建议什么：在单一紧凑解码器中联合门控与记忆锚定提示生成
Proactive assistants offer short, memory-grounded hints during a conversation, deciding when to assist and what to suggest. Current systems split this between a gate and a larger generator; we evaluate both together on 28,058 decision points. Stage-wise generation scores are oracle-gate ceilings: behind a real gate, end-to-end exact match is 0.2619 against a ceiling of 0.2971, and end-to-end precision is 0.2387, leaving substantial headroom. A gate pays off through precision and compute, whose saving falls from 4.39x with an 8B generator to 1.38x with a 1B one. The memory profile drives hint quality: removing it costs 24.7 exact-match points (rescaled BERTScore 0.57 to 0.35), while retrieval and a larger generator add nothing on exact match. One 1B decoder can do both, gating from an intermediate layer and generating in the same pass. At its final checkpoint it matches or exceeds the two-model pipelines on automatic end-to-end measures with 1.31-1.76x less wall-clock time than the matched 1B pipeline; across three seeds, its deeper exit ties the separate gate. A human evaluation (1,144 ratings over 32 dialogues) supports the memory effect and rates the single model level with the matched 1B pipeline and below the 8B one. Graded metrics and LlamaPIE's native judge track human ratings more closely than exact match, which we report as a strict lower bound.

主动助手在对话中提供简短、基于记忆的提示，决定何时协助及建议内容。当前系统将此任务拆分为门控模块和更大的生成模块；我们在28,058个决策点上共同评估两者。分阶段生成的得分是门控的上限：在真实门控后，端到端精确匹配得分为0.2619，而上限为0.2971，端到端精度为0.2387，仍有较大提升空间。门控通过提高精度和节省计算资源带来收益，其节省程度从8B生成器的4.39倍降至1B生成器的1.38倍。记忆特征驱动提示质量：移除记忆导致精确匹配下降24.7分（重标定BERTScore从0.57降至0.35），而检索和更大生成器在精确匹配上未带来增益。一个1B解码器可同时完成门控与生成，从中间层进行门控并在同一遍中生成。在其最终检查点，自动端到端指标上达到或超过双模型流水线，且比匹配的1B流水线快1.31至1.76倍；在三个种子下，其更深退出点与独立门控相当。人类评估（1,144条评分，覆盖32段对话）支持记忆效应，评分水平与匹配的1B流水线相当，低于8B流水线。分级指标和LlamaPIE原生评判者比精确匹配更贴近人类评分，因此我们将精确匹配视为严格下限。
👤 A. J. A. Jubayer Talukder, Mohammad Ariful Haque 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

agentic-rl
Multi-View MoE: Letting Every Router Shape Expert Computation
多视角MoE：让每个路由器塑造专家计算
Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) models expand parameter capacity by activating a small subset of experts per token. Expert selection directly affects model capabilities under a fixed active-expert budget. Dense evaluation of all experts reveals weak agreement between single-router rankings and expert-utility evaluation rankings, especially in middle and deep layers. Richer signals for selection and computation incur additional cost: selection based on internal expert activations requires preliminary computation, while processing multiple full representations separately through the same expert repeats its computation. We propose Multi-View MoE to improve expert selection with multiple routers and reuse their preferences to modulate expert computation under a fixed expert budget. Its Multi-Router submodule aggregates sigmoid scores from independently initialized routers. These routers develop highly differentiated expert preferences during training, allowing experts strongly favored by one router or jointly supported by several routers to be selected. Multi-View further uses each selected expert's relative scores across routers to mix learned latent views into its input, reusing routing preferences for both selection and computation. Each selected expert executes one forward pass, preserving the active-expert count, expert FLOPs, and all-to-all communication volume. On a 24-layer 10B-A1B model trained on 708B tokens, full Multi-View MoE improves the nine-task downstream mean by 1.18 percentage points over the baseline. Multi-Router alone yields a 0.6-percentage-point gain and improves the rank correlation between router scores and evaluated expert utility, with relative increases of 8.1% globally and approximately 58% in the layer with the lowest single-router alignment. Controlled mixing interventions show the model uses input differences that depend on both the expert and the token.
中文翻译
稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型通过每token激活少量专家来扩展参数容量。专家选择直接影响在固定活跃专家预算下的模型能力。对所有专家进行密集评估显示，单路由器排名与专家效用评估排名之间存在弱一致性，尤其在中层和深层更为明显。更丰富的选择与计算信号带来额外成本：基于内部专家激活的选择需要预计算，而通过同一专家分别处理多个完整表示会重复其计算。我们提出多视角MoE，通过多个路由器改进专家选择，并利用其偏好在固定专家预算下调节专家计算。其多路由器子模块聚合来自独立初始化路由器的sigmoid得分。这些路由器在训练过程中发展出高度差异化的专家偏好，使得被某一路由器强烈青睐或被多个路由器共同支持的专家得以被选中。多视角进一步利用各选定专家在不同路由器间的相对得分，将学习到的潜在视图混合到其输入中，复用路由偏好用于选择和计算。每个选定专家执行一次前向传播，保持活跃专家数量、专家FLOPs和全对全通信量不变。在一个24层10B-A1B模型上，使用708B令牌训练，完整的多视角MoE相比基线在九项下游任务上的平均性能提升1.18个百分点。仅多路由器部分即带来0.6个百分点的提升，并改善了路由器得分与评估专家效用之间的等级相关性，全局提升8.1%，在单路由器对齐最低的层中提升约58%。受控混合干预显示，模型利用了依赖于专家和token的输入差异。

PIVOT: Progressive Intervention via Online Teacher Correction
PIVOT：通过在线教师修正进行渐进式干预
Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is limited by sparse outcome-level feedback on hard reasoning tasks: when all rollouts in a group fail, rewards provide no learning signal. Stronger teachers can provide additional guidance, but this raises a central question: how can such signals be incorporated into RLVR to support successful rollouts while remaining learnable for the student? We distinguish two complementary mechanisms: trajectory-level intervention, which improves access to reward-bearing trajectories, and token-level supervision, which provides dense targets to guide student learning. Our controlled experiments show that either mechanism alone can remain insufficient, motivating their combination. We therefore introduce PIVOT, which inserts teacher-generated corrections into student rollouts and applies token-level KL supervision to these segments alongside the RLVR objective. Beyond their combination, we systematically analyze where to intervene and how to supervise, yielding a recipe for effective guidance. Across hard logic games and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, PIVOT consistently outperforms existing methods, nearly doubling mean avg@8 on logic games from 19.5% for the best-performing baseline to 35.6%.
中文翻译
基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在困难推理任务中受限于稀疏的结果级反馈：当一组中的所有轨迹均失败时，奖励无法提供学习信号。更强的教师可以提供额外指导，但核心问题在于如何将这些信号融入RLVR，以支持成功轨迹的同时仍保持学生模型的可学习性。我们区分了两种互补机制：轨迹级干预，提升对带奖励轨迹的访问；以及标记级监督，为学生学习提供密集目标。受控实验表明，单独使用任一机制可能不足，因此需要结合。为此，我们提出PIVOT，该方法在学生轨迹中插入教师生成的修正，并对这些片段应用标记级KL监督，同时结合RLVR目标。除了两者的结合，我们系统分析了干预位置与监督方式，得出有效的指导方案。在硬逻辑游戏和数学推理基准测试中，PIVOT持续优于现有方法，逻辑游戏的平均@8得分从最佳基线的19.5%提升至35.6%，几乎翻倍。
👤 Zhihao Yang, Boyuan Zheng, Puhui Tan, Yukun Chen, Kuan Zhang et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Every Token Needs Its Own Teacher: Multi-teacher Adaptive On-policy Self-distillation
每个标记都需要自己的教师：多教师自适应在线自我蒸馏
On-policy Self-distillation (OPSD) has proven effective for post-training large language models by constructing a teacher conditioned on privileged references. However, existing approaches keep the teacher fixed either throughout training by consistently exposing it to the full reference, or within each response by adaptively varying partial reference exposure only across responses. In this paper, we argue that neither of them is sufficient. Instead, every token needs its own teacher, as the teacher preference can vary token by token within the same response. Through a teacher-intervention pilot study, we validate that the teacher preference differs across tokens, and reveal that a preferred teacher can be identified for each token through majority voting and KL-based selection. Motivated by these findings, we propose Multi-teacher Adaptive On-policy Self-Distillation (MTA-OPSD), the first token-level adaptive OPSD method. Our method constructs a multi-teacher pool consisting of candidate teachers, and enables every token to adaptively select its preferred teacher during training. Experiments with Qwen3 models on mathematical reasoning tasks show that MTA-OPSD consistently outperforms existing OPSD approaches with an average gain of up to $+5.9$ over the base model, highlighting the importance of adapting teacher supervision at the token level. The code is available at https://github.com/xingzhejun/MTA-OPSD.
中文翻译
在线自我蒸馏（OPSD）已被证明在后训练大语言模型方面有效，其通过构建依赖特权参考的教师模型实现。然而，现有方法要么在整个训练过程中固定教师（始终暴露于完整参考），要么仅在每次响应内通过自适应调整部分参考暴露来变化教师。本文认为这两种方式均不充分。相反，每个标记都应有其专属教师，因为同一响应内的教师偏好可能随标记变化。通过教师干预试点研究，我们验证了不同标记间教师偏好存在差异，并发现可通过多数投票和基于KL的选择识别每个标记的优选教师。基于此，我们提出多教师自适应在线自我蒸馏（MTA-OPSD），这是首个实现标记级自适应的OPSD方法。该方法构建包含候选教师的多教师池，使每个标记在训练中可自适应选择其偏好教师。在Qwen3模型上针对数学推理任务的实验表明，MTA-OPSD持续优于现有OPSD方法，平均性能提升达+5.9，凸显了在标记级别进行教师监督自适应的重要性。代码已公开于https://github.com/xingzhejun/MTA-OPSD。
👤 Yifu Luo, Chuang Ma, Zeyu Chen, Enkelejda Kasneci, Qi Chen et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

How I Aligned Your Model: Scaling Up Multilingual Safety Training with Templates
如何对齐你的模型：通过模板实现多语言安全训练的规模化扩展
Safety alignment for Large Language Models is heavily focused on English with limited multilingual representation, particularly damaging low-resource language performance. Related open training datasets are often limited in size or do not follow region-localized harmful topics to broaden the scope of alignment beyond cultures. We propose a scalable template-based augmentation pipeline to produce massive multilingual refusal and benign corpora, extracting safe templates from harmful prompts, translating them and then filling in the slots with culturally authentic entities, both harmful and benign. As a result, we release MSafeTemp, a validated corpus spanning 60 languages useful for multilingual safety alignment and evaluation. Using the dataset, we show that English-only evaluation misjudges multilingual safety: for several models, attacks that fail in English succeed once translated, while culturally localized variants expose further failures that translation alone misses. We also confirm that augmenting safety training data through templates allows for higher diversity and quality of alignment, resulting in improved safety performance beyond English. MSafeTemp and the respective pipeline are aimed at facilitating research and development of safer and more responsible models for less-represented cultures.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的安全对齐目前高度集中于英语，对多语言覆盖有限，尤其损害低资源语言的表现。相关开源训练数据集通常规模较小，或未涵盖区域化有害主题，导致对齐范围局限于特定文化。我们提出一种可扩展的基于模板的增强流程，用于生成大规模多语言拒绝与良性语料库，通过从有害提示中提取安全模板，翻译后用文化上真实的实体（包括有害与良性）填充槽位。由此，我们发布了MSafeTemp，一个经验证的跨60种语言的语料库，可用于多语言安全对齐与评估。利用该数据集，我们发现仅以英语评估会误判多语言安全性：对于多个模型，原本在英语中失败的攻击在翻译后成功，而文化本地化变体暴露出翻译本身无法捕捉的更多失败。我们还证实，通过模板增强安全训练数据可提升对齐的多样性与质量，从而在英语之外也实现更优的安全表现。MSafeTemp及其相应流程旨在促进对代表性不足文化更安全、更负责任模型的研究与开发。

rTaste: Do Research Agents Have Taste? Evaluating Early Stage Research Judgment
rTaste：研究智能体是否有‘品味’？评估早期阶段的研究判断力
Do research agents have taste, the judgment of which experiments are worth running before their outcomes are known? We give six frontier models seven recent NLP papers. Each model, working as a research agent alone or as a team, proposes minimum viable experiments (MVEs), forecasts whether each will pass a threshold it sets and ranks them. A separate executor then attempts every MVE, including those ranked last, and three model auditors judge the results. Of 415 MVEs, 194 are judged sound, valuable and new, but fewer than half of these (73) are also judged beyond the seed paper and non obvious. The agents' first choices match the auditors' most valued experiment more often than a random pick, but are not shown to improve on choosing the experiment written first. Their forecasts beat a base rate but are not measurably more accurate than those of a fresh instance of the same model given only the paper and registrations. Teams cost more without a clear overall improvement in proposal quality. The agents produce many worthwhile experiments, but the evidence that their rankings improve the choice of which to run remains limited. The evaluation harness and a dataset of all 415 MVEs, with their forecasts and audited outcomes, are available at https://github.com/ArtKanke/rTaste.
中文翻译
研究智能体是否具备‘品味’——即在实验结果未知前判断哪些实验值得开展的能力？我们向六个前沿模型展示了七篇近期NLP论文。每个模型作为独立的研究智能体或团队，提出最小可行实验（MVE），预测每个实验是否能通过其设定的阈值，并对它们进行排序。随后，一个独立执行器尝试执行所有MVE，包括排名最低的，三位模型审计员对结果进行评判。在415个MVE中，有194个被判定为合理、有价值且新颖，但其中仅有73个同时被认为超越了初始论文且非显而易见。智能体的首选项比随机选择更接近审计员最看重的实验，但并未显示出优于直接选择论文首提实验的效果。它们的预测表现优于基线，但其准确性并未显著优于仅提供论文和注册信息的新实例。团队协作成本更高，且提案质量无明显提升。智能体生成了许多有价值的实验，但证据表明其排序并未显著改善实验选择。评估工具包及包含所有415个MVE及其预测和审计结果的数据集已公开于：https://github.com/ArtKanke/rTaste。

Glance: The Generator Is Its Own Reranker
Glance：生成器即是其自身重排序器
Retrieval-augmented generation commonly serves separate models for reranking and answering, duplicating model residency and passage processing. We introduce Glance, which equips the generator with a lightweight decision adapter and pointer head to score candidate passages from a shared prefill. Short relevance probes branch from the cached context, producing one score per passage without separately prefilling each query-passage pair. A single resident 9B backbone supports generation, general decisions and reranking, with approximately 0.1 GiB of additional memory per decision skill. After 39 minutes of MS MARCO fine-tuning, Glance achieves nDCG@10 of 0.741 on SciFact and 0.345 on NFCorpus, compared with 0.721 and 0.326 for bge-reranker-v2-m3. On 300 HotpotQA questions, its selected context improves answer F1 by 0.045 over cross-encoder-selected context under the same base generator (paired bootstrap 95% CI [0.014, 0.076]). In a scaling study, reranking quality keeps rising up to 200 candidates (43k tokens) scored from one shared prefill, where it exceeds the cross-encoder by 0.058 nDCG@10, and a probe-only activated adapter lets generation continue from the original passage cache. These initial results demonstrate generator-based reranking with one resident backbone; evaluation currently covers two retrieval datasets, one QA dataset and one backbone family.

检索增强生成通常使用独立模型分别完成重排序和回答任务，导致模型驻留和段落处理的重复。我们提出Glance，该方法为生成器配备轻量级决策适配器和指针头，从共享预填充中评分候选段落。短相关性探测从缓存上下文中分支，无需单独预填充每个查询-段落对，即可为每条段落生成一个得分。单一驻留的9B骨干模型支持生成、通用决策和重排序，每项决策技能仅增加约0.1 GiB内存。经过39分钟的MS MARCO微调后，Glance在SciFact上的nDCG@10达到0.741，在NFCorpus上达到0.345，分别优于bge-reranker-v2-m3的0.721和0.326。在300个HotpotQA问题上，其选择的上下文使答案F1相比交叉编码器选择的上下文提升了0.045（配对bootstrap 95%置信区间[0.014, 0.076]）。在扩展性研究中，重排序质量持续提升至最多200个候选（43k tokens）从一次共享预填充中评分，其nDCG@10超过交叉编码器0.058，且仅激活探测器的适配器允许生成从原始段落缓存继续。初步结果展示了基于生成器的重排序方法，仅需一个驻留骨干模型；当前评估覆盖两个检索数据集、一个问答数据集和一个骨干模型族。

Scaling Mathematical Proof Supervision from Structured Textbooks
从结构化教科书扩展数学证明监督
Mathematical supervision for language models is typically flattened into isolated problem-solution pairs, discarding the curricular organization and evidential structure of the original sources. We introduce TOME-Math (Textbook-Oriented Mathematical Evidence), a large-scale corpus that converts 2,118 textbooks spanning the core areas of university-level mathematics subjects into approximately 380K structured proof-supervision records while preserving chapter order, proposition-proof links, dependency references, and source provenance. Leveraging these structural signals, we explore curriculum learning across multiple mathematical subjects and characterize scaling patterns with supervision volume and source breadth. At full scale, fine-tuning on TOME-Math raises the unweighted mean score across our held-out evaluations and established public benchmarks by 16.7 percentage points over the base model, while exceeding all three public instruction-data baselines evaluated in aggregate. Our findings highlight structured textbooks as both a scalable source of mathematical supervision and an empirical framework for studying the impact of data organization on mathematical reasoning. We will release the corpus to facilitate further research on structured mathematical supervision.

Don't Lose the Thread: Empowering Long-Horizon LLM Agents with Cognitive Resource Self-Allocation
不要丢失主线：通过认知资源自分配赋能长时程LLM智能体
Agents powered by large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress in solving complex reasoning tasks. However, LLM agents often falter on long-horizon tasks due to cognitive overload, as their working memory becomes cluttered with expanding and irrelevant information, which dilutes their attention and hinders effective planning and reasoning. To mitigate this challenge, we introduce Cognitive Resource Self-Allocation (CORAL), a novel reasoning paradigm that empowers agents to proactively optimize their context. Implemented as an agent-callable working memory management toolset, CORAL allows an agent to checkpoint its task progress and verified facts, and adaptively initiate a new problem-solving episode by resetting cluttered context while preserving only the checkpointed information, enabling it to resume reasoning from a clean state. We further enhance the agent's checkpointing capabilities using a Multi-episode Agentic Reinforced Policy Optimization algorithm. On the GAIA benchmark, CORAL with an 8B model achieves 42.8% average accuracy, with particularly strong gains on the most challenging long-horizon tasks (Level 2 and Level 3). Crucially, these gains come at a substantially lower inference cost: CORAL consumes 6-7$\times$ fewer input tokens than a ReAct agent. It also generalizes to multi-hop Knowledge QA benchmarks and to the considerably harder BrowseComp, where it improves a strong proprietary agent by 8.0 points.

由大型语言模型（LLMs）驱动的智能体在解决复杂推理任务方面取得了显著进展。然而，LLM智能体在长时程任务中常因认知过载而失败，因为其工作记忆被不断扩展且无关的信息所充斥，导致注意力稀释，阻碍有效规划与推理。为缓解此挑战，我们提出认知资源自分配（CORAL），一种新型推理范式，赋予智能体主动优化其上下文的能力。CORAL作为可被智能体调用的工作记忆管理工具集，允许智能体检查点保存任务进展和已验证事实，并通过重置混乱的上下文而自适应地启动新的问题求解阶段，仅保留检查点信息，从而实现从干净状态恢复推理。我们进一步通过多阶段智能体强化策略优化算法增强智能体的检查点能力。在GAIA基准测试中，使用8B模型的CORAL实现了42.8%的平均准确率，尤其在最具挑战性的长时程任务（Level 2和Level 3）上取得显著提升。关键的是，这些提升带来的推理成本大幅降低：CORAL的输入token消耗仅为ReAct智能体的6-7倍。它还泛化到多跳知识问答基准和更具挑战性的BrowseComp，使强专有智能体性能提升8.0分。

Decision and System One models in rheumatology: accuracy and calibration on specialty-training examination questions and diagnostic case vignettes
风湿病学中决策与系统一体模型的准确性与校准：在专科培训考试题和诊断案例中的表现
Objectives: To evaluate the accuracy and calibration of Decision and System One models on rheumatology examination questions and diagnostic vignettes. Methods: Two benchmarks were analysed: 172 rheumatology questions from Spanish specialty-training examinations (expanded RheumaMIR dataset) in Spanish and English, and 70 English diagnostic vignettes from RheumBench with 5-18 candidate diagnoses, 52 of which included the reference diagnosis. Four System One models (Jev 1.13, Clef, Microsoft-Decision-1, Laya) and GPT-6 Luna (OpenAI Decisions API) were compared with GPT-6 Sol and two local generative models. The primary outcome was accuracy; secondary outcomes included calibration, discrimination, accuracy of answers with probability >=0.90 and language differences. Results: In RheumaMIR, Jev's accuracy was 93.0% in Spanish and 91.9% in English, below GPT-6 Sol (99.4%; Holm-adjusted P<=0.005); GPT-6 Luna reached 97.7%, Clef 91.9-93.0% and Microsoft-Decision-1 86.0-87.8%, without detectable language differences. Jev's answers with probability >=0.90 (76-77% of answers) were 98.5% correct. In RheumBench, accuracy was lower: GPT-6 Sol 76.9%, GPT-6 Luna 63.5%, Clef 59.6%, Jev 50.0% and Microsoft-Decision-1 40.4% (each System One model below GPT-6 Sol, Holm-adjusted P<=0.045; chance 9.6%). Jev became overconfident (mean probability 0.659; over/underconfidence index +0.159), and its answers with probability >=0.90 (25% of cases) were 84.6% correct, against 95.5% for GPT-6 Luna (42%). Laya performed near chance in both benchmarks. Conclusion: The best-performing System One models were highly accurate on examination questions but less accurate on diagnostic vignettes, remaining below GPT-6 Sol. Jev's high-confidence predictions were less reliable on vignettes, highlighting the need for task-specific evaluation and calibration.

Latent Path Exploration via Potential-Guided Subtrajectory Balance
通过势能引导的子轨迹平衡进行潜在路径探索
Continuous latent reasoning enables language models to perform intermediate computation without generating explicit reasoning traces. Advances in stochastic latent reasoning allow multiple paths to be sampled in continuous space, but stochasticity alone does not ensure effective exploration. A key challenge is guiding stochastic transitions between intermediate latent states toward useful reasoning paths. We introduce SubLAT, which couples a relative answer potential with Subtrajectory Balance to learn stochastic latent exploration. The relative potential evaluates the answer support of sampled states against replacement states, with a correctness signal at the terminal state. Subtrajectory Balance relates potential differences to forward and backward transition densities across subtrajectories of different lengths. The model is jointly trained with answer supervision, CoT supervision, and representation distillation. Experiments on arithmetic and deductive reasoning show increasing solution coverage with larger sampling budgets across in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, as well as improvements on several structural OOD benchmarks. Trajectory analyses reveal progressive divergence among sampled latent paths and higher mean relative potentials for correct trajectories across arithmetic benchmarks.

Mobilizing the Archive: Bitext Extraction at Scale from Archival Documents for Extremely Low-resource Languages
激活档案馆：从档案文献中大规模提取双语语料以支持极低资源语言
While mining parallel sentences from web pages has proven effective for low-resource machine translation, the majority of the world's languages have little to no web presence. What they do have, though, are resources that are the result of centuries of efforts by linguists, such as grammars, dictionaries, and other documentation materials. We propose extracting translation examples from such linguistic resources, which offer parallel data for languages under-represented online and could potentially scale to thousands more languages. We develop a pipeline to achieve this, leveraging an Optical Character Recognition (OCR) model as well as a vision language model (VLM), to ensure identified content to be aligned with visual layout information. We demonstrate the utility of the extracted bitexts for bootstrapping translation systems across 20 languages, both by training machine translation models and by applying in-context learning for large language models. For in-context learning, we observe improvements of +15.78 and +6.94 chrF2++ over zero-shot translation for into low- and high-resource languages, respectively.

AI Cosmos: An Agentic Infra for Situated Learning
AI宇宙：面向情境学习的智能体基础设施
Large language models (LLMs) can improve task performance during learning, yet these gains do not necessarily reflect learners' ability to thereafter apply the knowledge and skills independently. We present AI Cosmos, an agentic infrastructure for situated learning that organizes learning around two complementary principles: scenario-based participation and cumulative assets of practice. AI Cosmos organizes learners and role-specialized agents within learning scenarios that integrate task-relevant knowledge and, when needed, executable workbenches. An asset-memory loop preserves the processes and outcomes of practice as reusable assets with explicit provenance and dependencies. We evaluate scenario-based interaction through two preregistered randomized controlled studies examining the acquisition of legal reasoning and programming competence, holding the same underlying LLM across conditions. AI Cosmos achieves greater learning gains than a conventional LLM in legal reasoning and higher mastery of unseen programming problems than an LLM-as-Teacher baseline.

KV Cache Quantization for Diffusion Language Models
扩散语言模型的KV缓存量化
KV cache quantization is well studied for autoregressive language models, where cached prefix states are exact under causal attention and quantization is mainly a cache reconstruction problem. Diffusion language models (dLLMs) break this assumption. Fast dLLM inference reuses KV states across denoising steps, but bidirectional token representations can change as masked tokens are revealed, so the reused cache may already differ from full recomputation before quantization is applied. We study KV cache quantization under this cache-reuse error. We adapt KIVI and KVQuant to Fast-dLLM-style prefix and dual cache reuse, and introduce TRACE and dLLM-KVQ. TRACE reconstructs feasible dLLM reveal trajectories from prompt-answer pairs, providing calibration states that match iterative masked decoding. dLLM-KVQ uses these trajectories to choose per-layer K-cache entries that remain in high precision according to output- and attention-level behavior metrics, while applying standard low-bit value quantization. Across LLaDA and Dream evaluations on GSM8K, HumanEval, MATH, and MBPP, dLLM-KVQ achieves the highest average score among quantized methods across the reported 4-bit and 2-bit prefix/dual cache settings.
中文翻译
KV缓存量化在自回归语言模型中已有深入研究，其中缓存的前缀状态在因果注意力下是精确的，量化主要是一个缓存重建问题。然而，扩散语言模型（dLLMs）打破了这一假设。快速dLLM推理会在去噪步骤间复用KV状态，但由于掩码标记被逐步揭示，双向标记表示可能发生改变，因此在应用量化之前，复用的缓存可能已与完整重新计算的结果不同。我们在此缓存复用误差背景下研究KV缓存量化。我们对KIVI和KVQuant进行了适配，以支持Fast-dLLM风格的前缀和双缓存复用，并引入TRACE和dLLM-KVQ。TRACE从提示-答案对中重构可行的dLLM揭示轨迹，提供与迭代掩码解码相匹配的校准状态。dLLM-KVQ利用这些轨迹，根据输出和注意力级别的行为指标选择每层K缓存中应保持高精度的条目，同时对其他部分应用标准的低比特值量化。在LLaDA和Dream对GSM8K、HumanEval、MATH和MBPP的评估中，dLLM-KVQ在报告的4比特和2比特前缀/双缓存设置下，成为量化方法中平均得分最高的。

Grammar Control for Language Learning with Sequential Monte Carlo and Small Language Models
基于序列蒙特卡洛与小语言模型的语言学习语法控制
Second language learners benefit from exposure to level-appropriate grammar in interactive language use. While prompted frontier language models can usually produce target grammar structures, less resource-intensive models that can be run on device often fail in this task. We use sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) to steer dialogue turn generation in small lan- language models toward target grammar constructions from an expert-developed pedagogical resource. Incentivizing Self-Learning in Large Language Models via Reinforcement Learning
通过强化学习激励大语言模型的自我学习
Large language models (LLMs) draw on parametric knowledge and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to solve knowledge-intensive tasks. Yet problem solving in unfamiliar domains calls for tightly coupled knowledge acquisition and reasoning, beyond factual retrieval and aggregation or learning solely from a preassembled context. We study self-learning -- autonomously retrieving, studying, and applying knowledge from long-form reference materials on the fly. To investigate this capability, we formulate a textbook-based mathematical problem-solving task. LLMs must navigate textbook exposition, learn and apply relevant principles, and construct proofs for exercises, with reference solutions withheld. We introduce a BookNavigator interface to support this human-inspired study process and incentivize self-learning through reinforcement learning (RL) with fine-grained rewards. Experiments show that our method improves performance across in-domain and out-of-domain textbooks and subjects, raising Intern-S2-Preview-35B's overall proof accuracy from 51.5% to 79.5% and outperforming RL-trained textbook-free and fixed-retrieval baselines. These gains extend to complex long-context benchmarks involving different task types, with improvements over the base model of 1.7 and 2.3 percentage points on BrowseComp-Plus and CL-bench, respectively. Together, these findings suggest that RL can foster a domain-agnostic self-learning strategy, highlighting the potential of self-learning from long-form corpora to strengthen broader problem-solving capabilities on complex, long-context tasks.

Cord: Confidence-Anchored Order Residual Decoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models
Cord：基于置信度锚定的顺序残差解码用于掩码扩散语言模型
The decoding order of masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) can substantially affect generation quality, yet common decoders typically apply the same reveal rule throughout generation. Confidence provides a strong default, but it measures how decisively a position can be predicted now, not how useful revealing that position will be for subsequent decoding. We propose Cord, a lightweight prompt-conditioned policy that retains confidence decoding as a stable reference and learns when and how to depart from it through geometric residuals over generation direction, proximity to revealed context, and two-sided infilling. The residuals adapt to both the prompt and the evolving partial response. Rather than sampling a new policy action at every decoding step, Cord samples a single low-dimensional coefficient matrix per rollout, which induces an entire state-dependent reveal trajectory. This yields a contextual-bandit formulation trained directly from sequence-level rewards, without step-level credit assignment or backbone updates. Across three dLLMs and five reasoning, code, and constraint-satisfaction benchmarks, Cord improves average task success from 32.0% under confidence decoding to 48.2%, and further improves over the best per-domain fixed residual. Behavioral audits show that the learned residuals induce substantial, task-dependent changes in reveal locality and two-sided infilling. Cord adds only $0.53$M trainable parameters, requires no additional backbone forward passes, and incurs only 5.39% inference latency overhead.
中文翻译
掩码扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的解码顺序会显著影响生成质量，但常见解码器通常在整个生成过程中应用相同的揭示规则。置信度提供了一个强有力的默认策略，但它衡量的是当前位置当前被预测的确定程度，而非揭示该位置对未来解码的有用性。我们提出Cord，一种轻量级提示条件化策略，保留置信度解码作为稳定参考，并通过生成方向上的几何残差、与已揭示上下文的距离以及双向填充来学习何时以及如何偏离它。残差部分适应提示和不断演化的部分响应。与每步采样新策略动作不同，Cord在每次推理中仅采样一个低维系数矩阵，从而诱导出整个状态依赖的揭示轨迹。这形成了一种基于序列级奖励直接训练的上下文老虎机框架，无需步骤级信用分配或主干网络更新。在三个dLLMs和五个推理、代码及约束满足基准测试中，Cord将任务成功率从置信度解码下的32.0%提升至48.2%，并进一步优于最佳领域固定残差。行为审计显示，学习到的残差引发了显著的、任务相关的揭示局部性和双向填充变化。Cord仅增加0.53M可训练参数，无需额外主干前向传播，推理延迟开销仅为5.39%。

Grounding Multimodal Reasoning with Evidence-Ablated Negatives
基于证据缺失负样本的多模态推理定位
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful tool for improving multimodal reasoning. However, standard outcome supervision suffers from credit assignment ambiguity, as a model may receive positive rewards by exploiting language priors or dataset shortcuts rather than grounding its reasoning in the requisite visual evidence. This makes high-quality negative responses critical, since effective negatives should remain plausible under the current policy while exposing the shortcut that should be suppressed. In this paper, we propose EVAN, a multimodal reinforcement learning framework utilizing EVidence-Ablated Negatives to train LMMs against their own language-prior failures. EVAN first identifies highly visually dependent examples using a scaled image information gain metric and preserves data diversity with a mixed-ranking strategy. It then constructs evidence-ablated images by masking the most discriminative image patches, eliciting plausible but visually ungrounded responses from the current policy. During policy optimization, these negative responses are incorporated as bounded auxiliary signals under the clean image, penalizing shortcut learning while maintaining RL stability. Experiments across challenging multimodal reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EVAN mitigates modality imbalance and improves over strong RLVR baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/DawnLIU35/EVAN.git.
中文翻译
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升多模态推理能力的强大工具。然而，标准的结果监督存在信用分配模糊的问题，因为模型可能通过利用语言先验或数据集捷径获得正向奖励，而非基于必要的视觉证据进行推理。这使得高质量负样本至关重要，因为有效的负样本应在当前策略下仍具合理性，同时暴露应被抑制的捷径。本文提出EVAN，一种利用证据缺失负样本的多模态强化学习框架，用于训练大模型（LMMs）以克服其语言先验失败的问题。EVAN首先使用缩放后的图像信息增益度量识别高度依赖视觉的样本，并通过混合排序策略保持数据多样性；随后通过遮蔽最具判别性的图像块构建证据缺失图像，促使当前策略生成看似合理但缺乏视觉依据的响应。在策略优化过程中，这些负响应作为有界辅助信号引入，在干净图像条件下惩罚捷径学习的同时保持强化学习的稳定性。在多个具有挑战性的多模态推理基准上的实验表明，EVAN缓解了模态不平衡问题，并优于强基线RLVR方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/DawnLIU35/EVAN.git。
👤 Chenxi Liu, Tianyi Xiong, Yanshuo Chen, Heng Huang 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Tracing the Influence of Retrieved Literature on Scientific Ideation in LLMs
追踪检索文献对LLM科学创意生成的影响
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate research ideas from scientific literature. Existing systems combine literature retrieval with search, generation, and refinement, making it difficult to determine how the retrieval strategy itself shapes the ideas generated. We isolate this contribution by comparing five retrieval strategies and a No Literature baseline under a shared generation protocol, using 45,000 ideas across 15 machine learning topics and ten open-weight models. Relative to no literature basline, all five retrieval strategies reduce diversity among ideas generated from the same set of papers by $5.6\%$--$17.9\%$, while most increase diversity by $10.2\%$--$18.1\%$ when ideas from different sets are pooled. Effects on idea quality and novelty are modest on average but vary across topics. We further apply a JSD-based attribution method to trace the influence of individual input papers on each idea's motivation, core idea, approach, and validation. In $79.3\%$ of ideas, only one or two distinct papers receive the highest attribution scores across these four parts. These leading sources often recur across ideas generated from the same set of papers, and groups with more varied source attribution contain more diverse ideas ($r=0.70$). These findings motivate various sets of literature and the use of attribution to guide the choice of sources for subsequent ideas.

大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于从科学文献中生成研究创意。现有系统将文献检索与搜索、生成和优化相结合，导致难以判断检索策略本身如何影响生成的创意。我们通过在统一生成协议下比较五种检索策略与无文献基线，隔离了这一贡献因素，使用15个机器学习主题和十个开源权重模型生成的45,000个创意进行分析。相较于无文献基线，所有五种检索策略在相同论文集合生成的创意中降低了5.6%–17.9%的多样性，但在不同论文集合的创意合并后，多数策略提升了10.2%–18.1%的多样性。对创意质量和新颖性的影响平均较小，但随主题变化显著。我们进一步采用基于JSD的归因方法，追踪每篇输入文献对创意动机、核心思想、方法和验证部分的影响。在79.3%的创意中，仅有一至两篇不同的文献在四个部分中获得最高归因分数。这些主导来源常在来自同一论文集合的多个创意中重复出现，且来源归因更丰富的组别生成的创意更具多样性（r=0.70）。这些发现支持采用多样化的文献集合，并利用归因分析来指导后续创意的来源选择。
