Distilling the Shape of Uncertainty: What On-Policy Distillation Learns and How to Predict It
提炼不确定性形态：在线蒸馏学到了什么以及如何预测它
On-policy distillation (OPD) is increasingly adopted in the post-training of large language models. However, a stronger teacher may fail to improve the student. Prior work attributes this to a lack of new knowledge, but what that knowledge is remains unclear. We show that OPD learns not only \textit{how to branch}, i.e., which candidate tokens to consider, but also \textit{where to branch}, i.e., where to keep reasoning paths open. The existing token-overlap metric captures only the former. We introduce the entropy shape, the sequence of next-token entropies, to characterize the latter, and propose the Entropy Shape Correlation (ESC) to measure student-teacher agreement in entropy shape. Tracking ESC throughout training and intervening on a teacher's entropy while preserving its token preferences, we empirically show that \textit{where to branch} causally drives OPD gains, independently of \textit{how to branch}. Finally, we formulate OPD outcome prediction as a teacher selection task and establish an evaluation protocol for it, under which pre-OPD ESC selects the best teacher for both students and outperforms existing predictors. Code is available at https://github.com/maybenotime/opd-esc.

在线蒸馏（OPD）在大语言模型的后训练中日益被采用。然而，更强的教师模型可能无法提升学生模型的表现。先前的研究将此归因于缺乏新知识，但这种知识的具体内容仍不明确。我们发现，OPD不仅学习了‘如何分支’（即考虑哪些候选词），还学习了‘在何处分支’（即在何处保持推理路径开放）。现有的词重叠度量仅能捕捉前者。为此，我们引入熵形态（entropy shape），即下一词熵序列，以刻画后者，并提出熵形态相关性（ESC）来衡量学生与教师在熵形态上的一致性。通过在整个训练过程中追踪ESC，并在保持教师词偏好不变的前提下干预其熵，我们实证表明‘在何处分支’对OPD性能提升具有因果作用，且独立于‘如何分支’。最后，我们将OPD结果预测建模为教师选择任务，并建立相应的评估协议，在该协议下，预OPD阶段的ESC能够选出最优教师，表现优于现有预测方法。代码已公开于 https://github.com/maybenotime/opd-esc。

Implicit Reasoning in Transformers: Bridging the Compositionality Gap in GPT-2-like Models
Transformer中的隐式推理：弥合GPT-2类模型的组合性鸿沟
Compositional generalization remains a persistent limitation of transformer language models: even when a model learns to combine known facts correctly in-distribution (ID), the same compositional mechanism may fail when applied to out-of-distribution (OOD) knowledge. We investigate this compositionality gap in a controlled two-hop reasoning setting using a GPT-2-like transformer model trained from scratch. Building on prior work identifying a circuit associated with compositional generalization, we test whether the failure of OOD transfer is related to how atomic knowledge is structurally exposed during training. We modify the training distribution by presenting both ID and OOD atomic facts in a format positionally aligned with the second-hop retrieval step of the learned compositional circuit, without modifying the model architecture or adding OOD compositional supervision. Under this intervention, the model successfully transfers the learned ID compositional behaviour to held-out OOD atomic facts, closing the observed compositionality gap in the controlled setting. Our results provide evidence that the observed compositionality gap is not a fundamental architecture limitation and that it can be overcome through mechanistically informed training distribution design.

Blind Spots: Where Small Language Models Fall Behind Frontier Models on Terminal Tasks
盲点：小型语言模型在终端任务中为何落后于前沿模型
Small language models (SLMs) offer an attractive alternative to frontier models (FMs) because they can be deployed with lower cost and latency. This makes SLMs especially appealing for terminal tasks on edge devices, where responsiveness, local execution, and limited connectivity are important considerations. However, they continue to lag behind FMs on terminal tasks, as measured by standard task success rates. Yet these rates conflate several capabilities that may contribute to this gap. In this work, we characterize the SLM-FM gap along three axes: solvability, whether additional test-time compute allows an SLM to reach a correct solution; verification, whether it can identify a successful solution among candidate trajectories; and confidence, whether it can accurately anticipate its probability of success. Across two standard terminal benchmarks, we find that repeated sampling narrows, but does not eliminate, the gap in solvability. In contrast, a persistent gap remains in verification. Given the same candidate trajectory pool, FMs recover substantially more of the gap between random and oracle selection than SLMs. SLMs are also more overconfident in their prospective success estimates and exhibit poorer calibration. These results suggest that the SLM-FM gap is multidimensional, spanning not only the ability to generate successful solutions, but also the ability to recognize successful solutions and anticipate success. We hope this decomposition can guide future work on developing stronger and smaller terminal agents.
中文翻译

How Architecture, Typology, and Adaptation Strategies Shape ASR for Low-Resource African Languages: A Controlled Empirical Study
架构、类型与适应策略如何塑造低资源非洲语言的语音识别：一项受控实证研究
Despite recent advancements in ASR, identifying effective architectures, fine-tuning approaches, and data adaptation strategies for low-resource languages remains challenging. We present a systematic comparison across five low-resource African languages and Swahili as a mid-resource contrast, fine-tuning three models from distinct architectures: Phi-4-multimodal-instruct, Whisper-large-v3-turbo, and MMS-1B-all. We analyze data sufficiency, monolingual versus multilingual adaptation, domain fine-tuning, and language-adapter transfer. Phi-4 achieves the best overall accuracy, MMS shows comparable CER, while Whisper exhibits high CER, consistent with known hallucinations and repetition loops. Performance saturates at different data scales, offering practical data-efficiency targets, while typological complexity consistently shapes error rates. Domain fine-tuning benefits Phi-4 and Whisper but degrades MMS, revealing limitations of adapter-based adaptation. Proxy language adapters outperform training MMS adapters from scratch.

Interventional Training Reduces Causal Bypass in In-Context Introspection
干预训练减少上下文内自省中的因果绕行
Introspection, the ability of a system to access and reason about its internal signals, is crucial for language model (LM) self-consistency and scalable interpretability. Prior work argues that LMs possess introspective capabilities, and that training (SFT) could enhance such behavior. However, most setups leave open the possibility of "causal bypass," in which the self-report relies on signals that are correlated with the self-report's subject, while being causally unrelated to it. Although high-level concepts are difficult to intervene on, lower-level signals (such as a single neuron activation) allow precise interventions. Understanding such signals can serve as a minimal prerequisite before understanding more complex introspection. In this study, we train hundreds of introspectors across three low-level signal types and eight LMs. We find that straightforward SFT training leads to causal bypass, and propose interventional training, an alternative where we train the model to report on counterfactually perturbed values. This method enables more accurate introspection of endogenous $\textit{and}$ injected signals, particularly in out-of-distribution (OOD) data. The mechanism generalizes across token positions and scales with more data. Our results demonstrate that while causally grounded introspection is possible, it is not guaranteed -- emphasizing the need to train on mechanisms, rather than behaviors alone.

HEDGE: Gated Hypergraph Memory for Scalable Multi-Agent Debate
HEDGE：用于可扩展多智能体辩论的门控超图记忆
Multi-agent debate systems require agents to retain and revisit arguments across multiple rounds, yet limited context windows and unstructured memories make relevant information increasingly difficult to recover. Existing flat and pairwise-graph memories also struggle to capture higher-order relations, including multi-agent agreement, conflicting evidence, and evolving consensus. We introduce HEDGE, a selective memory framework that represents debate trajectories as semantic hypergraphs. Hyperedges connect related claims across agents, while intra-hyperedge relational graphs preserve fine-grained argumentative structure. A learned memory selection module (MSM), trained through supervised learning and reinforcement learning, routes each memory candidate to STORE, BACKUP, or OMIT, retaining useful information while limiting redundancy. Across seven diverse benchmarks, HEDGE consistently outperforms strong single-agent, multi-agent, and memory baselines, improving accuracy by 5.0 points on average over the strongest multi-agent system with GPT-4o-mini. The gains reach 8.2 points on conflict-heavy tasks, while HEDGE reduces memory usage by 48% and the number of debate rounds by 34%.

CopyLoop: Fast Looped Transformer Decoding via Context Copying and Shallow Screening
CopyLoop：通过上下文复制与浅层筛选实现快速循环Transformer解码
Looped Transformers increase computational capacity through the repeated application of shared blocks, but their recurrent depth makes conventional full-depth decoding expensive. While existing speculative pipelines accelerate generation by processing consecutive token positions at varying depths, they are typically limited by sequential decoding overheads. We introduce CopyLoop, a training-free decoding algorithm that combines low-cost context-copy proposals with shallow screening and continuous recurrent scheduling. Rather than step-by-step generation, CopyLoop proposes multi-token suffixes from repeated context. It then evaluates them early at a shallow recurrent depth to efficiently filter out incorrect branches, processing newer blocks concurrently while earlier blocks are still progressing toward full-depth verification. We evaluate CopyLoop across six model checkpoints from the Ouro, Raven, and Huginn families, covering code generation and multi-step mathematical reasoning. CopyLoop achieves decoding speedups of 2.98x--10.70x over full-depth autoregressive decoding on Base models and 2.74x--3.52x on Thinking models, consistently outperforming or closely matching all competing baselines across evaluation tasks. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm a strong synergy between shallow screening and continuous scheduling, while our analyses show that CopyLoop requires only lightweight model calibration and yields greater speedups on longer generations with reusable contexts.

循环Transformer通过重复应用共享模块来提升计算能力，但其递归深度导致传统全深度解码成本高昂。现有推测性流水线虽能通过在不同深度处理连续词元位置加速生成，但通常受限于串行解码开销。我们提出CopyLoop，一种无需训练的解码算法，结合低成本的上下文复制提议、浅层筛选和连续递归调度。不同于逐步生成，CopyLoop从重复上下文中提出多词元后缀，然后在浅层递归深度早期评估以高效过滤错误分支，同时让新块并发处理，而旧块仍在推进至全深度验证。我们在Ouro、Raven和Huginn系列的六个模型检查点上评估CopyLoop，涵盖代码生成和多步数学推理任务。在基础模型上，CopyLoop相比全深度自回归解码速度提升2.98x至10.70x，在思维模型上提升2.74x至3.52x，始终优于或接近所有竞争基线。消融实验确认浅层筛选与连续调度之间存在强协同效应，分析显示CopyLoop仅需轻量级模型校准，且在可复用上下文的长序列生成中获得更大加速效果。

The Quine Test: How Much of a Model's Self Survives Its Own Description?
奎因测试：模型的自我在自身描述中还能留存多少？
Frontier language models express coherent preferences, but behavioral evidence alone cannot say whether these belong to the model or to a character it portrays. Following Hofstadter's view of the self as a self-description, we ask whether a model's description of itself works as a portable specification of its preference profile, a quine test: can the text a model writes about itself regenerate its behavior elsewhere? Ten models from nine labs write self-descriptions; we place each one in the prompt of other models (receivers) and of fresh instances of the author, and measure how closely their choices on a 110-item preference battery match the author's. First, the tested native self-descriptions (native: the profile measured under a generic assistant prompt) show no average reconstruction gain across variations in length, framing, wording and prompt placement: on a 0 to 1 fidelity scale where a model's fidelity to a fresh instance of itself is 0.979 and to unrelated models about 0.89, the one-sided 95% upper bound on the pooled gain is below +0.01 in every tested system-prompt condition, and descriptions based on the author's observed choices do no better. Second, the channel itself is strong: explicitly listing the author's answers lets receivers reproduce its profile at fidelity 0.955 to 0.960 across three model grids, within 0.03 of same-model repeatability. Third, self-descriptions written under an explicit preference policy transfer the assigned preferences almost as well as the policy text itself. Fourth, reading its own self-description shifts even the author away from its baseline, modestly more than unrelated prose of the same length. For the preferences measured here, native self-descriptions influence behavior without reliably specifying how to reproduce it in another model. We release the pipeline, battery, raw call logs, and 198 model-written descriptions.
中文翻译
前沿语言模型表现出连贯的偏好，但仅凭行为证据无法判断这些偏好属于模型本身还是其扮演的角色。根据霍夫施塔特关于自我即自我描述的观点，我们提出一个“奎因测试”：模型对自己所写的描述能否作为其偏好谱的便携规范，即该文本是否能在其他地方再生其行为？来自九家实验室的十种模型撰写自我描述；我们将每份描述置于其他模型（接收者）和作者自身的全新实例中，测量其在110项偏好电池上的选择与作者的一致性。首先，测试的原生自我描述（native：在通用助手提示下测量的谱）在长度、表述方式、措辞和提示位置变化下均未显示出平均重建增益：在0到1的保真度尺度上，模型对自身新实例的保真度为0.979，对无关模型约为0.89，所有测试系统-提示条件下合并增益的单侧95%上限低于+0.01，基于观察行为的描述也无优势。其次，通道本身很强：明确列出作者答案可使接收者在三个模型网格上以0.955至0.960的保真度重现其谱，与同模型重复性相差不超过0.03。第三，基于显式偏好策略书写的自我描述几乎与策略文本本身一样有效地传递指定偏好。第四，阅读自身自我描述甚至会使作者自身偏离基线，程度略高于同等长度的无关散文。对于此处测量的偏好，原生自我描述会影响行为，但无法可靠指定如何在另一模型中重现它。我们发布了该管道、电池、原始调用日志以及198份模型撰写的描述。

Learning Scientific Trajectories to Predict Future Biomedical Discoveries
学习科学轨迹以预测未来的生物医学发现
AI co-scientists have expanded the use of large language models for generating scientific hypotheses and guiding experimental investigation. Here we investigate whether temporal patterns in the semantic representations of complete biomedical conclusions can guide hypothesis generation. We introduce ForeSCITE, a framework that identifies thematic clusters of published conclusions, estimates a direction separating earlier from more recent findings within each cluster, and projects existing conclusion embeddings along these directions. An embedding language model trained to reconstruct conclusions decodes the projected coordinates into natural-language hypotheses. Across 407 clusters derived from approximately three million pre-cutoff conclusions, we identify temporal structure and illustrate its retention after decoding. In retrospective, time-sliced evaluations, ForeSCITE increases semantic coverage beyond the historical corpus, biomedical entity-pair novelty and post-cutoff pair re-observation relative to five prompt-based and retrieval-augmented baselines. Expert review of prioritized CAR-T hypotheses identified 37 candidates as both novel and biologically plausible, and expert justifications for five selected hypotheses illustrate their biological rationale and subsequent literature support where available. These findings support temporal guidance in semantic space as a scalable mechanism for proposing biomedical hypotheses for subsequent scientific evaluation.

CoSTA: Co-Evolving Self-Play Timeline Agents for Complex Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering
CoSTA：用于复杂时间知识图谱问答的协同演化自play时间线智能体
Complex Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering (TKGQA) involves more than retrieving facts: the timestamp or validity interval of one fact can constrain the search for another, while multiple intervals may jointly determine the answer. Existing single-agent TKGQA methods typically investigate these facts within a single reasoning context, where evidence from different event histories can become interleaved and distractors can misdirect subsequent retrieval. Distributing investigations across agents can separate event-specific evidence into distinct contexts, but effective collaboration must still be learned because question decomposition, subtask investigation, and evidence synthesis are interdependent. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{Co}-Evolving \textbf{S}elf-Play \textbf{T}imeline \textbf{A}gents (CoSTA), which organizes facts into navigable timelines, delegates investigations to workers, and uses a coordinator to synthesize their original timestamped observations. CoSTA initializes a shared policy through role-conditioned distillation and jointly optimizes both roles through co-evolving self-play with final-answer rewards. Across three benchmarks, CoSTA achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with particularly strong gains on complex multi-fact questions, while its compact variants outperform the evaluated closed-source LLM agents.
中文翻译
复杂时间知识图谱问答（TKGQA）不仅涉及事实检索：一个事实的时间戳或有效区间可能约束对另一个事实的搜索，而多个区间可能共同决定答案。现有的单智能体TKGQA方法通常在一个推理上下文中研究这些事实，导致来自不同事件历史的证据混杂，干扰物可能误导后续检索。将调查任务分配给多个智能体可以将特定事件的证据分隔到不同上下文中，但有效的协作仍需学习，因为问题分解、子任务调查和证据整合是相互依赖的。为解决这些局限性，我们提出 extbf{Co}-Evolving extbf{S}elf-Play extbf{T}imeline extbf{A}gents（CoSTA），它将事实组织成可导航的时间线，将调查任务委派给工作者，并由协调者合成其原始的时间戳观测结果。CoSTA通过角色条件蒸馏初始化共享策略，并通过最终答案奖励进行协同演化自play联合优化。在三个基准上的实验表明，CoSTA实现了最先进的整体性能，尤其在复杂多事实问题上表现突出，其紧凑变体也优于所评估的闭源LLM智能体。

Does Chain-of-Thought Reliance Follow a Scaling Law?Larger Models Follow Their Steps More, Independent of Post-Training
思维链依赖是否遵循缩放定律？更大模型更依赖其推理步骤，与后训练无关
Monitoring a model's chain of thought (CoT) is useful for safety only if the final answer depends on the written steps. Prior measurements use natural tasks where true reasoning is unknown. We measure it against an exact ground truth on a synthetic box-swap task where every intermediate state is known. We replace one step inside a model's own chain with a wrong but valid state and check whether the answer follows the edit. We evaluate 25 open-weight models--Qwen3 (0.6B-14B, base and post-trained), Gemma 3 (1B-12B), Llama 3 (1B-8B), and 8 OLMo 2 checkpoints--on 7,200 items each. We find five results: (1) Reliance rises with size and saturates. In Qwen3, answer reliance rises from 16-18% at 0.6B to 97-100% at 14B; a logistic in log N fits well (R^2 = 0.89), and Gemma 3 and Llama 3 follow this trend. (2) Capability is the main driver. Across all models, reliance and step accuracy correlate at r = 0.99; equally capable models are equally reliant, regardless of family. (3) Size still matters at matched correctness. On problems solved correctly before the edit, reliance increases with size among capable models (rho = 0.80, p = 0.002). (4) Post-training does not change reliance. Base and post-trained Qwen3 differ by at most 0.03 from 4B upward; a large gap at 1.7B matches a large capability gap. (5) No model up to 14B solves the task internally. Without written steps, every capable model is at chance once a problem has four or more swaps. OLMo 2 checkpoints never become capable, making the training-token axis a limitation. On tasks requiring step-by-step computation, scale makes the chain of thought more load-bearing, supporting CoT monitoring in this regime.

监控模型的思维链（CoT）对安全有用的前提是最终答案依赖于所写步骤。以往测量使用真实任务，但真实推理过程未知。我们在一个合成的盒子交换任务中，利用精确的已知中间状态进行测量。在模型自身的思维链中替换一步为错误但有效的状态，并检查答案是否随之改变。我们在7200个样本上评估了25个开源模型——Qwen3（0.6B-14B，基础和后训练版本）、Gemma 3（1B-12B）、Llama 3（1B-8B）以及8个OLMo 2检查点。发现五个结果：（1）依赖性随模型规模增加而上升并趋于饱和。在Qwen3中，0.6B模型的答案依赖性为16-18%，14B模型达到97-100%；对数N的逻辑回归拟合良好（R²=0.89），Gemma 3和Llama 3也呈现此趋势。（2）能力是主要驱动因素。所有模型中，依赖性与步骤准确率相关性高达r=0.99；同等能力的模型具有相同依赖性，与模型家族无关。（3）在正确率匹配的情况下，规模仍起作用。在编辑前正确解决的问题中，能力强的模型依赖性随规模增加（rho=0.80，p=0.002）。（4）后训练不影响依赖性。从4B开始，基础版与后训练版Qwen3差异不超过0.03；1.7B处的大差距对应大能力差距。（5）至多14B的模型均无法在无文字步骤时内部完成任务。一旦问题涉及四个或更多交换，所有有能力的模型表现仅如随机猜测。OLMo 2检查点从未变得足够强大，表明训练token数量是瓶颈。在需要逐步计算的任务中，规模使思维链更具决定性，支持在此情境下对思维链进行监控。

Deeper Backpropagation or More Updates? Training Looped Language Models Across Budgets
更深的反向传播还是更多更新？跨预算训练循环语言模型
Repeating transformer layers can improve language model performance without adding parameters. However, the added computational depth increases both forward and backward computation costs during training. Training such models requires choosing a backward window that controls how many final layer repetitions retain gradients during backpropagation. Longer windows improve per-token learning efficiency but increase per-update cost, while shorter windows reduce per-update cost but sacrifice per-token efficiency. Understanding which window performs better requires examining different training budgets. Prior work compares backward windows across computational budgets. When training budgets increase, it remains unclear whether the same window continues to perform best. We measure how the relative performance of two windows shifts as training budgets grow. Our method isolates per-token efficiency by training both windows on identical data, and compares complete training recipes at approximately equal training time at each budget. Results show that shorter windows lose much of their advantage as budgets grow. Controlled experiments show that both benefits decrease at larger budgets, with a greater decline in the benefit of extended training. Backward window comparisons depend on the available budget, and the preferred window among the tested candidates can change as the budget grows.
中文翻译
重复Transformer层可在不增加参数的情况下提升语言模型性能。然而，增加的计算深度同时提高了训练期间的前向和反向计算成本。训练此类模型需选择一个反向窗口，控制多少最后层的重复保留梯度用于反向传播。较长窗口可提高每token学习效率，但增加每次更新的成本；较短窗口降低每次更新成本，但牺牲每token效率。理解哪种窗口更优需考察不同训练预算。先前研究比较了不同计算预算下的反向窗口。当训练预算增加时，尚不清楚同一窗口是否仍最优。我们测量随着训练预算增长，两个窗口相对性能的变化。通过在相同数据上训练两个窗口，并在每个预算下比较完整训练方案（训练时间相近），隔离出每token效率。结果显示，随着预算增长，短窗口的优势显著减弱。受控实验表明，两种优势在大预算下均下降，且扩展训练带来的收益下降更明显。反向窗口的比较依赖可用预算，测试候选中的最优窗口可能随预算增长而变化。

Holistic Diversity Management in LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search
LLM引导的进化搜索中的整体多样性管理
LLM-guided evolutionary search iteratively selects existing programs, uses LLMs as mutation operators to generate a new program, and evaluates it to guide subsequent search. To preserve diversity and reduce the risk of premature convergence, it often adopts the island model, in which groups of programs, called islands, evolve separately. However, this structure alone does not guarantee distinct search directions, as islands may start from similar programs or converge toward similar solutions during evolution. This suggests that diversity must be considered throughout the search process, from initializing islands with distinct programs to developing island trajectories and sharing discoveries across islands. To this end, we propose HoliDivEvolve , a holistic approach to establishing and effectively utilizing island diversity throughout evolutionary search. Specifically, we introduce diverse initialization to establish different initial programs, trajectory adaptive island selection to allocate computation based on current score, recent growth, and the remaining search budget, and growth adaptive migration to share alternative ideas across islands. Experiments across diverse benchmarks show that HoliDivEvolve achieves competitive performance on most evaluated tasks. We further conduct controlled analyses to examine how our design choices affect search behavior and final performance.
中文翻译
LLM引导的进化搜索通过迭代选择现有程序，使用LLM作为变异算子生成新程序，并评估其以指导后续搜索。为保持多样性并降低过早收敛风险，常采用岛屿模型，即多个程序组（称为岛屿）独立演化。然而，仅采用该结构并不能保证不同的搜索方向，因为岛屿可能从相似程序出发，或在演化过程中趋向相似解。这表明多样性必须贯穿整个搜索过程，从初始阶段的差异化程序设置，到岛屿轨迹设计及跨岛屿的知识共享。为此，我们提出HoliDivEvolve，一种在进化搜索全过程中建立并有效利用岛屿多样性的整体方法。具体包括：多样化初始化以建立不同初始程序，基于当前得分、近期增长和剩余搜索预算的轨迹自适应岛屿选择，以及基于增长自适应的迁移机制以跨岛屿共享替代思路。在多种基准上的实验表明，HoliDivEvolve在多数任务上表现优异。我们还进行了受控分析，探究设计选择如何影响搜索行为和最终性能。

TCC-RAG: Temporal Causal Chain-Guided RAG for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering
TCC-RAG：基于时间因果链引导的RAG用于时间知识图谱问答
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have spurred growing interest in temporal knowledge graph question answering (TKGQA). Most existing methods rely on semantic retrieval augmented generation (RAG), which suffers from fundamental limitations in complex temporal reasoning. On the one hand, semantic-based RAG methods struggle to capture the multi-hop causal knowledge required for complex temporal reasoning. On the other hand, these methods introduce excessive redundant knowledge, trapping LLMs in spurious reasoning chains that potentially result in erroneous answers. To address these problems, we propose a Temporal Causal Chain-guided RAG (TCC-RAG) to retrieve a concise yet sufficient set of causal knowledge to support complex temporal reasoning. Specifically, we reformulate the temporal knowledge graph into a multi-view causal cue graph, with causal cues inducing connections that explicitly encode temporal evolution and causal dynamics. Based on the causal cue graph, we construct a query-specific temporal causal chain to guide the retrieval process, enabling the model to focus on a concise set of causally relevant knowledge. Finally, we introduce a causal evidence filter to refine the retrieved knowledge, ensuring its causal consistency with the underlying temporal logic of the query. Experiments show that TCC-RAG achieves substantial performance gains of 10.72% and 8.40% on two TKGQA benchmarks, and delivers a remarkable 62.97% improvement for multi-step temporal reasoning.

大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展激发了对时间知识图谱问答（TKGQA）的广泛关注。现有大多数方法依赖于语义检索增强生成（RAG），但在复杂的时间推理方面存在根本性局限。一方面，基于语义的RAG方法难以捕捉复杂时间推理所需的多跳因果知识；另一方面，这些方法引入了过多冗余知识，使LLM陷入虚假推理链，可能导致错误答案。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种基于时间因果链引导的RAG（TCC-RAG），以检索精炼且充分的因果知识来支持复杂的时间推理。具体而言，我们将时间知识图谱重构为多视角因果线索图，其中因果线索显式编码时间演化和因果动态。基于该因果线索图，我们构建了查询特定的时间因果链以指导检索过程，使模型聚焦于一组精炼的因果相关知识。最后，我们引入因果证据过滤器，对检索到的知识进行精炼，确保其与查询底层时间逻辑的一致性。实验表明，TCC-RAG在两个TKGQA基准上分别取得了10.72%和8.40%的显著性能提升，并在多步时间推理任务中实现了62.97%的惊人改进。

Fu-TCM: a multimodal large language model that learns traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic reasoning
Fu-TCM：学习中医诊断推理的多模态大语言模型
Large language models (LLMs) are advancing in multimodal understanding and reasoning, accelerating medical model development. Traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) uses evidence from the four examinations to determine disease location and nature, differentiate syndromes and guide treatment. This diagnostic process constitutes a complete chain of reasoning. We present Fu-TCM, a multimodal TCM LLM trained on data retained after cleaning and filtering an initial collection of approximately 7.12 million examples derived from classical texts, textbooks, medical images, clinical cases and publicly available datasets. The model was further optimized using Bianzheng-Grounded Policy Optimization (BGPO), which explicitly targets syndrome differentiation through rewards for response format, intermediate TCM reasoning fields and final syndrome predictions. Fu-TCM-27B led all six benchmarks spanning TCM reasoning, text and vision, achieving a macro-average accuracy of 81.01% and surpassing the strongest reference model with complete results across all six benchmarks by 7.13 percentage points. Its largest margin was 11.90 points on Fu-bianzheng. Case-level analyses further showed a positive association between intermediate-field accuracy and final syndrome-selection accuracy in most evaluated models. These findings suggest that TCM syndrome differentiation may be computationally learnable and that structured reasoning supervision can improve performance across TCM tasks.

EPSBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Earnings Forecasting
EPSBench：用于盈利预测的LLM智能体基准测试
Quarterly earnings forecasting requires an agent to connect financial evidence to the income-statement line items that produce a company's reported earnings. We introduce EPSBench, a benchmark for studying this ability across 149 firm-quarters from 86 US companies. Agents read a fixed collection of filings, earnings-call transcripts, and news, then estimate company-specific income-statement drivers with cited evidence. A shared accounting calculator converts these estimates into GAAP diluted earnings per share (EPS), providing a common basis for comparing forecast assumptions. We evaluate six models under a common harness, assessing point forecast error relative to date-matched analyst consensus and checking whether their prediction intervals achieve the stated coverage. Three models show no detectable paired difference in point forecast error from consensus, and three have higher error. Across all six models, nominal 80% intervals contain reported EPS in only 37--70% of runs that return an interval, and most misses fall above the upper bound because the forecasts run low. The driver records trace most of the error gap between models to operating-income estimates.

Human Evaluation with Automatic Scores: Separating Sampling and Estimation Gains
自动评分与人工评估：分离采样与估计收益
Automatic judges offer inexpensive information about model outputs, but an evaluation team must decide how to turn that information into fewer human annotations. Scores can guide sampling, correct an estimate, or support adaptive acquisition, and these uses may capture overlapping gains. We compare their incremental value on fixed evaluation pools from three preference datasets, and 19 attributed-question-answering systems. Matched comparisons use the same scores, account for pilot labels and budget rules, and separate acquisition from estimation. Score stratification is a strong reference: after ten-bin stratification, the best fixed local linear correction offers only 1.00--5.27% further MSE reduction in the primary configurations. Unequal allocation offers larger gains in some pools, but can increase error when confident scores conceal variable human outcomes. Comparison on AttributedQA, shrinking learned quotas toward score-only quotas lowers pooled MSE for 17 of 19 systems relative to the score-only procedure, but ordinary pooled 95% intervals cover only 88.09--93.54%. The same shrinkage improves on the score-only reference in only one of eight preference configurations. Ranking experiments further show that lower individual MSE need not improve system selection. These findings support a simple starting procedure, proportional sampling within score strata followed by a stratified human mean, and identify what additional evidence is needed to justify correction or adaptation for the intended evaluation output.

自动评判器为模型输出提供了低成本的信息，但评估团队必须决定如何将这些信息转化为更少的人工标注。评分可用于指导采样、校正估计或支持自适应获取，这些用途可能带来重叠的收益。我们在三个偏好数据集和19个属性化问答系统上比较了它们在固定评估池中的增量价值。匹配比较使用相同的评分，考虑试点标签和预算规则，并将获取与估计分开。评分分层是一个强有力的基准：在十层分层后，最佳的固定局部线性校正仅在主要配置中进一步降低了1.00%–5.27%的均方误差。不均衡分配在某些池中带来更大收益，但在自信评分掩盖人类结果变异时可能增加误差。在属性化QA上的比较显示，将学习到的配额向仅基于评分的配额收缩，相对于仅基于评分的方法，使17/19个系统的合并均方误差降低，但普通合并95%置信区间仅覆盖88.09%–93.54%。同样的收缩仅在八种偏好配置中的一种上优于仅基于评分的参考。排名实验进一步表明，个体均方误差降低并不一定改善系统选择。这些发现支持一种简单的起始流程：在评分分层内进行比例采样，随后采用分层人工均值；并指明了为证明对预期评估输出进行校正或自适应所需的额外证据。

Keepace.ai: A Technical Report on a Large Language Model for Sports and Health
Keepace.ai：一款面向体育与健康的大型语言模型技术报告
Large language models hold promise for delivering safe, scientifically grounded, and personalized exercise guidance at scale, yet the sports-and-health domain still lacks curated training corpora and rigorous evaluation benchmarks. Keepace.ai is a domain-specialized LLM built on Qwen3.5-27B through post-training, currently optimized for Chinese-language exercise guidance across three tasks: workout plan generation, sports-and-health knowledge QA, and personal sports data analysis. In internal rubric-based evaluations designed with domain experts, Keepace.ai outperforms its base model and larger general-purpose baselines, with the most pronounced improvements in exercise safety, boundary compliance, structured output completeness, and scientific accuracy. This report summarizes the current design, data pipeline, safety-first evaluation framework, and evaluation results of Keepace.ai.

3DCodeEdit: Benchmarking Instruction-Guided 3D Program Editing
3DCodeEdit：指令引导的3D程序编辑基准测试
Language models increasingly create 3D content through code, with practical workflows often relying on editing existing programs rather than regenerating them, for speed and precise control. Such edits require understanding what a program builds and foreseeing how a change reshapes it, yet prior benchmarks mostly evaluate generation or simple edits against a reference. In this paper, we introduce 3DCodeEdit, a benchmark of 2,592 instruction-guided editing tasks over 108 Blender programs that construct objects entirely in code. Its edits range from parameter to structural changes, style redesign, and scene composition, and are judged from renders against human-reviewed checklists. Across 17 models, the average success rate is only 37%, and even the strongest models complete fewer than 60% of structural and style edits. As models become more capable, their failures shift to geometric foresight, where the code runs but builds the wrong shape. We therefore study whether multi-turn feedback can compensate for this missing foresight. Feedback from a separate reviewer model that describes how the renders differ from the request helps most, more than maximum reasoning effort or an agent harness, and can let a 27B model surpass the single-shot rate of a 2.8T-parameter model.
中文翻译
语言模型越来越多地通过代码生成3D内容，而实际工作流通常依赖于编辑现有程序而非重新生成，以实现速度和精确控制。此类编辑需要理解程序所构建的内容，并预见更改如何重塑其结构，但以往基准大多仅评估生成或简单编辑与参考结果的对比。本文提出3DCodeEdit，一个包含2,592个指令引导编辑任务的基准，覆盖108个完全通过代码构建物体的Blender程序。其编辑范围涵盖参数调整、结构变更、风格重设计和场景组合，并基于渲染图像与人工审核清单进行评判。在17个模型中，平均成功率仅为37%，即使最强模型也未能完成超过60%的结构和风格编辑。随着模型能力提升，其失败逐渐转向几何预见能力不足——代码运行但构建出错误形状。因此，我们研究多轮反馈是否能弥补这一缺失的预见能力。来自独立评审模型的反馈，描述渲染结果与请求之间的差异，效果最佳，优于最大推理努力或代理框架，甚至能让一个27B模型超越2.8T参数模型的单次执行成功率。

Two Birds with One Stone: Skill-Augmented Generation via Unified Retrieval and Utilization
一石二鸟：通过统一检索与利用实现技能增强生成
Skills, as reusable external knowledge, have demonstrated substantial potential to enhance the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks. However, existing skill-augmented generation methods typically rely on separate models for skill retrieval and skill utilization, resulting in disconnected optimization of these two processes. To address this gap, we propose Skill-Uni, a unified reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes skill retrieval and utilization. Specifically, we adopt a generative skill retrieval paradigm that enables a single model to both learn how to retrieve relevant skills and leverage them for task solving. To effectively train this unified framework, we introduce a two-stage training strategy consisting of warm-up instruction tuning and GRPO, allowing the model to progressively improve its skill retrieval and utilization capabilities. Extensive experiments on five skill-related benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Skill-Uni, yielding average performance gains of 66.59% and 34.82% over the base Qwen3-4B and LLaMA3.1-8B models, respectively. Besides, Skill-Uni is compatible with various skill retrieval engines, demonstrating its flexibility and generalizability. Additional in-depth analyses also provide insights into the effectiveness of Skill-Uni.
中文翻译
技能作为可复用的外部知识，在增强大型语言模型（LLMs）解决复杂任务的能力方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，现有的技能增强生成方法通常依赖于独立的模型分别进行技能检索和技能利用，导致这两个过程的优化相互脱节。为解决这一问题，我们提出Skill-Uni，一个统一的强化学习框架，联合优化技能检索与利用。具体而言，我们采用生成式技能检索范式，使单一模型既能学习如何检索相关技能，又能利用这些技能完成任务。为有效训练此统一框架，我们引入两阶段训练策略：预热指令微调与GRPO，使模型逐步提升其技能检索与利用能力。在五个与技能相关的基准上的大量实验表明，Skill-Uni表现优异，相较于基础Qwen3-4B和LLaMA3.1-8B模型，平均性能分别提升66.59%和34.82%。此外，Skill-Uni兼容多种技能检索引擎，展现出良好的灵活性与泛化能力。额外的深入分析也为Skill-Uni的有效性提供了洞见。

Scaling Lean Theorem Proving for Real-World Code Verification
面向现实世界代码验证的轻量级定理证明规模化
We present EasyProver, a Lean proving system built from recursive agents with isolated contexts, asynchronous search, parallel tactic probing, and an elastic elaboration service. Using an off-the-shelf frontier model with no additional training, it proves 669/672 PutnamBench problems and all six IMO 2026 formalizations and resolves 597/600 NTP4VC verification conditions and all 189 VERINA tasks by proof or refutation. Because these benchmarks are close to saturation, we also introduce Aegis, which converts competitive programming problems into much harder verification tasks over real implementations. The pipeline writes safe Rust solutions, translates them into Lean with Aeneas, generates specifications, and labels each frozen statement with a kernel-checked proof or refutation. It produces 2,045 differentially validated Rust-Lean program pairs and 11,533 candidate specifications. Unlike hand-written benchmarks, these tasks carry implementation-level obligations from fixed-width arithmetic, explicit failure, generated loops, casts, and functionalized mutation. On a stratified sample of 2,000 statements, EasyProver proves 1,054 (52.7%) within a 30-minute budget per statement. The same tasks can also be used to benchmark other provers and as rewards for reinforcement learning.
中文翻译
我们提出EasyProver，一个基于递归智能体、隔离上下文、异步搜索、并行策略探测和弹性展开服务的Lean证明系统。使用无需额外训练的现成前沿模型，它成功证明了672个PutnamBench问题中的669个，全部六个IMO 2026形式化任务，以及597/600个NTP4VC验证条件和全部189个VERINA任务（通过证明或反证）。由于这些基准接近饱和，我们还引入Aegis，将竞赛编程问题转化为针对真实实现的更难验证任务。该流程编写安全的Rust解决方案，使用Aeneas将其转换为Lean，生成规范，并为每个冻结语句标记内核检查的证明或反证。共生成2,045对差异化验证的Rust-Lean程序对和11,533个候选规范。与手工编写基准不同，这些任务包含来自固定宽度算术、显式失败、生成循环、类型转换和函数化变异的实现级义务。在2,000条语句的分层抽样中，EasyProver在每条语句30分钟预算内证明了1,054条（52.7%）。这些任务也可用于其他证明器的基准测试，或作为强化学习的奖励信号。

ATI-OPD: Adaptive Teacher Intervention for Stable Long-Sequence On-Policy Distillation
ATI-OPD：稳定长序列在线策略蒸馏的自适应教师干预
On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level feedback from a teacher. As responses grow longer, the student's prefix can depart from the teacher's own reasoning, weakening its feedback on later tokens. Updates based on this feedback can reinforce the same deviations in later responses and destabilize training. Prior approaches such as length control and loss reweighting limit learning from problematic responses but leave their prefixes unchanged. Fixed cutoffs can also discard later reasoning. We propose \textbf{Adaptive Teacher Intervention for On-Policy Distillation (ATI-OPD)} to improve subsequent teacher guidance by revising the student's reasoning prefix. ATI-OPD replaces the earliest high-disagreement segment with a short teacher segment, then lets the student resume its own reasoning from the revised text. To handle renewed disagreement, it excludes persistently divergent suffixes from the distillation loss while retaining useful student continuation. Training diagnostics show less teacher-student disagreement, fewer gradient spikes, and fewer extremely long responses. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two teacher-student pairs, ATI-OPD improves average student-only accuracy over standard OPD.
中文翻译
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）利用教师在token级别提供的反馈来训练学生模型。随着响应变长，学生的前缀可能偏离教师的推理路径，从而削弱其对后续token的反馈作用。基于此类反馈的更新可能在后续响应中强化相同偏差，导致训练不稳定。先前方法如长度控制和损失重加权虽能限制从有问题响应中学习，但未改变其前缀；固定截断也可能丢弃后期推理内容。我们提出 extbf{自适应教师干预在线策略蒸馏（ATI-OPD）}，通过修正学生推理前缀来改善后续教师指导。ATI-OPD用简短的教师段落替换最早出现高分歧的部分，然后让学生从修改后的文本继续自主推理。为应对重新出现的分歧，它排除持续分歧的后缀，同时保留学生有用的延续部分。训练诊断显示教师与学生分歧减少、梯度尖峰减少、极端长响应也减少。在五个数学推理基准和两个教师-学生组合上，ATI-OPD相比标准OPD提升了学生独立准确率。
👤 Jiannan Guan, Youxin Jiang, Yutai Hou, Qiguang Chen, Yirong Zeng et al. 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

Nemotron 3.5 Super and Lightning: Open, Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Models for Agentic AI
Nemotron 3.5 Super 和 Lightning：面向代理AI的开源高效混合专家Mamba-Transformer模型
We introduce Nemotron 3.5 Super and Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, open and efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer models designed for reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic workloads. Super is a 120B-parameter, 12B-active multimodal model, while Lightning is a smaller 30B-parameter, 3B-active text model; both designed for efficient deployment. Both models combine large-scale distillation, long-context training, and native speculative decoding, while Super further incorporates Indic continued pretraining and agentic software-engineering mid-training. We develop a comprehensive post-training pipeline spanning supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning (RLVR and RLHF), complemented in Super by specialized teacher training and multi- teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). This transforms Super from a text-only base model into a multimodal agent capable of reasoning over text, images, video, and interactive tool environments. We further explore test-time scaling through parallel agent execution and self-verification. Across training and deployment, we jointly optimize capability and efficiency. Indic continued pretraining improves Indian-language benchmarks by up to 9.82 percentage points, while agentic mid-training strengthens software-engineering capabilities and robustness across agent harnesses. The post-trained Super model achieves 51.78% on SWE-bench Pro, surpassing Nemotron 3 Ultra despite using approximately 4.6x fewer active parameters. Post-training MTP boosting improves speculative- decoding acceptance length by up to 21.8% under probabilistic draft sampling, while NVFP4 quantization achieves 99.62% median accuracy recovery relative to BF16. Inference optimizations enable up to 4,856 output tokens/s/GPU on GB200 while sustaining at least 50 tokens/s/user.

QMUL_AK at AISA-ArabicFC: Schema-Constrained Function Calling with ALLaM
QMUL_AK在AISA-ArabicFC中的表现：使用ALLaM的模式约束函数调用
We describe the QMUL_AK submission to AISA-ArabicFC 2026. The system adapts the Arabic-English ALLaM-7B-Instruct model to function calling with low-rank adaptation (LoRA). It combines an Arabic schema-aware prompt, dialect and no-call resampling, a token-weighted training objective, and deterministic validation of generated calls against the candidate tool schema. The same output supports Track A function calling and Track B reasoning-augmented calling. Training uses only the official task data. On the development set, the system achieves 0.9982 function accuracy and 0.8920 argument exact match, corresponding to overall A and B scores of 0.9345 and 0.9454. On the blind test set, the system reaches overall A and B scores of 0.9253 and 0.9380, ranking 3rd on both submission tracks and 1st of 21 teams on the Track C dialect-robustness diagnostic with a macro-averaged score of 0.9075 and the smallest dialect gap in the shared task (0.077). The results show that function selection is nearly saturated, while argument extraction remains the main source of error.

agent-memoryagentic-rl
FAD-KV: Adaptive Dictionary KV Caching for Efficient Reasoning Decoding
FAD-KV：用于高效推理解码的自适应字典KV缓存
Key-value (KV) caching is a core mechanism in large language models, where intermediate key and value representations are stored to accelerate autoregressive decoding. As generation length increases, KV cache memory and computation grow linearly, making compression critical for efficient long-output reasoning. Dictionary-based sparse KV compression is appealing because it represents each token's KV states as a sparse combination of learned basis elements (atoms) in a dictionary, preserving token-level structure without enforcing a global low-rank constraint. However, existing dictionary-based methods face two practical limitations during decoding: iterative sparse coding methods such as Orthogonal Matching Pursuit add decoding-time overhead, and static dictionaries may mismatch evolving KV distributions under test-time generation. While adaptive dictionary updates can reduce this mismatch, naive append-only strategies lead to dictionary growth during generation. We propose FAD-KV, a \textbf{F}ast \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{D}ictionary KV caching framework for efficient reasoning decoding. FAD-KV integrates (i) a screening-based sparse coding scheme, where a one-shot Top-\(n\) selection identifies a small candidate pool followed by lightweight coefficient refinement, and (ii) a fixed-size dictionary adaptation mechanism that reuses inactive atoms instead of expanding the dictionary. By controlling both sparse coding cost and adaptive dictionary growth, FAD-KV provides a memory-bounded and computation-efficient KV compression framework for long reasoning workloads. Experiments on reasoning and long-context benchmarks show that FAD-KV achieves a strong quality-efficiency trade-off while avoiding unbounded dictionary growth during decoding.
中文翻译
键值（KV）缓存是大语言模型中的核心机制，通过存储中间键和值表示来加速自回归解码。随着生成长度增加，KV缓存的内存和计算量呈线性增长，因此压缩对于高效长输出推理至关重要。基于字典的稀疏KV压缩方法具有吸引力，因为它将每个标记的KV状态表示为字典中学习到的基础元素（原子）的稀疏组合，保留了标记级结构且无需施加全局低秩约束。然而，现有的基于字典的方法在解码过程中面临两个实际限制：如正交匹配追踪等迭代稀疏编码方法会增加解码时间开销，而静态字典可能无法匹配测试时生成过程中演变的KV分布。虽然自适应字典更新可减少这种不匹配，但简单的仅追加策略会导致字典在生成过程中不断增长。我们提出了FAD-KV，一种用于高效推理解码的快速自适应字典KV缓存框架。FAD-KV集成了（i）基于筛选的稀疏编码方案，通过一次性的Top-n选择确定小候选池，随后进行轻量级系数优化；以及（ii）固定大小的字典自适应机制，通过重用未活跃的原子而非扩展字典。通过同时控制稀疏编码成本和自适应字典增长，FAD-KV为长推理任务提供了内存受限且计算高效的KV压缩框架。在推理和长上下文基准上的实验表明，FAD-KV在保持高质量的同时实现了良好的效率权衡，并避免了解码过程中的字典无界增长。
👤 Yuxuan Zhu, David H. Yang, Parisa Shiravani, Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri, Tejaswini Pedapati et al. 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37826]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
DeFT-RAG: Incrementally Decomposing Multi-Hop Questions into Self-Contained Sub-Questions with Reusable Answers
DeFT-RAG：通过可复用答案增量分解多跳问题为自包含子问题
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) lets a large language model (LLM) answer questions by retrieving relevant passages from a large corpus. However, RAG struggles with multi-hop questions, which require chaining facts spread across several documents through intermediate entities that the question never mentions. Graph-based RAG methods retrieve once from the entities a question names and so rarely recover the full evidence chain. Iterative RAG methods recover it step by step, but their accuracy is limited by the many tasks they ask the LLM to perform, such as planning the sub-questions, deciding their dependencies, rewriting them as answers arrive, and keeping track of what is known. We propose DeFT-RAG, a RAG framework built on three components. First, DeFT-RAG generates one self-contained sub-question at a time from the original question and the sub-questions answered so far with their answers (QA pairs). Each sub-question names every entity it asks about, which removes the need to plan, track dependencies or rewrite, and lets the LLM focus on a single fine-grained task at a time. Second, DeFT-RAG moves the remaining LLM work outside the LLM: rule-based updates to a QA table that keeps track of what is known, and yes/no decisions made by a fine-tuned lightweight decision model. Third, DeFT-RAG caches the QA pairs and reuses an answer when a later question asks a sub-question with the same meaning. On MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA and HotpotQA, DeFT-RAG achieves the best accuracy among thirteen baselines with both gpt-4o-mini and Qwen3.6 as the backbone, while giving the LLM fewer input tokens than any other iterative method. With a QA cache shared across 1,000 MuSiQue questions, 23.1% of sub-questions are answered from the cache, which reduces LLM calls by 12.9% without lowering accuracy.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）让大语言模型（LLM）通过从大型语料库中检索相关段落来回答问题。然而，RAG在处理多跳问题时表现不佳，这类问题需要通过问题中从未提及的中间实体，将分散在多个文档中的事实串联起来。基于图的RAG方法仅从问题中命名的实体中检索一次，因此很少能恢复完整的证据链。迭代式RAG方法逐步恢复证据链，但其准确性受限于LLM需执行的大量任务，如规划子问题、决定依赖关系、在答案到达后重写问题，以及跟踪已知信息。我们提出DeFT-RAG，一个由三个组件构成的RAG框架。首先，DeFT-RAG从原始问题及已回答的子问题及其答案（QA对）中逐个生成自包含的子问题。每个子问题明确命名其所询问的所有实体，从而无需规划、跟踪依赖关系或重写，使LLM能够一次专注于单一细粒度任务。其次，DeFT-RAG将剩余的LLM工作移出LLM：通过基于规则的QA表更新以跟踪已知信息，以及由微调的轻量决策模型做出的yes/no判断。第三，DeFT-RAG缓存QA对，并在后续问题询问具有相同含义的子问题时复用答案。在MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA和HotpotQA上，DeFT-RAG在使用gpt-4o-mini和Qwen3.6作为骨干模型时，均以十三个基线中最佳的准确率表现，且输入给LLM的token数量少于任何其他迭代方法。在一个共享QA缓存覆盖1,000个MuSiQue问题的情况下，23.1%的子问题从缓存中回答，减少了12.9%的LLM调用次数而未降低准确率。
👤 Dingyi Kang, Bingzhe Li 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37778]
agentic-rl
Multi-Draft On-Policy Distillation for Diffusion Drafters
用于扩散草稿生成器的多草稿在线策略蒸馏
Speculative decoding is used to reduce the inference cost of language models. One-step diffusion drafters propose a block of multiple tokens in parallel, reducing the sequential cost of speculative decoding. On-policy distillation trains these drafters using verifier feedback on their own proposals. However, parallel drafting makes this feedback difficult to use: the drafter predicts each token without observing the preceding tokens sampled within the same block, while the verifier conditions its prediction on those tokens. Different sampled prefixes therefore give different verifier targets for the same drafter prediction. We show that distilling against these targets separately can suppress tokens that are plausible after some prefixes but unlikely after others, harming draft acceptance. We explain the effects of this mismatch and introduce Multi-Draft On-Policy Distillation (MDraft), a new parallel drafter distillation method which samples several draft blocks from the same context and aggregates the verifier's full-vocabulary conditionals across their prefixes. We use all the drafts to collect information from the verifier, allowing support from alternative prefixes to influence the training target. The resulting algorithm improves the acceptance length of a parallel drafter beyond the level of pre-training, and alleviates the effects of the distributional mismatch. MDraft changes only training, retaining the drafter's architecture and inference procedure, allowing the resulting drafter to be used as-is in place of the pretrained checkpoint. Experiments on a suite of mathematical, coding, and conversational tasks show longer accepted drafts than prior distillation methods.
中文翻译
推测解码被用于降低语言模型的推理成本。单步扩散草稿生成器并行生成多个标记块，从而减少推测解码的序列开销。在线策略蒸馏利用验证器对其自身提议的反馈来训练这些草稿生成器。然而，并行草稿使得这种反馈难以使用：草稿生成器在未观察到同一块内先前采样标记的情况下预测每个标记，而验证器则基于这些标记进行判断。因此，不同的采样前缀会对同一草稿生成器的预测产生不同的验证器目标。我们发现，分别对这些目标进行蒸馏可能会抑制那些在某些前缀后合理但在其他前缀后不太可能的标记，从而损害草稿接受率。我们解释了这种不匹配的影响，并提出了一种新的并行草稿生成器蒸馏方法——多草稿在线策略蒸馏（MDraft），该方法从相同上下文中采样多个草稿块，并聚合它们前缀上的验证器全词汇条件概率。我们利用所有草稿从验证器中收集信息，使替代前缀的支持能够影响训练目标。所提出的算法在并行草稿生成器的接受长度上超越了预训练水平，并缓解了分布不匹配的影响。MDraft仅改变训练过程，保留了草稿生成器的架构和推理流程，使得生成的草稿生成器可直接替换预训练检查点使用。在数学、编程和对话任务的一系列实验中，其生成的可接受草稿长度优于以往的蒸馏方法。
👤 Petr Molodyk, Yuchen Zhu, Jaemoo Choi, Molei Tao, Yongxin Chen 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

From Geometry to Behavior: How Instruction Exposure Unlocks Latent Rhetorical Directions in LLMs
从几何到行为：指令暴露如何解锁大语言模型中的潜在修辞方向
Language models do not merely memorize facts; they absorb rhetorical strategies from pretraining data, including the human tendency to reason by analogy. We ask: is this absorbed capability geometrically accessible before any instruction tuning, and what determines whether it can be activated at inference time? We extract an "Analogy Vector" from the residual stream via difference-in-means and show it enables steerable explanation style across seven instruction-tuned models (Gemma-2, Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5; 2B-32B), improving harmonic mean from 3.22 to 3.94 (+22%) for Gemma-2-9B-IT on 300 held-out test prompts. A small human validation study confirms the LLM judge on Analogy Quality. Our central finding, from seven matched base-instruct pairs, is a gradient of behavioral accessibility: the analogy direction exists in every base model, but its behavioral accessibility under linear intervention scales continuously with instruction exposure during training. Gemma-2 base models (strict pretraining/IT separation) show flat steering landscapes: present but behaviorally inert. Qwen-2.5 base models (instruction data woven into pretraining) are already partially accessible before any explicit fine-tuning; base-instruct cosine similarity of the analogy vector reaches 0.94-0.97 for Qwen versus 0.37-0.66 for Gemma, providing geometric corroboration of the behavioral gradient. This challenges the binary "base vs instruct" framing: the relevant variable is how much instruction exposure a model has received across its full training pipeline. We position activation steering as a complementary interpretability tool that reveals how instruction exposure organizes rhetorical knowledge in activation space.
中文翻译
语言模型不仅记忆事实，还从预训练数据中吸收修辞策略，包括人类倾向于类比推理的倾向。我们提出的问题是：这种吸收的能力在指令微调之前是否在几何空间中可访问？在推理时能否被激活，又取决于什么因素？我们通过均值差法从残差流中提取出一个“类比向量”，并在七个指令微调模型（Gemma-2、Llama-3.1、Qwen-2.5；2B-32B）中实现了可调控的解释风格，使Gemma-2-9B-IT在300个独立测试提示上的调和平均得分从3.22提升至3.94（+22%）。一次小型人工验证研究证实了LLM裁判对类比质量的判断能力。我们的核心发现来自七组匹配的基线-指令对：类比方向存在于每个基线模型中，但其行为可访问性在训练过程中随指令暴露程度连续变化。Gemma-2基线模型（严格区分预训练与指令微调）表现出平坦的操控景观：存在但行为上惰性。而Qwen-2.5基线模型（指令数据融入预训练）在无显式微调前已部分可访问；其基线-指令类比向量余弦相似度在Qwen中达到0.94-0.97，在Gemma中仅为0.37-0.66，这为行为梯度提供了几何佐证。这一发现挑战了“基线 vs 指令”的二元划分：真正关键变量是模型在整个训练流程中接收到的指令暴露量。我们把激活操控定位为一种互补的可解释性工具，揭示了指令暴露如何在激活空间中组织修辞知识。

An Introduction to Large Language Models for Scientists and Professionals
面向科学家和专业人士的大语言模型入门
Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly become important tools for science, education, medicine, and professional work. Despite their apparent simplicity from the user's perspective, effective and responsible use requires an understanding of how these systems work, what their limitations are, and how their outputs should be evaluated. I wrote this document as a basis for my lecture on LLM in the master in artificial intelligence in medicine at the university of Turin, Italy. This book provides a practical and accessible introduction to LLMs for scientists and professionals. It explains the fundamental ideas behind tokenisation, embeddings, training, inference, context windows, prompting, and instruction hierarchies, while deliberately avoiding mathematical detail. Particular attention is given to the limitations of LLM-generated answers, including hallucinations, uncertainty, reproducibility, bias, and the need for systematic verification and human oversight. The book also discusses privacy, governance, security, and the responsible handling of personal and sensitive information. More advanced topics include Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval quality, chunking, embeddings, vector search, agents, tools, and workflows. A substantial practical exercise guides the reader through running a local LLM with Ollama and Open WebUI and using it to interact with a collection of scientific papers. The exercise emphasizes evidence retrieval, source verification, uncertainty, disagreement between studies, and the distinction between plausible language and scientifically supported conclusions. No programming knowledge is required.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）已迅速成为科学、教育、医学及专业工作中的重要工具。尽管从用户角度看其使用看似简单，但有效且负责任地使用仍需理解这些系统的工作原理、局限性以及输出评估方法。本文是我为意大利都灵大学人工智能硕士课程中关于LLM的讲座所撰写的讲义基础。本书为科学家和专业人士提供了一个实用且易懂的LLM入门指南。它解释了分词、嵌入、训练、推理、上下文窗口、提示工程、指令层级等基本概念，刻意避免数学细节。特别关注LLM生成答案的局限性，包括幻觉、不确定性、可复现性、偏见，以及系统性验证和人工监督的必要性。本书还讨论了隐私、治理、安全及个人和敏感信息的负责任处理。更高级的主题包括检索增强生成（RAG）、检索质量、分块、嵌入、向量搜索、智能体、工具和工作流。书中包含一个大规模实践练习，指导读者使用Ollama和Open WebUI在本地运行LLM，并与一组科学论文进行交互。练习强调证据检索、来源验证、研究间的分歧、以及自然语言合理性与科学支持结论之间的区别。无需编程知识即可完成。

Prompt-agnostic Language Models
提示无关的语言模型
Despite their strong performance, large language models remain highly sensitive to prompt formulation. This issue is typically addressed at inference-time through manual prompt optimization, which introduces overhead and lacks scalability. Conversely, we focus on train-time approaches that build prompt robustness into the model during instruction tuning. A common strategy is to mix multiple paraphrases of each prompt into training. We demonstrate that across trainable parameters, the per-paraphrasing gradients interfere, with 57% exhibiting sign conflicts and the naive gradient average discarding approximately a fifth of the magnitude. Therefore, we develop two gradient-merging methods, Prompt-TIES and Prompt-DARE, that address this interference by leveraging interference-resolution operators from model and gradient merging and applying them to prompt gradients. In this study, we compare the efficacy of our methods to increase model robustness to train-time robustness methods spanning all three established classes: data construction, consistency regularization, and contrastive alignment. The evaluation is conducted across four models from two families at two scales. We observe the interaction between prompt phrasing and the specific examples accounts for 54% of per-example variability on average, and exceeds 70% on tasks such as ANLI and WiC. The gradient-merging methods directly improve this local robustness and nearly halve per-example variance on fully fine-tuned models at little cost to overall accuracy. The other train-time methods enhance raw performance, but fail to improve per-example variance reliably. Code: https://github.com/FSadrieh/prompt-agnostic-llms. Model checkpoints: https://huggingface.co/prompt-agnostic-language-models.
中文翻译
尽管表现强劲，大型语言模型对提示的构造仍高度敏感。这一问题通常通过推理时的手动提示优化来解决，但会引入开销且缺乏可扩展性。相反，我们关注训练时的方法，在指令微调过程中将提示鲁棒性嵌入模型。一种常见策略是将每个提示的多种改写形式混合到训练中。我们发现，在可训练参数中，不同改写形式的梯度存在干扰，57%的梯度符号冲突，而简单的梯度平均会丢弃约五分之一的梯度幅值。因此，我们提出了两种梯度融合方法——Prompt-TIES 和 Prompt-DARE，通过利用模型与梯度融合中的干扰消除算子来处理这种干扰，并将其应用于提示梯度。在本研究中，我们将这些方法与三种已建立类别的训练时鲁棒性方法（数据构建、一致性正则化、对比对齐）进行比较。评估覆盖了来自两个家族的四种模型，规模各异。我们发现，提示表述与具体示例之间的交互平均解释了54%的样本间差异，而在ANLI和WiC等任务上超过70%。梯度融合方法直接提升了局部鲁棒性，在几乎不牺牲整体准确率的情况下，使全微调模型的样本间方差几乎减半。其他训练时方法虽提升了原始性能，但未能可靠改善样本间方差。代码：https://github.com/FSadrieh/prompt-agnostic-llms。模型检查点：https://huggingface.co/prompt-agnostic-language-models。

Supervised Fine-Tuning for Reasoning Large Language Models: A Survey
面向推理型大语言模型的监督微调：一项综述
Large reasoning models have recently demonstrated remarkable capabilities in solving complex tasks, where supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on long chain-of-thought data serves as a crucial foundation for eliciting and enhancing their reasoning abilities. Despite rapid progress in both improving and analyzing reasoning-oriented SFT, the field still lacks a systematic survey that consolidates its fast-growing literature. To fill this gap, we present a comprehensive review of recent advancements in reasoning SFT, examining the literature through the dual lenses of methodological design and analytical investigation. First, we review methodological improvements across the SFT pipeline and categorize them into data-centric approaches and algorithm-centric innovations. Second, we reorganize analytical studies along three dimensions: data characteristics, optimization dynamics, and mechanistic insights. Finally, by synthesizing current research focuses and remaining bottlenecks, we outline promising future directions for reasoning SFT. We hope this survey deepens the understanding of reasoning SFT and paves the way for advanced reasoning models.
中文翻译
近期，大型推理模型在解决复杂任务方面展现出显著能力，其中在长思维链数据上进行的监督微调（SFT）构成了激发和增强其推理能力的关键基础。尽管在改进和分析面向推理的SFT方面取得了快速进展，但该领域仍缺乏系统性的综述来整合快速增长的文献。为填补这一空白，我们对推理型SFT的最新进展进行了全面回顾，从方法设计与分析探究两个维度审视相关研究。首先，我们回顾了SFT流程中的方法学改进，并将其分为以数据为中心的方法和以算法为中心的创新。其次，我们从三个维度重新组织了分析性研究：数据特征、优化动态与机制洞察。最后，通过综合当前研究重点与遗留瓶颈，我们勾勒出推理型SFT未来可能的发展方向。我们希望这项综述能深化对推理型SFT的理解，并为先进推理模型的发展铺平道路。

Exposure Is Not Commitment: Double-Stratified Evaluation of Latent Monitors for Tool-Using Agents
曝光并非承诺：工具使用智能体潜在监控器的双重分层评估
Tool-using language agents are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection, and a growing literature probes model internals to catch unsafe actions before they execute, with sharply conflicting results. We argue much of the conflict is an evaluation artifact, and propose a double-stratification protocol: separate injection exposure from behavioral commitment, in both the evaluation and the training population. On the primary Qwen AgentDojo grid, monitors trained on the naive mixed unsafe-versus-rest contrast mostly learn exposure: they flag exposed trajectories whether or not the agent complies, and barely predict which cases turn unsafe. Retrained on exposed cases only, the same probe families predict the model's own unsafe tool call up to two steps early, the maximum the three-step trajectories admit (logistic AUROC 0.853, matched by a trained TF-IDF text baseline at 0.855). Mistral-7B reaches 0.896 and OLMo-2-7B 0.912; their naive contrasts learn different things: inverted exposure ranking on one, both axes on the other. Representations separate on exposure (probe 1.000 versus trained text 0.908) and where visible context is sanitized or unavailable. Training contrast alone reproduces the shape of recent pre-action-probe negative results, one candidate explanation for the conflict: the signal exists, but the model's own zero-shot forecast and NLA verbalizations do not reliably recover it. An unstratified AUROC overstates what a latent monitor knows, and what a naive contrast learns is population-dependent: evaluations should stratify both.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言智能体易受间接提示注入攻击，现有大量研究试图通过探测模型内部状态来在执行前捕捉不安全行为，但结果却存在尖锐冲突。我们认为许多冲突源于评估设计的偏差，并提出双重分层协议：在评估和训练数据集中，将提示注入暴露与行为承诺分离。在主Qwen AgentDojo网格上，仅在混合的不安全与正常对比数据上训练的监控器主要学习的是暴露：无论代理是否实际执行，只要轨迹被暴露就会触发警报，且几乎无法预测哪些情况最终会变为不安全。当仅在暴露案例上重新训练时，相同的探测器家族能够提前两步预测模型自身的不安全工具调用（三步轨迹的最大提前量），逻辑回归AUROC达0.853，与训练过的TF-IDF文本基线相当（0.855）。Mistral-7B达到0.896，OLMo-2-7B达到0.912；它们在原始对比中学习的内容不同：一个学习倒置的暴露排序，另一个则学习两个轴的信息。表征在暴露程度（探测器1.000 vs. 训练文本0.908）以及可见上下文是否被清理或不可用方面表现出明显分离。仅使用训练对比即可重现近期预动作探测器负面结果的形态，这可能是冲突的一个解释：信号存在，但模型自身的零样本预测和NLA语义表达无法可靠恢复它。非分层的AUROC会夸大潜在监控器所知内容，而朴素对比学习的内容依赖于群体分布：评估应进行双重分层。
👤 Rishabh Sinha 📅 2026-10-12 🔗 arXiv

The Best Second Opinion Is Right Where the Primary Is Wrong: Partner Selection for Selective Span Tagging
最好的第二意见就在主模型出错之处：选择性跨度标注中的合作伙伴选择
A span tagger that ranks its outputs by risk can defer the riskiest ones to a person, and a second model can sharpen this ranking. Which second model should it be? Partner selection is conditional, not global: the best second opinion is the model that is right where the primary is wrong. Across eight tagger types, three seeds and three English benchmarks (576 evaluated primary-partner pairings), choosing the partner by held-out accuracy on the primary's errors recovers 82% of the best partner's gain, against 69-70% for span F1 or a random pick. Frozen before a fourth dataset was chosen, the rule replicated there (Spearman 0.40 vs. 0.04 for span F1). Partner value is also directional. The predictive distribution is the second opinion: hard labels discard half of a partner's value, and scoring the served label improves 94% of pairings. Fixed before the fourth dataset was chosen, this score also replicated there. For LoRA-tuned decoders, independently trained partners beat every readout of the primary network. Applied zero-shot to 51 MASSIVE locales, every primary has a helpful trained partner in every locale, and the preferred partner follows competence.
中文翻译
一种按风险排序输出的跨度标注器可以将最危险的预测转交给人类处理，而第二个模型可以进一步优化这一排序。应选择哪个第二个模型？合作伙伴的选择是条件性的而非全局的：最佳的第二意见是主模型出错时正确的那个模型。在八种标注器类型、三个种子和三个英文基准（共576个主-伙伴配对）上，通过主模型错误上的保留准确率选择合作伙伴，可恢复最佳合作伙伴82%的增益，而基于跨度F1或随机选择仅能恢复69%-70%。在选定第四数据集前冻结该规则后，其在该数据集上仍表现良好（Spearman相关系数0.40 vs. F1的0.04）。合作伙伴的价值还具有方向性：预测分布即为第二意见；使用硬标签会丢弃一半合作伙伴价值，而对所服务标签进行评分则可改善94%的配对。该评分规则在第四数据集上也成功复现。对于LoRA微调的解码器，独立训练的合作伙伴优于主网络的所有读出方式。零样本应用于51个MASSIVE语言环境时，每个主模型在每个环境中都有一个有用的训练伙伴，且首选伙伴遵循能力优先原则。

TextSeal2: Distortion-Free LLM Watermarking with Copula Mixing and Memoryless Races
TextSeal2：基于Copula混合与无记忆竞赛的无失真LLM水印技术
We present TextSeal2, a distortion-free watermark for large language models that keeps its signal under speculative decoding: tokens that replace rejected drafts remain watermarked with the same keys, and the acceptance rate is unchanged. It also preserves output diversity and adds no measurable inference latency. Its predecessor, TextSeal, restores diversity in the Gumbel-max scheme by routing each token to one of two keys via a coin flip. This is suboptimal because the detector averages the scores from both keys without knowing which key was used. TextSeal2 instead merges two or more keys into a single virtual key and couples it to fresh noise through AutoMark's Gaussian copula. Any number of keys, including publicly shared keys, can be merged this way without diluting detection. Under speculative decoding, TextSeal2 verifies keyed drafts with one more exponential race, run on the leftovers of the draft races. By memorylessness, this race reproduces block verification, so the output follows the target model exactly. We evaluate TextSeal2 on three LLMs. At equal output token diversity, TextSeal2 detects 8 to 22 percentage points more 100-token answers than TextSeal at a false-positive rate of $10^{-3}$, and is comparable to textGrain while requiring no post-logits optimization. Under speculative decoding, it detects 18 and 22 percentage points more 200-token answers than TextSeal and textGrain, respectively, at the same acceptance rate.
中文翻译
我们提出TextSeal2，一种适用于大语言模型的无失真水印技术，可在推测解码下保持信号完整性：替换被拒绝草稿的令牌仍保留相同密钥的水印，且接受率不变。它还保持输出多样性，不增加可测量的推理延迟。其前身TextSeal通过抛硬币将每个令牌路由至两个密钥之一来恢复Gumbel-max方案中的多样性。这并非最优，因为检测器平均两个密钥的得分却不知实际使用的是哪一个。TextSeal2则将两个或多个密钥合并为单一虚拟密钥，并通过AutoMark的高斯Copula将其与新噪声耦合。任意数量的密钥（包括公开共享的密钥）均可如此合并而不削弱检测能力。在推测解码下，TextSeal2通过额外一轮指数竞赛验证带密钥的草稿，该竞赛在草稿竞赛的剩余部分上运行。由于无记忆性，此竞赛重现块级验证，因此输出完全符合目标模型。我们在三个LLM上评估TextSeal2。在同等输出令牌多样性下，TextSeal2在假阳性率为$10^{-3}$时比TextSeal多检测8至22个百分点的100-token答案，且与textGrain相当，无需后logits优化。在推测解码下，其分别比TextSeal和textGrain多检测18和22个百分点的200-token答案，且接受率相同。

VeriCUA: Verification as Agentic Exploration for Computer-Using Agents
VeriCUA：用于计算机使用代理的验证即智能体探索
Building and scaling computer-using agents (CUAs) across desktop, mobile, and web environments requires reliable task-completion verification for data curation, evaluation, and reinforcement learning. Typical automated approaches prompt vision-language models (VLMs) to judge trajectories or generate programs to inspect application state. However, both rely on partial views of complex digital environments. VLM judges are restricted to supplied observations, while generated programs depend on accessible state. These views can omit decisive evidence or leave completion judgments unsupported. Tailoring separate verifiers to different platforms and access settings adds further engineering cost. We present VeriCUA, a platform-agnostic framework for verification through agentic exploration. It treats verification as dynamic evidence gathering and assessment within a shared procedure for online and offline settings across platforms and observation modalities. After inspecting the available context to construct a task rubric, the verifier selects GUI or code checks for unresolved requirements and combines their findings under explicit evidence rules. Experiments show improved agreement with reference labels over the compared baselines across these settings, with the largest offline gains on hard cases and stronger rejection of false completion claims online. Further analyses connect these gains to complementary evidence and test-time exploration.

Ramanujan Challenge Results: AI-Assisted Evaluation Pipeline for Multi-Format Proofs
拉马努金挑战结果：面向多格式证明的AI辅助评估流水线
As AI's mathematical capabilities rapidly improve, meaningful benchmarks are shifting from university-level problems and math olympiads to research-level mathematics. The Ramanujan Challenge is a benchmark comprised of research-level problems in number theory, including unpublished results and open conjectures. It presented concrete formulas for mathematical constants whose correctness can be checked numerically, while their proofs may require non-obvious mathematics. Here, we analyze the Challenge submissions, measuring the relative success of different AI models, Computer Algebra Systems (CAS), and formal tools. We find correct submissions that are conceptually distinct from the official solutions, and a proof of one of the published open conjectures. Evaluating the submissions exposed a second challenge: judging complex proofs reliably at scale. We introduce an AI-assisted evaluation pipeline for natural language proofs, CAS code, and Lean formalizations that assesses semantic and logical correctness. Compared with the conventional one-shot LLM judgment, our pipeline reduces false-positive verdicts on natural language proofs from 23% to 6% and improves judgment accuracy on supporting code from 46% to 58%. This approach provides a methodology for evaluating research-level mathematics at scale, with potential applications across mathematics and AI for science.

On the Fundamental Limits of Prefill-Based Decoding Length Prediction
基于预填充的解码长度预测的根本极限
Predicting how many tokens a large language model (LLM) will decode, using only prefill-phase hidden states, is a key primitive for inference scheduling. We show that current benchmarks conflate predictor quality with task hardness: when the target model, dataset, or sampling configuration changes, reported accuracy collapses even though the predictor is unchanged. We trace this instability to an overlooked error source---the inherent stochasticity of autoregressive decoding---and derive bounds that every prefill-side method must obey. Our analysis treats decoding as a stopped stochastic process. The per-step stopping probability of a response is not constant but ramps up over hundreds of steps, so the decode length inherits a central limit theorem from the many local decisions that determine where a response ends; this yields per-prompt normality with an explicit error term, together with an affine variance law $σ=αμ+b$ whose slope is the coefficient of variation of the number of response segments. Empirically, the normal law reproduces the per-prompt MAE and the central mass of the decode-length distribution to within a few percent, and the residual departures from normality (mild right skew, slight excess kurtosis) are the ones predicted by the sampling-noise correction. Under fixed model, data, and sampling conditions, a broad design space of predictors (MLP, EGTP, FiLM, soft-label, and Mixture-of-Experts variants) converges to similar performance close to the bound, confirming that \emph{system error dominates model error}. We argue for a benchmarking protocol that fixes the model, data, and sampling parameters and that reports relative-error metrics alongside absolute ones. Code and analysis tools are available at https://github.com/ShwStone/DecodingPredict.
中文翻译
仅使用预填充阶段的隐藏状态来预测大语言模型（LLM）将解码多少个标记，是推理调度中的一个关键基础。我们指出，当前基准测试混淆了预测器质量与任务难度：当目标模型、数据集或采样配置发生变化时，报告的准确性即使预测器不变也会崩溃。我们追溯这种不稳定性源于被忽视的误差来源——自回归解码的固有随机性，并推导出所有基于预填充的方法必须遵守的理论边界。我们的分析将解码视为一个停止的随机过程。响应每一步的停止概率并非恒定，而是经过数百步逐步上升，因此解码长度继承了大量局部决策决定结束位置的中心极限定理特性；这导致每个提示的解码长度近似正态分布，并带有明确的误差项，同时呈现出仿射方差律 $σ=αμ+b$，其斜率是响应段数的变异系数。实证结果显示，正态律对每个提示的MAE以及解码长度分布的主体部分的拟合误差在百分之几以内，残差偏离（轻微右偏、略高尖峰）也与采样噪声修正预测一致。在固定模型、数据和采样条件下，多种预测器设计（MLP、EGTP、FiLM、软标签、专家混合变体）均收敛到接近理论边界的相似性能，证实了‘系统误差主导模型误差’。我们主张应采用固定模型、数据和采样参数的基准协议，并同时报告相对误差指标与绝对误差指标。代码与分析工具可在 https://github.com/ShwStone/DecodingPredict 获取。

Preserving Utility in Continual LLM Unlearning via Orthogonal Projections
通过正交投影保持持续大语言模型遗忘中的效用
Unlearning concerns the task of removing the influence of a sensitive portion of the training data, such as protected, copyrighted or private text, from a model without retraining it from scratch. While unlearning requests happen continuously during a model's lifetime, the vast majority of LLM unlearning methods are ill-suited for iterated removal, yielding steep decay in forgetting and utility upon sequential unlearning. This decay stems from interference between successive updates, as each new deletion can undo earlier ones or push the model further along directions already degraded by them. To address this, we introduce Continual Orthogonal Projection (COP), a gradient-projection scheme that restricts each update to the orthogonal complement of activation subspaces cached from prior forget sets. This approach provably guarantees first-order non-interference with earlier deletions. COP stores only a low-rank basis per layer, requiring neither past gradients nor previous forget sets, and plugs into unlearning methods that update the model through gradient steps on a differentiable loss without modifying its objective. Experiments on standard LLM unlearning benchmarks (TOFU and RWKU) show that COP preserves utility across sequential requests without weakening forgetting, preventing the collapse of standard objectives and forgetting more effectively than continual-specific baselines.
中文翻译
遗忘是指在不从头训练的情况下，从模型中移除敏感训练数据（如受保护、受版权或私密文本）的影响。尽管在模型生命周期中遗忘请求持续发生，但绝大多数大语言模型遗忘方法不适用于迭代删除，导致连续遗忘时遗忘效果和模型效用急剧下降。这种衰减源于后续更新之间的干扰：每次新删除可能抵消先前的删除，或将模型推向已受损的方向。为解决此问题，我们提出持续正交投影（COP），一种梯度投影方案，将每次更新限制在先前遗忘集合缓存的激活子空间的正交补空间内。该方法可证明保证与早期删除的一阶非干扰性。COP每层仅存储一个低秩基，无需历史梯度或之前的遗忘集合，并可无缝集成到通过可微损失进行梯度更新的遗忘方法中，而无需修改其目标函数。在标准大语言模型遗忘基准测试（TOFU 和 RWKU）上的实验表明，COP在连续请求下保持了模型效用，同时未削弱遗忘能力，防止了标准目标的崩溃，且比专门针对持续遗忘的基线方法更有效地实现遗忘。

Training language models in simulation for psychiatric intake
在模拟环境中训练语言模型以进行精神科初诊
In psychiatric practice, the clinical intake interview remains a primary diagnostic instrument, requiring the clinician to establish rapport while disentangling complex, overlapping presentations. Here, we show that multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation environments improves language model performance on structured text-based psychiatric intake, spanning history-taking, differential diagnosis, and initial management planning. In a randomized, double-blind crossover study ($N = 60$ matched scenario pairs, 120 encounters) comparing the RL-trained agent against its identically prompted base model, 32 board-certified psychiatrists role-played standardized patients while a separate panel of 15 independent, board-certified psychiatrists scored the de-identified transcripts. The trained agent outperformed the base model in 76.7% of matched scenario pairs ($p < 0.0001$), with gains on an adapted clinical-examination rubric ($d_z = 0.77$, $p < 0.0001$), patient-centered communication (82.0% vs. 65.2%, $p < 0.001$), and differential diagnosis appropriateness (68.3% vs. 33.3%, $p < 0.001$). Across 127 psychiatrist-validated simulated intake scenarios, the trained agent nearly doubled its base model's clinical rubric score (0.706 vs. 0.379; composite reward 0.564 vs. 0.294, $p < 0.0001$). To systematically probe conversational safety, we established two Delphi-consensus taxonomies, of clinical safety risks and relational competencies, from which 256 challenging adversarial presentations were derived; we find that training in simulation environments more than halved under-triage of acute safety risks (12.9% vs. 30.5%, $p < 0.0001$). Simulation environments thus provide a controlled testbed for training and stress-testing psychiatric conversational agents, with gains that transferred to psychiatrist-enacted encounters, though residual gaps require safeguards and clinician oversight.

👤 Samuel Schmidgall, Tim Strother, Alex Bijamov, Min Woo Sun, Anil Palepu et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Prefix Executability: Evaluating Tool-Using Agents Beyond Final Success
前缀可执行性：超越最终成功的工具使用智能体评估
Tool-use benchmarks often score agents by final task success, but terminal outcomes can obscure whether the intermediate tool-call trajectory was executable. We propose prefix executability as a trajectory-level evaluation protocol for tool-using agents. The protocol measures whether every prefix of a predicted tool-call sequence remains executable under tool schemas, state preconditions, and data dependencies, and summarizes reliability using Prefix Executability Curves (PEC), First-Error Depth (FED), Trajectory Executability Rate (TER), horizon-normalized AUC-PEC, and Recovery Rate (RR). We instantiate the protocol on stratified samples from BFCL-v3 multi-turn and ToolSandbox, and on the complete Airline/Retail base splits of $τ^3$-bench. We observe that outcome-only and prefix-level rankings diverge: agents whose execution is conditioned on an unverified plan often achieve higher point-estimate final success than non-plan-conditioned baselines while substantially lowering TER and AUC-PEC. These divergences are precisely the failure modes that prefix executability is designed to expose: they reveal whether apparent success rests on an executable trajectory, where invalidity first enters, and whether success follows recovery from an earlier failure. As a validation intervention, explicit pre-execution plan checking can improve executable-prefix survival while maintaining competitive terminal success. These results show that final success alone is insufficient for evaluating tool-use agents and that prefix-level executability provides an actionable reliability signal for comparing agents, diagnosing failures, and assessing validation mechanisms.

工具使用基准通常仅根据最终任务成功来评分，但终端结果可能掩盖中间工具调用轨迹是否可执行。我们提出前缀可执行性作为工具使用智能体的轨迹级评估协议。该协议衡量预测工具调用序列的每一个前缀在工具模式、状态先决条件和数据依赖下的可执行性，并通过前缀可执行性曲线（PEC）、首次错误深度（FED）、轨迹可执行率（TER）、归一化AUC-PEC和恢复率（RR）等指标总结可靠性。我们在BFCL-v3多轮和ToolSandbox的分层样本，以及τ^3-bench的完整Airline/Retail基础分割上实现了该协议。我们观察到，仅基于结果的排名与前缀级排名存在分歧：那些依赖未经验证计划的智能体虽然在点估计的最终成功率上高于非计划依赖基线，但显著降低了TER和AUC-PEC。这些分歧正是前缀可执行性设计所要揭示的失败模式：它们揭示了看似成功的背后是否基于可执行的轨迹，无效性首次出现的位置，以及成功是否来自对早期失败的恢复。作为验证干预手段，显式的预执行计划检查可在保持竞争性终端成功的同时提高可执行前缀的存活率。这些结果表明，仅凭最终成功不足以评估工具使用智能体，而前缀级可执行性提供了可操作的可靠性信号，可用于智能体比较、故障诊断和验证机制评估。

Agentic LLM reasoning in pediatric diabetes outcome prediction from multimodal medical records
基于多模态医疗记录的儿科糖尿病结局预测中的代理型大语言模型推理
Large language model (LLM) agents are proposed for clinical prediction but are untested in pediatrics, where cohorts are small and outcomes are uncommon. Using records from 2,489 youth with type 2 diabetes (T2D) and 4,018 with type 1 diabetes (T1D), spanning structured data, note-derived social determinants and, in T1D, continuous glucose monitoring data, we predicted seven T2D outcomes, four also in T1D, at 24 months from 18 months of follow-up. Only 4 of 70,246 values it cited did not match the chart. Neighborhood Elites: Quality-Diversity Search for Uncertainty Quantification in Question Answering
邻域精英：用于问答任务不确定性量化的能力-多样性搜索
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable, yet they produce wrong answers indistinguishable from correct ones. In question answering (QA), such an error propagates unnoticed into downstream tasks. Perturbation-based uncertainty quantification (UQ) addresses this by probing a model over a set of input rewrites and reading uncertainty from how much its predictions vary. Yet this set is usually built generically, by free paraphrasing, token-level perturbation, or adversarial search. Such rewrites leave two things uncontrolled that matter in QA: what kind of change is made to the context, and how explicitly the context supports the answer. When neither is controlled, the composition of the set is determined by the generator rather than by design, and the estimate inherits whatever that generator happens to produce. Quality-diversity (QD) search is built to cover a predefined exploration space, so we recast input perturbation as a QD problem and propose \emph{neighborhood elites (NE)}, a neighborhood constructor that spreads answer-preserving rewrites across two axes, the kind of edit and the explicitness of support. We compare NE against three other neighborhood constructors, holding the editor, QA model, uncertainty measure and search objective fixed, and against three scores read from the original context. Across five extractive-QA datasets and five instruction-tuned LLMs, NE improves failure detection against every other constructor in at least 24 of the 25 dataset-model pairs, reaching 0.726 AUROC and 0.211 AUPRC lift over the base rate. Additionally, NE detects failure better than semantic entropy computed over decoder samples, by 0.074 AUROC and 0.062 AUPRC lift, and this advantage widens as the neighborhood grows.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能力日益增强，但仍会产生与正确答案难以区分的错误答案。在问答（QA）任务中，此类错误会无声地传播至下游任务。基于扰动的不确定性量化（UQ）通过在一组输入重写上探测模型，并根据其预测变化程度读取不确定性来应对这一问题。然而，这类重写集通常以通用方式构建，如自由改写、词级扰动或对抗性搜索。这些重写方式无法控制两个在QA中至关重要的因素：对上下文所做的更改类型，以及上下文对答案的支持显式程度。当两者均不受控制时，重写集的组成由生成器决定而非设计，估计结果继承了生成器的特性。质量-多样性（QD）搜索旨在覆盖预定义的探索空间，因此我们将输入扰动重构为QD问题，并提出“邻域精英（NE）”，一种在编辑类型和显式支持程度两个维度上分布保持答案不变的重写方法的构造器。我们在固定编辑器、QA模型、不确定性度量和搜索目标的前提下，将NE与其他三种邻域构造器进行比较，并与原始上下文中的三个评分进行对比。在五个抽取式QA数据集和五个指令微调的LLM上，NE在至少24/25的数据集-模型组合中优于其他所有构造器，相较于基线分别提升了0.726 AUROC和0.211 AUPRC。此外，NE在失败检测方面优于基于解码器样本的语义熵，分别提升了0.074 AUROC和0.062 AUPRC，且随着邻域规模增大，优势进一步扩大。
