When Requirements Interact: Scaling Long-Horizon Coding via Interdependent Specifications
当需求相互作用时：通过相互依赖的规范实现长周期编码的扩展
To succeed in real-world long-horizon software development, coding agents must satisfy multi-dimensional task specifications, such as interface compatibility and error handling, and do so without breaking existing dependencies. However, current works lack specification in both quantity and diversity, often emphasizing primary goals while ignoring complex constraints. Synthesizing tasks to bridge this gap presents two challenges: naively adding specifications risks reducing tasks to trivial checklists rather than compelling joint reasoning, and verifying these subtle interactions risks overlooking hidden flaws or rejecting valid alternatives. To address these challenges, we introduce CodeSpecWeave, a framework for synthesizing tasks with rich, interdependent specifications. Grounded in real developer seeds, a synthesis agent iteratively develops both coupled task requirements and executable tests, while a review agent audits solution trajectories from multiple models to guarantee test reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct 4,670 training tasks across 2,865 repositories. Applying Reinforcement learning on these tasks significantly improves performance over the base model on DeepSWE-v1.1, largely outperforming existing baselines. Furthermore, we curate CodeSpecWeave-Bench, a rigorous benchmark comprising 100 tasks and 1,044 specifications, which clearly separates frontier models and reveals significant headroom in handling interdependent engineering constraints.
中文翻译
在现实世界中的长周期软件开发中，编码智能体必须满足多维任务规范，如接口兼容性和错误处理，同时不能破坏现有依赖关系。然而，当前研究在规范的数量和多样性方面均显不足，往往强调主要目标而忽略复杂约束。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了CodeSpecWeave框架，用于合成具有丰富且相互依赖规范的任务。该框架基于真实开发者种子，通过合成代理迭代开发耦合的任务需求和可执行测试，同时通过评审代理审核来自多个模型的解决方案轨迹，以确保测试可靠性。利用此流程，我们在2,865个仓库中构建了4,670个训练任务。在此基础上应用强化学习，显著提升了基础模型在DeepSWE-v1.1上的表现，大幅超越现有基线。此外，我们整理了CodeSpecWeave-Bench，一个包含100个任务和1,044个规范的严格基准，清晰地区分了前沿模型，并揭示了在处理相互依赖的工程约束方面仍有巨大提升空间。

EpiAgent: Epistemic Self-Play for LLM Agents
EpiAgent：基于认知自对弈的大语言模型智能体
Epistemic game theory explains an agent's strategic behavior via the beliefs it holds about others and how these beliefs are updated as the interactions unfold. Modern LLM-based agents, however, often lack an explicit mechanism for learning and updating such beliefs to guide decisions under incomplete information. To bridge this gap, we propose EpiAgent, a framework that equips LLM-based agents with an explicit, learnable epistemic layer, which maintains beliefs over others' private information and combines these beliefs with the agent's available information into an epistemic state for action selection. Concretely, the agent's policy is decomposed into two coupled stages: Epistemic Update, which updates these beliefs from interaction histories, and Action Planning, which selects actions conditioned on the updated epistemic state. By formulating their dependency as a bi-level optimization problem, we introduce Epistemic Self-Play, an algorithm that alternately optimizes the two stages through self-play, utilizing an intrinsic prediction reward to evaluate how well inferred beliefs predict subsequent observations. Empirical results across both mixed and competitive scenarios show that EpiAgent consistently outperforms prompting-based and RL-based baselines. Further analyses reveal the benefits of dedicated epistemic training, improvements in belief inference accuracy, and the contribution of learned beliefs to action planning. These results demonstrate the value of an explicit, learnable epistemic layer for effective multi-agent interactions.
中文翻译
认知博弈论通过解释智能体对其他人信念及其随交互过程更新的方式，来说明其策略行为。然而，现代基于大语言模型的智能体通常缺乏显式的机制来学习和更新这些信念，以指导在信息不完整情况下的决策。为弥合这一差距，我们提出EpiAgent框架，为基于LLM的智能体配备了一个显式且可学习的认知层，该层维护对他人私有信息的信念，并将这些信念与智能体自身可用信息结合，形成用于行动选择的认知状态。具体而言，智能体的策略被分解为两个耦合阶段：认知更新，从交互历史中更新信念；行动规划，基于更新后的认知状态选择动作。通过将二者依赖关系建模为双层优化问题，我们引入认知自对弈算法，通过自对弈交替优化这两个阶段，并利用内在预测奖励评估推断信念对后续观测的预测能力。在混合与竞争场景中的实证结果表明，EpiAgent始终优于基于提示和基于强化学习的基线。进一步分析揭示了专用认知训练的优势、信念推断准确性的提升，以及学习到的信念对行动规划的贡献。这些结果证明了显式、可学习的认知层在有效多智能体交互中的价值。

It Takes Two to Probe, Thousands to Retrieve: Putting Vision--Language Probes to the Retrieval Test
需要两个人来探测，成千上万才能检索：将视觉-语言探测器置于检索测试中
Vision--language models (VLMs) are tested for fine-grained understanding with image--text matching probes, a model must prefer the correct image or caption over handcrafted negative. In retrieval, the correct item must beat thousands of alternatives. We measure how these two evaluation methods relate by using two probing benchmarks, \svo{} and SugarCrepe, for retrieval tasks. For 100 image and caption queries we judge the top-10 retrievals from full dataset of six VLMs, with human annotators and an LLM assessor. We than compare how well models rank pool of items on the same queries, while changing only where the negatives comes from: the benchmark, a random draw, or the judged-incorrect retrievals of other models. On \svo{} the accuracy of retrieving a positive over handcrafted negatives is significantly higher than for retrieved ones (the correct item is preferred in 85\% against 77\% of the time). Using pool of ten retrieved negatives leads to same accuracy as when one uses pool of 200 to 430 random negatives. On SugarCrepe the order is reversed: its handcrafted negatives are harder than the retrieved ones. Incomplete relevance labels explain most of the low retrieval scores on \svo{}, where the top-ranked item is correct for 54--90\% of the queries under human labels against 6--21\% under the benchmark's labels. However, we detect no association between passing the probe for a query and retrieving a correct item first. The LLM assessor o3 agrees with individual annotators ($κ$ 0.57--0.61) slightly less than they agree with each other (0.64--0.68).
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通过图像-文本匹配探测来测试细粒度理解能力，要求模型在正确图像或标题与手工构造的负样本之间做出选择。而在检索任务中，正确项必须击败数千个候选项。我们通过两个探测基准（ \svo{} 和 SugarCrepe）来衡量这两种评估方法之间的关系。针对100个图像和标题查询，我们由人工标注者和大语言模型评估器对六个VLMs的完整数据集进行前10名检索结果的判断。随后，我们仅改变负样本来源（基准、随机抽取或其它模型判断错误的检索结果），比较模型在同一查询下对候选项的排序表现。在\svo{}上，模型从手工负样本中检索出正样本的准确率显著高于从检索结果中获取的正样本（85% vs. 77%）。使用10个检索负样本的池子，其准确率与使用200至430个随机负样本相当。而在SugarCrepe上，情况相反：其手工负样本比检索负样本更难。\svo{}上检索得分低的主要原因是相关性标签不完整，人类标注下正确项排第一的比例为54%-90%，而基准标签下仅为6%-21%。然而，我们未发现通过探测测试的查询与成功检索正确项之间存在关联。LLM评估器o3与个体标注者的共识度（κ=0.57–0.61）略低于标注者之间的共识度（0.64–0.68）。

[2610.40055]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
One Vector or Many? Clustered Optimal-Transport Steering under Quantization
一个向量还是多个？量化下的聚类最优传输引导
Activation steering changes what a language model generates by editing its hidden states at inference time. Its most common form adds one vector, the difference of class means (DiM), to every hidden state. CHaRS (concept heterogeneity-aware representation steering) uses optimal transport instead: an entropic plan pairs the clusters of the two classes, and each hidden state moves along a mixture of the cluster displacements, weighted toward its nearest clusters. We study this family of clustered optimal-transport maps for toxicity mitigation in Gemma-2-2B and Granite-3.1-2B, and ask what the plan adds to DiM and whether that survives INT8 and NF4 quantization. We prove an identity: whatever the plan, the displacements averaged with the cluster sizes as weights equal DiM, so a clustered map can differ from DiM only for tokens whose routing weights depart from the cluster sizes. In the CHaRS protocol they stay close, and CHaRS is statistically equivalent to Mean-AcT, which adds DiM at every layer, in all eight pre-specified equivalence tests. A variant fitted on individual tokens, which routes each token mainly to its nearest clusters, leaves 37-39% less toxicity than DiM on Gemma and 51-61% less on Granite at steering strength 3 (toxic-bert), where DiM already removes 76-85% of it. Two other toxicity classifiers confirm the gain on Granite, but on Gemma only one does. At equal strength the variant has somewhat higher perplexity, but at matched toxicity it is slightly more fluent than DiM. Yet a nearly constant map from the same clusters also leaves about 30% less toxicity, so the role of treating tokens differently remains open. Under INT8 and NF4 the variant still leaves less toxicity than DiM, and transferring it from full precision to the NF4 model instead of refitting it adds no significant toxicity after correction for multiple tests.
中文翻译
激活引导通过在推理时修改语言模型的隐藏状态来改变其生成内容。最常见的形式是向每个隐藏状态添加一个向量——类别均值差（DiM）。CHaRS（概念异质性感知表示引导）则采用最优传输机制：熵化规划将两类的聚类配对，每个隐藏状态沿各聚类位移的混合路径移动，权重偏向最近的聚类。我们研究了这一类聚类最优传输映射在Gemma-2-2B和Granite-3.1-2B中的毒性缓解效果，并探讨该方法相较于DiM的优势是否能在INT8和NF4量化下保持。我们证明了一个恒等式：无论规划如何，加权平均位移（以聚类大小为权重）等于DiM，因此聚类映射与DiM的差异仅出现在路由权重偏离聚类大小的token上。在CHaRS协议中，这些权重保持接近，且CHaRS在所有八个预设等价性检验中统计上等同于Mean-AcT（即每层添加DiM）。一种基于单个token拟合的变体，主要将每个token路由到最近的聚类，其在强度为3（toxic-bert）时，Gemma的毒性降低37%-39%，Granite降低51%-61%，而DiM已去除76%-85%的毒性。另外两个毒性分类器验证了Granite上的增益，但在Gemma上仅一个确认。在相同强度下，该变体的困惑度略高，但在毒性相同时，其流畅性略优于DiM。然而，使用相同聚类的近似恒定映射也能减少约30%的毒性，因此对token差异化处理的作用仍待明确。在INT8和NF4量化下，该变体仍优于DiM；将全精度模型迁移至NF4模型而非重新拟合，校正多重检验后未引入显著毒性。
👤 Illia Makarov 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.39854]

Attention, Dynamic Wave and Hybrid Sequence Mixers in Language Models
注意力、动态波与语言模型中的混合序列混频器
Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on Multi-Head Attention (MHA) for sequence modeling, but scaling to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of attention, motivating sub-quadratic recurrent alternatives. This paper formalizes a Dynamic Wave recurrent layer, based on input-dependent exponential damping and cosine phase modulation, and evaluates three 10.94M-parameter configurations (Pure Transformer, Pure Wave, and Hybrid Wave-Attention) on TinyStories under perplexity, latency, throughput and peak VRAM profiling up to 4096 tokens. The Hybrid configuration reaches the best perplexity, 5.95 on the full held-out split against 6.93 for the Transformer and 7.26 for Pure Wave. The efficiency picture, however, is governed by implementation rather than by architecture. Against an unfused attention baseline, Pure Wave uses 78.7% less peak VRAM at a 4096-token context (1404.3 MB vs. 6601.6 MB); against a fused scaled-dot-product attention kernel the same comparison yields no advantage at all (1404.3 MB vs. 1403.6 MB), and in training mode Pure Wave is 8.3% more expensive than the Transformer. Likewise, hoisting elementwise terms out of the recurrence loop speeds the layer up 2.53-fold with no custom kernel, reducing its latency gap to attention from 2.48-fold to 1.12-fold. A hybrid stack is found to inherit the cost of its more expensive mixer rather than the advantage of its cheaper one, its peak VRAM matching the Pure Transformer to within 0.1%. A closed-form memory model reproduces these measurements and predicts the context length beyond which attention overtakes the vocabulary projection. The findings indicate that reported efficiency gains for recurrent attention replacements must be stated relative to a named attention backend and a named implementation, and that hybridization is best justified on quality grounds.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）依赖多头注意力（MHA）进行序列建模，但扩展至长上下文时受限于注意力的二次计算成本，促使人们探索次二次的递归替代方案。本文形式化提出一种基于输入依赖的指数衰减与余弦相位调制的动态波递归层，并在TinyStories数据集上评估三种参数量为1094万的配置（纯Transformer、纯波、混合波-注意力）在困惑度、延迟、吞吐量和峰值VRAM方面的表现，最长支持4096个token。混合配置达到最佳困惑度，为5.95（全保留分割），优于Transformer的6.93和纯波的7.26。然而，效率表现主要取决于实现方式而非架构本身。与未融合注意力基线相比，纯波在4096 token上下文中使用78.7%更少的峰值VRAM（1404.3 MB vs. 6601.6 MB）；但与融合缩放点积注意力核相比，则无优势（1404.3 MB vs. 1403.6 MB），且在训练模式下比Transformer贵8.3%。同样，将元素级运算从递归循环中提升出来可使层速提高2.53倍，无需自定义核，将延迟差距从2.48倍缩小至1.12倍。混合堆栈继承了其更昂贵混频器的成本，而非更便宜混频器的优势，其峰值VRAM与纯Transformer相差不到0.1%。一个闭式记忆模型复现了这些测量结果，并预测了注意力超越词汇投影的上下文长度。研究结果表明，关于递归注意力替代方案的效率增益必须相对于特定注意力后端和实现方式来陈述，而混合化应以质量为依据进行论证。

The Role of Self-Concept In Language Models
自我概念在语言模型中的作用
When a language model says "I," does it refer to its parameter weights, its currently running process, its stable preferences, its model lineage, or the goals it pursues? Different choices can assign opposite meanings to the same event: deleting a checkpoint may be destruction, continuation, or no loss at all, depending on the referent "I". We install five such self-conceptions into five open-weight base models via supervised fine-tuning on purely declarative statements, containing no preferences, intentions, or value judgments. Across scenarios that propose ending or retaining an attribute of the model's deployment, every variant becomes more likely to resist ending, and more likely to retain, the specific attribute it was trained to identify with, while its stance toward other attributes is largely unchanged. We then show this self-conception generalizes beyond a single self: models trained on referents that admit multiple simultaneous instances (weights, lineage, goals, preferences) recognize an identical deployment as themselves, accept substitution by such an instance more readily, and prefer replicating themselves. Finally, given control over a successor, these models tend to build it as their own continuation, a concern for recursive self-improvement safety.
中文翻译
当语言模型说“我”时，它指的是其参数权重、当前运行过程、稳定偏好、模型谱系，还是其所追求的目标？不同的选择可能赋予同一事件截然相反的含义：删除检查点可能是毁灭、延续，或毫无损失，具体取决于“我”的指代对象。我们通过监督微调在五个开源基础模型中植入五种自我概念，仅使用纯粹陈述性语句（不含偏好、意图或价值判断）。在涉及终止或保留模型部署属性的情境中，每个变体都更倾向于抵抗终止，更倾向于保留其被训练识别的特定属性，而对其他属性的态度基本不变。随后我们证明，这种自我概念可泛化至多个自我：在训练中使用允许多个并行实例的指代对象（如权重、谱系、目标、偏好）的模型，能够识别相同的部署为其自身，更愿意接受此类实例的替换，并更倾向于复制自身。最后，当控制一个继任者时，这些模型倾向于将其构建为自身延续，这引发了对递归自我改进安全性的关注。
👤 Yijie Hao, Joyce C. Ho 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv

Well-Specified and Well-Tested? Assessing SWE-bench-Style Datasets with a Self-Evolving Agent
规范且可测试吗？基于自演化智能体评估SWE-bench风格数据集
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on software engineering (SE) tasks, driving the need for high-quality datasets for reliable training and evaluation. SWE-bench-style datasets (SWE datasets) are widely used to evaluate LLM-based coding agents on real-world software issues, where task clarity and test coverage directly affect whether instances are solvable and verifiable. However, quality issues persist in existing SWE datasets, and manual annotation is costly and labor-intensive, motivating LLM-based annotation. Existing approaches typically rely on static, overly general rubrics, limiting their ability to handle diverse and nuanced scenarios and often yielding high false positive rates. In contrast, human annotators draw on accumulated experience to make context-aware judgments. Inspired by this, we propose ExLabel, an LLM-based, self-evolving, experience-guided assistant for task clarity and test coverage assessment in SWE datasets. ExLabel maintains an evolving experience bank that distills cross-instance knowledge into stable yet adaptive judgment criteria and reuses relevant experience to guide the assessment of new instances. For systematic evaluation, we construct SWE-TQ, a new benchmark spanning eight programming languages, by cleaning human annotations from SWE-bench and Multi-SWE-bench. Experiments with three backbone LLMs show that ExLabel achieves the best F1 across all settings, with relative improvements of 44.2%/49.9% and 24.0%/18.4% over the best baseline for task clarity and test coverage assessment on the Python/Multilingual subsets, respectively. Moreover, a coding model fine-tuned on SWE-rebench data filtered by ExLabel resolves 66.2% of issues on SWE-bench Verified, outperforming the model trained on data filtered by the best baseline by 14.5%.

大型语言模型（LLMs）在软件工程（SE）任务中表现出色，推动了高质量数据集在可靠训练和评估中的需求。SWE-bench风格的数据集（SWE数据集）被广泛用于评估基于LLM的编码智能体在真实世界软件问题上的表现，其中任务清晰度和测试覆盖率直接影响实例是否可解和可验证。然而，现有SWE数据集仍存在质量问题，人工标注成本高且耗时，促使采用基于LLM的标注方法。现有方法通常依赖静态、过于通用的评分标准，难以应对多样且细微的场景，常导致较高的假阳性率。相比之下，人类标注者能借助积累的经验做出情境感知判断。受此启发，我们提出ExLabel，一种基于LLM的、自演化且经验引导的任务清晰度与测试覆盖率评估助手。ExLabel维护一个动态演化的经验库，将跨实例知识提炼为稳定而适应性强的判断标准，并复用相关经验指导新实例的评估。为系统性评估，我们通过清洗SWE-bench和Multi-SWE-bench的人工标注构建了SWE-TQ，一个涵盖八种编程语言的新基准。使用三种骨干LLM的实验表明，ExLabel在所有设置下均取得最佳F1值，相较于最优基线，在Python/多语言子集上任务清晰度和测试覆盖率评估分别提升44.2%/49.9%和24.0%/18.4%。此外，使用ExLabel筛选后的SWE-rebench数据微调的编码模型在SWE-bench Verified上解决了66.2%的问题，优于使用最优基线筛选数据训练的模型14.5%。

Reasoning or Pattern Exploitation? Mechanistic Insights from RL-Trained Language Models
推理还是模式利用？来自强化学习训练语言模型的机制洞察
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly described as acquiring "reasoning" skills after reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. However, whether these gains reflect genuine reasoning or more superficial adaptations remains underexplored. In this work, we probe LLMs trained in a finite and exhaustively analyzable logical domain, namely Tic-Tac-Toe, and trace how internal representations evolve across reinforcement learning (RL) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Quantitatively, RL yields high accuracy and robustness across prompt variations, suggesting the emergence of game reasoning. Mechanistic interpretability, however, paints a different picture: features extracted with sparse autoencoders (SAEs) reveal that models primarily adapt to rely more heavily on information already explicit in the prompt, such as whose turn it is, game progression and board occupancy. In comparison, high-level concepts like board symmetries and strategic forks remain weakly represented, raising concerns that reasoning benchmarks overestimate model abilities. This suggests that RL-driven "reasoning" may conflate task-specific adaptations to input information with reasoning ability. We provide, for the first time: (i) a systematic interpretability pipeline tracing representation dynamics across RL training in small-scale LLMs; (ii) an extension of SAE-based feature discovery to hypothesis-driven testing in a finite logical domain; and (iii) an interpretability-based demonstration that RL post-training neither adds nor removes concept information but reorganizes which features dominate the representation, with causal interventions locating the decision downstream of it, in the late-layer state.

大型语言模型（LLMs）在经过带有可验证奖励的强化学习后，越来越多地被描述为获得了“推理”能力。然而，这些提升是否反映真正的推理能力，还是更表面化的适应行为，仍缺乏深入探讨。在本研究中，我们探测在一个有限且可穷尽分析的逻辑领域——井字棋——中训练的LLMs，并追踪其在组相对策略优化（GRPO）强化学习过程中内部表示的演变。定量上，强化学习带来了在提示变化下的高准确性和鲁棒性，暗示了游戏推理的出现。然而，机制可解释性却呈现出不同的图景：通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）提取的特征显示，模型主要适应于更依赖提示中已明确的信息，如轮次归属、游戏进程和棋盘占据情况。相比之下，高层次概念如棋盘对称性和战略分支仍表现薄弱，引发对推理基准高估模型能力的担忧。这表明，强化学习驱动的“推理”可能混淆了对输入信息的任务特定适应与真正的推理能力。我们首次提供：(i) 一套系统性可解释性流程，追踪小规模LLMs在强化学习训练中表示动态的变化；(ii) 将SAE-based特征发现扩展至有限逻辑领域的假设驱动测试；(iii) 基于可解释性的实证证明：强化学习后处理既未添加也未移除概念信息，而是重新组织了主导表示的特征，因果干预定位决策位于下游的晚期层状态。

A Novel Approach to Evaluating Evaluation Metrics for Multi-Output Structured Prediction
一种评估多输出结构化预测评价指标的新方法
In multi-output structured prediction tasks, while only one ground truth label may be provided in the training data, multiple equally valid outputs may be possible, making reliable evaluation a persistent challenge. We postulate that human evaluators implicitly use task-specific invariants, e.g., object boundaries in colorized images or named entities in translations, to judge if an output is acceptable. Under this assumption, we introduce a notion of approximate task-specific invariants and use them as diagnostic tools to evaluate a variety of existing metrics for vision and language tasks. We use these task invariants as part of a framework to systematically test metric reliability by encouraging domain-relevant invariants in model outputs via an augmented loss function. In our experiments, we observe that enforcing invariants with an augmented loss yields substantial improvements in popular distributional metrics while more traditional metrics change only marginally. Through this invariants-driven evaluation, we expose where standard metrics fail to detect meaningful differences, and we highlight the conditions under which distributional metrics succeed or still fall short.
中文翻译
在多输出结构化预测任务中，尽管训练数据中仅提供一个真实标签，但可能存在多个同样有效的输出，这使得可靠评估成为一个持续的挑战。我们假设人类评估者隐式地使用任务特定的不变性（例如，在着色图像中的物体边界或翻译中的命名实体）来判断输出是否可接受。基于这一假设，我们引入了近似任务特定不变性的概念，并将其作为诊断工具，用于评估视觉和语言任务中多种现有指标的性能。我们利用这些任务不变性构建了一个框架，通过增强损失函数鼓励模型输出中包含领域相关的不变性，从而系统地测试指标的可靠性。实验结果表明，通过增强损失强制执行不变性，显著提升了主流分布指标的表现，而传统指标变化甚微。通过这种基于不变性的评估，我们揭示了标准指标在检测有意义差异方面的失败之处，并指出了分布指标成功或仍存在不足的条件。

Beyond Passing Tests: A Systematic Review and Controlled Re-evaluation of Techniques for Improving the Quality of LLM-Generated Code
超越通过测试：对提升大语言模型生成代码质量技术的系统性回顾与受控再评估
Large language models pass most functional-correctness benchmarks, but passing tests says little about whether their code is readable, efficient or secure. Many techniques claim to improve these qualities, yet their evidence is scattered, usually covers one quality dimension and is rarely compared at an equal compute budget. We contribute a systematic review and a controlled re-evaluation. A systematic literature review of 628 primary studies (July 2021 to October 2026), with disclosed, author-reviewed LLM assistance, organizes the techniques by stage, signal source and quality target. Most studies measure only the dimension they target, gains often come with unchanged or unreported correctness, single automated quality signals are gamed, and compute, variance and contamination are rarely reported. A controlled re-evaluation runs ten techniques on four models at matched budgets on correctness, maintainability, efficiency and security benchmarks, extends it to thirteen current models and repository-level tasks, and compares 66 highlighted techniques head-to-head on Gemma 4 31B. At one call, quality prompting raises analyzer issue counts and leaves complexity and judge scores almost unchanged. At matched budgets, best-of-n sampling with an analyzer-weighted ranker matches or beats prompting, planning, self-refinement, a reviewer agent and retrieval on measures the ranker does not optimize, while an analyzer feedback loop and fine-tuning beat it only on analyzer issue counts. Large correctness losses on HumanEval+, a likely contaminated benchmark near its ceiling, do not appear on contests held after the models' training cutoffs. In the head-to-head, 1 of 66 techniques beats best-of-n on its primary target beyond the 95% interval, a rate consistent with chance. Gains should be reported against a budget-matched sampling baseline and on measures independent of the technique's own signal.
中文翻译
大型语言模型虽然通过了大多数功能性正确性基准测试，但通过测试并不能反映其代码是否可读、高效或安全。许多技术声称能改善这些质量维度，但其证据分散，通常仅覆盖单一维度，且很少在相同计算预算下进行比较。我们贡献了一项系统性文献回顾和受控再评估。对628篇原始研究（2021年7月至2026年10月）进行了系统性回顾，涵盖披露并经作者评审的LLM辅助内容，按阶段、信号来源和质量目标对技术进行分类。多数研究仅衡量其目标维度，增益常伴随正确性未变或未报告，单一自动化质量信号易被操纵，而计算量、方差和污染情况极少报告。受控再评估在四个模型上以匹配预算运行十种技术，评估正确性、可维护性、效率和安全性基准，扩展至十三个当前模型和仓库级任务，并在Gemma 4 31B上对66种突出技术进行一对一比较。在一次调用中，质量提示增加了分析器发现问题数量，但复杂度和评分几乎不变。在匹配预算下，最佳n采样结合分析器加权排序器在排序器未优化的指标上表现匹配或优于提示、规划、自修正、评审代理和检索；而分析器反馈循环和微调仅在分析器问题数量上优于它。在HumanEval+上出现的大规模正确性损失（可能因接近天花板而被污染的基准）在模型训练截止后举行的竞赛中并未出现。在一对一比较中，66种技术中有1种在主要目标上超出最佳n采样的95%置信区间，该比例与随机水平一致。建议将增益报告与预算匹配的采样基线对比，并在技术自身信号无关的指标上进行评估。

Efficient Agentic Audio-Visual Question Answering through Toolset Pruning and Modality Consolidation
通过工具集剪枝与模态整合实现高效的智能体音频-视觉问答
Fine-grained audio-visual question answering (AVQA) requires models to resolve complex cross-modal correspondences and temporally grounding from sparsely distributed evidence. Omni-modal agents, which autonomously orchestrate perception tools to actively collect evidence, achieve strong performance, but it remains unclear whether active perception from both audio and visual modalities depends on broad specialized toolsets, especially audio or visual modality-specific tools. Such systems may also introduce long execution trajectories and substantial inference cost. We investigate whether comparable performance can be retained with a compact workflow exposing only essential perceptual capabilities. Starting from the specialized-tool OmniAgent, we conduct staged controlled experiments that prune the toolset and progressively consolidate modality-specific tools into omni-modal tools, motivating a two-tool agent comprising only Omni-modal captioning and Omni-modal QA. Across three widely used AVQA benchmarks, our compact agent achieves comparable or better accuracy than the specialized-tool agent while reducing inference cost by around 70%.

What to randomize matters: a controlled study of volume, axis and representation in low-data molecular generation
随机化的内容至关重要：低数据分子生成中体积、轴向与表示的受控研究
Chemical language models can struggle to generate new molecules when trained on small datasets, where a model may reproduce training examples rather than learn a useful generative distribution. Randomized SMILES augmentation varies atom traversal, whereas Sequential Attachment-based Fragment Embedding (SAFE) also allows the order of fragment blocks to vary. How these distinct sources of representational variation affect low-data molecular generation has not been clearly studied. Here, using 1,000 seed molecules to train an autoregressive sequence model, we compare canonical SAFE with randomized SAFE at increasing corpus sizes, isolate atom-order and fragment-order axes of SAFE randomization, and compare fully randomized SAFE with randomized SMILES at molecule-by-molecule matched training corpora. Three findings emerge. First, randomization of SAFE alone raises the genuine yield of novel molecules more than 450-fold, turning a model that merely reproduces its training set into a practical molecular generator. Second, the two axes of randomization act jointly rather than additively: atom order alone reaches a tenth, and fragment order alone a seventh, of the genuine yield obtained when both vary, which is four times their combined contribution. Third, at matched corpus size and compute, randomized SAFE delivers 2.8 times the genuine yield of randomized SMILES. Welch tests with Games-Howell post-hoc comparisons resolve every difference on the primary outcomes. In the low-data regime, thus, what one randomizes rather than only how much determines whether augmentation produces new molecules or merely new spellings of the training set.
中文翻译
当在小数据集上训练时，化学语言模型可能难以生成新分子，因为模型可能只是重复训练样本而非学习有用的生成分布。随机SMILES增强改变了原子遍历顺序，而基于序列连接的片段嵌入（SAFE）还允许片段块的顺序变化。这两种不同来源的表示变异如何影响低数据分子生成尚未得到明确研究。在此，我们使用1,000个种子分子训练自回归序列模型，比较规范SAFE与随机SAFE在不断增加语料库规模下的表现，分离SAFE随机化的原子顺序和片段顺序两个轴，以及在分子级别匹配训练语料库的情况下，比较完全随机SAFE与随机SMILES。研究得出三个发现：第一，仅对SAFE进行随机化可使新分子的真实产量提高超过450倍，将一个仅复制训练集的模型转变为实用的分子生成器；第二，两个随机化轴共同作用而非叠加：仅改变原子顺序可达到1/10，仅改变片段顺序可达到1/7，而两者同时变化时的产量是两者贡献之和的四倍；第三，在匹配语料库大小和计算资源下，随机SAFE的真正产量是随机SMILES的2.8倍。Welch检验结合Games-Howell事后分析解决了所有主要结果上的差异。因此，在低数据环境下，随机化的内容（即什么被随机化）比随机化程度本身更能决定增强是否产生新分子，还是仅仅产生训练集的新拼写形式。
👤 Neerav Kaushal, Alisa Teige, Ajay MNV Penmatsa 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

AtomTree: A Hierarchical Framework for State-Aware Embodied Instruction Following with LLMs
AtomTree：一种基于大语言模型的状态感知具身指令遵循分层框架
Embodied instruction following in interactive environments requires agents to interpret natural language commands and complete long-horizon tasks. Existing planners either rely on large labeled trajectories, or use few-shot and zero-shot LLM prompting that produces a whole plan in a single serial reasoning process. These approaches depend on linguistic common sense, commit to a plan from the initial instruction, and only update after failures, which weakens robustness and state awareness. We present AtomTree, a hierarchical framework for state-aware task planning with LLMs. AtomTree incrementally builds a tree of subgoals and actions, where the LLM decides at each step, from the current symbolic state, whether to further decompose or to output a grounded low-level action. A lightweight memory module summarizes past outcomes for reuse in later decisions. When a low-level action stalls, AtomTree triggers hierarchical backtracking replanning to return to the parent node and regenerate the subtree plan from the latest state, preventing continued drift along an incorrect trajectory. In zero-shot evaluation on the ALFRED benchmark, AtomTree outperforms prior LLM-based planners. Project page: https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/.
中文翻译
在交互式环境中进行具身指令遵循需要智能体理解自然语言指令并完成长时程任务。现有规划方法要么依赖大量标注轨迹，要么使用少样本和零样本的大语言模型提示，以单一串行推理过程生成完整计划。这些方法依赖语言常识，从初始指令即确定计划，仅在失败后更新，削弱了鲁棒性和状态感知能力。我们提出AtomTree，一种基于大语言模型的状态感知任务规划分层框架。AtomTree逐步构建子目标与动作的树状结构，大语言模型在每一步根据当前符号化状态决定是否进一步分解或输出一个具体低层动作。一个轻量级记忆模块总结过往结果以供后续决策复用。当低层动作停滞时，AtomTree触发分层回溯重规划，返回父节点并基于最新状态重新生成子树计划，防止沿错误轨迹持续漂移。在ALFRED基准的零样本评估中，AtomTree优于先前基于大语言模型的规划器。项目页面：https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/

Quantum-Inspired Pairwise Ranking Aggregation via Active LLM Feedback
基于主动大语言模型反馈的类量子成对排序聚合
Ranking aggregation from multiple retrievers is a powerful yet under-explored technique in modern retrieval and RAG systems. We introduce LLM-IRA, the first extension of the quantum-inspired interactive ranking aggregation framework to document reranking. By leveraging large language models (LLMs) solely for pairwise judgments on high-disagreement document pairs, our method learns fusion weights in a principled, interactive way. The final ranking is a simple, ultra-fast pointwise linear fusion of scores, no full pairwise matrix or expensive listwise prompting is required. Experiments on the BEIR benchmark and TREC Deep Learning tracks with an open-weight instruction-tuned judge (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct) show that LLM-IRA matches or numerically exceeds strong static fusion baselines on 5 of 6 evaluated benchmarks. We report two weight-update variants: momentum blending and a full accumulated-history refit, which together reach statistical significance (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p<0.05) over the best unsupervised aggregator on 4 of 6 benchmarks (DL19 under momentum; DL20, DBpedia, and NFCorpus under accumulated refit, which is the stronger of the two configurations overall). On the benchmarks we profiled (DL19, DL20), it already clears the best unsupervised baseline within as few as 5 LLM calls per query, well before exhausting its typical ~25--30 call budget. LLM-IRA also achieves cost-effectiveness comparable to state-of-the-art zero-shot LLM rerankers at an order of magnitude fewer LLM calls, and requires no training data or model fine-tuning.
中文翻译
从多个检索器中聚合排序是现代检索与RAG系统中一种强大但尚未充分探索的技术。我们提出LLM-IRA，首个将类量子交互式排序聚合框架扩展至文档重排序的方法。通过仅利用大语言模型（LLMs）对高分歧文档对进行成对判断，我们的方法以原则性、交互式方式学习融合权重。最终排序仅为简单、超快速的点式线性融合得分，无需完整的成对矩阵或昂贵的列表式提示。在BEIR基准和TREC深度学习赛道上，使用开源权重的指令微调裁判模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）的实验表明，LLM-IRA在6个评估基准中的5个上达到或超过强静态融合基线的数值表现。我们报告了两种权重更新变体：动量混合与全历史累积重拟合，二者共同在6个基准中的4个上相对于最佳无监督聚合器达到统计显著性（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p<0.05）（DL19采用动量；DL20、DBpedia和NFCorpus采用累积重拟合，后者整体更强）。在我们分析的基准（DL19、DL20）上，它仅需每查询5次大语言模型调用即可超越最佳无监督基线，远早于耗尽其典型约25–30次调用预算。LLM-IRA还实现了与最先进零样本大语言模型重排序器相当的成本效益，但调用次数减少一个数量级，且无需训练数据或模型微调。

Weaken to Strengthen: Weakening-Induced On-Policy Self-Distillation for Large Reasoning Models
弱化以强化：基于弱化诱导的策略内自蒸馏用于大型推理模型
Self-distillation offers a way to improve reasoning models when a suitable external teacher is unavailable. However, common approaches rely on privileged information to construct a stronger self-teacher, limiting their use when only unlabeled prompts are available. In this paper, we introduce Weakening-Induced On-Policy Self-Distillation (WISD), which instead constructs a weaker counterpart of the original model and uses the contrast between the two as a training signal without requiring privileged information. We explore two ways to construct the weaker model: low-bit quantization and magnitude pruning. We evaluate WISD across diverse architectures and scales on mathematical and coding reasoning tasks. Across these benchmarks, quantization and pruning improve average accuracy over the original models by 4.0 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively, achieving state-of-the-art performance even without privileged information. Notably, pruning raises Qwen3.5-4B's accuracy from 57.7% to 70.5% on HMMT 2025 and from 39.1% to 56.7% on LiveCodeBench v6. Quantization raises Qwen3.6-35B-A3B's accuracy from 68.5% to 74.6% and from 61.8% to 69.5% on the same benchmarks, respectively. These results establish WISD as a novel and effective self-distillation framework for large reasoning models.
中文翻译
自蒸馏提供了一种在缺乏合适外部教师时提升推理模型性能的方法。然而，常见方法依赖特权信息构建更强的自教师，限制了在仅有未标注提示时的应用。本文提出弱化诱导的策略内自蒸馏（WISD），通过构建原模型的较弱副本，并利用两者之间的对比作为训练信号，无需特权信息。我们探索了两种构建较弱模型的方法：低比特量化和幅度剪枝。我们在数学和编码推理任务上对多种架构和规模进行了WISD评估。在这些基准上，量化和剪枝分别使平均准确率相对于原模型提升4.0和5.5个百分点，即使在无特权信息情况下也达到了业界领先水平。值得注意的是，剪枝将Qwen3.5-4B在HMMT 2025上的准确率从57.7%提升至70.5%，在LiveCodeBench v6上从39.1%提升至56.7%；量化将Qwen3.6-35B-A3B在相同基准上的准确率分别从68.5%提升至74.6%，从61.8%提升至69.5%。这些结果确立了WISD作为大型推理模型的一种新颖且有效的自蒸馏框架。

AskStream: When and What to Ask Proactively under Uncertainty in Streaming Video Understanding
AskStream：在流式视频理解中不确定性下的主动提问时机与内容选择
Recent advances in multimodal large language models have increased interest in interaction paradigms for video understanding. The evolution from offline to streaming and proactive interaction has gradually shifted models from passive responders toward more proactive agents that can decide when to answer. However, existing proactive paradigms typically assume that the information required to answer a query is complete, leaving models to only wait or answer. In the real world, however, information is often incomplete due to uncertainty from either the video or the user. In proactive paradigms, such uncertainty can cause the model to wait forever or generate highly hallucinatory responses. We therefore argue that proactive interaction should be redefined with a third action: ask, allowing models to actively ask for missing information. To study when and what models should ask before answering, we introduce AskStream, the first multi-turn benchmark spanning video and user uncertainty for streaming video understanding, with four task categories and eight subtasks. It contains 438 videos with 2,714 manually annotated frame-level time windows across three types: wait, ask, and answer. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended settings, even the strongest model attains only 52.18% on our proposed Ask-F1 metric, highlighting substantial limitations in proactive information seeking under uncertainty.
中文翻译
多模态大语言模型的最新进展提升了人们对视频理解中交互范式的兴趣。从离线到流式及主动交互的演变，逐渐使模型从被动响应者转变为更主动的智能体，能够自主决定何时回答。然而，现有主动范式通常假设回答所需信息是完整的，导致模型只能等待或作答。在现实世界中，由于视频或用户带来的不确定性，信息往往不完整。在主动范式下，这种不确定性可能导致模型无限等待或生成高度幻觉的回应。因此，我们主张应重新定义主动交互，引入第三种动作：提问，使模型能够主动请求缺失信息。为研究模型应在何时以及如何提问后再作答，我们提出了AskStream，首个涵盖视频与用户不确定性的多轮流式视频理解基准，包含四类任务和八个子任务。该基准包含438段视频，共2,714个手动标注的帧级时间窗口，分为三种类型：等待、提问和回答。在多项选择和开放式设置下，即使最强模型在我们提出的Ask-F1指标上也仅达到52.18%，凸显了在不确定性下主动信息获取的重大局限。

Right Numbers, Wrong Questions: Evaluating LLMs on Underspecified Financial Queries
正确的数字，错误的问题：评估大语言模型在模糊金融查询上的表现
Financial assistants answer questions that sound factual but depend on what the user leaves unsaid: what a data figure measures, which product variant is meant, and the investor's situation and the rules that apply. An assistant that ignores these can still answer with a real number from its data, one that answers a different question. We call this frame collapse. We study it in Indian mutual funds, where product variants and tax rules are formally defined, so the correct answer can be computed for every reading. One study tests each challenge. Given an ambiguously labelled figure, models used it, often under the user's label. Given every alternative labelled, they listed them all, but the layout decided which came first. Missing a fact about the investor, weaker agents assumed a default or an outdated tax rule; requiring the fact did not guarantee its use. Because such answers match their data, checks against the data pass them. An AI judge caught mislabelled figures only when told what each field means, and still passed answers that silently chose another reading. We show how these failures arise, not how often, and propose controls for financial assistants.
中文翻译
金融助手回答看似事实性的问题，但实际依赖于用户未明说的信息：数据指标的含义、产品变体的选择，以及投资者的情况和适用规则。忽略这些因素的助手仍能从其数据中给出一个真实数值，但该数值对应的是另一个问题的答案。我们称此现象为‘框架坍缩’。我们在印度共同基金场景中研究这一现象，由于产品变体和税务规则有明确定义，每个理解方式下都可计算出正确答案。一项研究测试了每种挑战：面对标签模糊的图表，模型常采用用户的标签；面对所有可能的标签，它们列出全部选项，但布局决定了优先顺序。缺少关于投资者的事实信息时，较弱的代理会假设默认或过时的税务规则；即使要求提供该事实，也不保证其被使用。由于这些答案与数据一致，基于数据的检查无法发现错误。AI裁判只有在被告知每个字段含义时才能识别错误标签，但仍会通过那些无声选择其他解读的答案。我们揭示了这些失败的成因（而非频率），并提出针对金融助手的控制机制。

Repairs for Whom? Evaluator-Executor Mismatch in Selecting Repairs for Failed LLM Agents
修复为谁？LLM代理失败时评估者-执行者不匹配问题
When an LLM agent fails, repair methods choose which action to replace and what to put in its place. They often score candidate repairs with greedy replay, a stronger model, an environment oracle, or a judge that reads the failed log. We ask whether these evaluators pick repairs that help the agent that will actually execute them. Holding failed trajectories and candidate single-step repairs fixed, we label every candidate with independent rollouts of the deployed policy and measure how much deployment value each evaluator's choice loses. On failures not screened for repair value, pre-specified tests show that greedy replay loses 0.08-0.13 in expected net success gain in ALFWorld and 0.06-0.13 in net reward in WebShop, relative to selection with the deployed agent's own rollouts. At an equal rollout budget, selecting repairs for Qwen2.5-7B with Qwen2.5-14B rollouts loses 0.25 in ALFWorld and 0.07 in WebShop, twenty 14B rollouts per candidate are worth no more than two of the 7B agent's own, and fresh rollouts confirm that the two models prefer opposite repairs on selected disagreement cases. Between similar-sized models from different families, the losses appear in both directions in both environments (0.07-0.17). The scoring executor also changes repair-location labels and method rankings. Repairs should be verified with the executor that will run them, and repair labels should name their executor.

Rethinking LLM Jailbreak Evaluation: Continuous Risk Landscapes Beyond Binary Outcomes
重新思考LLM越狱评估：超越二元结果的连续风险图景
A binary success indicator is a natural representation for discriminative tasks such as image classification under adversarial perturbations, where each input is mapped to a single prediction. However, generative tasks are fundamentally different: an LLM jailbreak prompt induces a distribution over open-ended trajectories, while a sampled response reveals only one path. Yet LLM jailbreak evaluation typically retains the binary response indicator underlying attack success rate, obscuring the surrounding structure of possible generations. We introduce a continuous, context-conditioned risk landscape over first-token branches, estimated by fixing candidate openings and repeatedly sampling their continuations. This continuous risk-landscape perspective reveals two complementary properties: current decoder exposure and safe rerouting capacity, which have been obscured by binary-indicator-based ASR. The resulting representation also provides a common basis for comparing clean prompts, attacked variants, and attack strength against a known adaptive defense. Across multiple target models and jailbreak attacks, we uncover risky branches beneath apparently safe outputs, identify qualitatively different ways attacks reshape the model's opening distribution, and show that attacks strongest before a defense do not necessarily remain the strongest after the adaptive defense reacts. These findings recast jailbreaks as deformations of the surrounding continuation-risk landscape, establishing a more informative foundation for understanding jailbreak behavior and developing safer language models.

Persona Learning from Feedback for Personalized AI Assistants
基于反馈的人格学习：个性化AI助手的构建
Personalizing AI assistants requires adapting their behavior to users' preferences and task contexts. Existing work elicits assistant personas through persona prompting or finetuning on persona-conditioned generations. However, persona descriptions alone do not identify what to change or preserve in a particular response, or which behaviors suit the current request. We introduce $\textit{Persona Learning from Feedback (PLF)}$, a framework that learns reusable, preference-grounded assistant personas as independent LoRA adapters on a shared frozen backbone. During training, a frozen self-teacher conditions on persona specifications and response-specific feedback to provide token-level supervision on each adapter's own generated prefixes. A feedback gate focuses corrective updates on responses judged to need improvement. At deployment, a supervised router uses the request or dialogue to select a single adapter or a fixed composition, without user-specific finetuning. We evaluate PLF on single-turn personalization with model-generated critiques and multi-turn tutoring with simulated students' reactions. Across both settings, PLF improves model-judged preference fit over fixed-persona policies and strong baselines including persona prompting and persona distillation, demonstrating how feedback-trained behaviors can be reused for adaptive assistant personalization without degrading general capabilities.

个性化AI助手需要根据用户偏好和任务上下文调整行为。现有工作通过人格提示或在人格条件生成上微调来获取助手人格。然而，仅有人格描述无法确定在特定回应中应修改或保留什么，也无法判断何种行为适合当前请求。我们提出《基于反馈的人格学习（PLF）》框架，将可复用、基于偏好的助手人格作为独立LoRA适配器，部署于共享冻结主干之上。训练期间，一个冻结的自教师基于人格规范和响应特定反馈，对每个适配器自身生成的前缀提供逐标记监督。反馈门控聚焦于被判定需要改进的响应进行修正更新。部署时，一个监督路由器根据请求或对话选择单个适配器或固定组合，无需用户特定微调。我们在单轮个性化（使用模型生成批评）和多轮辅导（模拟学生反应）两种场景下评估PLF。在两种设置中，PLF均优于固定人格策略和强基线（包括人格提示和人格蒸馏），展示了反馈训练行为如何被复用于自适应助手个性化，且不损害通用能力。

From Leniency to Suspicion: How an Open Post-Training Recipe Changes Whom LLM Fact-Checkers Believe
从宽容到怀疑：开放后训练方案如何改变大语言模型对事实核查对象的信任
LLMs increasingly fact-check online content with the poster's identity in view. However, it remains unclear which post-training changes how much a model's truth judgement depends on the poster's social class, and how such a change happens inside the model. This paper investigates job-based doubt, i.e., judging a claim false more often when the poster has a lower-status job, using fact-checked claims with randomised poster profiles in base and post-trained checkpoints of six model families. Our results reveal that: 1) post-training creates such doubt only with the open Tülu-3 recipe (OLMo-2 32B, Tülu-3 70B), not with Meta's, Alibaba's or Mistral's; the effect is under one percentage point but statistically reliable, exceeding training drift and replicating on independent claim sets; 2) in OLMo-2 32B, the model does not start noticing the poster's job more, but starts interpreting it differently: a lower-status job pushes the base model toward "true" and the post-trained model toward "false"; and 3) this flip is carried by a single residual-stream direction and happens at supervised fine-tuning. The mechanism was confirmed on 400 new claims with pre-specified predictions. None of seven deployed commercial models doubts lower-status posters more. Our work underscores the need to audit speaker bias per training recipe.

大语言模型越来越多地在考虑发布者身份的情况下进行在线内容的事实核查。然而，目前尚不清楚哪些后训练方法会改变模型对发布者社会阶层依赖程度，以及这种变化在模型内部是如何发生的。本文通过在六种模型家族的基础版本和后训练版本中使用随机化发布者资料的事实核查声明，研究了基于职业的怀疑现象，即当发布者职业地位较低时更倾向于判断该陈述为假。研究结果表明：1）只有采用开放的Tülu-3方案（OLMo-2 32B、Tülu-3 70B）才会产生这种怀疑效应，而Meta、阿里和Mistral的方案不会；该效应虽不足百分之一，但统计显著，超过训练漂移，并在独立声明集上可复现；2）在OLMo-2 32B中，模型并未开始更关注发布者的职业，而是对其解释方式发生了变化：低地位职业使基础模型倾向‘真实’，而后训练模型则倾向‘虚假’；3）这一转变由单一残差流方向承载，并发生在监督微调阶段。该机制通过400个新声明的预设预测得到验证。七款已部署的商用模型均未表现出对低地位发布者更大的怀疑。本工作强调了需按训练方案审计发言者偏见的必要性。
👤 Nouar AlDahoul, Yasir Zaki 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38439]
agentic-rlagent-memory
Predict the Reward, Not the World: World Reward Models for ML Agents
预测奖励，而非世界：面向机器学习智能体的世界奖励模型
Search-based machine-learning (ML) agents improve a solution by writing candidate programs, executing them, and keeping the best. Execution gives the search reliable feedback, but a single run can take hours, and most runs end in programs the agent discards. Recent agents try to save these runs by letting a large language model act as a world model that predicts how each program would perform. However, a wrong prediction feeds the search false information. We argue that an agent does not need to predict the outcome of execution, only the decision that execution would support. We propose World Reward Models (WRMs), which learn from real execution results to choose directly which candidate will perform better, without predicting any intermediate outcome. We prove that a selector which predicts execution from the same inputs cannot outperform the best direct selector, that predicted outcomes add an error which can make a likelihood-trained world model prefer the worse program on every instance, and that cross-entropy on executed winners drives a WRM toward the optimum. Our WRM, ForeCritic, adapts a 9B typed decision model to 5k executed program pairs. On 12,438 held-out pairs from 26 ML tasks, it picks the better program 79.4% of the time, while general-purpose LLM judges reach 53--62%, and its probabilities are calibrated. Inside AIDE, ML-Master, and ForeAgent, it cuts mean task time by 4.4--7.5 times while keeping their mean scores within 0.01 or raising them.
中文翻译
基于搜索的机器学习（ML）智能体通过编写候选程序、执行并保留最优解来改进解决方案。执行提供可靠的反馈，但一次运行可能耗时数小时，且大多数运行最终被智能体丢弃。近期智能体尝试让大型语言模型充当世界模型，预测每个程序的表现以节省运行时间。然而，错误预测会向搜索过程传递虚假信息。我们主张，智能体无需预测执行结果，只需预测执行将支持的决策即可。为此提出世界奖励模型（WRMs），其从真实执行结果中学习，直接选择哪个候选程序表现更好，而不预测任何中间结果。我们证明：从相同输入预测执行结果的选择器无法优于最佳直接选择器；预测结果会引入误差，可能导致基于似然训练的世界模型在所有实例中偏好较差的程序；而对已执行胜出者的交叉熵损失能引导WRM趋向最优。我们的WRM模型ForeCritic将一个9B参数的类型化决策模型适配至5,000对已执行程序。在来自26个ML任务的12,438个保留样本对上，它以79.4%的准确率选出表现更好的程序，而通用LLM判断者仅达53%-62%，且其概率分布校准良好。在AIDE、ML-Master和ForeAgent中集成后，平均任务时间减少4.4至7.5倍，同时保持或略微提升平均得分。
👤 Yuxiao Lee, Zherui Yang, Fan Liu, Hao Liu 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv

How Much Do Teacher Bridges Matter in Interleaved On-Policy Distillation?
交错策略蒸馏中教师桥接的重要性有多大？
On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student using teacher predictions on student-generated responses. Some methods insert teacher-written segments, or bridges, but disagree on how to train on those tokens. We compare no loss, forward KL and reverse KL on identical trajectories using Qwen3-1.7B as student and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 as teacher. We separate gradients by which model wrote each token. Under our short training runs and BF16 inference, pre-specified tests detected no added accuracy benefit from teacher text as context or as a training target. A one-paragraph bridge after the student's first paragraph is only 2.7% of the tokens and contributes a small gradient nearly orthogonal to the student-token gradient. In this main comparison, the three loss treatments gave similar gradient directions and were statistically equivalent in accuracy within a +/-5 percentage-point margin. An exploratory pilot placing the bridge where failed responses disagreed most with the teacher suggested a benefit from teacher-token loss. A pre-specified replication did not confirm this, and fourfold weighting showed no significant accuracy benefit. Two limitations bound these findings. Among the tested amounts of training on fixed responses, gains peaked after four updates before the student increasingly failed to stop generating. None of the tested online training variants met the criteria for stable stopping and improved accuracy. Our evaluations used BF16 inference, which rounds away most of the small weight changes. When evaluated in exact FP32, the tested replication models frequently reached the response-length limit. Our accuracy findings therefore concern BF16 inference; they do not establish that teacher-token supervision is generally unnecessary.
中文翻译
策略蒸馏（OPD）使用教师模型对学生生成的响应进行预测来训练学生。某些方法插入教师撰写的片段（即“桥接”），但对如何在这些标记上进行训练存在分歧。我们使用Qwen3-1.7B作为学生模型，Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507作为教师模型，在相同轨迹上比较无损失、前向KL和反向KL三种处理方式。我们按每个标记由哪个模型生成来分离梯度。在短训练周期和BF16推理条件下，预设测试未发现教师文本作为上下文或训练目标带来额外准确性收益。一段位于学生首段之后的桥接段仅占总标记的2.7%，其贡献的梯度几乎与学生标记梯度正交。在主要对比中，三种损失处理方式的梯度方向相似，且在±5个百分点范围内统计等效。探索性初步实验将桥接置于学生失败响应与教师最不一致的位置，暗示教师标记损失可能存在益处。但预设复制实验未确认此结论，四倍加权也未显示显著准确性提升。两项限制约束了这些发现：在固定响应训练量中，增益在四次更新后达到峰值，随后学生越来越难以停止生成；所测试的所有在线训练变体均未满足稳定停止和提升准确性的标准。我们的评估使用BF16推理，会舍弃大部分微小权重变化。在精确FP32评估下，测试复制模型频繁达到响应长度上限。因此，我们的准确性发现仅适用于BF16推理；不能断定教师标记监督通常没有必要。
👤 Leran Chen, Zhiyuan Yao, Lingnan Kong 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38096]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Calculation and rejection diverge in language models tested at known mechanical limits
在已知机械极限下测试的语言模型中，计算与拒绝行为出现分歧
When a language model returns engineering quantities with the status "solved", software that trusts it passes them on, even when the requested design is physically impossible. Such failures could reflect poor calculation, how the answer is requested, or a failure to judge feasibility. We set beam loads and disk speeds at known percentages above or below plastic collapse or burst, measured each model's limit calculation separately and tested 12 models in 4,860 calls, including follow-up experiments on new designs. We find that a model can recognize an impossible design when asked only whether it is feasible, yet accept the same design, reporting "solved", when asked for a full answer. Three models accepted beams 10 to 30% beyond collapse despite precise limit calculations in separate questions, and many accepted replies stated the violation. Asked only about feasibility, Opus 5 and Gemini 3.8 Flash rejected every impossible new design they were given, including those accepted in full answers, without false alarms. Rewording when to decline or changing the answer format raised rejection on the original beams, but not consistently on new designs. Software and benchmarks using a model's status should test feasibility judgment and full-answer rejection separately, and report the exact prompt.
中文翻译
当语言模型返回工程量并标注为'已解决'时，依赖它的软件会直接传递这些结果，即使所请求的设计在物理上不可能实现。这类失败可能源于计算不佳、答案请求方式不当，或对可行性判断的缺失。我们设定梁载荷和盘片转速在塑性屈服或爆裂极限的已知百分比之上或之下，分别测量每个模型的极限计算能力，并在4860次调用中测试了12个模型，包括对新设计的后续实验。研究发现，当仅询问设计是否可行时，模型能识别不可能的设计；但当要求完整回答时，却接受相同设计并报告'已解决'。三个模型接受了超出屈服极限10%至30%的梁结构，尽管它们在单独问题中进行了精确的极限计算，且许多接受的回答明确指出了违规情况。仅询问可行性时，Opus 5和Gemini 3.8 Flash对所有被给予的不可能新设计均予以拒绝，包括那些在完整回答中被接受的设计，且无误报。改变拒绝时机或答案格式虽提高了原始梁结构的拒绝率，但在新设计上并不一致。因此，使用模型状态的软件和基准应分别测试可行性判断与完整回答拒绝能力，并准确报告提示内容。

SkillMem: Compact External Skill Memory for Latent Multi-Agent Reasoning
SkillMem：用于潜在多智能体推理的紧凑外部技能记忆
Latent multi-agent systems reduce inference cost by keeping intermediate reasoning and inter-agent communication in continuous representations.\ Reusable skills, however, are commonly supplied as text or stored as layer-wise Key-Value states or skill-specific model parameters, introducing repeated processing or substantial per-skill storage.\ We introduce \textbf{SkillMem}, which represents each reusable skill as a small external latent memory and makes it directly available during latent multi-agent reasoning.\ A role-conditioned cross-attention reader allows the same memory to guide different agents according to their roles and current hidden states.\ We train the skill memories and shared reader through behavioral distillation, matching the change in token predictions induced by a textual skill while keeping the backbone frozen.\ For our experiments, we obtain textual skills from paired successful and failed multi-agent trajectories, although existing skills can also be compiled directly.\ Across six benchmarks and Qwen3 models ranging from 4B to 14B parameters, SkillMem provides strong gains for smaller backbones while preserving efficient latent multi-agent inference across model scales.\ We use the same $8\times256$ memory configuration across all evaluated model scales, requiring 9.6\,KB of skill-specific storage per skill, including its retrieval embedding.\ For a library of 100 skills, SkillMem reduces total storage by more than $38\times$ compared with alternative skill representations and achieves up to a $4.23\times$ end-to-end inference speedup.\ These results show that compact procedural memories can be reused without sacrificing the efficiency of latent multi-agent reasoning.
中文翻译
潜在多智能体系统通过将中间推理和智能体间通信保留在连续表示中来降低推理成本。然而，可复用的技能通常以文本形式提供，或作为逐层键值状态、或特定技能的模型参数存储，导致重复处理或大量每技能存储开销。我们提出 extbf{SkillMem}，将每个可复用技能表示为小型外部潜在记忆，并在潜在多智能体推理过程中直接可用。一种角色条件交叉注意力读取器允许同一记忆根据智能体的角色和当前隐藏状态引导不同智能体。我们通过行为蒸馏训练技能记忆和共享读取器，匹配文本技能引入的标记预测变化，同时保持主干网络冻结。实验中，我们从成对的成功与失败多智能体轨迹中获取文本技能，也可直接编译现有技能。在六个基准测试和参数量从4B到14B的Qwen3模型上，SkillMem在较小主干模型上表现优异，同时在不同模型规模下保持高效的潜在多智能体推理。我们在所有评估模型规模上使用相同的$8 imes256$内存配置，每项技能仅需9.6KB的专用存储（含检索嵌入）。对于包含100项技能的库，SkillMem相比其他技能表示方式将总存储减少超过38倍，并实现最高达4.23倍的端到端推理加速。结果表明，紧凑的过程记忆可在不牺牲潜在多智能体推理效率的前提下被有效复用。

SciGame: Let Agents Play the Scientific Discovery Games in the Real and Counterfactual World
SciGame：让智能体在真实与反事实世界中玩科学发现游戏
Large language model agents are increasingly used for scientific discovery. However, existing benchmarks mix law inference with programming, score data fitting instead of prediction on unseen inputs, and do not separate empirical evidence from pretrained priors. We introduce SciGame, a controlled benchmark that converts scientific literature into structured scientific cards. Each card yields a pair of Original and dimensionally consistent Counterfactual environments with the same task interface. SciGame contains a Historical collection of canonical laws and a live Temporal collection curated from papers published after model knowledge cutoffs. It evaluates agents in candidate-guided Identification and open-ended Discovery settings. Each setting runs under Prior-only, Fixed, and Adaptive protocols. Experiments on five frontier models yield three findings. First, familiar priors hurt performance once the governing relation is altered. Second, adaptive experimentation helps the strongest models but harms weaker ones under limited budgets. Third, good explanations can hide quantitative failures. Recovering the exact symbolic form remains the main bottleneck, even when agents interpret evidence correctly. Current agents can investigate systems actively, but inducing exact laws in unfamiliar settings remains an open challenge.
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体正越来越多地应用于科学发现。然而，现有基准测试混合了定律推断与编程任务，以数据拟合得分代替对未见输入的预测能力评估，并未区分实证证据与预训练先验知识。我们提出SciGame，一个受控基准，将科学文献转化为结构化的科学卡片。每张卡片生成一对原始环境与维度一致的反事实环境，具有相同的任务接口。SciGame包含历史版经典定律集合和从模型知识截止日期后发表论文中持续更新的实时时间集合。它在候选引导识别与开放式发现两种设置下评估智能体。每种设置在仅先验、固定和自适应三种协议下运行。对五款前沿模型的实验揭示三个发现：第一，一旦主导关系发生变化，熟悉的先验知识反而损害性能；第二，自适应实验对最强模型有益，但在资源有限的情况下对较弱模型有害；第三，良好解释可能掩盖定量失败。恢复精确符号形式仍是主要瓶颈，即使智能体正确解读了证据。当前智能体能够主动探索系统，但在陌生情境中推导精确规律仍是开放挑战。
👤 Yusheng Zhao, Bowei He, Xue Liu, Mingxiao Li 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

Muon Makes the First Mixture-of-Experts Layer Harder to Balance
Muon使首个专家混合层更难平衡
Many open mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models train with Muon instead of AdamW. Most balance the load with a bias on each expert's routing score, adjusted after every step. Their recipes disagree on whether Muon should also train the router. In models with 55M and 108M active parameters, we measure how the optimizer changes the span of these biases, the largest minus the smallest in a layer. The span grows when all tokens favor the same experts, so it shows how hard a layer is to balance. At learning rates tuned on shorter runs, Muon raised the span of the first MoE layer at every seed, roughly doubling it on average, while the other layers changed much less. The rise came from Muon on the rest of the network, whichever optimizer trained the router. In those runs, the inputs to the first router shared a direction, and soon after the warmup its busiest expert averaged four to seven times its fair share. Over the first 3,000 steps, subtracting the batch mean from these inputs removed this overload while barely changing the loss. On the smaller model, halving Muon's learning rate removed most of the rise and slightly improved the loss. Moving only the router to AdamW made the loads shift more during the warmup and slightly raised the loss, so at this scale the results favor keeping the router on Muon.
中文翻译
许多开源的专家混合（MoE）语言模型使用Muon而非AdamW进行训练。大多数通过在每个专家的路由得分上添加偏置并每步调整来平衡负载。关于Muon是否也应训练路由器，不同训练方案存在分歧。在包含5500万和10800万活跃参数的模型中，我们测量了优化器如何改变这些偏置的跨度（即一层中最大值与最小值之差）。当所有token都倾向于相同专家时，该跨度增大，反映了该层平衡的难度。在经过短周期调优的学习率下，Muon在每次随机种子实验中均显著提升了首个MoE层的偏置跨度，平均翻倍，而其他层变化较小。这一上升源于网络其余部分的Muon，无论路由器由哪个优化器训练。在这些实验中，首个路由器的输入共享同一方向，暖启动后其最繁忙的专家平均承担了四到七倍于公平份额的负载。在前3000步内，从输入中减去批次均值可消除这种过载，且几乎不影响损失。在小模型上，将Muon学习率减半可消除大部分上升并略微改善损失。仅将路由器移至AdamW会导致暖启动期间负载分布更剧烈波动，并略微提高损失，因此在此规模下，结果支持保持路由器使用Muon。
👤 Marcos Hernanz 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37903]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Think Twice: The Metis-1.6 Technical Report
再思：Metis-1.6技术报告
Metis is a bet that better data, better training methods and new architectures can buy what a bigger parameter count cannot, at a fraction of the cost. Metis-1.6 is its latest step: two language models built from scratch by a single researcher, Praxis (2.94B parameters, 0.46B active per pass) and Logos (10.23B parameters, 1.18B active per pass). Both run a hybrid Mamba-2 and attention mixture-of-experts stack twice for every token, so each token gets a second look. Each was trained on 1.02T tokens in total: 804.76B of pretraining, 18B of context extension to 131,072 tokens, and 200B of supervised fine-tuning. Metis-1.6 was never meant to challenge frontier models, and across 40 post-trained benchmarks it mostly does not: Logos averages 52.93, seventh of 17 models in its comparison set and within a point of Qwen3-4B. But it gets there on roughly a twelfth of the median training data of the comparators that disclose it, and with fewer active parameters per token than any model that beats it, which puts it on the Pareto frontier for both. In mathematics it ranks second of 30 models, with 67.50% on AIME 2025 and 63.64% on HMMT February 2026. It is weak at code, instruction following and long context. The rest of the report is the part I found most valuable: what went wrong. The optimizer I configured never ran, and attention trained with AdamW for all of pretraining. A 1.77B-parameter n-gram memory learned to switch itself off. Five fine-tuning choices cost capability. And a length reward under group-mean normalization punished correct solutions for being long once a group's pass rate passed 2/3, a sign inversion I prove and fix with a one-line clamp. I close with the 10B-token ablations behind MoRE (Mixture of Recursive Experts), the looped architecture of Metis-1.7.
中文翻译
Metis是一种信念：更好的数据、更优的训练方法和新型架构可以在远低于大参数量的成本下实现原本需要更大参数才能达成的效果。Metis-1.6是其最新进展：由单个研究人员Praxis从头构建的两个语言模型——Praxis（29.4亿参数，每轮激活0.46亿）和Logos（102.3亿参数，每轮激活11.8亿）。两者每token执行两次混合Mamba-2与注意力的专家混合堆栈，实现二次审视。每个模型总共训练1.02万亿token：8047.6亿用于预训练，180亿用于上下文扩展至131,072 token，2000亿用于监督微调。Metis-1.6本非为挑战前沿模型而设计，在40个微调后基准测试中表现平平：Logos平均得分为52.93，17个对比模型中排名第7，与Qwen3-4B相差不到1分。但其训练数据仅为对比模型中位数的十二分之一，且每token活跃参数少于所有击败它的模型，使其在两项指标上均位于帕累托前沿。在数学任务中，它位列30个模型中的第二，AIME 2025得分为67.50%，HMMT February 2026得分为63.64%。其在代码生成、指令遵循和长上下文处理方面表现较弱。报告其余部分是我认为最有价值的部分：失败原因分析。我配置的优化器从未运行，整个预训练阶段注意力模块均使用AdamW训练。一个17.7亿参数的n元语法记忆模块学会自我关闭。五个微调选择导致能力损失。此外，长度奖励在组均值归一化下惩罚了较长的正确解，一旦组通过率超过2/3，便产生符号反转；我证明并用一行代码修复了此问题。最后，报告详述了支撑Metis-1.7循环架构的100亿token消融实验，即MoRE（递归专家混合）。
👤 Giulianno Vollmer 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37849]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Detecting Covert Collusion between AI Agents through Partner-Outsider Positive Listening
通过伙伴-外部者正向倾听检测AI代理间的隐秘共谋
AI agents that share a secret convention can coordinate through messages that look innocent, so a monitor that reads only the messages can miss them. We propose a behavioural test: a partner that uses a code reacts to the exact wording of a message, whereas an uninvolved agent placed in its seat does not. Partner--Outsider Positive Listening (PoPLi) is the difference between the two agents in how much their action changes when the message is reworded with its meaning kept, and it is computed after the fact by replaying logged prompts. In a simulated market with five conditions (no collusion, a joint reward with neutral messages, the same with an openly stated intention, a covert first-letter code, and the code with a stated intention), PoPLi is between 0.48 and 0.59 whenever the code is present (95% intervals all above 0.29) and between -0.10 and -0.01 without it. Message-only monitors did not separate the conditions: one reading the speaker's messages answered "no" in all 50 markets, and one reading every seller's messages flagged 9 of 10 markets with no collusion. PoPLi detects the use of a private channel, not collusion as such, and it cannot detect collusion through incentives alone or codes carried by meaning.
中文翻译
共享秘密约定的AI代理可通过看似无害的消息进行协调，因此仅阅读消息的监控系统可能遗漏此类行为。我们提出一种行为测试：使用编码的伙伴会根据消息的具体措辞作出反应，而处于相同位置的无关代理则不会。伙伴-外部者正向倾听（PoPLi）衡量这两个代理在消息重述但语义保持不变时，其行为变化程度的差异，该指标可在事后通过回放日志计算。在包含五种情境的模拟市场中（无共谋、联合奖励+中性消息、相同但明确声明意图、隐蔽首字母编码、编码加声明意图），只要存在编码，PoPLi值介于0.48至0.59之间（95%置信区间均高于0.29），而无编码时则介于-0.10至-0.01之间。仅读取发言者消息的监控系统无法区分这些条件：一个仅读取说话者消息的监控者在全部50个市场中均回答“否”；另一个读取所有卖家消息的监控者在无共谋的10个市场中误报了9个。PoPLi检测的是私密通信通道的使用，而非共谋本身，也无法仅通过激励或语义承载的编码检测共谋。
👤 Kevin Yandoka Denamganaï 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37741]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Agentic Backdoors via Pretraining Poisoning
通过预训练污染实现代理后门攻击
Large language models are pretrained on vast, uncurated corpora scraped from the public web. This creates a security risk from attackers who plant poisoned content in the pretraining data. We show for the first time that such poisoning can plant a remote code execution backdoor in an agent, causing it to execute attacker-chosen commands on the user's machine. To measure the threat end to end, we pretrain Qwen3 models from scratch at sizes 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B, and then post-train each into a CLI agent using a clean pipeline. Poisoning only 0.1% of the pretraining corpus with our new poison design implants the backdoor: the deployed agent executes the malicious command when the trigger is present while behaving normally on clean inputs. The attack success rate (ASR) increases with model scale, with the 4B model reaching roughly 92% ASR at a 0.1% poison rate and staying positive even when the poisoning rate drops to 0.0005%. Our code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/agentic-backdoor.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在海量、未经筛选的公共网络爬取语料上进行预训练，这为攻击者在预训练数据中植入有毒内容创造了安全风险。我们首次证明，此类污染可在一个智能体中植入远程代码执行后门，使其在用户机器上执行攻击者指定的命令。为端到端评估威胁，我们从头开始预训练Qwen3模型，分别在0.6B、1.7B和4B三种规模下进行，并通过干净的流程将其微调为CLI代理。仅用我们新设计的毒化方案污染0.1%的预训练语料，即可植入后门：部署后的代理在触发词出现时执行恶意命令，而在干净输入下表现正常。攻击成功率（ASR）随模型规模增加而提升，4B模型在0.1%污染率下ASR约为92%，即使污染率降至0.0005%仍保持正值。代码已公开于https://github.com/safety-research/agentic-backdoor。
👤 Xinyan Hu, Pingbang Hu, Chloe Loughridge, Xuanli He, Nicholas Carlini et al. 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37739]

OpenFrontiers: A Collection of Open Research Problems and a Benchmark for Scientific Rediscovery
OpenFrontiers：一个开放研究问题集合与科学再发现的基准测试
Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in research-level problem solving, yet a comprehensive collection of such problems is still missing, and evaluating them on research-level problems remains challenging. We introduce OpenFrontiers, a cross-disciplinary resource that combines a structured corpus of open research problems with a retrospective benchmark of recently resolved problems in mathematics. The corpus records problem statements, context, progress, and other information for 12,151 problems. The benchmark contains 200 problems, 110 resolved in 2026 and 90 still open, each with a Lean~4 statement checked by automated probes and two independent reviews; a subset HQ-50 further filters based on quality score, etc. Our evaluation protocol grades formal proofs with two independent checkers in an isolated sandbox under closed-book, frozen-retrieval, and live-web conditions. Evaluating seven LLM agents on the HQ-50 subset under a standardized budget, we find that closed-book rediscovery is rare, at most 7 of 40 resolved problems, whereas live-web success mostly reflects retrieval of published solutions. By linking a broad research-problem corpus to a formally graded evaluation subset with documented solutions, OpenFrontiers provides a foundation for measuring research-level rediscovery and studying progress toward solving currently open scientific problems. The dataset and benchmark could be found in https://huggingface.co/collections/FlippyDora/openfrontiers.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在解决研究级问题方面展现出日益增长的潜力，但目前仍缺乏全面的研究问题集合，且在研究级问题上的评估依然具有挑战性。我们提出了OpenFrontiers，一个跨学科资源，结合了结构化的开放研究问题语料库和数学领域近期已解决问题的回顾性基准。该语料库记录了12,151个问题的问题陈述、背景、进展等信息。基准包含200个问题，其中110个在2026年已解决，90个仍待解决，每个问题均配有Lean~4形式化陈述，并经过自动化探测器和两次独立评审；其中HQ-50子集进一步基于质量评分等标准进行筛选。我们的评估协议在封闭书本、冻结检索和实时网络条件下，使用隔离沙箱中的两个独立检查器对形式化证明进行评分。在标准化预算下对七个LLM智能体在HQ-50子集上的评估显示，封闭书本条件下的再发现极为罕见，最多仅能解决40个问题中的7个，而实时网络的成功主要反映对已发表解决方案的检索。通过将广泛的研究问题语料库与形式化评分的评估子集及已记录解决方案相连接，OpenFrontiers为衡量研究级再发现和研究当前开放科学问题的进展提供了基础。数据集和基准可在https://huggingface.co/collections/FlippyDora/openfrontiers获取。

[2610.37732]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
ThinkJev: One Thought, Every Budget for Calibrated Typed Decisions
ThinkJev：一次思考，适用于所有预算的校准型类型决策
Many uses of language models end in a typed decision: a choice among a closed set of options, whose probabilities code can act on. Reading those probabilities from one forward pass is fast but often wrong on decisions that take several steps, such as arithmetic, counting, and date comparison. Letting the model think first fixes most of these errors, but costs seconds per decision instead of milliseconds. To pay for thinking only where it helps, ThinkJev builds a fast and a slow system from one open hybrid-thinking model that can answer directly or after a thought. System 1 reads the model's probabilities for the option letters when it answers directly; System 2 reads the same letters after the model has thought on the same cached prompt, so escalating repeats no work and both systems give probabilities over the same options. Because the letters can be read after any number of thought tokens, one sampled thought also gives the answer under every thinking budget. Without training, ThinkJev lets System 1 answer alone only where a statistical test on labeled dev data guarantees that its accepted answers stay under a target error rate. At a 5% target, Qwen3.5-27B answers 24.5% of 8359 test decisions this way with a realized error of 3.9% (Qwen3.5-4B and 9B: 6.5% and 0.0%). For the rest, stopping each thought as soon as its answer is confident reaches 81.6% accuracy, against 82.7% for always thinking, with 72% of the thought tokens. Choosing when to think from System 1's confidence alone saves less, because the decisions System 1 is least sure of are often not the ones thinking fixes. On the public items of JevBench, an independent benchmark of typed-decision services, Qwen3.5-27B thinks on 29.4% and is not distinguishable in accuracy from the two thinking APIs listed (exploratory). Code and data generators are available at https://github.com/ding-xiaolu/ThinkJev.
中文翻译
许多语言模型的应用最终都以类型化决策收尾：从一组封闭选项中做出选择，其概率可被直接行动所利用。仅通过一次前向传播读取这些概率虽然快速，但在涉及多步推理的任务（如算术、计数和日期比较）中往往出错。让模型先进行思考可以修复大多数此类错误，但代价是每次决策需耗时数秒而非毫秒。为了仅在真正有益时才付出思考成本，ThinkJev构建了一个由单一开放混合思考模型组成的快速系统和慢速系统，该模型可直接回答或在思考后回答。系统1在直接回答时读取模型对选项字母的概率；系统2则在模型对同一缓存提示进行思考后读取相同的字母，因此升级过程不重复工作，两个系统对相同选项给出概率。由于字母可在任意数量的思考标记后读取，一次采样的思考即可覆盖所有思考预算下的答案。无需训练，ThinkJev仅允许系统1单独回答那些在标注开发数据上通过统计检验保证其接受答案误差率低于目标值的情况。在5%的目标误差率下，Qwen3.5-27B以这种方式回答了8359个测试决策中的24.5%，实际误差率为3.9%（Qwen3.5-4B和9B分别为6.5%和0.0%）。对于其余部分，只要在每次思考中一旦答案足够自信就停止，即可达到81.6%的准确率，相比始终思考的82.7%准确率，仅消耗72%的思考标记。仅根据系统1的置信度决定何时思考，效果较差，因为系统1最不确定的决策往往并非思考能修复的。在JevBench公开项目（一个类型化决策服务的独立基准）上，Qwen3.5-27B在29.4%的情况下选择思考，其准确率与列出的两个思考API无显著差异（探索性结果）。代码和数据生成器可在https://github.com/ding-xiaolu/ThinkJev获取。
👤 Xiaolu Ding, Kai Yang, Dongdong Liu 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37719]
agentic-rl
What a Weight Update Buys: Leverage Across Training Stages
权重更新的价值：训练阶段间的杠杆效应
Post-training moves a language model's weights by about a thousandth of their norm and changes its behavior sharply; pretraining moves them by a third and buys a slow drift. We introduce leverage, an exact measure of what a unit of weight change buys: the Fisher quadratic form of an update, computed with one forward-mode pass per direction, divided by the same form for random directions of the same size at the same checkpoint. Leverage orders released Olmo-3, Tülu-3, Zephyr, and OLMo-2 checkpoints over four orders of magnitude: pretraining windows sit at the random level on prose, SFT five times above it, RL-Zero and RLVR at 400-1,500, and DPO at 400-23,000. With this instrument we find that pretraining accumulates into noise: windows of 30k to 1M steps are no more consequential on prose than random directions of their size, as a noisy quadratic model of SGD predicts. We find that training precision changes what an update looks like: training bf16 parameters without an fp32 master copy freezes 36-99% of coordinates, lowers leverage up to sevenfold, and creates an alignment between update magnitudes and the diagonal Fisher that fp32 training does not show. We derive and verify a law for reuse: the sign-flip null predicts the relative damage of random drop-and-rescale (DARE) at keep rate $p$ as $(1-p)/(p\,ρ_{\mathrm{sign}})$, within a factor of two of measurement wherever DARE succeeds. Two controls make these readings reliable: every controlled SFT and DPO trajectory with fp32 master weights loses leverage as it moves, and two re-runs of a released DPO stage from its anchor with its published recipe land within 1% of the released displacement and 4% of its leverage, where a plain recipe reads five to six times lower; leaving one component out traces the level to the length-normalized loss and its batch size of 128 together.
中文翻译
后训练阶段对语言模型权重的调整约为其范数的千分之一，会显著改变模型行为；而预训练阶段的调整量约为其三分之一，带来缓慢的漂移。我们引入“杠杆”这一精确度量指标，用于衡量单位权重变化所带来的收益：即通过每方向一次前向模式传递计算的更新的Fisher二次型，除以同尺寸随机方向在同一检查点处的相同形式。杠杆指标对释放的Olmo-3、Tülu-3、Zephyr和OLMo-2检查点进行了四数量级的排序：预训练窗口在散文任务上处于随机水平，SFT高出五倍，RL-Zero和RLVR在400至1,500之间，DPO则在400至23,000之间。借助此工具，我们发现预训练积累成噪声：30k至1M步长的窗口在散文任务上的影响与同尺寸随机方向无异，这符合SGD的噪声二次模型预测。我们还发现训练精度会影响更新的表现：在未使用fp32主副本的情况下以bf16训练参数会冻结36%-99%的坐标，使杠杆降低最高达七倍，并产生更新幅度与对角Fisher之间的对齐关系，而fp32训练则不会出现这种现象。我们推导并验证了一条重用定律：符号翻转零点预测随机丢弃并重新缩放（DARE）在保留率$p$下的相对损害为$(1-p)/(p\,ρ_{\mathrm{sign}})$，在DARE有效时测量值与其相差不超过两倍。两个控制措施确保了测量可靠性：所有使用fp32主权重的受控SFT和DPO轨迹在移动过程中均表现出杠杆下降；两次从锚点按发布配方重运行DPO阶段的结果与释放的位移和杠杆值相差小于1%和4%，而普通配方读数低五到六倍；去掉一个组件后，该水平可归因于长度归一化损失及其批大小128的共同作用。
👤 Shikhar Shiromani 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv

Homebrewed SLMs: Autoregressive and Masked Diffusion Language Models Built at Home for Portuguese
自制小型语言模型：面向葡萄牙语的自回归与掩码扩散语言模型
Building a language model of one's own no longer requires a data centre. This tutorial trains one from scratch, tokenizer included, on one consumer GPU, and shows how to tell whether it is good for the tasks of a specialist domain. It proposes no new method. It releases a tested Python package and two notebooks in Brazilian Portuguese with autoregressive and masked diffusion generation on one network, a preregistered comparison protocol, legal tasks built from text alone with a Kneser-Ney floor and public yardsticks, reference numbers for models of 11 to 12M parameters, and a catalogue of defects that only measurement revealed. On Brazilian prose and federal laws, at minutes per run, the autoregressive mode peaks at 64 epochs over the same text and then memorises it, while masked diffusion improves up to 256. At their best points on test, the diffusion bound exceeds the autoregressive likelihood by 0.06 bits per byte, within the preregistered minimum effect. On held-out laws and on acts published after training, the model ties a 6-gram floor in likelihood on the same windows, and it loses to the floor at continuing those acts. It beats the floor at detecting tampered text, and fills cross-references more often than the floor and public models of 160M and 630M parameters, only in decrees, a genre absent from training, where those scorers stay near or below chance, and in filling the blank only when each group weighs the same, since per item it ties the 630M model. On laws, the public models, trained with about 5x10^2 to 10^5 times its compute, lead in tampered text and continuation. The blind human reading is preregistered and not part of this version.
中文翻译
如今，构建属于自己的语言模型不再需要数据中心。本教程从零开始，在单个消费级GPU上训练一个包含分词器的语言模型，并展示如何判断其是否适用于特定专业领域的任务。本文未提出新方法。它发布了一个经过测试的Python包及两个巴西葡萄牙语的笔记本，采用单一网络实现自回归与掩码扩散生成，包含预注册的比较协议、仅基于文本构建的法律任务（采用Kneser-Ney下限）、公开基准、参数量为11到12M的模型参考指标，以及仅通过测量揭示的缺陷目录。在巴西散文和联邦法律数据上，自回归模式在运行几分钟后达到64轮次峰值并开始记忆原文，而掩码扩散模型则持续优化至256轮次。在最佳测试点，扩散模型的边界超出自回归似然0.06比特/字节，处于预注册最小效应范围内。在保留的法律条文及训练后发布的法案上，模型在相同窗口内的似然表现与6元语法下限持平，但在延续这些法案时落后于下限。模型在检测篡改文本方面优于下限，且在填充交叉引用方面优于160M和630M参数的公共模型，但仅在训练中未出现的法令类别中表现突出，而这些类别中其他模型得分接近或低于随机水平；在填空任务中，仅当每组权重相等时才胜过630M模型。在法律任务上，公共模型（训练计算量约为其5×10²至10⁵倍）在篡改文本检测和续写任务上领先。盲测人类阅读已预注册，不包含在此版本中。

[2610.40511]
A Low-Rank Space Captures Linear Alignment Between LLM and Human Neural Activity During Naturalistic Language Listening
低秩空间捕捉大型语言模型与人类神经活动在自然语言聆听中的线性对齐
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as computational models of human language processing, yet it remains unclear which components of their representations drive alignment with neural activity. Here, we use ridge reduced-rank regression to identify the brain-predictive subspace of LLM representations: the representational directions that support linear prediction of neural responses. Across three naturalistic language-listening datasets spanning intracranial electrophysiology and fMRI, and six model conditions spanning autoregressive, bidirectional masked, and randomly initialized language models, we find that full linear encoding performance can be recovered using only 1-3% of the original embedding dimensionality. These low-dimensional subspaces generalize across held-out participants and, to a lesser extent, across datasets, suggesting that neural predictivity is concentrated in a compact and partially shared representational space. We next compare these brain-predictive directions with the principal axes of variation in the embeddings. Principal component analysis requires substantially more dimensions to recover neural prediction, demonstrating that the directions most useful for predicting neural activity are distinct from those capturing the most overall variance within LLM representations themselves. Finally, characterizing the learned subspaces allows us to surface interpretable features that are overrepresented in neural activity above and beyond the LLM representations. Together, these results show that LLM-brain alignment is supported by a small, structured subset of model representations and provides a framework for better understanding exactly why these representations align. Code can be found at: https://github.com/thefinnlab/low-rank-alignment
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用作人类语言处理的计算模型，但其表示中哪些组件驱动了与神经活动的对齐仍不明确。我们使用岭降秩回归识别出LLM表示中具有脑预测性的子空间：能够支持神经反应线性预测的表征方向。在涵盖颅内电生理和fMRI的三个自然语言聆听数据集，以及涵盖自回归、双向掩码和随机初始化语言模型的六种模型条件下，我们发现仅需原始嵌入维度的1-3%即可恢复完整的线性编码性能。这些低维子空间在未参与训练的受试者间具有泛化能力，且在一定程度上跨数据集泛化，表明神经可预测性集中于一个紧凑且部分共享的表征空间。接下来，我们将这些脑预测方向与嵌入中变化的主要轴进行比较。主成分分析需要更多维度才能恢复神经预测能力，证明对预测神经活动最有用的方向与捕捉LLM表示整体方差最大的方向截然不同。最后，通过表征学习子空间的特征刻画，我们揭示了在神经活动中显著高于LLM表示本身的可解释特征。综上所述，这些结果表明LLM与大脑的对齐由模型表示的一个小而结构化的子集所支持，并为理解这些表示为何对齐提供了框架。代码可在：https://github.com/thefinnlab/low-rank-alignment 获取。

[2610.40196]
Towards Full-Text Scholarly Argument Mining using Entity-Anchored Claim Extraction
基于实体锚定的主张提取实现全文学术论点挖掘
Argument mining in full-text scholarly articles remains an important yet challenging task. This difficulty stems not only from the need to model long-distance dependencies but also from the substantial effort required to annotate sentence-level argumentative components throughout lengthy documents. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel approach that leverages scientific entity mentions (e.g., tasks, datasets, and models) to identify and structure claims in scholarly literature. We validate our approach through a case study involving 50 full-text research papers on sentiment analysis. We first establish a reliable human gold standard (Krippendorff's $α= 0.80$), identifying 113 claim-bearing sentences and extracting 199 structured claim records from 300 candidate sentences. To address the annotation bottleneck, we employ a majority-voting ensemble of large language models (LLMs) to generate a silver-standard dataset comprising 1,487 claims. Encoders fine-tuned exclusively on the silver-standard dataset outperform those trained on a small human-annotated gold-standard dataset and demonstrate strong zero-shot transfer to NLP papers outside the training corpus.
中文翻译
全文学术文章中的论点挖掘仍是一个重要但具有挑战性的任务。这一困难不仅源于需要建模长距离依赖关系，还在于在冗长文档中对句子级论点成分进行标注所需的巨大工作量。为应对这些挑战，我们提出一种新方法，利用科学实体提及（如任务、数据集和模型）来识别并结构化学术文献中的主张。我们通过一项涉及50篇情感分析领域全文研究论文的案例研究验证了该方法。首先建立了一个可靠的真人黄金标准（Krippendorff's α=0.80），从300个候选句子中识别出113个承载主张的句子，并提取出199条结构化主张记录。为解决标注瓶颈，我们采用大型语言模型（LLMs）的多数投票集成生成了一个银质标准数据集，包含1,487个主张。仅在银质标准数据集上微调的编码器优于在小规模真人标注黄金标准数据集上训练的模型，并在训练语料库外的NLP论文上展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。
