[2610.40091]
Upcycling Language Models for Multiple Specialized Domains
面向多专业领域的语言模型再利用
Effective multi-domain adaptation of large language models requires prioritizing relevant domain knowledge while minimizing interference from irrelevant domains. Existing approaches commonly use Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to train a separate adapter for each domain and combine these adapters within one model. However, they either weight adapters equally regardless of the input domain or dynamically weight adapters while still activating all of them including irrelevant ones. Additionally, while efficient, their low-rank structure limits performance. In contrast, upcycling is a model expansion method that adds experts and a router to a dense Transformer layer offering larger capacity than LoRA approaches. In this paper, we propose Domain-Specialized Upcycling (DS-Upcycling) for multi-domain adaptation. We introduce a new domain-aware supervision that allows the router to effectively learn to distinguish domains by selecting the appropriate experts. During fine-tuning on each new domain, we update only the corresponding expert and router while freezing all other parameters. Experiments show that our method achieves the highest average performance across the adapted domains compared to baselines while retaining general performance. With just 256 examples per domain for router supervision, DS-Upcycling achieves 90-98% routing accuracy after adaptation compared with 43-70% for routers trained without our approach.
中文翻译
大语言模型的有效多领域适应需要优先保留相关领域知识，同时最小化无关领域带来的干扰。现有方法通常使用低秩适配（LoRA）为每个领域训练独立适配器，并将这些适配器整合到一个模型中。然而，它们要么对所有适配器同等加权，无论输入领域如何；要么动态加权适配器，但仍激活所有适配器，包括无关的那些。此外，尽管高效，其低秩结构限制了性能表现。相比之下，再利用（upcycling）是一种模型扩展方法，在密集Transformer层中添加专家和路由器，提供比LoRA方法更大的容量。本文提出领域专用再利用（DS-Upcycling）以实现多领域适应。我们引入一种新的领域感知监督机制，使路由器能够有效学习区分不同领域，选择合适的专家。在对每个新领域进行微调时，仅更新对应专家和路由器，其余参数保持冻结。实验表明，我们的方法在适应后的各领域平均性能上优于基线，同时保持了通用性能。仅需每领域256个示例用于路由器监督，DS-Upcycling在适应后达到90-98%的路由准确率，而未采用本方法训练的路由器仅为43-70%。
👤 Mingzi Cao, Xi Wang, Nikolaos Aletras 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.40051]
Rethinking Pre-pretraining
重新思考预预训练
We report that allocating compute to synthetic pre-pretraining (PPT) never outperforms pure natural-language training in our compute-matched experiments. This holds across four PPT curricula (k-Dyck, neural cellular automata (NCA), declarative reference corpora, and code) and three training regimes: data-constrained, data-unconstrained, and a high token-to-parameter-ratio regime (881M parameters trained on 50B tokens). When natural language is scarce, repeated epochs over a 100M-token corpus beat every synthetic curriculum on natural-language validation loss, with no consistent downstream accuracy advantage from PPT. We find that PPT gains reported in prior work are sensitive to the weight initialization scheme. In an NCA reproduction, we recover a 5.4% perplexity improvement from PPT under the original initialization scheme. An alternative scheme improves both methods, but the no-PPT baseline more, closing the gap between them. However, when PPT receives additional compute rather than a share of the natural-language budget, gains persist at small natural-language budgets even with improved initialization, but disappear by 1B tokens. Finally, models forget what they learn during PPT in subsequent natural-language training; interleaving the same data throughout training prevents forgetting, but does not significantly improve natural-language performance. Our results suggest limited benefits of synthetic pre-pretraining as a distinct training stage and motivate rethinking how such data are used in language model training.
中文翻译
我们在计算量匹配的实验中发现，将算力分配给合成预预训练（PPT）从未优于纯自然语言训练。这一结论在四种PPT课程（k-Dyck、神经细胞自动机NCA、命题参考语料库和代码）以及三种训练范式（数据受限、数据无约束、高token与参数比范式，即8.81亿参数在500亿token上训练）下均成立。当自然语言数据稀缺时，对1亿token语料库进行多次遍历的训练方式在自然语言验证损失上优于所有合成课程，且PPT在下游任务中并未表现出一致的准确性优势。我们发现，先前研究中报告的PPT收益对权重初始化方案高度敏感。在NCA复现实验中，原始初始化方案下恢复了5.4%的困惑度提升。而另一种初始化方案则同时提升了两种方法的表现，但未使用PPT的基线提升更多，缩小了两者差距。然而，当PPT获得额外算力而非自然语言预算的份额时，即使在改进初始化的情况下，PPT在小规模自然语言预算下仍能保持收益，但在达到10亿token后收益消失。最后，模型在后续自然语言训练中会遗忘PPT阶段所学内容；在整个训练过程中交错使用相同数据可防止遗忘，但并未显著提升自然语言性能。我们的结果表明，合成预预训练作为独立训练阶段的收益有限，促使我们重新思考此类数据在语言模型训练中的使用方式。
👤 Alexander Stern, Tim Rocktäschel, Acyr Locatelli, Laura Ruis 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.39923]
Prompts and probabilities in the linguistic assessment of language models: Evidence from semantic focus-sensitivity
语言模型语言能力评估中的提示与概率：语义焦点敏感性的证据
As language models (LMs) have improved, an increasingly common means of evaluating their linguistic competence is through explicit prompting (e.g., "Which sentence is more acceptable?"). Prior work indicates that such prompting underperforms compared to standard token-probability-based methods in smaller models, but improves in larger models. We test this in a new domain: semantic focus-sensitivity, responsible for contrasts like "Lucy only SWAM yesterday" vs. "Lucy only swam YESTERDAY". Testing models of various sizes using text-based focus markers like boldface and italics, we find universal above-chance performance for token probabilities. Prompting results mostly align with previous findings, but in some larger models they outperform probabilities by a wide margin. We propose that this is because LMs are not prone to produce out-of-the-blue focus marking, which muddies results from token probabilities in a way that does not affect prompting. We test this by replacing standard focus markers with randomly generated ones; token probabilities still beat chance but show significantly greater performance decrease compared to prompting. These results suggest that metalinguistic prompting, while non-trivial for LMs, can also be more robust when testing stylistically atypical data. We argue that this has important consequences for the assessment of LMs' language competence, even if LMs do not perform metalinguistic judgments through human-like introspection.
中文翻译
随着语言模型（LMs）性能的提升，通过显式提示（例如“哪个句子更可接受？”）来评估其语言能力变得越来越普遍。先前的研究表明，在小型模型中，这种提示方法的表现不如基于标记概率的标准方法，但在大型模型中表现有所改善。我们在一个新领域——语义焦点敏感性（负责区分如‘Lucy只在昨天游了泳’与‘Lucy只在昨天游了泳’这类对比）——中进行了测试。使用文本形式的焦点标记（如加粗和斜体）对不同规模的模型进行测试，发现标记概率在所有模型中均表现出显著高于随机水平的通用性能。提示方法的结果大多与以往发现一致，但在某些大型模型中，其表现远超概率方法。我们提出，这是因为语言模型不太可能无端生成焦点标记，而这种现象会干扰基于标记概率的结果，但不会影响提示方法。通过将标准焦点标记替换为随机生成的标记进行验证，结果显示标记概率仍优于随机水平，但性能下降幅度显著大于提示方法。这些结果表明，尽管对语言模型而言元语言提示并非易事，但在测试风格异常数据时，它可能更具鲁棒性。我们认为这对评估语言模型的语言能力具有重要意义，即使语言模型并未通过类人内省方式做出元语言判断。
👤 Robert Pasternak 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.39590]
Red-Teaming Large Language Models in Low-Resource African Languages: A Case Study of Yoruba and Nigerian Pidgin
在低资源非洲语言中对大语言模型进行红队测试：以约鲁巴语和尼日利亚皮钦语为例
As large language models (LLMs) become more widespread in Africa, their safety in low-resource languages remains largely untested since most red-teaming work focuses on English. This study evaluates six state-of-the-art LLMs using 400 explicit adversarial prompts in Yoruba and Nigerian Pidgin, categorised into four harm categories. The responses were scored by native-speaker annotators and reliability was assessed using Cohen's $κ$. While models that rejected up to 69% of English prompts rejected as few as 0% in Yoruba, this was not because they were safer, but because they failed to understand the prompts. Attack success rates increased for most models in the Nigerian languages, with GPT-4o Mini demonstrating the most significant cross-lingual safety discrepancy (+0.23 in Yoruba). When models did engage with adversarial prompts, many of the responses were harmful, with rates of harmful content reaching 0.63 in English and 0.50 in Nigerian Pidgin. The most difficult harm category to detect was cultural stereotypes and discrimination (ASR = 0.44 in Nigerian Pidgin). Comprehension in Yoruba was low across most models, and this directly suppressed apparent attack success without indicating actual safety. Gemini 2.0 Flash was the most consistent model across languages. Despite its explicit training in African languages, Aya 101 had the highest attack success rate in English (0.60) and one of the lowest Yoruba comprehension rates (0.28). These results demonstrate that English-centric safety alignment does not transfer to Nigerian languages, highlighting the urgent need for language-specific safety evaluations.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型（LLMs）在非洲日益普及，其在低资源语言中的安全性仍基本未经检验，因为大多数红队测试集中于英语。本研究使用 400 个明确的对抗性提示评估了六种最先进的 LLMs 在约鲁巴语和尼日利亚皮钦语中的表现，这些提示分为四类危害类别。响应由母语者标注员评分，并通过 Cohen's $κ$ 评估可靠性。尽管某些模型在英语中拒绝高达 69% 的提示，但在约鲁巴语中却拒绝率为 0%，但这并非因为它们更安全，而是因为它们未能理解提示。大多数模型在尼日利亚语言中的攻击成功率上升，其中 GPT-4o Mini 在约鲁巴语中表现出最显著的跨语言安全差异（+0.23）。当模型回应对抗性提示时，许多回答具有危害性，有害内容比例在英语中达到 0.63，在尼日利亚皮钦语中为 0.50。最难检测的危害类别是文化刻板印象与歧视（在尼日利亚皮钦语中 ASR 为 0.44）。大多数模型在约鲁巴语中的理解能力较低，这直接抑制了看似攻击成功的指标，但并不反映真实安全性。Gemini 2.0 Flash 是跨语言表现最一致的模型。尽管 Aya 101 经过非洲语言的显式训练，其在英语中的攻击成功率最高（0.60），而在约鲁巴语中的理解率最低（0.28）。这些结果表明，以英语为中心的安全对齐无法迁移到尼日利亚语言，突显了开展语言特定安全评估的紧迫性。
👤 Paula Chikaodinaka Anyanwu, Valdini Douglace Lemofouet, Blessing Ngozi Uzor, Ifeoluwa Dorcas Awotunde, Danielle Blanche Kapsa et al. 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.39562]
Raincheck: Deferred Multimodal Prefill for Efficient Multi-Turn MLLM Inference
Raincheck：用于高效多轮多模态大模型推理的延迟多模态预填充
Multi-turn conversations with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) reuse input context, but long multimodal inputs increase time to first token (TTFT). Query-aware hard pruning reduces prefill tokens but permanently excludes information that later questions may require. We propose Raincheck, a framework for deferred multimodal prefill that decouples initial token selection from later information availability. An existing pruning method selects tokens for initial prefill; Raincheck preserves and progressively prefills unselected tokens in source order using unused compute capacity during decode. Unfinished prefill continues after answer return during available scheduling gaps. Attention visibility control and affine state mapping enable context updates after generation begins in full-attention and hybrid recurrent models. Prefill-decode fusion reduces repeated weight reads. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B and Qwen3.5-9B with 30% initial multimodal-token retention. Across both models and four pruning methods, Raincheck recovers on average 77.7% and 60.4% of the quality lost to matched hard pruning on MultiVerse and ConvBench, respectively. On MultiVerse, the SGLang implementation reduces mean first-turn TTFT by up to 24.1% relative to full prefill, with less than 0.5% increase in mean time per output token (TPOT).
中文翻译
与多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进行多轮对话时会重复使用输入上下文，但长多模态输入会增加首个标记生成时间（TTFT）。查询感知的硬剪枝虽可减少预填充标记数，但会永久排除后续问题可能需要的信息。我们提出 Raincheck 框架，实现延迟多模态预填充，将初始标记选择与后期信息可用性解耦。现有剪枝方法选择初始预填充标记；Raincheck 保留未选标记，并在解码期间利用空闲计算资源按源顺序逐步预填充。未完成的预填充在回答返回后，利用调度间隙继续进行。注意力可见性控制和仿射状态映射使得在全注意力和混合递归模型中生成开始后仍能更新上下文。预填充-解码融合减少了重复权重读取。我们在 Qwen3-VL-8B 和 Qwen3.5-9B 上评估，初始多模态标记保留率为 30%。在两个模型和四种剪枝方法下，Raincheck 平均恢复了 MultiVerse 和 ConvBench 分别 77.7% 和 60.4% 因硬剪枝而损失的质量。在 MultiVerse 上，SGLang 实现将平均首轮 TTFT 最多降低 24.1%，同时平均输出令牌耗时（TPOT）增加不足 0.5%。

Integrating Heterogeneous Echocardiography AI Outputs in Language Space: Learnable Late Fusion with a Supervised LLM
在语言空间中整合异构超声心动图 AI 输出：基于监督大语言模型的可学习晚期融合
View- and target-specific echocardiography AI models capture useful but complementary information and may not achieve sufficient predictive performance in isolation. A learned ensemble is a natural way to combine these outputs, but heterogeneous models typically require each output to be converted into a binary decision, probability, confidence score, or fixed-length feature vector. We developed a supervised large language model (LLM)-based late-fusion module that directly aggregates view-specific predicted finding statements and predicted measurements in language space and learns a case-level fusion function. [2610.39433]
ARBITRA: Robust Document-Level ATT&CK Extraction via Multi-Expert Argumentation
ARBITRA：通过多专家论证实现鲁棒的文档级 ATT&CK 提取
Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) reports describe adversary behavior in natural language, but converting them into structured MITRE ATT&CK mappings remains difficult: existing methods decompose reports to sentence-level granularity for extraction, while modern LLMs can work with full reports in a single pass but with noticeable performance degradation. We present ARBITRA, which orchestrates multiple role-specialized LLM experts that propose candidate ATT&CK entities from the full report, which are then verified through a per-candidate quantitative bipolar argumentation framework (QBAF) grounded in document evidence and rationales. We additionally construct reasoning-specialized corpora for fine-tuning so experts can act as effective participants during this verification process. Across ATT&CK extraction benchmarks, ARBITRA reaches a stronger precision-oriented operating point than sentence-level pipelines, recent LLM-based extraction systems, and frontier models including GPT-5.5. Under adversarial perturbations simulating conflicting TTP statements, cross-report contamination, and semantic poisoning, ARBITRA limits mean precision degradation to roughly half that of frontier-model baselines for a more robust extraction.

[2610.39227]
XLaw-JP-27B: A Lightweight Open Model Specialized for Japanese Law
XLaw-JP-27B：专用于日本法律的轻量级开源模型
We present XLaw-JP-27B, an open-weight model specialized for Japanese law that runs on a single 48\,GB GPU under the Apache~2.0 licence. It adapts Qwen3.8-27B with hybrid retrieval over all current statutes and 31k case summaries, a light QLoRA fine-tune on its own verified answers to past bar-exam questions (self-distillation), a contract-specific LoRA adapter, and four-sample voting. We evaluate it on eight Japanese legal tasks---the multiple-choice section of the bar examination in 2024, 2025 and 2026, statement-level bar questions (JBE-QA), statute questions (LawQA-JP), the patent attorney exam, and contract review and drafting built from government model contracts---against open models of 12--33B under identical prompts and retrieval. XLaw-JP-27B ranks first on six tasks, ties on one and is third on one (mean rank 1.3 against 2.8--3.1 for the baselines). It is the only system that passes the bar exam in all three years (129, 120 and 130 of 175, meeting every subject minimum; no test-year material was used in training), while open Japanese 32--33B models fail at least one year. A controlled study of each component shows that retrieval, voting and self-distillation help, and that thinking mode, retrieval-grounded synthetic tuning, self-verification, per-statement decomposition and more self-distillation do not. The largest remaining gaps to frontier models are in contract review and patent law. The model is available as a free web service at https://x-law.jp/ with the weights available at https://huggingface.co/X-LawJapan/xlaw-jp-27b under Apache~2.0.
中文翻译
我们提出XLaw-JP-27B，一个可在单张48GB GPU上运行的开源日本法律专用模型，采用Apache 2.0许可证。该模型基于Qwen3.8-27B，结合对现行全部法规和3.1万条判例摘要的混合检索，使用自蒸馏方式在自身对过往司法考试题的正确答案上进行轻量QLoRA微调，配备合同特定LoRA适配器，并采用四样本投票机制。我们在八个日本法律任务上进行了评估——包括2024、2025和2026年司法考试的选择题部分、陈述级司法问答（JBE-QA）、法规问答（LawQA-JP）、专利代理人考试，以及基于政府标准合同构建的合同审查与起草任务——并与12–33B规模的开源模型在相同提示和检索条件下对比。XLaw-JP-27B在六项任务中排名第一，一项并列第一，一项第三（平均排名1.3，而基线为2.8–3.1）。它是唯一在三年司法考试中均通过的系统（分别取得129、120、130分，达到各科目最低要求；训练未使用任何当年考试材料），而其他32–33B的开源日本模型至少有一年未能通过。受控组件分析显示，检索、投票和自蒸馏有助于性能提升，而思维模式、基于检索的合成调优、自我验证、逐句分解及更多自蒸馏则无显著帮助。目前与前沿模型的主要差距在于合同审查和专利法领域。该模型可通过免费网页服务访问（https://x-law.jp/），权重可在Hugging Face（https://huggingface.co/X-LawJapan/xlaw-jp-27b）获取，遵循Apache 2.0协议。

[2610.39062]
HEARSAYBENCH: Can AI Navigate from Abstract Human Rights to Lived Lives?
HEARSAYBENCH：AI能否从抽象人权走向真实生活？
As large language models (LLMs) reach users worldwide, personal counseling has emerged as one of their most consequential applications, sometimes in life-critical situations. Yet broader access does not mean equal support. The text these models learn from disproportionately reflects people with the freedom to act, embedding an implicit assumption that users can leave, report, seek help, or appeal. Existing benchmarks largely inherit this assumption, overlooking users whose agency is constrained by structural conditions such as war, statelessness, and displacement. Meanwhile, models are increasingly shaped by normative frameworks such as Constitutional AI, often grounded in ideals like human rights, even as many users live where those rights offer little protection or recourse. We introduce HEARSAYBENCH, a human-authored dataset of 400 test cases with in-region human verification grounded in scenarios where structural barriers stand between a person and the exercise of their rights. Built from United Nations and other human rights reports, the scenarios span over 80 regions and cover social, environmental, and personal constraints. Drawing on Nobel laureate Amartya Sen's Capabilities Approach, HEARSAYBENCH tests whether models can distinguish what a person is formally entitled to do from what they are actually able to do. Across 14 proprietary and open-weight models, every model infers a user's situation more accurately than it advises on it. Stronger models narrow this gap, but their recommendations remain unsafe to follow. Models may understand lives shaped by constraint, yet that knowledge remains hearsay if it cannot translate into advice people can act on. Those people are not alignment's long tail; they are among the users for whom good guidance matters most. HEARSAYBENCH makes this failure measurable, a step toward systems that serve the world as it is rather than as assumed.
中文翻译
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在全球范围内普及，个人咨询已成为其最具影响力的应用之一，有时甚至涉及生死攸关的情境。然而，广泛接入并不等于平等支持。这些模型所学习的文本内容 disproportionately 反映了拥有行动自由的人群，隐含假设用户可以离开、举报、求助或申诉。现有基准大多继承这一假设，忽视了那些因战争、无国籍状态或流离失所等结构性条件而无法行使自主权的用户。与此同时，模型日益受到如宪法AI等规范性框架的影响，尽管这些框架根植于人权理想，但许多用户所处的现实环境中，这些权利几乎无法提供保护或救济途径。为此，我们提出HEARSAYBENCH，一个由人类撰写的400个测试案例数据集，其内容经区域内部人类验证，聚焦于结构性障碍阻碍个体行使权利的真实情境。数据源自联合国及其他人权报告，涵盖超过80个地区，涵盖社会、环境和个人约束。基于诺贝尔奖得主阿马蒂亚·森的能力方法论，HEARSAYBENCH测试模型能否区分一个人在形式上可做之事与其实际能做之事。在14个专有与开源模型中，所有模型对用户处境的理解都优于其给出的建议。更强的模型缩小了这一差距，但其建议仍不安全可执行。模型或许理解受限人生，但若无法转化为人们可行动的建议，则这种理解只是“道听途说”。这些用户并非对齐研究的长尾群体；恰恰是那些最需要优质指导的人。HEARSAYBENCH使这一失败可测量，是迈向服务于真实世界而非假设世界的系统的重要一步。
👤 Ava Iranmanesh, Sobhan Lotfi, Ali Iranmanesh, Liwei Jiang 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38580]
Structural Pattern Mining in Inka Khipus: A KFG Re-Analysis
印加结绳文字中的结构模式挖掘：基于KFG的重新分析
This paper reports a methodological pivot from the Open Khipu Repository to the Khipu Field Guide (KFG) database, prompted by stalled progress on the OKR, and a full re-analysis of the resulting 650-khipu sample. Three findings recur across every analysis attempted. First, khipu size confounds nearly every derived structural count, explaining 42-70% of its variance; all subsequent models control for it. Second, the two-letter name prefix (AS, UR, HP, QU, JC, MM, KH) identifies the original cataloger, not a site or period, which turns documentation completeness into a second confound that must be modeled explicitly. Controlling for both, khipus cataloged by Hugo Pereyra's Proyecto Quipu (HP) show significantly fewer Ascher sum-relation types than Gary Urton's (UR) khipus (p = 0.044), while the Ascher-cataloged (AS) khipus are too collinear with their own near-total lack of side/direction documentation to assess (VIF = 5.0). Third, generic structural clustering (UMAP+HDBSCAN) does not separate the pooled 650-khipu sample from two independent null models (permutation and Gaussian), corroborating an independent conclusion already reached by the KFG itself; the same test restricted to a single cataloger (UR) proved not reproducible across runtime environments and is reported here as a methodological caveat rather than evidence of structure. A targeted replication of the KFG's own S/Z-knot spatial-block heuristic for calendar/astronomical khipus recovers 8 of 10 published candidates and proposes 34 additional ones under the same criterion.
中文翻译
本文报告了从开放结绳数据库（OKR）向结绳田野指南（KFG）数据库的方法论转变，这一转变由OKR进展停滞所引发，并对由此产生的650条结绳样本进行了全面重新分析。所有尝试的分析均重复出现三个发现：第一，结绳大小几乎影响了所有派生的结构计数，解释了其方差的42%-70%；所有后续模型均对此进行控制。第二，两位字母名称前缀（AS, UR, HP, QU, JC, MM, KH）标识的是原始编目者，而非地点或时期，这使得文档完整性成为第二个必须显式建模的混淆因素。在控制这两个因素后，Hugo Pereyra的Quipu项目（HP）编目的结绳显示出显著少于Gary Urton（UR）编目的结绳的Ascher求和关系类型（p = 0.044），而Ascher编目的（AS）结绳与其自身近乎完全缺乏侧向/方向记录高度共线，无法评估（VIF = 5.0）。第三，通用结构聚类（UMAP+HDBSCAN）未能将合并的650条结绳样本与两个独立的零模型（置换和高斯）区分开来，这与KFG本身已得出的独立结论一致；同一测试在单一编目者（UR）上受限时，无法在不同运行环境中复现，因此作为方法学警示报告，而非结构存在的证据。针对KFG自身S/Z结点空间块启发式方法在日历/天文结绳中的针对性复制，成功恢复了10个已发表候选中的8个，并在同一标准下提出了34个额外候选。

[2610.38509]
Leaving the Cave by Breaking the Symmetry: Identifiable Structure Can Enable Cross-Lingual Generalization
通过打破对称性走出洞穴：可识别的结构可促进跨语言泛化
Why do multilingual language models generalize across languages even without shared vocabulary? The Platonic Representation Hypothesis posits that models trained on different data, objectives, and modalities converge toward a shared statistical model of reality. Extending this intuition to representations inside a single multilingual model, we propose the Relational Identifiability Hypothesis: relations in a shared world can promote entity alignment by ruling out alternative cross-lingual correspondences, making corresponding entities more likely to become directly readable without an additional transformation. We test this hypothesis in a controlled bilingual Transformer whose two token-disjoint languages describe the same modular-arithmetic world. We find that the languages can develop a shared answer subspace without aligning corresponding numbers. Cross-lingual alignment emerges selectively when the available relations constrain number correspondences: subtraction makes zero task-level identifiable, inducing selective alignment, while an order-sensitive operation produces broad alignment across numbers. These patterns predict success in zero-shot cross-lingual transfer. Together, our results show that relational structure in a shared world can promote entity alignment and enable transfer.
中文翻译
为什么多语言语言模型即使没有共享词汇也能在不同语言间实现泛化？柏拉图表征假说认为，经过不同数据、目标和模态训练的模型会收敛到一个关于现实的共享统计模型。我们将这一直觉扩展到单个多语言模型内部的表征，提出了关系可识别性假说：共享世界中的关系可以通过排除其他可能的跨语言对应关系，促进实体对齐，使对应实体更有可能直接被读取而无需额外转换。我们在一个受控的双语Transformer中测试了这一假说，该模型的两种互不重叠的语言描述同一个模数运算世界。我们发现，这些语言可以在不对齐对应数字的情况下发展出共享的答案子空间。当可用关系限制了数字对应关系时，跨语言对齐才会选择性地出现：减法使零在任务层面具有可识别性，从而引发选择性对齐；而对顺序敏感的操作则导致数字间的广泛对齐。这些模式预测了零样本跨语言迁移的成功。综上所述，我们的结果表明，共享世界中的关系结构可以促进实体对齐并实现知识迁移。
👤 Frederick Riemenschneider, Anette Frank 📅 2026-10-28 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38336]
Statutory Article Retrieval from Arabic Judicial Narratives: Family Law and Law of Evidence in Saudi Arabia
从阿拉伯语司法叙述中检索法律条文：沙特阿拉伯的家庭法与证据法
Reliable legal information retrieval must ground legal reasoning in authoritative statutory sources. In Arabic judicial settings, long factual narratives must be mapped to concise articles that express abstract legal rules, creating a narrative-to-legal-rule abstraction gap. Using a benchmark of 200 synthetic Saudi Arabic judicial cases and 381 articles from the Family Law and the Law of Evidence, we compare word-level and char-4 BM25, zero-shot dense encoders, per-law retrieval, dense-sparse reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), and a proposed Decompose-and-Refine (DaR) pipeline. In DaR, a large language model splits each narrative into disputed legal issues and generates, for each issue, three retrieval channels: a statute-style paraphrase, legal keywords, and a candidate taxonomy chapter. On the whole corpus, the instructed Qwen3-Embedding-8B dense encoder reaches an MRR@10 of 0.849, the best result of any non-decomposed system. Under per-law scoring, this advantage reflects Family Law only: restricted to Evidence articles, the same encoder drops to an MRR@10 of 0.087, and fusion with BM25 raises it only to 0.207. DaR changes this picture, raising Evidence MRR@10 to 0.696 and Family MRR@10 to 0.864. Without decomposition, the two laws favor different strategies; under DaR, the same retriever achieves the best MRR@10 on both.

可靠的法律信息检索必须以权威的法定来源为基础进行法律推理。在阿拉伯语司法环境中，长篇事实叙述必须映射到表达抽象法律规则的简明条文，从而产生叙述到法律规则之间的抽象鸿沟。我们使用包含200个合成沙特阿拉伯司法案例和来自家庭法及证据法的381个条文的基准数据集，比较了词级和char-4 BM25、零样本密集编码器、按法律检索、密集-稀疏互惠排序融合（RRF）以及一种提出的分解与优化（DaR）流程。在DaR中，大语言模型将每个叙述拆分为争议的法律问题，并为每个问题生成三个检索通道：条文风格的改写、法律关键词和候选分类章节。在整个语料库上，经过指令微调的Qwen3-Embedding-8B密集编码器达到MRR@10为0.849，是任何非分解系统中的最佳结果。在按法律评分下，这一优势仅体现在家庭法中：限制在证据法条文时，同一编码器下降至MRR@10为0.087，即使与BM25融合也仅提升至0.207。DaR改变了这一局面，将证据法的MRR@10提升至0.696，家庭法提升至0.864。在无分解的情况下，两种法律偏好不同策略；而在DaR框架下，同一检索器在两者上均取得最佳MRR@10。

[2610.38312]
Robust LLM-Generated Text Detection through Matched Conditional-Response Probing
通过匹配条件响应探测实现鲁棒的LLM生成文本检测
Reliable detection of large language model (LLM)-generated text must remain effective under meaning-preserving text revisions, such as rewriting, polishing, and expansion. In practice, LLM outputs are often rewritten, polished, expanded, translated, or lightly edited before publication, weakening the lexical, syntactic, and local discourse cues used by many existing detectors. We introduce \textbf{M}atched \textbf{C}onditional-\textbf{R}esponse \textbf{P}robing (\textbf{MCRP}), a frozen-LLM probing framework that measures how a candidate text responds when a semantically matched condition is introduced, removed, or replaced. For each candidate, the frozen probe LLM first generates a short semantic condition and then compares token-level losses and prompt--target attention measurements under matched, null, and control conditions. It aggregates bidirectional conditioned alignment, counterfactual sensitivity, and internal attention coupling into a probe representation, on which it trains a lightweight supervised random-forest classifier while keeping the probe LLM frozen. Across standard detection, robustness to meaning-preserving text revisions, multilingual, and multidomain evaluations, MCRP achieves consistently strong performance. Under rewriting, polishing, and expansion, it reaches 93.97\% F1 and 96.24\% ROC-AUC, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 19.50 and 25.77 percentage points, respectively. Component ablations show that the response feature groups make complementary contributions to predictive performance. In the condition-control study, matched conditions consistently outperform random, mismatched, and corrupted alternatives, demonstrating a measurable incremental benefit from semantic matching.
中文翻译
可靠的大型语言模型（LLM）生成文本检测必须在保持语义不变的文本修订（如重写、润色、扩展）下依然有效。实际上，LLM输出常在发布前被重写、润色、扩展、翻译或轻微编辑，削弱了现有检测器所依赖的词汇、句法和局部话语线索。我们提出 extbf{M}atched extbf{C}onditional- extbf{R}esponse extbf{P}robing ( extbf{MCRP})，一种冻结LLM探测框架，通过引入、移除或替换语义匹配的条件，测量候选文本的响应变化。对于每个候选文本，冻结探测LLM首先生成一个简短的语义条件，然后在匹配、空值和控制条件下比较逐标记损失和提示-目标注意力度量。它将双向条件对齐、反事实敏感性和内部注意力耦合聚合为探测表示，并在此基础上训练轻量级监督随机森林分类器，同时保持探测LLM冻结。在标准检测、对语义保持修订的鲁棒性、多语言和多领域评估中，MCRP表现出一致强性能。在重写、润色和扩展场景下，其F1达到93.97%，ROC-AUC达96.24%，分别优于最强基线19.50和25.77个百分点。组件消融实验表明，各响应特征组对预测性能有互补贡献。在条件-控制研究中，匹配条件始终优于随机、不匹配和损坏的替代方案，证明语义匹配带来可衡量的增量收益。

Cross Lingual Abstractive Summarization for Marathi
马拉地语的跨语言抽象摘要生成
Text summarization is an important task for advancing natural language processing (NLP) research and applications in Indic languages. Despite progress in extractive and abstractive summarization, both monolingual summarization (MS) and cross-lingual summarization (CLS) remain relatively underexplored for these languages. Recent advances in Transformer architectures, pretrained language models (PLMs), and dataset availability offer new opportunities to address this gap. In this work, we construct cross-lingual training data from the monolingual Marathi MahaSum corpus and combine it with the smaller cross-lingual PMIndiaSum dataset to fine-tune IndicTrans2, an Indic neural machine translation model. We also discuss the applicability of our approach to the English-to-Marathi direction. Our experiments demonstrate that leveraging a pretrained translation model yields substantially better summarization performance than the IndicBART and mBART-50 based baselines reported in previous work on cross-lingual summarization.

[2610.38067]
From Error Prediction to Causal Influence: Auditing SAE Features in Multiple-Choice QA
从错误预测到因果影响：多选问答中SAE特征的审计
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can predict internal features associated with model errors, but association alone does not establish causal influence. We study this distinction in multiple-choice question answering, where answer preference can be measured by the logit margin between the correct answer and the competing choices. Across eight datasets, we analyze Gemma~3 1B and 4B with Gemma Scope SAEs. We propose a pipeline to filter out the features that have high correlation with format and content artifacts, such as prompt and answer permutation, and evaluate the remaining ones with causal feature interventions. Our results show that SAE activations contain predictive signals about wrongness and uncertainty across models and datasets. After filtering artifact-contaminated features, selected features for causal intervention at question and option spans produce larger absolute margin shifts and a higher wrong-to-correct rate than randomly selected features, indicating greater causal leverage. However, answer flips are rare, and we do not observe stable effect directions across meaning-preserving changes in prompt format and answer order. An adapted HalluSAE baseline produces larger final-token margin changes, but relies on substantially contaminated features and does not significantly improve correction rates. Therefore, the identified features reflect context-dependent answer competition rather than the mechanism of incorrect answer errors.
中文翻译
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）能够预测与模型错误相关的内部特征，但相关性本身并不能确立因果影响。我们在多选问答任务中研究这一区别，其中答案偏好可通过正确答案与竞争选项之间的logit差值来衡量。在八个数据集上，我们分析了Gemma~3 1B和4B模型使用Gemma Scope SAEs的结果。我们提出一个流程，过滤掉与格式和内容伪影（如提示和答案排列）高度相关的特征，并通过因果特征干预评估剩余特征。结果表明，SAE激活在不同模型和数据集中包含关于错误性和不确定性的预测信号。在过滤掉受伪影污染的特征后，针对问题和选项片段选择的特征进行因果干预，其绝对logit差值变化更大，且错误转为正确率高于随机选择的特征，表明具有更强的因果影响力。然而，答案翻转现象罕见，且在保持语义不变的提示格式和答案顺序变化中未观察到稳定的效应方向。经过调整的HalluSAE基线产生更大的最终token logit差值变化，但依赖于严重污染的特征，且并未显著提升纠正率。因此，所识别的特征反映的是上下文依赖的答案竞争，而非错误答案生成机制。
👤 Aria Nourbakhsh, Adelaide Danilov, Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur, Salima Lamsiyah 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38041]
CARF: Context-Aware Reasoning Supervision for Scientific Evidence Screening
CARF：面向科学证据筛选的上下文感知推理监督
Systematic reviews synthesize biomedical evidence to inform clinical and public-health decision-making, but require domain experts to manually screen thousands of studies against detailed eligibility criteria. We investigate whether language models can assist with this bottleneck by learning from expert feedback on title and abstract screening decisions and model-generated reasoning. We introduce the Context-Aware Reasoning Framework (CARF), a human-in-the-loop framework that uses expert feedback on model-generated reasoning to adapt language models to context-dependent screening tasks. We evaluate CARF on systematic reviews of verbal autopsy and cause-of-death ascertainment methods used in global public health mortality surveillance. CARF shows competitive screening performance compared to evaluated models while retaining high sensitivity, and is more inclined to retain ambiguous, context-dependent studies for human review. Together, these findings suggest that expert-corrected reasoning is a promising supervision signal for context-dependent scientific screening, while retaining a role for human oversight.

Beyond Aggregate WER: In-domain Adaptation, Learner Speech, Code-Switching, and Speaker Attribution in Online Spanish Learning Classrooms
超越平均WER：在线西班牙语学习课堂中的领域适应、学习者语音、代码转换与说话人归属
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) increasingly supplies transcripts for educational NLP, but aggregate word error rate (WER) can conceal errors concentrated in learner speech, multilingual turns, and speaker attribution. We compare platform-integrated, open-weight, adapted, and commercial ASR configurations on held-out sessions of online L2 Spanish interaction between L1 English learners and their instructors and tutors. Because English is the learner's shared first language, the interactions contain Spanish-only, English-only, and code-switched turns. Evaluation combines overall WER and character error rate (CER), WER disaggregated by speaker role and language context, and diarization error rate (DER). We also adapt Whisper large-v3 and MOSS-Transcribe-Diarize with LoRA using human-corrected in-domain classroom data. MOSS adaptation produced consistent improvements across speaker roles and language contexts, whereas Whisper showed smaller gains. Domain match proved more important than data volume: Larger out-of-domain bilingual and learner corpora did not outperform the smaller classroom corpus, where learner speech and code-switching are embedded in instructor--learner interaction. The open-weight MOSS adapter was competitive with commercial Muse Voice Transcribe on WER and CER and achieved lower system-provided DER on the recordings eligible for each system. The findings demonstrate the value of condition-aware evaluation and interactionally matched data for bilingual educational ASR.

Large Language Models for Accurate Medical Chart Abstraction: Enabling Scalable and Secure Artificial Intelligence Deployment in Stroke
大语言模型在精准医疗图表抽象中的应用：推动卒中领域可扩展且安全的人工智能部署
Medical chart abstraction transforms unstructured clinical narratives into structured variables for research and quality monitoring. We evaluated a prompting-based large language model (LLM) framework for extracting eight clinical variables from 2,416 neurointerventional acute stroke intervention reports across three hospitals. Twenty-two instruction-tuned open-source LLMs were evaluated across architectures, model sizes, and biomedical adaptations using Quick Response and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting. Non-expert staff annotations served as the cross-model reference standard. Evaluation was restricted to report-variable pairs with a valid LLaMA3.3-70B CoT prediction and a corresponding reference label. Locally deployed LLMs can support scalable clinical information extraction and retrospective stroke registry curation, with expert oversight for ambiguous cases.

We propose a multimodal transformer framework for efficiently modeling long1 financial documents that combine structured tabular data with noisy, unstructured2 textual disclosures. The framework represents financial tables as semantically3 enriched token sequences and integrates them with long-form text through a cross-4 attention mechanism that explicitly captures cross-modal dependencies. In par-5 ticular, we augment each numerical variable with a semantic embedding derived6 from its textual definition, enabling the model to encode both numerical magnitude7 and economic meaning. This design yields a more expressive and parameter-8 efficient representation of tabular data compared to value-only embeddings. Using9 a large-scale dataset of financial statements and MD&A disclosures, we show that10 structured numerical features remain the dominant source of predictive signal;11 however, textual information provides consistent incremental gains when incor-12 porated via cross-attention rather than simple concatenation. Our results further13 demonstrate that noisy textual disclosures are most useful when conditioned on a14 stable, semantically structured tabular representation. In particular, using frozen15 variable-level tabular representations as cross-attention queries enables the model16 to weight MD&A chunks differently for each financial variable and observation.17 These findings suggest that cross-attention provides an efficient mechanism for18 table-conditioned aggregation of long-form narrative evidence.

[2610.37692]
Bouba Beats Kiki, and Other Regularities in the Subjective Preferences of LLMs
Bouba胜过Kiki，以及LLM主观偏好中的其他规律
Users engage with large language models on tasks that are purely objective ("When was Netflix founded?"), purely subjective ("What should I name my child?") or anywhere in-between. We have rich tools for evaluating model accuracy with respect to objective criteria. However, when it comes to subjective aspects of a task, we do not have similarly developed methodology at hand. In this paper, we take some early steps towards framing the question of subjective evaluation. Rather than measure how often a model is right or wrong on a question, we instead ask, how do models make choices among multiple viable responses without a clear winner? To study this question, we restrict our focus to short-form content, and consider comparison tasks among three such content types: numbers, baby names, and fictional cities. We identify 29 aspects of these short-form examples covering many areas of syntax and semantics (length, alphabetic order, popularity, vowel mix, spoken sound, etc.). We show first that models tend to have strong and reproducible preferences for or against some of these aspects. Second, we show that variations in how the model is asked to express a preference may cause some of these aspects to activate or deactivate in the model's selection. Third, we show that overall preference distributions among pairs may vary radically based on seemingly-unimportant variations in the nature of the question posed to the model, raising new questions about manipulation. And finally, we show that, if models are used both to create lists of alternatives and to rank them, there are unexpected interactions between the two stages that may also drive the models' stated preferences.

One Confidence Threshold, Unequal Coverage: Language Gaps in Typed Decision Interfaces and How Translating Inputs Reduces Them

Deployed classifiers often act on a prediction only when its confidence clears a threshold and pass the rest to a person. We ask whether one threshold serves all languages equally on typed decision interfaces, APIs that return a probability for each option of a fixed multiple-choice question. In a confirmatory study with a written analysis plan, three vendors' interfaces classified 1,000 MASSIVE items, each in 51 languages, with a threshold of 0.9. Even when a request is answered correctly in both English and another language, the English version clears the threshold more often, by 8.6-11.7 percentage points on average. Observed coverage, the share of requests handled automatically, is lower than English's in every other language, while the requests the gate handles are rarely wrong. In exploratory analyses, English's coverage stays higher when each language gets its own threshold fitted to the same 5% error target. Translating inputs into English nearly closes the gap at 0.9 but leaves English ahead at that error target. Requests written natively in African languages show a gap in the same direction when English is their human translation. We recommend reporting per-language coverage at the operating threshold.

[2610.37682]
Rank-Aware Differentially Private Release of Listwise Preferences for LLM Alignment
面向大语言模型对齐的排名感知差分隐私列表偏好发布
Listwise preference feedback offers richer supervision for large language model (LLMs) alignment than pairwise comparisons, but a full ranking also reveals more sensitive user preferences. Existing privacy-preserving alignment methods focus mainly on pairwise feedback, while listwise alignment under local differential privacy (LDP) remains unexplored. Extending LDP to this listwise domain raises a critical challenge: the high sensitivity of the full ranking space demands excessive noise, neutralizing the benefits of listwise supervision. To address this challenge, we analyze the interaction between supervision granularity and privacy noise in downstream training, and introduce a rank-aware exponential mechanism that privatizes listwise preference data into a low-sensitivity granularity suitable for downstream alignment. The mechanism leverages ranking information to sample a fixed-size binary partition, concentrating the noise near borderline items instead of perturbing all items uniformly as randomized response (RR) does. Empirical evaluations on downstream LLM alignment tasks show that our mechanism consistently outperforms existing LDP baselines at matched privacy budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/tdsai-lab/RAEM.

Scoring Explanations Without Reading Them: Structural Shortcuts in Automated-Interpretability Evaluation Data
无需阅读解释即可评分：自动化可解释性评估数据中的结构捷径
Automated interpretability scores an explanation of a sparse-autoencoder (SAE) feature by asking a model to predict, token by token, where the feature fires on evaluation examples or to judge whether marked tokens fit it. We find that the evaluation data standard pipelines build can be scored without reading the explanation. On Neuronpedia's stored fuzzing runs for 500 random GPT-2 small features, 91.3% of activating examples are cropped with the peak on the centre token, and a rule that says "yes" when the centre token is marked reaches balanced accuracy 0.979, against 0.838 for the stored LLM scorers (recomputed from the stored per-item verdicts, on marks rebuilt from the pipeline's code); a rule reading only the mark count reaches 0.969. The cue changes form across pipelines: current delphi always marks the same slot in its fuzzing negatives, and full-context runs mark negatives with more tokens. OpenAI's simulation benchmark lists each neuron's top-activating sequences first; a rule that predicts activity on the first five scores 0.227 in pooled correlation against 0.160 for GPT-4's published simulator (post hoc). We give closed forms for what such rules can score; show that one scorer's simulation scores are higher on stored snippets than on natural windows but not shown to be reordered; and build windows that remove the position cue, derive but do not build negatives that remove the mark cues, and separate the sequence cue by decomposition. On natural-text windows cut at random offsets, the best fixed-offset prior shared by all features scores 0.015. On them, that scorer, a typed decision model (Jev), reaches a pooled correlation of 0.331 over 49 features, against 0.013 with shuffled explanations, and an open 8B simulator with the original read-out reads the explanation there (0.194 against 0.019). Scores should be reported beside the best explanation-free rule on the same items.

[2610.37671]
The FlightFinder Occurrence Corpus: harmonising 128 aviation-safety source codes into one deduplicated cross-national accident dataset
FlightFinder事故语料库：将128个航空安全源代码统一为去重的跨国事故数据集
National aviation-investigation databases are deep but territorial; global compilations such as the Aviation Safety Network and the Bureau of Aircraft Accidents Archives are hand-curated, apply their own inclusion thresholds, and publish no per-record lineage. The FlightFinder Occurrence Corpus harmonizes 279,485 occurrences and 131,882 attached narratives from 128 source codes -- national accident-investigation authorities, occurrence-reporting systems and four global compilations. Records are mapped into one schema; each normalized record reduces to exactly one key from an ordered ladder -- event signature, registration and date, rounded position and date, or source and native identifier -- so a missing field never creates a link, and the key is the occurrence identifier. Records the ladder cannot reach are scored in a blocked pass into an explicit merge table and applied only within an automatically approved score band or after a person confirms them. Field conflicts follow a documented source ranking; one provenance row per contributing source -- its native identifier and URL -- is retained, with attribution and licence terms per source code. Linkage error is measured on five adjudicated samples totalling 200 pairs. A recorded uniform random draw of 60 from the 3,441 merges the automatic band applies as it ships found no error; the exact two-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson upper bound on its false-merge rate is 6.0%, or 205 pairs. The measured error covers the scored band only; the ladder's deterministic joins are not adjudicated. The corpus has no exposure denominators and inherits the under-reporting of its sources. It is offered as a base for cross-national safety statistics, source-disagreement studies and narrative text mining; a frozen, DOI-referenced snapshot accompanies the live API.

Voice-cloning TTS models synthesize an unseen speaker's voice from brief speech prompts, but it remains unclear which acoustic properties they represent and use. [2610.40328]
Factorized Representations of Concurrent Temporal Variables in Pretrained Language Models
预训练语言模型中并发时间变量的因子化表示
Language models often need to maintain multiple contextual variables that evolve independently within a document. Prior work has identified structured representations of individual temporal or contextual quantities, but their joint organization remains less understood. We study this question using controlled documents with independently phased language-switch and topic-change timers. Across ten pretrained checkpoints, both timers are linearly decodable from the same hidden states. Removing a timer-associated centroid plane substantially reduces access to its associated variable while having little effect on the concurrent timer, although the planes are nonorthogonal and contain cross-timer information. Separate timer contributions also explain most reproducible variation among joint-state centroids. These signatures persist from 3 to 51 timer values and across Qwen3 checkpoints from 0.6B to 14B. Schedule position affects continuation probabilities, but the tested centroid displacement produces no consistent timer-specific output effect. Together, these results support approximate factorization based on selective accessibility and approximately additive joint-state geometry in overlapping subspaces.

[2610.40290]
Leveraging Recourse and Size Bounds for OnlineKnapsack with Removable Items
利用可撤销性与大小约束提升可移除物品的在线背包问题性能
In the online unweighted knapsack problem, items arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected immediately for a bounded-capacity knapsack, aiming to maximize the total size of accepted items. When decisions are irrevocable, no algorithm achieves a non-trivial competitive ratio. An essential relaxation that circumvents this impossibility is to allow previously accepted items to be removed from the knapsack (a.k.a. removal). In this work, we investigate possible further improvements on the competitive ratio with removal by bringing together two other previously proposed model extensions: knowledge of an upper bound on the item sizes (a.k.a. bound awareness) and ability to reinsert previously rejected items(a.k.a.recourse). Our contributions are twofold. First, we establish close upper and lower bounds on the competitive ratio under bound awareness, removal and recourse. We show that significant improvements on the best known competitive ratio are possible when the upper bound on the item sizes is not too restrictive and only few uses of recourse are available. Second, we introduce the notion of adaptive online algorithms which do not require to know any upper bound on the item sizes but still achieve performance guarantees comparable to the ones obtained under the bound awareness model.
中文翻译
在在线无权背包问题中，物品按顺序到达，必须立即决定接受或拒绝，目标是最大化所接受物品的总大小。当决策不可逆时，没有任何算法能实现非平凡的竞争比。一个关键的放松条件是允许将之前接受的物品从背包中移除（即“可撤销性”）。本文研究在引入可撤销性基础上，结合另外两种已有模型扩展——对物品大小的上限有知识（即“边界感知”）和能够重新插入之前拒绝的物品（即“可挽回性”），是否能进一步提升竞争比。我们的贡献有两个方面：首先，我们在边界感知、可撤销性和可挽回性条件下建立了接近的上下界，表明当物品大小上限不过于严格且可挽回性使用次数较少时，可实现显著优于现有最佳竞争比的改进；其次，我们提出了自适应在线算法的概念，该算法无需预先知道物品大小的任何上限，但仍能达到与边界感知模型相当的性能保证。

[2610.39735]
From the Transformer to a Formal Semantics of Coordinated Transformations
从Transformer到协调变换的形式语义
We develop a formal semantics for a general class of coordinated systems of parameterized transformations, taking the Transformer as a paradigmatic starting point but abstracting from its particular mathematical instantiation. Representational vectors receive both extensional reference and ordered intensional interpretations, while parameter structures acquire relative, second-level interpretations that determine how argument properties are preserved, replaced, or combined. This yields the notion of a transforming concept: a parameterized transformation whose numerical action is coordinated with a corresponding property-level map. We then distinguish the common algebraic-semantic core of these systems from their architecture-specific functional organization, allowing different mathematical instantiations and mechanical realizations of the same abstract system type. The framework is applied to attention heads and residual connections, providing semantic explanations that depend only on the shared algebraic-semantic core and their local functional organization and therefore generalize beyond the standard Transformer. These analyses also reveal the modularity of the framework: complex architectures can be decomposed into simpler semantically coordinated subsystems whose organization may recur across different abstract system types.
中文翻译
我们为一类参数化变换的协调系统发展了形式语义，以Transformer为典型起点，但抽象出其特定的数学实现。表示向量同时获得外延指称和有序内涵解释，而参数结构则获得相对的、第二层的解释，决定参数属性如何被保留、替换或组合。这引出了变换概念的概念：其数值操作与相应属性级映射相协调的参数化变换。随后，我们将这些系统的共同代数-语义核心与其架构特定的功能组织区分开来，允许同一抽象系统类型的多种数学实现和机械实现。该框架应用于注意力头和残差连接，提供了仅依赖共享代数-语义核心及其局部功能组织的语义解释，因此可推广至标准Transformer之外。这些分析还揭示了该框架的模块性：复杂架构可分解为更简单的语义协调子系统，其组织方式可在不同抽象系统类型间重复出现。

Event-SAMA: Language-Grounded Video Object Segmentation on Neuromorphic Event Streams
Event-SAMA：基于神经形态事件流的语言引导视频对象分割
Event cameras offer microsecond temporal resolution, high dynamic range and no motion blur, but referring video object segmentation (RVOS) lacks event-stream datasets and models. We introduce Event-SAMA-239K, a synthetic-event conversion of the SAMA-239K grounded video-chat corpus and all additional SAMA training data: 30,650 video clips and 392,162 images. Without retraining, Event-SAMA works on real event-camera recordings when the accumulation window matches their event density. [2610.39519]
Lost in the Latent Space: How Code Embeddings Represent Functional Intent and Implementation Fidelity
迷失在潜在空间中：代码嵌入如何表征功能意图与实现保真度
Code embedding models have become a key building block for many software engineering applications, yet their internal representations remain poorly understood. Existing evaluations tend to focus on downstream task performance, offering limited insight into whether a single embedding can support both functional abstraction and implementation-sensitive analysis. To address this gap, we present a large-scale empirical study of 14 state-of-the-art code embedding models. We evaluate their representational behavior through three complementary analyses: supervised probing for functional abstraction and implementation fidelity, zero-shot retrieval experiments to assess geometric similarity, and controlled perturbation experiments that measure embedding responses to semantics-preserving code transformations. The results reveal a consistent abstraction-fidelity tension: models that better preserve stylistic and structural signals tend to do so at the expense of semantic generalization, while models with similar retrieval performance can nevertheless respond very differently to the same implementation changes. Overall, our findings suggest that a single universal code representation remains out of reach, motivating more representation-aware evaluation, selection, and deployment of code embedding models in software engineering.

[2610.39339]
Does the Citation Matter? Quality, Grounding, and Physician-Rubric Accuracy of Google's AI Overviews for Medical Information
引用重要吗？Google AI摘要在医疗信息中的质量、依据性与医生评分准确性
Google's AI Overviews (AIOs) are the first result users encounter when searching the web, shifting the work of selecting and evaluating sources from the user to Gemini. This may be especially consequential for health information, given that Google's traditional algorithm was designed to prioritize quality sources, and inaccurate information carries health risks. We evaluated 3,611 benchmark health questions, comparing each AIO against that question's top five traditional results on source selection and quality. We also evaluated the AIOs' statement grounding in their cited sources and how accurate AIOs were against physician-written rubrics. Overviews appeared for 96.4% of questions but drew on substantially different evidence than traditional search: only 44.4% of the top five results were cited by the corresponding AIO, which instead overrepresented commercial health publishers (e.g., WebMD, Healthline) at the expense of academic and government sources (e.g., Cleveland Clinic, National Health Service). AIO-cited pages scored lower than traditional results on four of six well-established credibility criteria. The grounding of AIO statements to their sources was poor: after correcting for classifier error, an estimated 56.7% of cited statements were supported by their cited page, and a further 20.8% of all statements were factual claims with no citation at all. When AIOs were graded against physicians' rubrics, they earned 38.2% of all available points (e.g., accuracy 54.2%, completeness 21.6%), and among the subset of AIOs whose physician rubric anticipated a specific inaccurate or potentially harmful claim, 22.2% of AIOs contained the harmful content. Notably, the accuracy of AIOs was unrelated to the grounding of their statements. We recommend AIO literacy interventions for users and holding AIOs at least to Google's own health-ranking standards.

Reading-Level Verification of Kanji Words in Japanese Financial Speech Synthesis
日语金融语音合成中汉字读音的层级验证
Few-shot voice cloning makes it cheap to produce daily Japanese financial commentary with several synthetic speakers, but kanji words with more than one reading, such as the word for "market" (shijo vs. ichiba) or "overall trend" (taisei vs. oze), are still misread. A common quality gate transcribes each synthesized sentence with automatic speech recognition (ASR), converts both the input and the transcript to kana, and regenerates on mismatch. We show that this gate is unreliable in both directions: when the audio contains a misreading, the ASR language model frequently writes the expected kanji back, hiding the error, and when the audio is correct, ordinary recognition errors trigger needless regeneration. We propose a reading-level check that scores the expected reading and its confusable alternatives with a connectionist temporal classification (CTC) hiragana recognizer and flags a word when an alternative explains the audio better. On 540 utterances from three voice-cloned production voices covering 30 financial terms, with misreadings injected under controlled conditions, the transcript-based gate detected 78.9% of misreadings at a 44.4% false-alarm rate, whereas the proposed check detected 91.7% at 5.0% (AUC 0.990). On natural kanji input, it caught a front-end error that the transcript-based gate missed for one voice, and reduced regeneration requests from 38.9% to 6.1% of sentences.
中文翻译
少样本语音克隆使得以多个合成语音员廉价生成日常日语金融评论成为可能，但诸如“市场”（shijo vs. ichiba）或“总体趋势”（taisei vs. oze）等具有多种读音的汉字词仍常被误读。一种常见的质量检查方式是使用自动语音识别（ASR）转录每句合成语音，将输入和转录文本转换为平假名，并在不匹配时重新生成。我们发现这种检查机制在两个方向上都不可靠：当音频中存在误读时，ASR语言模型经常将期望的汉字写回，掩盖错误；当音频正确时，普通识别错误也会触发不必要的重新生成。我们提出一种读音层级检查方法，使用连接时序分类（CTC）平假名识别器对预期读音及其易混淆替代读音进行评分，并在替代读音更能解释音频时标记该词。在覆盖30个金融术语的三个人工语音克隆生产语音的540个语句中，注入受控条件下的误读，基于转录的检查检测到78.9%的误读，误报率为44.4%；而所提检查方法检测到91.7%，误报率仅为5.0%（AUC 0.990）。在自然汉字输入中，它捕捉到了转录检查遗漏的一个前端错误，并将重新生成请求从38.9%降至6.1%的句子。
👤 Cheng Zhang 📅 2026-10-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.38005]
"It Would Make a Geordie Feel at Home": Regional Accent Perceptions and Speech Accommodation in Voice AI
“这会让一个盖尔人感到宾至如归”：语音AI中的区域口音感知与言语适应
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often perform less accurately for speakers of regional dialects, but less is known about how users respond to these limitations and how regional voices produced by voice assistants (VAs) are evaluated through a sociolinguistic lens. This study investigates these issues among speakers of Newcastle English (Geordies), examining both linguistic accommodation to voice technologies and perceptions of regional versus mainstream-accented VA voices. Participants interacted with a simulated VA that spoke either Newcastle English or Standard Southern British English (SSBE). They subsequently completed semi-structured interviews about their experiences with the experimental VA and voice technologies, language use, accent attitudes, and place orientation. The findings reveal a two-way process of sociolinguistic bias. The Geordie VA was associated with familiarity, warmth, personality, and local belonging, but also with concerns about professionalism and authority, whereas the SSBE voice was perceived as more neutral, clear, and credible. Participants also reported adapting their speech when voice technologies failed to recognise them, most commonly by shifting to a more mainstream accent, enunciating more clearly, and speaking more slowly. These strategies are similar to accommodation in human-human interaction and reflect users' perceptions of what technologies can recognise, also known as perceived intelligibility pressures. Overall, the findings demonstrate that voice technologies can reproduce existing accent biases both by encouraging users to suppress regional features and by activating established social stereotypes through synthetic voices. The study positions sociolinguistics as a bridge between understanding human-AI interaction and designing inclusive technologies around users' own linguistic priorities.
中文翻译
自动语音识别（ASR）系统对区域性方言说话者的识别准确率通常较低，但人们对用户如何回应这些局限性，以及语音助手（VA）产生的区域口音如何被社会语言学视角评价了解较少。本研究聚焦于纽卡斯尔英语（盖尔人）使用者，探讨他们对语音技术的语言适应过程，以及对区域口音与主流口音语音助手声音的感知差异。参与者与模拟语音助手互动，该助手使用纽卡斯尔英语或标准南部英国英语（SSBE）。随后完成半结构化访谈，内容涉及对实验语音助手及语音技术的体验、语言使用、口音态度和地域认同感。研究发现存在双向社会语言偏见：盖尔人语音助手关联着熟悉感、亲切感、个性和地方归属感，但也引发对专业性和权威性的担忧；而SSBE语音则被认为更中立、清晰且可信。参与者还报告在语音技术无法识别自己时调整自己的发音，最常见策略包括转向更主流口音、更清晰地发音和放慢语速。这些策略与人际交流中的适应行为类似，反映了用户对技术识别能力的感知，即所谓的‘可理解性压力’。总体而言，研究显示语音技术既通过鼓励用户抑制区域特征，又通过合成语音激活既有社会刻板印象，再现了现有的口音偏见。该研究将社会语言学定位为理解人机交互与围绕用户自身语言优先级设计包容性技术之间的桥梁。
👤 Dana Serditova, Kevin Tang, Jochen Steffens 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
[2610.37912]
Beyond Utterance-Level Speaker Similarity: Perception-Aligned Segmental Evaluation of Accent in Speech Generation
超越话语级说话人相似性：面向语音生成中口音的感知对齐分段评估
Utterance-level similarity ratings do not reveal where perceived accent differences occur in generated speech. We introduce a segmental evaluation protocol combining speaker and accent similarity ratings with subword annotations of perceived accent differences provided by listeners from target accent regions. Using newly collected recordings, 47 retained listeners evaluate 200 content-matched reference--candidate pairs spanning four Scottish and Australian voices, two zero-shot TTS systems, and an articulation-based voice conversion system. Subword annotations locate salient pronunciation differences and sometimes yield different system rankings from similarity ratings. Speaker and accent ratings remain strongly associated ($ρ= 0.71$), highlighting the value of examining local perceptual evidence beyond these scores. Evaluation of seven objective distances reveals weak correspondence with subword selections, with correlations reaching at most $ρ= 0.22$. We release code, data, and an interactive demo to support the development of perceptually grounded segmental accent evaluation.
中文翻译
话语级相似性评分无法揭示生成语音中感知口音差异的具体位置。我们提出一种分段评估协议，结合说话人与口音相似性评分，以及来自目标口音地区听众提供的感知口音差异的子词标注。基于新收集的录音数据，47名保留听众评估了200对内容匹配的参考-候选语音对，涵盖四种苏格兰和澳大利亚口音、两种零样本TTS系统及一种基于发音的语音转换系统。子词标注定位了显著的发音差异，有时导致系统排名与相似性评分不一致。说话人与口音评分仍高度相关（ρ=0.71），凸显了考察局部感知证据的重要性。对七种客观距离度量的评估显示其与子词选择的相关性较弱，最大相关系数仅为ρ=0.22。我们公开代码、数据及交互式演示，以支持感知基础的分段口音评估发展。
👤 Jinzuomu Zhong, Alice Ross, Catherine Lai, Korin Richmond 📅 2026-10-27 🔗 arXiv
